
==================== FILE: C:\Users\ASUS\Downloads\TOOL_2D_ANIM_API_FIX_1\TOOL_2D_ANIM\.github\workflows\render-2k-video.yml ====================
name: Distributed 1080p 60FPS Video Renderer

on:
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      scriptId:
        description: 'Identifier for the animation script'
        required: true
        default: 'story_001'
      totalFrames:
        description: 'Total number of frames to render (e.g. 18000 for 5 mins @ 60fps)'
        required: true
        default: '480'
      chunkCount:
        description: 'Number of parallel chunks (1 = Full single-runner, 2-10 = Parallel chunks)'
        required: true
        default: '1'
      scenePayloadUrl:
        description: 'Signed URL or raw JSON string containing the Scene Graph'
        required: false
        default: ''

permissions:
  contents: write

jobs:
  prepare-matrix:
    name: Calculate Frame Chunks
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    outputs:
      chunks: ${{ steps.set-matrix.outputs.chunks }}
    steps:
      - name: Compute Frame Ranges
        id: set-matrix
        uses: actions/github-script@v7
        with:
          script: |
            let totalFrames = parseInt('${{ github.event.inputs.totalFrames }}', 10) || 480;
            const chunkCount = Math.max(1, parseInt('${{ github.event.inputs.chunkCount }}', 10) || 1);
            const rawPayload = `${{ github.event.inputs.scenePayloadUrl }}`.trim();
            if (rawPayload && rawPayload.startsWith('{')) {
              try {
                const parsed = JSON.parse(rawPayload);
                if (parsed.totalDurationInFrames) {
                  totalFrames = parseInt(parsed.totalDurationInFrames, 10);
                } else if (Array.isArray(parsed.scenes) && parsed.scenes.length > 0) {
                  const maxEnd = parsed.scenes.reduce((acc, s) => {
                    const start = Number(s?.startFrame) || 0;
                    const dur = Number(s?.durationInFrames) || 120;
                    return Math.max(acc, start + dur);
                  }, 0);
                  if (maxEnd > 0) totalFrames = maxEnd;
                }
              } catch (e) {
                // Ignore parse errors, stick to totalFrames
              }
            }
            const chunkSize = Math.ceil(totalFrames / chunkCount);
            const chunks = [];

            for (let i = 0; i < chunkCount; i++) {
              const start = i * chunkSize;
              const end = Math.min((i + 1) * chunkSize - 1, totalFrames - 1);
              if (start <= totalFrames - 1) {
                chunks.push({
                  index: i,
                  startFrame: start,
                  endFrame: end,
                  paddedIndex: String(i).padStart(3, '0')
                });
              }
            }
            core.setOutput('chunks', JSON.stringify(chunks));

  render-chunk:
    name: Render Chunk ${{ matrix.chunk.paddedIndex }}
    needs: prepare-matrix
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        chunk: ${{ fromJson(needs.prepare-matrix.outputs.chunks) }}
    steps:
      - name: Checkout Codebase
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Setup Node.js
        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: 22
          cache: 'npm'
          cache-dependency-path: web-studio/package.json

      - name: Install System Dependencies for Headless Chromium
        run: |
          sudo apt-get update
          sudo apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
            ffmpeg \
            libnss3 \
            libatk-bridge2.0-0 \
            libx11-xcb1 \
            libxcomposite1 \
            libxdamage1 \
            libxrandr2 \
            libgbm1 \
            fonts-noto-color-emoji || true
          sudo apt-get install -y libasound2t64 || sudo apt-get install -y libasound2 || true

      - name: Install Project Dependencies
        working-directory: web-studio
        run: npm ci --prefer-offline || npm install --prefer-offline

      - name: Prepare Scene Payload & Props File
        working-directory: web-studio
        env:
          RAW_PAYLOAD: ${{ github.event.inputs.scenePayloadUrl }}
          TOTAL_FRAMES: ${{ github.event.inputs.totalFrames }}
        run: |
          mkdir -p ../dist/chunks
          node -e "
            const fs = require('fs');
            const raw = process.env.RAW_PAYLOAD || '';
            const total = parseInt(process.env.TOTAL_FRAMES || '480', 10);
            let payload = { totalFrames: total };
            if (raw.trim().startsWith('{')) {
              try {
                const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
                payload = {
                  totalFrames: total,
                  sceneGraph: parsed,
                  ...parsed,
                };
              } catch (e) {
                console.warn('Fallback to default props:', e.message);
              }
            }
            fs.writeFileSync('../dist/props.json', JSON.stringify(payload));
          "

      - name: Render Video Chunk via Remotion & FFmpeg In-Memory Pipe
        working-directory: web-studio
        env:
          START_FRAME: ${{ matrix.chunk.startFrame }}
          END_FRAME: ${{ matrix.chunk.endFrame }}
          CHUNK_INDEX: ${{ matrix.chunk.paddedIndex }}
        run: |
          echo "Rendering frames $START_FRAME to $END_FRAME (Chunk $CHUNK_INDEX)..."
          
          # Render chunk using Remotion with libx264 animation profile
          npx remotion render src/remotion/index.ts MainScene ../dist/chunks/chunk_${CHUNK_INDEX}.mp4 \
            --frames=${START_FRAME}-${END_FRAME} \
            --props=../dist/props.json \
            --pixel-format=yuv420p \
            --codec=h264 \
            --crf=18 \
            --concurrency=4 \
            --log=info

      - name: Upload Chunk Artifact
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: chunk-${{ matrix.chunk.paddedIndex }}
          path: dist/chunks/chunk_${{ matrix.chunk.paddedIndex }}.mp4
          retention-days: 1

  stitch-and-release:
    name: Assemble & Publish 1080p Video
    needs: [prepare-matrix, render-chunk]
    if: always() && needs.prepare-matrix.result == 'success'
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Checkout Repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Setup FFmpeg
        run: sudo apt-get update && sudo apt-get install -y ffmpeg || true

      - name: Download All Chunk Artifacts
        uses: actions/download-artifact@v4
        with:
          path: downloaded_chunks
          pattern: chunk-*
          merge-multiple: true

      - name: Assemble Video (Full Mode or Concat)
        run: |
          mkdir -p output
          ls -lah downloaded_chunks/

          CHUNK_FILES=( $(ls downloaded_chunks/chunk_*.mp4 2>/dev/null | sort -V) )
          COUNT=${#CHUNK_FILES[@]}
          echo "TÃ¬m tháº¥y $COUNT chunk video."

          if [ "$COUNT" -eq 0 ]; then
            echo "Lá»–I: KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y file chunk video nÃ o Ä‘Æ°á»£c render!"
            exit 1
          fi

          if [ "$COUNT" -eq 1 ]; then
            echo "Chá»‰ cÃ³ 1 file duy nháº¥t (Cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ Full Single Runner) -> Copy trá»±c tiáº¿p, khÃ´ng cáº§n ghÃ©p concat!"
            cp "${CHUNK_FILES[0]}" output/final_raw.mp4
          else
            echo "GhÃ©p $COUNT chunks theo Ä‘Ãºng thá»© tá»±..."
            for f in "${CHUNK_FILES[@]}"; do
              echo "file '$PWD/$f'" >> concat_list.txt
            done
            cat concat_list.txt
            ffmpeg -f concat -safe 0 -i concat_list.txt -c copy output/final_raw.mp4
          fi

          # Tá»‘i Æ°u hÃ³a FastStart cho Web Streaming
          ffmpeg -i output/final_raw.mp4 -c copy -movflags +faststart output/story_${{ github.event.inputs.scriptId }}_1080p60fps.mp4

      - name: Create GitHub Release and Upload 1080p Video
        id: create_release
        uses: softprops/action-gh-release@v2
        env:
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
        with:
          tag_name: render-${{ github.event.inputs.scriptId }}-${{ github.run_id }}
          name: "Render 1080p 60FPS: ${{ github.event.inputs.scriptId }}"
          body: |
            ### Automated Render Completion
            - **Script ID:** `${{ github.event.inputs.scriptId }}`
            - **Resolution:** `1920x1080 (Full HD)`
            - **Frame Rate:** `60 FPS`
            - **Total Frames:** `${{ github.event.inputs.totalFrames }}`
            - **Parallel Chunks:** `${{ github.event.inputs.chunkCount }}`
          files: output/story_${{ github.event.inputs.scriptId }}_1080p60fps.mp4
          draft: false
          prerelease: false

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const dir = path.join(__dirname, '../public/assets/props');
fs.readdirSync(dir).filter(f => f.endsWith('.svg')).forEach(f => {
  const c = fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir, f), 'utf8');
  const m = c.match(/viewBox=["']([^"']+)["']/);
  console.log(f.padEnd(20), m ? m[1] : 'NO VIEWBOX');
});


==================== FILE: C:\Users\ASUS\Downloads\TOOL_2D_ANIM_API_FIX_1\TOOL_2D_ANIM\web-studio\scripts\export_joint_timeline.ts ====================
import * as fs from "fs";
import * as path from "path";
import { computeStickmanFK } from "../src/remotion/components/character/StickmanRig";
import { StickmanState, Point2D } from "../src/remotion/types/character";
import {
  solveThighAngleForWorldFootX,
  computeWorldFootXFromThighAngle,
} from "../src/remotion/compositions/WalkRunReferenceScene";

function interpolate(
  input: number,
  inputRange: number[],
  outputRange: number[]
): number {
  if (input <= inputRange[0]) return outputRange[0];
  if (input >= inputRange[inputRange.length - 1])
    return outputRange[outputRange.length - 1];

  for (let i = 0; i < inputRange.length - 1; i++) {
    const minIn = inputRange[i];
    const maxIn = inputRange[i + 1];
    if (input >= minIn && input <= maxIn) {
      const minOut = outputRange[i];
      const maxOut = outputRange[i + 1];
      const t = (input - minIn) / (maxIn - minIn);
      return minOut + t * (maxOut - minOut);
    }
  }
  return outputRange[outputRange.length - 1];
}

function calculateFrameState(frame: number) {
  const fps = 60;
  const GROUND_Y = 850;
  const PART1_END_FRAME = 180;

  const isPart1 = frame < PART1_END_FRAME;

  let charX = 1150;
  let charY = GROUND_Y;
  let facing: "left" | "right" = "right";
  let torsoTilt = 0;
  let headTilt = 0;
  let pupilGaze: Point2D = { x: -0.7, y: 0 };
  let leftUpperArmAngle = 12;
  let leftLowerArmAngle = 6;
  let rightUpperArmAngle = -18;
  let rightLowerArmAngle = -140;
  let leftThighAngle = 9.47;
  let leftCalfAngle = 0;
  let rightThighAngle = -9.47;
  let rightCalfAngle = 0;
  let phaseName = "";
  let stanceFoot = "Cáº£ 2 chÃ¢n";
  let targetPlantX: number | null = null;

  const PLANT_R_X = 1694.03;
  const PLANT_L_X = PLANT_R_X - 60; // 1634.03
  const SETTLED_X = PLANT_R_X - 30; // 1664.03

  if (isPart1) {
    facing = "right";
    pupilGaze = { x: -0.7, y: 0 };
    torsoTilt = 0;
    headTilt = 0;

    rightUpperArmAngle = -18;
    rightLowerArmAngle = -140;
    leftUpperArmAngle = 12;
    leftLowerArmAngle = 6;

    if (frame <= 110) {
      const walkProg = frame / 110;
      const easedWalkProg = interpolate(walkProg, [0, 0.1, 0.85, 1], [0, 0.08, 0.88, 1]);
      charX = 1150 - 310 * easedWalkProg;

      if (frame < 10) {
        const p = frame / 10;
        const targetSpan = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [100, 24]);
        const targetFootL_X = charX - targetSpan / 2;
        const targetFootR_X = charX + targetSpan / 2;
        leftThighAngle = solveThighAngleForWorldFootX(charX, targetFootL_X, true);
        rightThighAngle = solveThighAngleForWorldFootX(charX, targetFootR_X, false);
        leftCalfAngle = 0;
        rightCalfAngle = 0;
        phaseName = "Má»Ÿ cáº£nh: Tiáº¿n sang trÃ¡i, thu chÃ¢n";
        stanceFoot = "ChÃ¢n pháº£i trá»¥";
      } else if (frame < 52) {
        const stepProgress = (frame - 10) / 42;
        const cycleRad = stepProgress * Math.PI;
        const swingProgress = Math.sin(cycleRad);
        const currentSpan = 24 + swingProgress * (178 - 24);
        const targetFootL_X = charX - currentSpan / 2;
        const targetFootR_X = charX + currentSpan / 2;

        leftThighAngle = solveThighAngleForWorldFootX(charX, targetFootL_X, true);
        rightThighAngle = solveThighAngleForWorldFootX(charX, targetFootR_X, false);
        leftCalfAngle = swingProgress * 5;
        rightCalfAngle = 0;
        stanceFoot = "ChÃ¢n pháº£i trá»¥ (f10-f52)";
        phaseName = "Pha bÆ°á»›c 1 (ChÃ¢n trÃ¡i vung)";
      } else if (frame < 94) {
        const stepProgress = (frame - 52) / 42;
        const cycleRad = stepProgress * Math.PI;
        const swingProgress = Math.sin(cycleRad);
        const currentSpan = 24 + swingProgress * (180 - 24);
        const targetFootR_X = charX - currentSpan / 2;
        const targetFootL_X = charX + currentSpan / 2;

        leftThighAngle = solveThighAngleForWorldFootX(charX, targetFootL_X, true);
        rightThighAngle = solveThighAngleForWorldFootX(charX, targetFootR_X, false);
        leftCalfAngle = 0;
        rightCalfAngle = swingProgress * 5;
        stanceFoot = "ChÃ¢n trÃ¡i trá»¥ (f52-f94)";
        phaseName = "Pha bÆ°á»›c 2 (ChÃ¢n pháº£i vung)";
      } else {
        const p = (frame - 94) / 16;
        const currentSpan = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [24, 134]);
        const targetFootL_X = charX - currentSpan / 2;
        const targetFootR_X = charX + currentSpan / 2;

        leftThighAngle = solveThighAngleForWorldFootX(charX, targetFootL_X, true);
        rightThighAngle = solveThighAngleForWorldFootX(charX, targetFootR_X, false);

        phaseName = "Háº¡ tay khá»i mÃ¡, xoay máº¯t vá» trÆ°á»›c";

      leftLowerArmAngle = interpolate(stepP, [0, 1], [-45, -60]);

      stanceFoot = "ChÃ¢n trÃ¡i trá»¥ (KhoÃ¡ X=773) | ChÃ¢n pháº£i nháº¥c vÃ²ng cung";

      torsoTilt = interpolate(prepP, [0, 1], [0, 15]);
      headTilt = 0;
      pupilGaze = { x: 0.8, y: 0 };

      rightUpperArmAngle = interpolate(prepP, [0, 1], [-15, 25]);
      rightLowerArmAngle = interpolate(prepP, [0, 1], [-60, -75]);
      leftUpperArmAngle = interpolate(prepP, [0, 1], [10, -45]);
      leftLowerArmAngle = interpolate(prepP, [0, 1], [-60, -75]);

      const startLeftThigh = solveThighAngleForWorldFootX(840, 773, true);
      const startRightThigh = solveThighAngleForWorldFootX(840, 883, false);

      leftThighAngle = interpolate(prepP, [0, 1], [startLeftThigh, -30]);
      leftCalfAngle = interpolate(prepP, [0, 1], [0, 40]);
      rightThighAngle = interpolate(prepP, [0, 1], [startRightThigh, 30]);
      rightCalfAngle = 0;

      phaseName = "NghiÃªng thÃ¢n 15Â° & hÃ²a nhá»‹p váº­n tá»‘c xuáº¥t phÃ¡t cháº¡y";
      stanceFoot = "ChÃ¢n trÃ¡i Ä‘áº¡p Ä‘Ã  sau, chÃ¢n pháº£i vung trÆ°á»›c";
    } else if (frame < 330) {
      const runFrames = frame - 195;
      const totalRunProg = runFrames / 134;
      const easedRunProg = interpolate(totalRunProg, [0, 0.25, 1], [0, 0.18, 1]);
      charX = 840 + 715 * easedRunProg;

      torsoTilt = 15;
      headTilt = 0;
      pupilGaze = { x: 0.8, y: 0 };

      const runRad = ((runFrames % 52) / 52) * Math.PI * 2 - Math.PI / 2;
      const flightLift = Math.max(0, Math.sin(runRad * 2) * 14);
      charY = GROUND_Y - flightLift;

      leftThighAngle = Math.sin(runRad) * 30;
      leftCalfAngle = Math.max(0, -Math.sin(runRad) * 40);
      rightThighAngle = -Math.sin(runRad) * 30;
      rightCalfAngle = Math.max(0, Math.sin(runRad) * 40);

      leftUpperArmAngle = -10 + Math.sin(runRad) * 35;
      leftLowerArmAngle = -65 - Math.abs(Math.sin(runRad)) * 10;
      rightUpperArmAngle = -10 - Math.sin(runRad) * 35;
      rightLowerArmAngle = -65 - Math.abs(Math.sin(runRad)) * 10;

      const isFlight = flightLift > 5;
      stanceFoot = isFlight
        ? "BAY TRÃŠN KHÃ”NG (Flight Phase)"
        : Math.sin(runRad) > 0
        ? "ChÃ¢n trÃ¡i tiáº¿p Ä‘áº¥t"
        : "ChÃ¢n pháº£i tiáº¿p Ä‘áº¥t";
      phaseName = isFlight
        ? "Giai Ä‘oáº¡n bay (Flight Phase, Lift: 14px)"
        : "Cháº¡y Ä‘á»u (Cadence: 0.43s / bÆ°á»›c)";
    } else {
      charY = GROUND_Y;

      if (frame < 333) {
        const p = (frame - 329) / 4;
        charX = 1555 + (1572 - 1555) * p;

        rightThighAngle = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [-26.55, -20]);
        rightCalfAngle = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [35.40, 0]);

        leftThighAngle = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [26.55, 15]);
        leftCalfAngle = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [0, 10]);

        torsoTilt = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [15, 13]);
        headTilt = 0;

        leftUpperArmAngle = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [20.98, 0]);
        leftLowerArmAngle = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [-73.85, -50]);
        rightUpperArmAngle = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [-40.98, -25]);
        rightLowerArmAngle = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [-73.85, -50]);

        phaseName = "HÃ£m tá»‘c: Káº¿ thá»«a chÃ¢n pháº£i cháº¡m Ä‘áº¥t (Háº¡ gá»‘i)";
        stanceFoot = "ChÃ¢n pháº£i tiáº¿p Ä‘áº¥t (Pha háº¡ chÃ¢n)";
      } else if (frame < 355) {
        const q = (frame - 333) / 22;
        const easedQ = q * (2 - q);
        charX = 1572 + (SETTLED_X - 1572) * easedQ;

        rightThighAngle = solveThighAngleForWorldFootX(charX, PLANT_R_X, false);
        rightCalfAngle = 0;
        targetPlantX = PLANT_R_X;

        const leftTargetX = interpolate(q, [0, 1], [1500.37, PLANT_L_X]);
        leftThighAngle = solveThighAngleForWorldFootX(charX, leftTargetX, true);
        leftCalfAngle = Math.sin(q * Math.PI) * 18;

        torsoTilt = interpolate(q, [0, 1], [13, 2]);
        headTilt = 0;

        leftUpperArmAngle = interpolate(q, [0, 1], [0, 10]);
        leftLowerArmAngle = interpolate(q, [0, 1], [-50, -20]);
        rightUpperArmAngle = interpolate(q, [0, 1], [-25, -16]);
        rightLowerArmAngle = interpolate(q, [0, 1], [-50, -20]);

        phaseName = "HÃ£m tá»‘c: KhoÃ¡ chÃ¢n pháº£i trá»¥, chÃ¢n trÃ¡i bÆ°á»›c Ä‘á»¡";
        stanceFoot = `ChÃ¢n pháº£i trá»¥ (KhoÃ¡ X=${PLANT_R_X.toFixed(1)}) | ChÃ¢n trÃ¡i bÆ°á»›c Ä‘á»¡`;
      } else {
        const settleP = Math.min(1, (frame - 355) / 35);
        charX = SETTLED_X;

        rightThighAngle = solveThighAngleForWorldFootX(charX, PLANT_R_X, false);
        rightCalfAngle = 0;
        leftThighAngle = solveThighAngleForWorldFootX(charX, PLANT_L_X, true);
        leftCalfAngle = 0;
        targetPlantX = PLANT_R_X;

        torsoTilt = interpolate(settleP, [0, 1], [2, 0]);
        headTilt = 0;

        leftUpperArmAngle = 10;
        leftLowerArmAngle = interpolate(settleP, [0, 1], [-20, -12]);
        rightUpperArmAngle = -16;
        rightLowerArmAngle = interpolate(settleP, [0, 1], [-20, -10]);

        stanceFoot = "Cáº£ 2 chÃ¢n khoÃ¡ Ä‘áº¥t á»•n Ä‘á»‹nh 100%";
        phaseName = settleP >= 1 ? "Äá»©ng á»•n Ä‘á»‹nh thÄƒng báº±ng (ThÃ¢n tháº³ng)" : "á»”n Ä‘á»‹nh tháº¿ Ä‘á»©ng hai chÃ¢n";
      }
    }
  }

  const stickmanState: StickmanState = {
    x: charX,
    y: charY,
    scale: 1.0,
    facing,
    pose: "standing",
    eyes: "neutral",
    eyebrows: "neutral",
    mouth: "smile",
    pupilGaze,
    hat: true,
    showDebugMarkers: true,
    limbAngles: {
      torso: torsoTilt,
      head: headTilt,
      leftUpperArm: leftUpperArmAngle,
      leftLowerArm: leftLowerArmAngle,
      rightUpperArm: rightUpperArmAngle,
      rightLowerArm: rightLowerArmAngle,
      leftThigh: leftThighAngle,
      leftCalf: leftCalfAngle,
      rightThigh: rightThighAngle,
      rightCalf: rightCalfAngle,
    },
  };

  const fk = computeStickmanFK(stickmanState);
  const legSpan = Math.abs(fk.rightFoot.x - fk.leftFoot.x);

  let slipPx = 0;
  if (targetPlantX !== null) {
    const isStanceRight = stanceFoot.includes("ChÃ¢n pháº£i trá»¥") || stanceFoot.includes("Cáº£ 2 chÃ¢n");
    const activeStanceFootX = isStanceRight ? fk.rightFoot.x : fk.leftFoot.x;
    slipPx = Math.abs(activeStanceFootX - targetPlantX);
  }

  return {
    frame,
    timeSec: frame / fps,
    phase: phaseName,
    charX,
    charY,
    torsoTilt,
    leftThighAngle,
    leftCalfAngle,
    rightThighAngle,
    rightCalfAngle,
    leftUpperArmAngle,
    leftLowerArmAngle,
    rightUpperArmAngle,
    rightLowerArmAngle,
    fk,
    legSpan,
    stanceFoot,
    targetPlantX: targetPlantX ?? 0,
    slipPx,
  };
}

export function generateCsv() {
  const TOTAL_FRAMES = 391; // 0 to 390
  const rows: string[] = [];

  rows.push(
    [
      "frame",
      "time_s",
      "phase",
      "root_x",
      "root_y",
      "torso_tilt_deg",
      "head_center_x",
      "head_center_y",
      "neck_x",
      "neck_y",
      "left_shoulder_x",
      "left_shoulder_y",
      "right_shoulder_x",
      "right_shoulder_y",
      "left_elbow_x",
      "left_elbow_y",
      "right_elbow_x",
      "right_elbow_y",
      "left_wrist_x",
      "left_wrist_y",
      "right_wrist_x",
      "right_wrist_y",
      "left_hip_x",
      "left_hip_y",
      "right_hip_x",
      "right_hip_y",
      "left_knee_x",
      "left_knee_y",
      "right_knee_x",
      "right_knee_y",
      "left_foot_x",
      "left_foot_y",
      "right_foot_x",
      "right_foot_y",
      "leg_span_px",
      "stance_foot",
      "target_plant_x",
      "slip_error_px",
    ].join(",")
  );

  for (let f = 0; f < TOTAL_FRAMES; f++) {
    const d = calculateFrameState(f);
    const row = [
      d.frame,
      d.timeSec.toFixed(3),
      `"${d.phase.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`,
      d.charX.toFixed(2),
      d.charY.toFixed(2),
      d.torsoTilt.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.headCenter.x.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.headCenter.y.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.neck.x.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.neck.y.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.leftShoulder.x.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.leftShoulder.y.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.rightShoulder.x.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.rightShoulder.y.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.leftElbow.x.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.leftElbow.y.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.rightElbow.x.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.rightElbow.y.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.leftWrist.x.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.leftWrist.y.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.rightWrist.x.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.rightWrist.y.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.leftHip.x.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.leftHip.y.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.rightHip.x.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.rightHip.y.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.leftKnee.x.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.leftKnee.y.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.rightKnee.x.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.rightKnee.y.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.leftFoot.x.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.leftFoot.y.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.rightFoot.x.toFixed(2),
      d.fk.rightFoot.y.toFixed(2),
      d.legSpan.toFixed(2),
      `"${d.stanceFoot.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`,
      d.targetPlantX.toFixed(2),
      d.slipPx.toFixed(4),
    ].join(",");
    rows.push(row);
  }

  const outDir = path.resolve(__dirname, "../../out");
  if (!fs.existsSync(outDir)) {
    fs.mkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true });
  }
  const csvPath = path.join(outDir, "joint_coordinates_timeline.csv");
  fs.writeFileSync(csvPath, rows.join("\n"), "utf-8");
  console.log(`Successfully generated CSV timeline at ${csvPath} (${rows.length} rows)`);
}

generateCsv();

import { computeStickmanFK } from "../src/remotion/components/character/StickmanRig";
import { StickmanState } from "../src/remotion/types/character";
import {
  solveThighAngleForWorldFootX,
  computeWorldFootXFromThighAngle,
} from "../src/remotion/compositions/WalkRunReferenceScene";

function interpolate(
  input: number,
  inputRange: number[],
  outputRange: number[]
): number {
  if (input <= inputRange[0]) return outputRange[0];
  if (input >= inputRange[inputRange.length - 1])
    return outputRange[outputRange.length - 1];

  for (let i = 0; i < inputRange.length - 1; i++) {
    const minIn = inputRange[i];
    const maxIn = inputRange[i + 1];
    if (input >= minIn && input <= maxIn) {
      const minOut = outputRange[i];
      const maxOut = outputRange[i + 1];
      const t = (input - minIn) / (maxIn - minIn);
      return minOut + t * (maxOut - minOut);
    }
  }
  return outputRange[outputRange.length - 1];
}

console.log("Testing braking transition around f329-f335...");

import * as fs from "fs";
import * as path from "path";
import {
  solveThighAngleForWorldFootX,
  computeWorldFootXFromThighAngle,
} from "../src/remotion/compositions/WalkRunReferenceScene";
import { computeStickmanFK } from "../src/remotion/components/character/StickmanRig";
import { StickmanState } from "../src/remotion/types/character";

console.log("=================================================================");
console.log("1. KIá»‚M CHá»¨NG Dá»® LIá»†U CSV Táº I CÃC Má»C QUAN TRá»ŒNG (KEY FRAMES)");
console.log("=================================================================");

const csvPath = path.resolve(__dirname, "../../out/joint_coordinates_timeline.csv");
const lines = fs.readFileSync(csvPath, "utf-8").trim().split("\n");

function getFrameData(frameNum: number) {
  const line = lines[frameNum + 1];
  const parts: string[] = [];
  let current = "";
  let inQuotes = false;
  for (let i = 0; i < line.length; i++) {
    const c = line[i];
    if (c === '"') {
      inQuotes = !inQuotes;
    } else if (c === "," && !inQuotes) {
      parts.push(current);
      current = "";
    } else {
      current += c;
    }
  }
  parts.push(current);

  return {
    frame: parseInt(parts[0]),
    time: parts[1],
    phase: parts[2],
    rootX: parseFloat(parts[3]),
    rootY: parseFloat(parts[4]),
    torsoTilt: parseFloat(parts[5]),
    leftShoulder: { x: parseFloat(parts[10]), y: parseFloat(parts[11]) },
    rightShoulder: { x: parseFloat(parts[12]), y: parseFloat(parts[13]) },
    leftWrist: { x: parseFloat(parts[18]), y: parseFloat(parts[19]) },
    rightWrist: { x: parseFloat(parts[20]), y: parseFloat(parts[21]) },
    leftHip: { x: parseFloat(parts[22]), y: parseFloat(parts[23]) },
    rightHip: { x: parseFloat(parts[24]), y: parseFloat(parts[25]) },
    leftKnee: { x: parseFloat(parts[26]), y: parseFloat(parts[27]) },
    rightKnee: { x: parseFloat(parts[28]), y: parseFloat(parts[29]) },
    leftFoot: { x: parseFloat(parts[30]), y: parseFloat(parts[31]) },
    rightFoot: { x: parseFloat(parts[32]), y: parseFloat(parts[33]) },
    legSpan: parseFloat(parts[34]),
    stance: parts[35],
    targetPlantX: parseFloat(parts[36]),
    slipError: parseFloat(parts[37]),
  };
}

const checkFrames = [150, 179, 180, 185, 194, 195, 329, 330, 333, 350, 389];
for (const f of checkFrames) {
  const d = getFrameData(f);
  console.log(`\n>>> FRAME f${d.frame} (${d.time}s) | Pha: ${d.phase}`);
  console.log(`  Root: (${d.rootX}, ${d.rootY}) | Torso: ${d.torsoTilt}Â°`);
  console.log(`  Left Hip: (${d.leftHip.x}, ${d.leftHip.y}) -> Knee: (${d.leftKnee.x}, ${d.leftKnee.y}) -> Foot: (${d.leftFoot.x}, ${d.leftFoot.y})`);
  console.log(`  Right Hip: (${d.rightHip.x}, ${d.rightHip.y}) -> Knee: (${d.rightKnee.x}, ${d.rightKnee.y}) -> Foot: (${d.rightFoot.x}, ${d.rightFoot.y})`);
  console.log(`  Leg Span: ${d.legSpan} px | Stance: ${d.stance} | Slip Error: ${d.slipError} px`);

  // Check 1: Inverted V - Left foot < Left hip, Right foot > Right hip at f150, f179, f180
  if (f === 150 || f === 179 || f === 180) {
    const isLeftFootLeftOfHip = d.leftFoot.x < d.leftHip.x;
    const isRightFootRightOfHip = d.rightFoot.x > d.rightHip.x;
    const noCrossing = d.leftFoot.x < d.rightFoot.x && d.leftKnee.x < d.rightKnee.x;
    console.log(`  [ASSERT] KhÃ´ng báº¯t chÃ©o (Inverted V): LeftFoot < LeftHip (${isLeftFootLeftOfHip}), RightFoot > RightHip (${isRightFootRightOfHip}), NoCrossing (${noCrossing})`);
    if (!isLeftFootLeftOfHip || !isRightFootRightOfHip || !noCrossing) {
      console.error(`  FAIL: Thigh crossing detected at frame ${f}!`);
      process.exit(1);
    }
  }

  // Check 2: f389 Leg span should be exactly 60.00px
  if (f === 389) {
    console.log(`  [ASSERT] Khoáº£ng cÃ¡ch chÃ¢n táº¡i f389: ${d.legSpan} px (Má»¥c tiÃªu: 60.00 px)`);
    if (Math.abs(d.legSpan - 60.0) > 0.05) {
      console.error(`  FAIL: Leg span at f389 is ${d.legSpan}, expected 60.00px!`);
      process.exit(1);
    }
    console.log(`  [ASSERT] Sai sá»‘ trÆ°á»£t chÃ¢n trá»¥ táº¡i f389: ${d.slipError} px (Má»¥c tiÃªu: 0.00 px)`);
    if (d.slipError > 0.01) {
      console.error(`  FAIL: Slip error at f389 is ${d.slipError}, expected 0.00px!`);
      process.exit(1);
    }
  }
}

// Check 3: Continuity across boundaries
console.log("\n=================================================================");
console.log("2. KIá»‚M TRA Äá»˜ LIÃŠN Tá»¤C Váº¬N Tá»C Táº I RANH GIá»šI HÃ€NH Äá»˜NG");

  { name: "Facing Right, Scale 1.0, Right Foot (Outward stance)", facing: "right", scale: 1.0, charX: 840, targetFootWorldX: 907, isLeft: false },
  { name: "Facing Left, Scale 1.0, Left Foot (Outward stance)", facing: "left", scale: 1.0, charX: 840, targetFootWorldX: 907, isLeft: true },
  { name: "Facing Left, Scale 1.0, Right Foot (Outward stance)", facing: "left", scale: 1.0, charX: 840, targetFootWorldX: 773, isLeft: false },
  { name: "Facing Right, Scale 0.75, Left Foot", facing: "right", scale: 0.75, charX: 500, targetFootWorldX: 450, isLeft: true },
  { name: "Facing Right, Scale 0.75, Right Foot", facing: "right", scale: 0.75, charX: 500, targetFootWorldX: 550, isLeft: false },
  { name: "Facing Left, Scale 0.75, Left Foot", facing: "left", scale: 0.75, charX: 500, targetFootWorldX: 550, isLeft: true },
  { name: "Facing Left, Scale 0.75, Right Foot", facing: "left", scale: 0.75, charX: 500, targetFootWorldX: 450, isLeft: false },
  { name: "Travel Distance 450px: Plant Left", facing: "right", scale: 1.0, charX: 1600, targetFootWorldX: 1570, isLeft: true },
  { name: "Travel Distance 450px: Plant Right", facing: "right", scale: 1.0, charX: 1600, targetFootWorldX: 1630, isLeft: false },
];

    tc.charX,
    tc.targetFootWorldX,
    tc.isLeft,
    tc.scale,
    tc.facing
  );

  const state: StickmanState = {
    x: tc.charX,
    y: 850,
    scale: tc.scale,
    facing: tc.facing,
    pose: "standing",
    limbAngles: {
      leftThigh: tc.isLeft ? solvedThighAngle : 0,
      rightThigh: !tc.isLeft ? solvedThighAngle : 0,
    },
  };
  const fkResult = computeStickmanFK(state);
  const actualFootX = tc.isLeft ? fkResult.leftFoot.x : fkResult.rightFoot.x;
  const error = Math.abs(actualFootX - tc.targetFootWorldX);

  console.log(`    FK Render Foot X: ${actualFootX.toFixed(4)}px | Error: ${error.toFixed(6)}px`);

  if (error > 0.001) {
    console.error(`    FAIL: Error exceeds 0.001px threshold!`);
    allPassed = false;
  }
}

/**
 * AnimScript v0.1 Asset & Rig Registry
 * Strongly typed registry for props, character rigs, anchors, and interaction slots.
 */

import { Vector2, CharacterPose } from "../types";

export interface PropAnchorDef {
  name: string;
  // Offset relative to the prop pivot point
  calcOffset: (w: number, h: number) => Vector2;
}

export interface PropAssetDef {
  type: string;
  defaultWidth: number;
  defaultHeight: number;
  // Pivot point relative to bounding box top-left (0,0)
  pivot: (w: number, h: number) => Vector2;
  anchors: Record<string, (w: number, h: number) => Vector2>;
  occlusionTags?: string[];
}

export interface CharacterBoneLengths {
  headRadius: number;
  neckLength: number;
  torsoLength: number;
  pelvicHalfWidth: number;
  shoulderHalfWidth: number;
  upperArmLength: number;
  forearmLength: number;
  handLength: number;
  thighLength: number;
  shinLength: number;
  footLength: number;
}

export interface JointLimits {
  minDeg: number;
  maxDeg: number;
}

export interface CharacterRigDef {
  type: string;
  defaultHeight: number;
  getBones: (height: number) => CharacterBoneLengths;
  jointLimits: Record<string, JointLimits>;
}

// ============================================================================
// CHARACTER RIG DEFINITION (HUMANOID)
// ============================================================================

export function getHumanoidBones(height: number): CharacterBoneLengths {
  const scale = height / 600;
  return {
    headRadius: 46 * scale,
    neckLength: 16 * scale,
    torsoLength: 190 * scale,
    pelvicHalfWidth: 16 * scale,
    shoulderHalfWidth: 5 * scale,
    upperArmLength: 115 * scale,
    forearmLength: 110 * scale,
    handLength: 15 * scale,
    thighLength: 155 * scale,
    shinLength: 155 * scale,
    footLength: 22 * scale,
  };
}

export const HUMANOID_RIG: CharacterRigDef = {
  type: "human",
  defaultHeight: 600,
  getBones: getHumanoidBones,
  jointLimits: {
    leftShoulder: { minDeg: -180, maxDeg: 180 },
    rightShoulder: { minDeg: -180, maxDeg: 180 },
    leftElbow: { minDeg: -150, maxDeg: 150 },
    rightElbow: { minDeg: -150, maxDeg: 150 },
    leftHip: { minDeg: -120, maxDeg: 120 },
    rightHip: { minDeg: -120, maxDeg: 120 },
    leftKnee: { minDeg: -140, maxDeg: 140 },
    rightKnee: { minDeg: -140, maxDeg: 140 },
    neck: { minDeg: -40, maxDeg: 40 },
  },
};

/**
 * TÃ­nh toÃ¡n vá»‹ trÃ­ cÃ¡c Ä‘iá»ƒm neo vÃ  khá»›p trÃªn Character dá»±a trÃªn chiá»u cao vÃ  pose hiá»‡n táº¡i.
 */
export function calculateCharacterAnchors(
  rootX: number,
  rootY: number,
  height: number,
  facing: "left" | "right",
  pose?: CharacterPose
): Record<string, Vector2> {
  const bones = getHumanoidBones(height);
  const facingDir = facing === "left" ? -1 : 1;

  const isSitting = Boolean(pose?.isSitting);
  const standingLegLength = bones.thighLength + bones.shinLength; // 310 px for h=600
  let hipY: number;
  if (pose?.sittingHipY !== undefined) {
    const sittingRatio = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (pose.sittingHipY - (-350)) / 130));
    hipY = rootY - standingLegLength + sittingRatio * (standingLegLength - 180);
  } else {
    hipY = isSitting ? rootY - 180 : rootY - standingLegLength;
  }
  if (pose?.crouchDepth) {
    hipY += pose.crouchDepth;
  }

  const spineTiltRad = ((pose?.headTilt ?? 0) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const neckX = rootX - Math.sin(spineTiltRad) * bones.torsoLength * facingDir;
  const neckY = hipY - Math.cos(spineTiltRad) * bones.torsoLength;

  const headCenterX = neckX - Math.sin(spineTiltRad) * (bones.neckLength + bones.headRadius) * facingDir;
  const headCenterY = neckY - Math.cos(spineTiltRad) * (bones.neckLength + bones.headRadius);
  const headTopY = headCenterY - bones.headRadius;

  // Äiá»ƒm neo vai náº±m sÃ¡t 2 bÃªn Ä‘á»‰nh thÃ¢n, gáº§n chá»— ná»‘i cá»• vá»›i thÃ¢n (neck + 4px theo há»‡ trá»¥c thÃ¢n)
  const neckDownX = Math.sin(spineTiltRad) * 4 * facingDir;
  const neckDownY = Math.cos(spineTiltRad) * 4;
  const cosTilt = Math.cos(spineTiltRad);
  const sinTilt = Math.sin(spineTiltRad);

  const leftShoulderX = neckX + neckDownX - cosTilt * bones.shoulderHalfWidth * facingDir;
  const leftShoulderY = neckY + neckDownY - sinTilt * bones.shoulderHalfWidth;
  const rightShoulderX = neckX + neckDownX + cosTilt * bones.shoulderHalfWidth * facingDir;
  const rightShoulderY = neckY + neckDownY + sinTilt * bones.shoulderHalfWidth;
  const shoulderY = (leftShoulderY + rightShoulderY) / 2;

  const leftHipX = rootX - bones.pelvicHalfWidth * facingDir;
  const rightHipX = rootX + bones.pelvicHalfWidth * facingDir;

  // Láº¥y gÃ³c tay tá»« pose theo quy Æ°á»›c SVG: gÃ³c Ã¢m vÆ°Æ¡n vá» phÃ­a trÆ°á»›c, gÃ³c dÆ°Æ¡ng lÃ¹i vá» sau
  const rUAngle = ((pose?.limbAngles?.rightUpperArm ?? (isSitting ? -38 : -18)) * Math.PI) / 180;
  let rFAngle = rUAngle;
  if (pose?.limbAngles?.rightForearm !== undefined) {
    rFAngle = (pose.limbAngles.rightForearm * Math.PI) / 180;
  } else if (pose?.limbAngles?.rightLowerArm !== undefined) {
    rFAngle = (((pose?.limbAngles?.rightUpperArm ?? 0) + pose.limbAngles.rightLowerArm) * Math.PI) / 180;
  } else {
    rFAngle = ((isSitting ? -82 : -34) * Math.PI) / 180;
  }

  const lUAngle = ((pose?.limbAngles?.leftUpperArm ?? (isSitting ? -34 : -14)) * Math.PI) / 180;
  let lFAngle = lUAngle;
  if (pose?.limbAngles?.leftForearm !== undefined) {
    lFAngle = (pose.limbAngles.leftForearm * Math.PI) / 180;
  } else if (pose?.limbAngles?.leftLowerArm !== undefined) {
    lFAngle = (((pose?.limbAngles?.leftUpperArm ?? 0) + pose.limbAngles.leftLowerArm) * Math.PI) / 180;
  } else {
    lFAngle = ((isSitting ? -82 : -26) * Math.PI) / 180;
  }

  // TÃ­nh vá»‹ trÃ­ khuá»·u, cá»• tay vÃ  bÃ n tay pháº£i (Ä‘á»“ng nháº¥t 100% vá»›i StickmanRig)
  const rightElbow = {
    x: rightShoulderX - Math.sin(rUAngle) * bones.upperArmLength * facingDir,
    y: shoulderY + Math.cos(rUAngle) * bones.upperArmLength,
  };
  const rightWrist = {
    x: rightElbow.x - Math.sin(rFAngle) * bones.forearmLength * facingDir,
    y: rightElbow.y + Math.cos(rFAngle) * bones.forearmLength,
  };
  const rightHand = {
    x: rightWrist.x - Math.sin(rFAngle) * bones.handLength * facingDir,
    y: rightWrist.y + Math.cos(rFAngle) * bones.handLength,
  };

  // TÃ­nh vá»‹ trÃ­ khuá»·u, cá»• tay vÃ  bÃ n tay trÃ¡i
  const leftElbow = {
    x: leftShoulderX - Math.sin(lUAngle) * bones.upperArmLength * facingDir,
    y: shoulderY + Math.cos(lUAngle) * bones.upperArmLength,
  };
  const leftWrist = {
    x: leftElbow.x - Math.sin(lFAngle) * bones.forearmLength * facingDir,
    y: leftElbow.y + Math.cos(lFAngle) * bones.forearmLength,
  };
  const leftHand = {
    x: leftWrist.x - Math.sin(lFAngle) * bones.handLength * facingDir,
    y: leftWrist.y + Math.cos(lFAngle) * bones.handLength,
  };

  // Vá»‹ trÃ­ Ä‘áº§u gá»‘i vÃ  bÃ n chÃ¢n
  const leftThighRad = ((pose?.limbAngles?.leftThigh ?? (isSitting ? -85 : 0)) * Math.PI) / 180;
  let leftShinWorldRad = leftThighRad;
  if (pose?.limbAngles?.leftShin !== undefined) {
    leftShinWorldRad = (pose.limbAngles.leftShin * Math.PI) / 180;
  } else if (pose?.limbAngles?.leftCalf !== undefined) {
    leftShinWorldRad = (((pose?.limbAngles?.leftThigh ?? 0) + pose.limbAngles.leftCalf) * Math.PI) / 180;
  } else {
    leftShinWorldRad = (isSitting ? 0 : 0);
  }

  const rightThighRad = ((pose?.limbAngles?.rightThigh ?? (isSitting ? -88 : 0)) * Math.PI) / 180;
  let rightShinWorldRad = rightThighRad;
  if (pose?.limbAngles?.rightShin !== undefined) {
    rightShinWorldRad = (pose.limbAngles.rightShin * Math.PI) / 180;
  } else if (pose?.limbAngles?.rightCalf !== undefined) {
    rightShinWorldRad = (((pose?.limbAngles?.rightThigh ?? 0) + pose.limbAngles.rightCalf) * Math.PI) / 180;
  } else {
    rightShinWorldRad = (isSitting ? 0 : 0);
  }

  const leftKnee = {
    x: leftHipX - Math.sin(leftThighRad) * bones.thighLength * facingDir,
    y: hipY + Math.cos(leftThighRad) * bones.thighLength,
  };
  const rightKnee = {
    x: rightHipX - Math.sin(rightThighRad) * bones.thighLength * facingDir,
    y: hipY + Math.cos(rightThighRad) * bones.thighLength,
  };

  const leftFoot = pose?.feet?.left ?? (isSitting ? { x: leftKnee.x, y: rootY } : {
    x: leftKnee.x - Math.sin(leftShinWorldRad) * bones.shinLength * facingDir,
    y: leftKnee.y + Math.cos(leftShinWorldRad) * bones.shinLength,
  });
  const rightFoot = pose?.feet?.right ?? (isSitting ? { x: rightKnee.x, y: rootY } : {
    x: rightKnee.x - Math.sin(rightShinWorldRad) * bones.shinLength * facingDir,
    y: rightKnee.y + Math.cos(rightShinWorldRad) * bones.shinLength,
  });

  return {
    topmid: { x: rootX, y: headTopY },
    bottommid: { x: rootX, y: rootY },
    center: { x: rootX, y: (headTopY + rootY) / 2 },
    face: { x: rootX, y: headCenterY },
    eyes: { x: rootX + 12 * facingDir, y: headCenterY - 4 },
    mouth: { x: rootX + 14 * facingDir, y: headCenterY + 12 },
    "head.topmid": { x: rootX, y: headTopY },
    "head.center": { x: rootX, y: headCenterY },
    "head.bottommid": { x: rootX, y: headCenterY + bones.headRadius },
    neck: { x: rootX, y: neckY },
    torso: { x: rootX, y: (neckY + hipY) / 2 },
    pelvis: { x: rootX, y: hipY },
    left_shoulder: { x: leftShoulderX, y: shoulderY },
    right_shoulder: { x: rightShoulderX, y: shoulderY },
    left_elbow: leftElbow,
    right_elbow: rightElbow,
    left_wrist: leftWrist,
    right_wrist: rightWrist,
    left_hand: leftHand,
    right_hand: rightHand,
    left_hip: { x: leftHipX, y: hipY },
    right_hip: { x: rightHipX, y: hipY },
    left_knee: leftKnee,
    right_knee: rightKnee,
    left_ankle: leftFoot,
    right_ankle: rightFoot,
    left_foot: leftFoot,
    right_foot: rightFoot,
  };
}

// 1. Kiá»ƒm tra 4 hÆ°á»›ng gÃ³c chuáº©n: 0Â° up, 90Â° right, 180Â° down, 270Â° left
test("Standard Cardinal Angles (0Â° up, 90Â° right, 180Â° down, 270Â° left)", () => {
  // Vector convention: 0 rad is up (0, -1), PI/2 is right (1, 0), PI is down (0, 1), 3PI/2 is left (-1, 0)
  const angleToVec = (deg: number) => {
    const rad = (deg * Math.PI) / 180;
    const x = Math.round(Math.sin(rad) * 1000) / 1000;
    const y = Math.round(-Math.cos(rad) * 1000) / 1000;
    return {
      x: Object.is(x, -0) ? 0 : x,
      y: Object.is(y, -0) ? 0 : y,
    };
  };

  const up = angleToVec(0);
  assert.equal(up.x, 0);
  assert.equal(up.y, -1);

  const right = angleToVec(90);
  assert.equal(right.x, 1);
  assert.equal(right.y, 0);

  const down = angleToVec(180);
  assert.equal(down.x, 0);
  assert.equal(down.y, 1);

  const left = angleToVec(270);
  assert.equal(left.x, -1);
  assert.equal(left.y, 0);
});

// 2. Kiá»ƒm tra Anchor tÃ­nh Ä‘Ãºng sau scale, flip, vÃ  di chuyá»ƒn

  assert.equal(

    "Symmetric flip offset"

// 3. Khá»›p ná»‘i vÃ  báº£o toÃ n chiá»u dÃ i xÆ°Æ¡ng trong IK (2-bone analytical IK)
test("2-Bone IK preserves bone lengths and reaches in-range targets", () => {
  const bones = HUMANOID_RIG.getBones(600);
  const base = { x: 500, y: 500 };
  const target = { x: 620, y: 580 }; // In reach: dist ~ 144px, max reach = 85 + 100 = 185px

  const sol = solve2BoneIK({
    base,
    target,
    length1: bones.upperArmLength,
    length2: bones.forearmLength + bones.handLength,
    facing: "right",
  });

  assert.equal(sol.reached, true, "Target must be reached within max reach");

  // Check bone 1 length: distance from base to elbow
  const d1 = Math.hypot(sol.elbowPos.x - base.x, sol.elbowPos.y - base.y);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(d1 - bones.upperArmLength) < 0.01, "Upper arm length preserved exactly");

  // Check bone 2 length: distance from elbow to hand
  const d2 = Math.hypot(sol.endEffectorPos.x - sol.elbowPos.x, sol.endEffectorPos.y - sol.elbowPos.y);
  const expectedLen2 = bones.forearmLength + bones.handLength;
  assert.ok(Math.abs(d2 - expectedLen2) < 0.01, "Forearm+hand length preserved exactly");

  // End effector lands on target
  assert.ok(Math.hypot(sol.endEffectorPos.x - target.x, sol.endEffectorPos.y - target.y) < 0.5, "Hand lands on target");
});

// 4. IK khÃ´ng tá»± kÃ©o dÃ i xÆ°Æ¡ng khi má»¥c tiÃªu ngoÃ i táº§m vá»›i
test("IK clamps without bone stretching when target is out of reach", () => {
  const bones = HUMANOID_RIG.getBones(600);
  const base = { x: 500, y: 500 };
  const outOfReachTarget = { x: 1000, y: 500 }; // 500px away, max reach is 185px

  const sol = solve2BoneIK({
    base,
    target: outOfReachTarget,
    length1: bones.upperArmLength,
    length2: bones.forearmLength + bones.handLength,
    facing: "right",
  });

  assert.equal(sol.reached, false, "Target is marked not reached");
  const totalLength = Math.hypot(sol.endEffectorPos.x - base.x, sol.endEffectorPos.y - base.y);
  assert.ok(
    Math.abs(totalLength - sol.maxReach) < 0.1,
    "Limb extends to max reach without stretching bones"
  );
});

// 5. Grab / Release khÃ´ng lÃ m váº­t nháº£y vá»‹ trÃ­ (World transform preservation)
test("Grab and Release preserve continuous world transform", () => {
  const script = `

        move(c, to=[900, 850], duration_ms=500)

  // Evaluate frame right before release (frame 59 ~ frame 50-60 is release end)
  const fBefore = evaluateFrame(ir, 55, 60);

  assert.equal(fAfter.objects.c.pose?.heldObjects?.rightHand, undefined, "Object is released from hand");
});

// 12. CÃºi nháº·t Ä‘á»“: kiá»ƒm tra cÃ¡c pha crouch -> contact -> LIFT WHILE CROUCHED -> rise
test("Floor pickup verifies lift_clear while crouched and zero teleportation", () => {
  const script = `
video(width=1920, height=1080, fps=60)
with scene("test_pickup", duration_ms=2500):
    c = character("human", height=600, x=500, y=850)
    p = prop("cup", width=60, height=90)
    place(p, at=[580, 850])
    pickup(c, object=p, hand="right", duration_ms=2500)
`;
  const { ir } = compileAnimScript(script);
  // Total 150 frames.
  // Phase 1 (0..0.35 = 0..52): lower_body & reach. Cup must be at floor y=850.
  const f30 = evaluateFrame(ir, 30, 60);
  assert.equal(f30.objects.p.transform.y, 850, "Cup remains on floor during crouch & reach");
  assert(f30.objects.c.pose?.crouchDepth && f30.objects.c.pose.crouchDepth > 50, "Character is crouching");

  // Phase 2 (0.35..0.45 = 53..67): grasp. Cup is on floor at y=850.
  const f60 = evaluateFrame(ir, 60, 60);
  assert.equal(f60.objects.p.transform.y, 850, "Cup still on floor at moment of grasp");
  assert.equal(f60.objects.c.pose?.heldObjects?.rightHand, "p", "Object attached to hand");

  // Phase 3 (0.45..0.65 = 68..97): LIFT_CLEAR while STILL CROUCHED!
  const f85 = evaluateFrame(ir, 85, 60);
  assert(
    f85.objects.p.transform.y < 820,
    `Cup lifted off floor during lift_clear: y=${f85.objects.p.transform.y} < 820`
  );
  assert(
    f85.objects.c.pose?.crouchDepth && f85.objects.c.pose.crouchDepth > 50,
    "Character is STILL CROUCHED while lifting cup off the floor"
  );

  // Phase 4 & 5 (120..150): Standing upright holding cup
  const f145 = evaluateFrame(ir, 145, 60);
  assert.equal(f145.objects.c.pose?.crouchDepth, 0, "Character is standing upright");
  assert(f145.objects.p.transform.y < 650, "Cup held at waist level");

  // Continuity check: between any two consecutive frames, no teleportation (max delta < 15px)
  for (let f = 1; f < 150; f++) {
    const prev = evaluateFrame(ir, f - 1, 60).objects.p.transform;
    const curr = evaluateFrame(ir, f, 60).objects.p.transform;
    const d = Math.hypot(curr.x - prev.x, curr.y - prev.y);
    assert(d < 15, `Cup displacement at frame ${f} must be continuous (got ${d}px)`);
  }
});

// 13. Ngá»“i xuá»‘ng vÃ  Ä‘á»©ng lÃªn liÃªn tá»¥c, khÃ´ng teleport
test("Continuous sit and stand with seatedHipY trajectory", () => {
  const script = `

with scene("test_sit_stand", duration_ms=4000):
    c = character("human", height=600, x=500, y=850)
    ch = prop("chair", width=220, height=400)
    place(ch, at=[520, 850])
    with sequence():
        sit(c, on=ch.seat, duration_ms=2000)
        stand(c, duration_ms=2000)
`;
  const { ir } = compileAnimScript(script);
  // Frame 0: Standing hip at -350
  const f0 = evaluateFrame(ir, 0, 60);
  // Frame 60: Halfway sitting (hip ~ -285)
  const f60 = evaluateFrame(ir, 60, 60);
  // Frame 120: Fully seated (hip -220)
  const f120 = evaluateFrame(ir, 120, 60);
  // Frame 180: Halfway standing (hip ~ -285)
  const f180 = evaluateFrame(ir, 180, 60);
  // Frame 240: Fully standing (hip -350)
  const f240 = evaluateFrame(ir, 239, 60);

  assert(f60.objects.c.pose?.sittingHipY! > -350 && f60.objects.c.pose?.sittingHipY! < -220, "Smooth descent");
  assert.equal(Math.round(f120.objects.c.pose?.sittingHipY!), -220, "Fully seated at frame 120");
  assert(f180.objects.c.pose?.sittingHipY! < -220 && f180.objects.c.pose?.sittingHipY! > -350, "Smooth ascent");

});

    if (s.objects.c.transform.y < 850) {
      foundFlight = true;
      break;

});

/**
 * AnimScript v0.1 AST Definitions
 * Strongly typed AST with line/column/span metadata for diagnostics.
 */

export interface SourceLocation {
  line: number;   // 1-based
  column: number; // 1-based
  offset: number; // 0-based
}

export interface NodeSpan {
  start: SourceLocation;
  end: SourceLocation;
}

export type ASTNode =
  | ProgramNode
  | StatementNode
  | ExpressionNode;

export interface ProgramNode {
  type: "Program";
  span: NodeSpan;
  body: StatementNode[];
}

export type StatementNode =
  | AssignmentStatementNode
  | ExpressionStatementNode
  | WithSceneStatementNode
  | WithSequenceStatementNode
  | WithParallelStatementNode;

export interface AssignmentStatementNode {
  type: "AssignmentStatement";
  span: NodeSpan;
  target: string;
  value: ExpressionNode;
}

export interface ExpressionStatementNode {
  type: "ExpressionStatement";
  span: NodeSpan;
  expression: ExpressionNode;
}

export interface WithSceneStatementNode {
  type: "WithSceneStatement";
  span: NodeSpan;
  sceneName: string;
  options: Record<string, ExpressionNode>;
  body: StatementNode[];
}

export interface WithSequenceStatementNode {
  type: "WithSequenceStatement";
  span: NodeSpan;
  body: StatementNode[];
}

export interface WithParallelStatementNode {
  type: "WithParallelStatement";
  span: NodeSpan;
  body: StatementNode[];
}

export type ExpressionNode =
  | NumberLiteralNode
  | StringLiteralNode
  | BooleanLiteralNode
  | CoordinateLiteralNode
  | IdentifierNode
  | PropertyAccessNode
  | BinaryExpressionNode
  | CallExpressionNode;

export interface NumberLiteralNode {
  type: "NumberLiteral";
  span: NodeSpan;
  value: number;
}

export interface StringLiteralNode {
  type: "StringLiteral";
  span: NodeSpan;
  value: string;
}

export interface BooleanLiteralNode {
  type: "BooleanLiteral";
  span: NodeSpan;
  value: boolean;
}

export interface CoordinateLiteralNode {
  type: "CoordinateLiteral";
  span: NodeSpan;
  elements: ExpressionNode[];
}

export interface IdentifierNode {
  type: "Identifier";
  span: NodeSpan;
  name: string;
}

export interface PropertyAccessNode {
  type: "PropertyAccess";
  span: NodeSpan;
  object: ExpressionNode;
  property: string;
}

export interface BinaryExpressionNode {
  type: "BinaryExpression";
  span: NodeSpan;
  operator: "+" | "-";
  left: ExpressionNode;
  right: ExpressionNode;
}

export interface CallArgument {
  name?: string; // Optional: named argument
  value: ExpressionNode;
}

export interface CallExpressionNode {
  type: "CallExpression";
  span: NodeSpan;
  callee: string;
  args: CallArgument[];
}

/**
 * AnimScript v0.1 Compiler
 * Compiles parsed AST into deterministic intermediate representation (IR).
 * Performs symbol checking, anchor validation, cycle detection, and action conflict verification.
 */

import {
  ProgramNode,
  StatementNode,
  WithSceneStatementNode,
  WithSequenceStatementNode,
  WithParallelStatementNode,
  AssignmentStatementNode,
  ExpressionStatementNode,
  ExpressionNode,
  CallExpressionNode,
  PropertyAccessNode,
  IdentifierNode,
  CoordinateLiteralNode,
  BinaryExpressionNode,
} from "./ast";
import {
  AnimScriptIR,
  SceneIR,
  SceneObjectIR,
  ActionClipIR,
  ActionType,
  CompilerDiagnostic,
  VideoConfig,
  Vector2,
} from "./types";
import {
  PROP_REGISTRY,
  HUMANOID_RIG,
  calculateCharacterAnchors,
} from "./registry/assetRegistry";

const VALID_COMMANDS = new Set([
  "video",
  "place",
  "align",
  "narrate",
  "say",
  "label_prop",
  "camera_focus",
  "camera_reset",
  "wait",
  "move",
  "rotate",
  "follow",
  "unfollow",
  "walk",
  "jump",
  "look_at",
  "reach",
  "grab",
  "release",
  "put_down",
  "sit",
  "stand",
  "run",
  "pickup",
]);

export class Compiler {
  private diagnostics: CompilerDiagnostic[] = [];
  private videoConfig: VideoConfig = { width: 1920, height: 1080, fps: 60 };

  constructor() {}

  public compile(program: ProgramNode): AnimScriptIR {
    this.diagnostics = [];
    const scenes: SceneIR[] = [];

    // 1. First pass: look for top-level video(...) configuration
    for (const stmt of program.body) {
      if (stmt.type === "ExpressionStatement" && stmt.expression.type === "CallExpression") {
        if (stmt.expression.callee === "video") {
          this.compileVideoConfig(stmt.expression);
        }
      }
    }

    // 2. Second pass: compile scenes
    let currentStartFrame = 0;
    let sceneIndex = 1;

    for (const stmt of program.body) {
      if (stmt.type === "WithSceneStatement") {
        const scene = this.compileScene(stmt, `scene_${sceneIndex}`, currentStartFrame);
        scenes.push(scene);
        currentStartFrame += scene.durationFrames;
        sceneIndex++;
      } else if (stmt.type === "ExpressionStatement" && stmt.expression.type === "CallExpression") {
        if (stmt.expression.callee !== "video") {
          this.diagnostics.push({
            code: "ERR_STATEMENT_OUTSIDE_SCENE",
            message: `Lá»‡nh '${stmt.expression.callee}(...)' pháº£i náº±m bÃªn trong khá»‘i 'with scene(...)'.`,
            line: stmt.span.start.line,
            column: stmt.span.start.column,
            severity: "error",
            fixSuggestion: "ÄÆ°a lá»‡nh nÃ y vÃ o trong má»™t khá»‘i 'with scene(...):'.",
          });
        }
      }
    }

    if (scenes.length === 0) {
      this.diagnostics.push({
        code: "ERR_NO_SCENES",
        message: "KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y khá»‘i 'with scene(...):' nÃ o trong ká»‹ch báº£n.",
        line: 1,
        column: 1,
        severity: "error",
        fixSuggestion: "Bá»• sung Ã­t nháº¥t má»™t khá»‘i 'with scene(\"name\"):'.",
      });
    }

    return {
      video: this.videoConfig,
      scenes,
      totalDurationFrames: currentStartFrame,
      diagnostics: this.diagnostics,
    };
  }

  private compileVideoConfig(call: CallExpressionNode): void {
    for (const arg of call.args) {
      if (arg.name === "width" && arg.value.type === "NumberLiteral") {
        this.videoConfig.width = arg.value.value;
      } else if (arg.name === "height" && arg.value.type === "NumberLiteral") {
        this.videoConfig.height = arg.value.value;
      } else if (arg.name === "fps" && arg.value.type === "NumberLiteral") {
        this.videoConfig.fps = arg.value.value;
      }
    }
  }

  private compileScene(
    sceneStmt: WithSceneStatementNode,
    sceneId: string,
    startFrame: number
  ): SceneIR {
    const sceneName = sceneStmt.sceneName;
    const objects: Record<string, SceneObjectIR> = {};
    const actionClips: ActionClipIR[] = [];
    const subtitles: SceneIR["subtitles"] = [];
    const speechBubbles: SceneIR["speechBubbles"] = [];
    const propLabels: SceneIR["propLabels"] = [];
    const cameraKeyframes: SceneIR["cameraKeyframes"] = [];

    // Context for compiling blocks
    let currentFrame = 0;
    const clipChannels: Record<string, { start: number; end: number; clipId: string }[]> = {};

    // Helper to evaluate expressions to values or Vector2
    const evalExpr = (expr: ExpressionNode): any => {
      if (expr.type === "NumberLiteral") return expr.value;
      if (expr.type === "StringLiteral") return expr.value;
      if (expr.type === "BooleanLiteral") return expr.value;
      if (expr.type === "CoordinateLiteral") {
        return expr.elements.map((el) => evalExpr(el));
      }
      if (expr.type === "Identifier") {
        return expr.name;
      }
      if (expr.type === "PropertyAccess") {
        return this.resolvePropertyAccess(expr, objects);
      }
      if (expr.type === "BinaryExpression") {
        const leftVal = evalExpr(expr.left);
        const rightVal = evalExpr(expr.right);
        if (typeof leftVal === "number" && typeof rightVal === "number") {
          return expr.operator === "+" ? leftVal + rightVal : leftVal - rightVal;
        }
        if (Array.isArray(leftVal) && Array.isArray(rightVal)) {
          return [
            expr.operator === "+" ? leftVal[0] + rightVal[0] : leftVal[0] - rightVal[0],
            expr.operator === "+" ? leftVal[1] + rightVal[1] : leftVal[1] - rightVal[1],
          ];
        }
      }
      return null;
    };

    // Helper to resolve an anchor string e.g. "desk.surface.right" or "person.right_hand"
    const resolveAnchor = (expr: ExpressionNode): { objectId: string; anchorName: string } | null => {
      if (expr.type === "PropertyAccess") {
        const parts: string[] = [];
        let curr: ExpressionNode = expr;
        while (curr.type === "PropertyAccess") {
          parts.unshift(curr.property);
          curr = curr.object;
        }
        if (curr.type === "Identifier") {
          parts.unshift(curr.name);
          const objId = parts[0];
          const anchorName = parts.slice(1).join(".");
          return { objectId: objId, anchorName };
        }
      }
      return null;
    };

    // Process statements in scene body
    const compileStatements = (stmts: StatementNode[], isParallel = false, isSequence = false): number => {
      let maxDuration = 0;
      const initialFrame = currentFrame;

      for (const stmt of stmts) {
        if (isParallel) {
          currentFrame = initialFrame;
        }

        if (stmt.type === "AssignmentStatement") {
          // Object declaration: obj = character(...) or obj = prop(...)
          this.compileObjectDeclaration(stmt, objects);
        } else if (stmt.type === "ExpressionStatement" && stmt.expression.type === "CallExpression") {
          const call = stmt.expression;
          const callee = call.callee;

          if (!VALID_COMMANDS.has(callee)) {
            this.diagnostics.push({
              code: "ERR_UNKNOWN_COMMAND",
              message: `Lá»‡nh '${callee}' khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c há»— trá»£ trong AnimScript v0.1.`,
              line: call.span.start.line,
              column: call.span.start.column,
              severity: "error",
              fixSuggestion: `CÃ¡c lá»‡nh há»£p lá»‡: ${Array.from(VALID_COMMANDS).join(", ")}.`,
            });
            continue;
          }

          if (callee === "place") {
            this.compilePlace(call, objects);
          } else if (callee === "align") {
            this.compileAlign(call, objects);
          } else if (callee === "narrate") {
            const clipDur = this.compileNarrate(call, isSequence ? currentFrame : 0, subtitles);
            if (isSequence) {
              currentFrame += clipDur;
              maxDuration = Math.max(maxDuration, currentFrame - initialFrame);
            }
          } else if (callee === "say") {
            const clipDur = this.compileSay(call, currentFrame, speechBubbles, objects);
            currentFrame += clipDur;
            maxDuration = Math.max(maxDuration, currentFrame - initialFrame);
          } else if (callee === "label_prop") {
            this.compileLabelProp(call, currentFrame, propLabels, objects);
          } else if (callee === "camera_focus") {
            const clipDur = this.compileCameraFocus(call, currentFrame, cameraKeyframes, objects);
            currentFrame += clipDur;
            maxDuration = Math.max(maxDuration, currentFrame - initialFrame);
          } else if (callee === "camera_reset") {
            const clipDur = this.compileCameraReset(call, currentFrame, cameraKeyframes);
            currentFrame += clipDur;
            maxDuration = Math.max(maxDuration, currentFrame - initialFrame);
          } else if (callee === "wait") {
            const durMs = this.getNamedNumber(call, "duration_ms", 1000);
            const frames = Math.round((durMs / 1000) * this.videoConfig.fps);
            currentFrame += frames;
            maxDuration = Math.max(maxDuration, currentFrame - initialFrame);
          } else {
            // Motion or interaction clip
            const clip = this.compileActionClip(call, currentFrame, objects, evalExpr);
            if (clip) {
              this.checkActionConflicts(clip, clipChannels);
              actionClips.push(clip);
              currentFrame += clip.durationFrames;
              maxDuration = Math.max(maxDuration, currentFrame - initialFrame);
            }
          }
        } else if (stmt.type === "WithSequenceStatement") {
          const seqDur = compileStatements(stmt.body, false, true);
          maxDuration = Math.max(maxDuration, currentFrame - initialFrame);
        } else if (stmt.type === "WithParallelStatement") {
          const parDur = compileStatements(stmt.body, true, false);
          currentFrame = initialFrame + parDur;
          maxDuration = Math.max(maxDuration, currentFrame - initialFrame);
        }
      }

      return isParallel ? maxDuration : currentFrame - initialFrame;
    };

    compileStatements(sceneStmt.body, false, false);

    // Calculate total duration
    let sceneDuration = currentFrame;
    if (sceneStmt.options.duration_ms && sceneStmt.options.duration_ms.type === "NumberLiteral") {
      const explicitFrames = Math.round(
        (sceneStmt.options.duration_ms.value / 1000) * this.videoConfig.fps
      );
      sceneDuration = Math.max(sceneDuration, explicitFrames);
    }
    sceneDuration = Math.max(sceneDuration, 60); // Minimum 1 second

    return {
      id: sceneId,
      name: sceneName,
      startFrame,
      durationFrames: sceneDuration,
      objects,
      actionClips,
      cameraKeyframes,
      subtitles,
      speechBubbles,
      propLabels,
    };
  }

  // --- Sub-compilers ---

  private compileObjectDeclaration(
    stmt: AssignmentStatementNode,
    objects: Record<string, SceneObjectIR>
  ): void {
    const varName = stmt.target;
    if (stmt.value.type !== "CallExpression") {
      this.diagnostics.push({
        code: "ERR_INVALID_OBJECT_DECL",
        message: `Biáº¿n '${varName}' pháº£i Ä‘Æ°á»£c khá»Ÿi táº¡o báº±ng hÃ m character(...) hoáº·c prop(...).`,
        line: stmt.span.start.line,
        column: stmt.span.start.column,
        severity: "error",
      });
      return;
    }

    const call = stmt.value;
    const kind = call.callee;

    if (kind === "character") {
      const charType = (call.args[0]?.value as any)?.value || "human";
      const height = this.getNamedNumber(call, "height", 600);
      const x = this.getNamedNumber(call, "x", 960);
      const y = this.getNamedNumber(call, "y", 850);
      const facing = (this.getNamedString(call, "facing", "right") as "left" | "right");

      objects[varName] = {
        id: varName,
        name: varName,
        kind: "character",
        assetType: charType,
        initialTransform: { x, y, rotationDeg: 0, scaleX: 1, scaleY: 1, opacity: 1, zIndex: 10 },
        initialPose: { x, y, facing, isSitting: false },
        props: { height, facing },
      };
    } else if (kind === "prop") {
      const propType = (call.args[0]?.value as any)?.value || "desk";
      const propDef = PROP_REGISTRY[propType];

      if (!propDef) {
        this.diagnostics.push({
          code: "ERR_UNKNOWN_PROP_TYPE",
          message: `KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y loáº¡i Ä‘áº¡o cá»¥ '${propType}' trong registry.`,
          line: stmt.span.start.line,
          column: stmt.span.start.column,
          severity: "error",
          fixSuggestion: `Chá»n má»™t trong cÃ¡c loáº¡i cÃ³ sáºµn: ${Object.keys(PROP_REGISTRY).join(", ")}.`,
        });
        return;
      }

      const width = this.getNamedNumber(call, "width", propDef.defaultWidth);
      const height = this.getNamedNumber(call, "height", propDef.defaultHeight);
      const x = this.getNamedNumber(call, "x", 960);
      const y = this.getNamedNumber(call, "y", 850);

      objects[varName] = {
        id: varName,
        name: varName,
        kind: "prop",
        assetType: propType,
        initialTransform: { x, y, rotationDeg: 0, scaleX: 1, scaleY: 1, opacity: 1, zIndex: 5 },
        props: { width, height },
      };
    }
  }

  private compilePlace(call: CallExpressionNode, objects: Record<string, SceneObjectIR>): void {
    const targetVar = (call.args[0]?.value as any)?.name;
    const obj = objects[targetVar];
    if (!obj) {
      this.diagnostics.push({
        code: "ERR_UNKNOWN_OBJECT",
        message: `Äá»‘i tÆ°á»£ng '${targetVar}' chÆ°a Ä‘Æ°á»£c khai bÃ¡o.`,
        line: call.span.start.line,
        column: call.span.start.column,
        severity: "error",
      });
      return;
    }

    const atArg = call.args.find((a) => a.name === "at")?.value;
    if (atArg && atArg.type === "CoordinateLiteral" && atArg.elements.length >= 2) {
      const x = (atArg.elements[0] as any).value;
      const y = (atArg.elements[1] as any).value;
      obj.initialTransform.x = x;
      obj.initialTransform.y = y;
      if (obj.initialPose) {
        obj.initialPose.x = x;
        obj.initialPose.y = y;
      }
    }
  }

  private compileAlign(call: CallExpressionNode, objects: Record<string, SceneObjectIR>): void {
    const sourcePropAccess = call.args[0]?.value;
    const targetPropAccess = call.args.find((a) => a.name === "to")?.value;

    if (!sourcePropAccess || !targetPropAccess) return;

    const srcAnchor = this.parseAnchorPath(sourcePropAccess);
    const tgtAnchor = this.parseAnchorPath(targetPropAccess);

    if (!srcAnchor || !tgtAnchor) {
      this.diagnostics.push({
        code: "ERR_INVALID_ANCHOR",
        message: "Lá»‡nh align(...) yÃªu cáº§u Ä‘iá»ƒm neo há»£p lá»‡ dáº¡ng object.anchor (vÃ­ dá»¥: cup.bottommid, desk.surface.right).",
        line: call.span.start.line,
        column: call.span.start.column,
        severity: "error",
      });
      return;
    }

    const srcObj = objects[srcAnchor.objectId];
    const tgtObj = objects[tgtAnchor.objectId];

    if (!srcObj || !tgtObj) {
      this.diagnostics.push({
        code: "ERR_UNKNOWN_OBJECT",
        message: `Äá»‘i tÆ°á»£ng trong align khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i (${!srcObj ? srcAnchor.objectId : tgtAnchor.objectId}).`,
        line: call.span.start.line,
        column: call.span.start.column,
        severity: "error",
      });
      return;
    }

    // Validate anchor names
    this.validateAnchorName(srcObj, srcAnchor.anchorName, call.span.start.line, call.span.start.column);
    this.validateAnchorName(tgtObj, tgtAnchor.anchorName, call.span.start.line, call.span.start.column);

    // Compute alignment position
    const tgtWorldPos = this.calculateAnchorWorldPos(tgtObj, tgtAnchor.anchorName, objects);
    const srcLocalOffset = this.calculateAnchorLocalOffset(srcObj, srcAnchor.anchorName);

    // Offset argument if present
    const offsetArg = call.args.find((a) => a.name === "offset")?.value;
    let dx = 0;
    let dy = 0;
    if (offsetArg && offsetArg.type === "CoordinateLiteral" && offsetArg.elements.length >= 2) {
      dx = (offsetArg.elements[0] as any).value || 0;
      dy = (offsetArg.elements[1] as any).value || 0;
    }

    srcObj.initialTransform.x = tgtWorldPos.x - srcLocalOffset.x + dx;
    srcObj.initialTransform.y = tgtWorldPos.y - srcLocalOffset.y + dy;
    if (srcObj.initialPose) {
      srcObj.initialPose.x = srcObj.initialTransform.x;
      srcObj.initialPose.y = srcObj.initialTransform.y;
    }

    // Set supportedBy relationship if aligning to surface
    if (tgtAnchor.anchorName.startsWith("surface") && srcObj.kind === "prop") {
      srcObj.props = {
        ...srcObj.props,
        supportedBy: tgtAnchor.objectId,
        supportOffsetX: dx,
      };
    }
  }

  private compileNarrate(
    call: CallExpressionNode,
    startFrame: number,
    subtitles: SceneIR["subtitles"]
  ): number {
    const textArg = call.args[0]?.value;
    const text = textArg && textArg.type === "StringLiteral" ? textArg.value : "";
    const durMs = this.getNamedNumber(call, "duration_ms", 2500);
    const frames = Math.max(30, Math.round((durMs / 1000) * this.videoConfig.fps));

    subtitles.push({
      id: `sub_${subtitles.length + 1}`,
      text,
      startFrame,
      durationFrames: frames,
    });

    return frames;
  }

  private compileSay(
    call: CallExpressionNode,
    startFrame: number,
    speechBubbles: SceneIR["speechBubbles"],
    objects: Record<string, SceneObjectIR>
  ): number {
    const charVar = (call.args[0]?.value as any)?.name;
    const text = (call.args.find((a) => a.name === "text" || !a.name && a !== call.args[0])?.value as any)?.value || "";
    const durMs = this.getNamedNumber(call, "duration_ms", 2000);
    const frames = Math.max(30, Math.round((durMs / 1000) * this.videoConfig.fps));

    speechBubbles.push({
      id: `bubble_${speechBubbles.length + 1}`,
      characterId: charVar,
      text,
      startFrame,
      durationFrames: frames,
    });

    return frames;
  }

  private compileLabelProp(
    call: CallExpressionNode,
    startFrame: number,
    propLabels: SceneIR["propLabels"],
    objects: Record<string, SceneObjectIR>
  ): void {
    const propVar = (call.args[0]?.value as any)?.name;
    const text = (call.args.find((a) => a.name === "text" || !a.name && a !== call.args[0])?.value as any)?.value || "";
    const slot = this.getNamedString(call, "slot", "label_slot");

    propLabels.push({
      id: `label_${propLabels.length + 1}`,
      propId: propVar,
      text,
      slot,
      startFrame,
      durationFrames: 99999, // Permanent on prop
    });
  }

  private compileCameraFocus(
    call: CallExpressionNode,
    startFrame: number,
    cameraKeyframes: SceneIR["cameraKeyframes"],
    objects: Record<string, SceneObjectIR>
  ): number {
    const targetsArg = call.args.find((a) => a.name === "targets")?.value;
    const padding = this.getNamedNumber(call, "padding", 140);
    const durMs = this.getNamedNumber(call, "duration_ms", 1000);
    const frames = Math.max(1, Math.round((durMs / 1000) * this.videoConfig.fps));

    let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;

    if (targetsArg && targetsArg.type === "CoordinateLiteral") {
      for (const el of targetsArg.elements) {
        const objName = (el as any).name;
        const obj = objects[objName];
        if (obj) {
          const ox = obj.initialTransform.x;
          const oy = obj.initialTransform.y;
          const w = obj.props?.width || 200;
          const h = obj.props?.height || 400;
          minX = Math.min(minX, ox - w / 2);
          maxX = Math.max(maxX, ox + w / 2);
          minY = Math.min(minY, oy - h);
          maxY = Math.max(maxY, oy);
        }
      }
    }

    if (!isFinite(minX)) {
      minX = 700; maxX = 1220; minY = 400; maxY = 800;
    }

    const boundW = Math.max(200, maxX - minX + padding * 2);
    const boundH = Math.max(200, maxY - minY + padding * 2);
    const zoomX = this.videoConfig.width / boundW;
    const zoomY = this.videoConfig.height / boundH;
    const zoom = Math.min(2.5, Math.max(1.0, Math.min(zoomX, zoomY)));

    const centerX = (minX + maxX) / 2;
    const centerY = (minY + maxY) / 2;

    cameraKeyframes.push({
      frame: startFrame,
      x: centerX,
      y: centerY,
      zoom,
      easing: "ease_in_out",
    });

    return frames;
  }

  private compileCameraReset(
    call: CallExpressionNode,
    startFrame: number,
    cameraKeyframes: SceneIR["cameraKeyframes"]
  ): number {
    const durMs = this.getNamedNumber(call, "duration_ms", 800);
    const frames = Math.max(1, Math.round((durMs / 1000) * this.videoConfig.fps));

    cameraKeyframes.push({
      frame: startFrame,
      x: this.videoConfig.width / 2,
      y: this.videoConfig.height / 2,
      zoom: 1.0,
      easing: "ease_in_out",
    });

    return frames;
  }

  private compileActionClip(
    call: CallExpressionNode,
    startFrame: number,
    objects: Record<string, SceneObjectIR>,
    evalExpr?: (expr: ExpressionNode) => any
  ): ActionClipIR | null {
    const action = call.callee as ActionType;
    const durMs = this.getNamedNumber(call, "duration_ms", 1000);
    const durationFrames = Math.max(1, Math.round((durMs / 1000) * this.videoConfig.fps));
    const endFrame = startFrame + durationFrames;

    // First argument is usually the target object or anchor
    const targetExpr = call.args[0]?.value;
    let targetId = "";
    if (targetExpr?.type === "Identifier") {
      targetId = targetExpr.name;
    } else if (targetExpr?.type === "PropertyAccess") {
      const anchor = this.parseAnchorPath(targetExpr);
      targetId = anchor ? `${anchor.objectId}.${anchor.anchorName}` : "";
    }

    if (action === "move" && targetId.includes(".")) {
      this.diagnostics.push({
        code: "ERR_INVALID_TARGET",
        message: `Lá»‡nh 'move' chá»‰ Ã¡p dá»¥ng cho toÃ n bá»™ Ä‘á»‘i tÆ°á»£ng (root), khÃ´ng Ã¡p dá»¥ng trá»±c tiáº¿p cho Ä‘iá»ƒm neo hoáº·c chi '${targetId}'. DÃ¹ng lá»‡nh 'reach' Ä‘á»ƒ di chuyá»ƒn tay.`,
        line: call.span.start.line,
        column: call.span.start.column,
        severity: "error",
        fixSuggestion: `Sá»­ dá»¥ng reach(${targetId}, to=...) hoáº·c move(${targetId.split(".")[0]}, to=...).`,
      });
      return null;
    }

    const params: Record<string, any> = {};
    for (const arg of call.args) {
      if (arg.name) {
        if (arg.value.type === "NumberLiteral") params[arg.name] = arg.value.value;
        else if (arg.value.type === "StringLiteral") params[arg.name] = arg.value.value;
        else if (arg.value.type === "BooleanLiteral") params[arg.name] = arg.value.value;
        else if (arg.value.type === "Identifier") params[arg.name] = arg.value.name;
        else if (arg.value.type === "CoordinateLiteral") {
          params[arg.name] = evalExpr ? evalExpr(arg.value) : arg.value.elements.map((el) => (el as any).value ?? 0);
        } else if (arg.value.type === "PropertyAccess") {
          if (arg.value.property === "x" || arg.value.property === "y") {
            params[arg.name] = this.resolvePropertyAccess(arg.value, objects);
          } else {
            const anchor = this.parseAnchorPath(arg.value);
            if (anchor) {
              const tgtObj = objects[anchor.objectId];
              if (tgtObj) {
                this.validateAnchorName(tgtObj, anchor.anchorName, call.span.start.line, call.span.start.column);
              }
              params[arg.name] = anchor;
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }

    return {
      id: `clip_${action}_${startFrame}`,
      targetId,
      action,
      startFrame,
      durationFrames,
      endFrame,
      params,
    };
  }

  private checkActionConflicts(
    clip: ActionClipIR,
    channels: Record<string, { start: number; end: number; clipId: string }[]>
  ): void {
    let channelKey = clip.targetId;
    if (clip.action === "walk" || clip.action === "run" || clip.action === "jump") {
      channelKey = `${clip.targetId}.legs`;
    } else if (clip.action === "reach" || clip.action === "grab" || clip.action === "release" || clip.action === "pickup") {
      let hand = clip.params.hand || (clip.targetId.includes("left") ? "leftHand" : "rightHand");
      if (hand === "left" || hand === "left_hand" || hand === "leftHand") hand = "leftHand";
      if (hand === "right" || hand === "right_hand" || hand === "rightHand") hand = "rightHand";
      channelKey = `${clip.targetId.split(".")[0]}.${hand}`;
    }

    const existing = channels[channelKey] || [];
    for (const entry of existing) {
      // Overlap condition
      if (Math.max(clip.startFrame, entry.start) < Math.min(clip.endFrame, entry.end)) {
        this.diagnostics.push({
          code: "ERR_ACTION_CONFLICT",
          message: `Xung Ä‘á»™t hÃ nh Ä‘á»™ng: '${clip.action}' vÃ  clip trÆ°á»›c cÃ¹ng Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn '${channelKey}' trong khoáº£ng frame ${clip.startFrame} -> ${clip.endFrame}.`,
          line: 1,
          column: 1,
          severity: "error",
          fixSuggestion: "Äáº·t cÃ¡c hÃ nh Ä‘á»™ng cÃ¹ng chi vÃ o khá»‘i 'sequence()' hoáº·c dÃ¹ng cÃ¡c tay/chÃ¢n khÃ¡c nhau.",
        });
      }
    }

    existing.push({ start: clip.startFrame, end: clip.endFrame, clipId: clip.id });
    channels[channelKey] = existing;
  }

  // --- Helper Methods ---

  private getNamedNumber(call: CallExpressionNode, name: string, defVal: number): number {
    const arg = call.args.find((a) => a.name === name)?.value;
    return arg && arg.type === "NumberLiteral" ? arg.value : defVal;
  }

  private getNamedString(call: CallExpressionNode, name: string, defVal: string): string {
    const arg = call.args.find((a) => a.name === name)?.value;
    return arg && arg.type === "StringLiteral" ? arg.value : defVal;
  }

  private parseAnchorPath(expr: ExpressionNode): { objectId: string; anchorName: string } | null {
    if (expr.type === "PropertyAccess") {
      const parts: string[] = [];
      let curr: ExpressionNode = expr;
      while (curr.type === "PropertyAccess") {
        parts.unshift(curr.property);
        curr = curr.object;
      }
      if (curr.type === "Identifier") {
        parts.unshift(curr.name);
        return { objectId: parts[0], anchorName: parts.slice(1).join(".") };
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  private validateAnchorName(
    obj: SceneObjectIR,
    anchorName: string,
    line: number,
    column: number
  ): void {
    if (obj.kind === "prop") {
      const propDef = PROP_REGISTRY[obj.assetType];
      if (propDef && !propDef.anchors[anchorName]) {
        this.diagnostics.push({
          code: "ERR_UNKNOWN_ANCHOR",
          message: `Äiá»ƒm neo '${anchorName}' khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i trÃªn Ä‘áº¡o cá»¥ '${obj.id}' (${obj.assetType}).`,
          line,
          column,
          severity: "error",
          fixSuggestion: `CÃ¡c Ä‘iá»ƒm neo há»£p lá»‡ cho '${obj.assetType}': ${Object.keys(propDef.anchors).join(", ")}.`,
        });
      }
    }
  }

  private calculateAnchorLocalOffset(obj: SceneObjectIR, anchorName: string): Vector2 {
    if (obj.kind === "prop") {
      const propDef = PROP_REGISTRY[obj.assetType];
      if (propDef && propDef.anchors[anchorName]) {
        const w = obj.props?.width || propDef.defaultWidth;
        const h = obj.props?.height || propDef.defaultHeight;
        return propDef.anchors[anchorName](w, h);
      }
    }
    return { x: 0, y: 0 };
  }

  private calculateAnchorWorldPos(
    obj: SceneObjectIR,
    anchorName: string,
    allObjects: Record<string, SceneObjectIR>
  ): Vector2 {
    if (obj.kind === "prop") {
      const offset = this.calculateAnchorLocalOffset(obj, anchorName);
      return {
        x: obj.initialTransform.x + offset.x,
        y: obj.initialTransform.y + offset.y,
      };
    } else if (obj.kind === "character") {
      const height = obj.props?.height || 600;
      const facing = obj.props?.facing || "right";
      const anchors = calculateCharacterAnchors(
        obj.initialTransform.x,
        obj.initialTransform.y,
        height,
        facing,
        obj.initialPose
      );
      return anchors[anchorName] || { x: obj.initialTransform.x, y: obj.initialTransform.y };
    }
    return { x: obj.initialTransform.x, y: obj.initialTransform.y };
  }

  private resolvePropertyAccess(
    expr: PropertyAccessNode,
    objects?: Record<string, SceneObjectIR>
  ): any {
    const parts: string[] = [];
    let curr: ExpressionNode = expr;
    while (curr.type === "PropertyAccess") {
      parts.unshift(curr.property);
      curr = curr.object;
    }
    if (curr.type === "Identifier") {
      parts.unshift(curr.name);
    } else {
      return 0;
    }

    const objId = parts[0];
    const obj = objects ? objects[objId] : undefined;
    if (!obj) {
      return 0;
    }

    const lastPart = parts[parts.length - 1];
    if (lastPart === "x" || lastPart === "y") {
      const anchorName = parts.slice(1, -1).join(".");
      if (anchorName) {
        const worldPos = this.calculateAnchorWorldPos(obj, anchorName, objects || {});
        return lastPart === "x" ? worldPos.x : worldPos.y;
      } else {
        return lastPart === "x" ? obj.initialTransform.x : obj.initialTransform.y;
      }
    }

    const anchorName = parts.slice(1).join(".");
    if (anchorName) {
      const worldPos = this.calculateAnchorWorldPos(obj, anchorName, objects || {});
      return [worldPos.x, worldPos.y];
    }

    return 0;
  }
}
