/**
 * AnimScript v0.1 2-Bone Analytical Inverse Kinematics (IK)
 * Preserves bone lengths, calculates exact shoulder/elbow or hip/knee angles.
 * Enforces joint limits and reports reachability errors without stretching bones.
 */

import { Vector2 } from "./types";

export interface IKSolution {
  reached: boolean;
  distance: number;
  maxReach: number;
  upperAngleDeg: number;  // Angle in degrees
  foreAngleDeg: number;   // Angle in degrees
  elbowPos: Vector2;
  endEffectorPos: Vector2;
}

export interface IKSolveOptions {
  base: Vector2;             // Shoulder or Hip joint
  target: Vector2;           // Hand or Foot target
  length1: number;           // Upper arm / Thigh length
  length2: number;           // Forearm+Hand / Shin+Foot length
  facing: "left" | "right";  // Character facing direction
  bendDirection?: 1 | -1;    // 1 for natural human bend, -1 for reverse
  isLeg?: boolean;           // If true, knee bends backwards
}

/**
 * Solves 2-bone analytical IK in 2D plane.
 * Angles returned:
 * 0Â° = pointing straight down (+Y) in character local limb convention,
 * or converted to limbAngles delta from standard standing rest pose.
 */
export function solve2BoneIK(opts: IKSolveOptions): IKSolution {
  const { base, target, length1, length2, facing, bendDirection = 1, isLeg = false } = opts;
  const facingSign = facing === "left" ? -1 : 1;

  // Vector from base to target
  const dx = (target.x - base.x) * facingSign;
  const dy = target.y - base.y;

  const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;
  const dist = Math.sqrt(distSq);
  const maxReach = length1 + length2;
  const minReach = Math.abs(length1 - length2);

  const reached = dist <= maxReach + 0.001;

  // Clamp distance within valid triangle inequality range
  const clampedDist = Math.max(minReach + 0.001, Math.min(maxReach - 0.001, dist));

  // Base angle from shoulder to target (0 rad = pointing down along +Y)
  // atan2(dx, dy): dy is down (+Y), dx is forward (+X)
  const baseAngle = Math.atan2(dx, dy);

  // Law of Cosines
  // cos(alpha) = (L1^2 + D^2 - L2^2) / (2 * L1 * D)
  const cosAlpha = (length1 * length1 + clampedDist * clampedDist - length2 * length2) / (2 * length1 * clampedDist);
  const alpha = Math.acos(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, cosAlpha)));

  // cos(beta) = (L1^2 + L2^2 - D^2) / (2 * L1 * L2)
  const cosBeta = (length1 * length1 + length2 * length2 - clampedDist * clampedDist) / (2 * length1 * length2);
  const beta = Math.acos(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, cosBeta)));

  // Bend direction: For human arm reaching forward, bendDirection=1
  // For knee, bend backwards (dx inverted)
  const bendSign = (isLeg ? -1 : 1) * bendDirection;

  // Upper bone angle (relative to straight down)
  const upperRad = baseAngle + bendSign * alpha;

  // Forearm / lower bone angle (relative to upper bone: 0 is straight continuation, PI is fully folded)
  // Or absolute relative to straight down:
  const elbowInteriorAngle = Math.PI - beta;
  const foreRad = upperRad - bendSign * elbowInteriorAngle;

  const upperAngleDeg = (upperRad * 180) / Math.PI;
  const foreAngleDeg = (foreRad * 180) / Math.PI;

  // Calculate forward positions
  const elbowX = base.x + Math.sin(upperRad) * length1 * facingSign;
  const elbowY = base.y + Math.cos(upperRad) * length1;

  const endX = elbowX + Math.sin(foreRad) * length2 * facingSign;
  const endY = elbowY + Math.cos(foreRad) * length2;

  return {
    reached,
    distance: dist,
    maxReach,
    upperAngleDeg,
    foreAngleDeg,
    elbowPos: { x: elbowX, y: elbowY },
    endEffectorPos: { x: endX, y: endY },
  };
}

/**
 * AnimScript v0.1 Public Interface
 */

import { Lexer } from "./lexer";
import { Parser } from "./parser";
import { Compiler } from "./compiler";
import { AnimScriptIR, CompilerDiagnostic } from "./types";
import { evaluateFrame } from "./runtime";

export * from "./types";
export * from "./ast";
export * from "./lexer";
export * from "./parser";
export * from "./compiler";
export * from "./runtime";
export * from "./ik";
export * from "./registry/assetRegistry";

export function compileAnimScript(source: string): { ir: AnimScriptIR; diagnostics: CompilerDiagnostic[] } {
  const parser = new Parser(source);
  const { program, diagnostics: parseDiagnostics } = parser.parse();

  const compiler = new Compiler();
  const ir = compiler.compile(program);

  const allDiagnostics = [...parseDiagnostics, ...ir.diagnostics];
  ir.diagnostics = allDiagnostics;

  return { ir, diagnostics: allDiagnostics };
}


==================== FILE: C:\Users\ASUS\Downloads\TOOL_2D_ANIM_API_FIX_1\TOOL_2D_ANIM\web-studio\src\animscript\lexer.ts ====================
/**
 * AnimScript v0.1 Lexer
 * Python-style indentation tokenization with line/column tracking.
 */

export type TokenType =
  | "IDENTIFIER"
  | "KEYWORD"
  | "NUMBER"
  | "STRING"
  | "BOOLEAN"
  | "ASSIGN"         // =
  | "LPAREN"         // (
  | "RPAREN"         // )
  | "LBRACKET"       // [
  | "RBRACKET"       // ]
  | "COMMA"          // ,
  | "DOT"            // .
  | "COLON"          // :
  | "PLUS"           // +
  | "MINUS"          // -
  | "NEWLINE"
  | "INDENT"
  | "DEDENT"
  | "EOF";

export interface Token {
  type: TokenType;
  value: string | number | boolean;
  line: number;   // 1-based
  column: number; // 1-based
  offset: number; // 0-based
}

export interface LexerError {
  code: string;
  message: string;
  line: number;
  column: number;
}

const KEYWORDS = new Set(["with", "as", "in", "scene", "sequence", "parallel"]);

export class Lexer {
  private src: string;
  private pos = 0;
  private line = 1;
  private col = 1;
  private indentStack: number[] = [0];
  private parenDepth = 0;
  private tokens: Token[] = [];
  public errors: LexerError[] = [];

  constructor(source: string) {
    this.src = source.replace(/\r\n/g, "\n").replace(/\r/g, "\n");
  }

  public tokenize(): Token[] {
    this.tokens = [];
    this.indentStack = [0];
    this.parenDepth = 0;
    this.errors = [];
    this.pos = 0;
    this.line = 1;
    this.col = 1;

    let isLineStart = true;

    while (this.pos < this.src.length) {
      if (isLineStart && this.parenDepth === 0) {
        // Read indentation of new line
        const indentStartLine = this.line;
        const indentStartCol = this.col;
        const indentStartPos = this.pos;

        let indentSpaces = 0;
        while (this.pos < this.src.length) {
          const ch = this.src[this.pos];
          if (ch === " ") {
            indentSpaces++;
            this.advance();
          } else if (ch === "\t") {
            indentSpaces += 4; // Tab counts as 4 spaces
            this.advance();
          } else {
            break;
          }
        }

        // Check if blank line or comment
        if (this.pos < this.src.length) {
          const ch = this.src[this.pos];
          if (ch === "\n") {
            this.advance();
            continue; // Ignore blank lines
          }
          if (ch === "#") {
            this.skipComment();
            if (this.pos < this.src.length && this.src[this.pos] === "\n") {
              this.advance();
            }
            continue; // Ignore comment-only lines
          }
        } else {
          break;
        }

        // Now process indentation level
        const currentIndent = this.indentStack[this.indentStack.length - 1];
        if (indentSpaces > currentIndent) {
          this.indentStack.push(indentSpaces);
          this.tokens.push({
            type: "INDENT",
            value: indentSpaces,
            line: indentStartLine,
            column: indentStartCol,
            offset: indentStartPos,
          });
        } else if (indentSpaces < currentIndent) {
          while (
            this.indentStack.length > 1 &&
            this.indentStack[this.indentStack.length - 1] > indentSpaces
          ) {
            this.indentStack.pop();
            this.tokens.push({
              type: "DEDENT",
              value: indentSpaces,
              line: indentStartLine,
              column: indentStartCol,
              offset: indentStartPos,
            });
          }
          if (this.indentStack[this.indentStack.length - 1] !== indentSpaces) {
            this.errors.push({
              code: "ERR_UNINDENT_MISMATCH",
              message: `Má»©c thá»¥t lá» (${indentSpaces} khoáº£ng tráº¯ng) khÃ´ng khá»›p má»©c nÃ o trÆ°á»›c Ä‘Ã³.`,
              line: indentStartLine,
              column: indentStartCol,
            });
          }
        }
        isLineStart = false;
      }

      const ch = this.src[this.pos];

      // Skip whitespace within line
      if (ch === " " || ch === "\t") {
        this.advance();
        continue;
      }

      // Comments
      if (ch === "#") {
        this.skipComment();
        continue;
      }

      // Newline
      if (ch === "\n") {
        const line = this.line;
        const col = this.col;
        const offset = this.pos;
        this.advance();
        if (this.parenDepth === 0) {
          // Only emit NEWLINE if previous token is not already NEWLINE or INDENT
          const prev = this.tokens[this.tokens.length - 1];
          if (prev && prev.type !== "NEWLINE" && prev.type !== "INDENT") {
            this.tokens.push({ type: "NEWLINE", value: "\n", line, column: col, offset });
          }
          isLineStart = true;
        }
        continue;
      }

      // Parens / Brackets
      if (ch === "(") {
        this.parenDepth++;
        this.emit("LPAREN", "(");
        continue;
      }
      if (ch === ")") {
        this.parenDepth = Math.max(0, this.parenDepth - 1);
        this.emit("RPAREN", ")");
        continue;
      }
      if (ch === "[") {
        this.parenDepth++;
        this.emit("LBRACKET", "[");
        continue;
      }
      if (ch === "]") {
        this.parenDepth = Math.max(0, this.parenDepth - 1);
        this.emit("RBRACKET", "]");
        continue;
      }

      // Operators and punctuation
      if (ch === "=") {
        this.emit("ASSIGN", "=");
        continue;
      }
      if (ch === ":") {
        this.emit("COLON", ":");
        continue;
      }
      if (ch === ",") {
        this.emit("COMMA", ",");
        continue;
      }
      if (ch === ".") {
        this.emit("DOT", ".");
        continue;
      }
      if (ch === "+") {
        this.emit("PLUS", "+");
        continue;
      }
      if (ch === "-") {
        // Can be minus or part of negative number
        const nextChar = this.peek(1);
        const prev = this.tokens[this.tokens.length - 1];
        const isUnary = !prev || prev.type === "ASSIGN" || prev.type === "COMMA" || prev.type === "LPAREN" || prev.type === "LBRACKET" || prev.type === "COLON" || prev.type === "PLUS" || prev.type === "MINUS";
        if (isUnary && nextChar && /[0-9]/.test(nextChar)) {
          this.readNumber();
        } else {
          this.emit("MINUS", "-");
        }
        continue;
      }

      // Strings
      if (ch === '"' || ch === "'") {
        this.readString(ch);
        continue;
      }

      // Numbers
      if (/[0-9]/.test(ch)) {
        this.readNumber();
        continue;
      }

      // Identifiers / Keywords / Booleans
      if (/[a-zA-Z_]/.test(ch)) {
        this.readIdentifier();
        continue;
      }

      // Unknown character
      this.errors.push({
        code: "ERR_UNEXPECTED_CHAR",
        message: `KÃ½ tá»± khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡ '${ch}'.`,
        line: this.line,
        column: this.col,
      });
      this.advance();
    }

    // Emit trailing NEWLINE and DEDENTs if needed
    const prev = this.tokens[this.tokens.length - 1];
    if (prev && prev.type !== "NEWLINE") {
      this.tokens.push({
        type: "NEWLINE",
        value: "\n",
        line: this.line,
        column: this.col,
        offset: this.pos,
      });
    }

    while (this.indentStack.length > 1) {
      this.indentStack.pop();
      this.tokens.push({
        type: "DEDENT",
        value: 0,
        line: this.line,
        column: this.col,
        offset: this.pos,
      });
    }

    this.tokens.push({
      type: "EOF",
      value: "",
      line: this.line,
      column: this.col,
      offset: this.pos,
    });

    return this.tokens;
  }

  private advance() {
    if (this.pos < this.src.length) {
      if (this.src[this.pos] === "\n") {
        this.line++;
        this.col = 1;
      } else {
        this.col++;
      }
      this.pos++;
    }
  }

  private peek(offset = 0): string {
    const p = this.pos + offset;
    return p < this.src.length ? this.src[p] : "";
  }

  private emit(type: TokenType, val: string | number | boolean) {
    this.tokens.push({
      type,
      value: val,
      line: this.line,
      column: this.col,
      offset: this.pos,
    });
    this.advance();
  }

  private skipComment() {
    while (this.pos < this.src.length && this.src[this.pos] !== "\n") {
      this.advance();
    }
  }

  private readString(quote: string) {
    const startLine = this.line;
    const startCol = this.col;
    const startPos = this.pos;

    this.advance(); // skip opening quote
    let str = "";

    while (this.pos < this.src.length) {
      const ch = this.src[this.pos];
      if (ch === quote) {
        this.advance(); // skip closing quote
        this.tokens.push({
          type: "STRING",
          value: str,
          line: startLine,
          column: startCol,
          offset: startPos,
        });
        return;
      }
      if (ch === "\\") {
        this.advance();
        const esc = this.src[this.pos];
        if (esc === "n") str += "\n";
        else if (esc === "t") str += "\t";
        else if (esc === "\\" || esc === '"' || esc === "'") str += esc;
        else str += esc;
        this.advance();
      } else if (ch === "\n") {
        this.errors.push({
          code: "ERR_UNTERMINATED_STRING",
          message: "Chuá»—i chÆ°a Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘Ã³ng trÆ°á»›c khi xuá»‘ng dÃ²ng.",
          line: startLine,
          column: startCol,
        });
        return;
      } else {
        str += ch;
        this.advance();
      }
    }

    this.errors.push({
      code: "ERR_UNTERMINATED_STRING",
      message: "Chuá»—i chÆ°a Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘Ã³ng trÆ°á»›c khi káº¿t thÃºc táº­p tin.",
      line: startLine,
      column: startCol,
    });
  }

  private readNumber() {
    const startLine = this.line;
    const startCol = this.col;
    const startPos = this.pos;

    let numStr = "";
    if (this.src[this.pos] === "-") {
      numStr += "-";
      this.advance();
    }

    let hasDot = false;
    while (this.pos < this.src.length) {
      const ch = this.src[this.pos];
      if (/[0-9]/.test(ch)) {
        numStr += ch;
        this.advance();
      } else if (ch === "." && !hasDot && /[0-9]/.test(this.peek(1))) {
        hasDot = true;
        numStr += ch;
        this.advance();
      } else {
        break;
      }
    }

    const val = hasDot ? parseFloat(numStr) : parseInt(numStr, 10);
    this.tokens.push({
      type: "NUMBER",
      value: val,
      line: startLine,
      column: startCol,
      offset: startPos,
    });
  }

  private readIdentifier() {
    const startLine = this.line;
    const startCol = this.col;
    const startPos = this.pos;

    let id = "";
    while (this.pos < this.src.length && /[a-zA-Z0-9_]/.test(this.src[this.pos])) {
      id += this.src[this.pos];
      this.advance();
    }

    if (id === "True" || id === "true") {
      this.tokens.push({ type: "BOOLEAN", value: true, line: startLine, column: startCol, offset: startPos });
    } else if (id === "False" || id === "false") {
      this.tokens.push({ type: "BOOLEAN", value: false, line: startLine, column: startCol, offset: startPos });
    } else if (KEYWORDS.has(id)) {
      this.tokens.push({ type: "KEYWORD", value: id, line: startLine, column: startCol, offset: startPos });
    } else {
      this.tokens.push({ type: "IDENTIFIER", value: id, line: startLine, column: startCol, offset: startPos });
    }
  }
}


==================== FILE: C:\Users\ASUS\Downloads\TOOL_2D_ANIM_API_FIX_1\TOOL_2D_ANIM\web-studio\src\animscript\parser.ts ====================
/**
 * AnimScript v0.1 Parser
 * Recursive descent parser generating typed AST with location metadata.
 */

import { Lexer, Token, TokenType } from "./lexer";
import {
  ProgramNode,
  StatementNode,
  AssignmentStatementNode,
  ExpressionStatementNode,
  WithSceneStatementNode,
  WithSequenceStatementNode,
  WithParallelStatementNode,
  ExpressionNode,
  NumberLiteralNode,
  StringLiteralNode,
  BooleanLiteralNode,
  CoordinateLiteralNode,
  IdentifierNode,
  PropertyAccessNode,
  BinaryExpressionNode,
  CallExpressionNode,
  CallArgument,
  NodeSpan,
  SourceLocation,
} from "./ast";
import { CompilerDiagnostic } from "./types";

export class Parser {
  private tokens: Token[] = [];
  private current = 0;
  public diagnostics: CompilerDiagnostic[] = [];

  constructor(private source: string) {}

  public parse(): { program: ProgramNode; diagnostics: CompilerDiagnostic[] } {
    const lexer = new Lexer(this.source);
    this.tokens = lexer.tokenize();
    this.current = 0;
    this.diagnostics = [];

    // Transfer lexer errors to diagnostics
    for (const le of lexer.errors) {
      this.diagnostics.push({
        code: le.code,
        message: le.message,
        line: le.line,
        column: le.column,
        severity: "error",
      });
    }

    const startLoc = this.getLoc();
    const body: StatementNode[] = [];

    this.skipNewlines();
    while (!this.isAtEnd()) {
      try {
        const stmt = this.parseStatement();
        if (stmt) {
          body.push(stmt);
        }
      } catch (err: any) {
        // Synchronize on error to next newline/dedent
        this.synchronize();
      }
      this.skipNewlines();
    }

    const endLoc = this.getLoc();
    const program: ProgramNode = {
      type: "Program",
      span: { start: startLoc, end: endLoc },
      body,
    };

    return { program, diagnostics: this.diagnostics };
  }

  private parseStatement(): StatementNode | null {
    this.skipNewlines();
    if (this.isAtEnd()) return null;

    // With statement (scene, sequence, parallel)
    if (this.matchKeyword("with")) {
      return this.parseWithStatement();
    }

    // Check for assignment: ID = Expr
    if (this.check("IDENTIFIER") && this.peekNext()?.type === "ASSIGN") {
      return this.parseAssignmentStatement();
    }

    // Otherwise: Expression statement
    const exprStart = this.getLoc();
    const expr = this.parseExpression();
    this.consumeNewlineOrEof("Mong Ä‘á»£i káº¿t thÃºc dÃ²ng sau biá»ƒu thá»©c.");
    return {
      type: "ExpressionStatement",
      span: { start: exprStart, end: expr.span.end },
      expression: expr,
    };
  }

  private parseWithStatement(): StatementNode {
    const startLoc = this.previous().line
      ? { line: this.previous().line, column: this.previous().column, offset: this.previous().offset }
      : this.getLoc();

    const blockTypeToken = this.consumeAny(
      ["IDENTIFIER", "KEYWORD"],
      "Mong Ä‘á»£i 'scene', 'sequence' hoáº·c 'parallel' sau 'with'."
    );
    const blockType = String(blockTypeToken.value);

    if (blockType === "scene") {
      this.consume("LPAREN", "Mong Ä‘á»£i '(' sau 'scene'.");
      const nameToken = this.consume("STRING", "Mong Ä‘á»£i tÃªn cáº£nh trong cáº·p ngoáº·c kÃ©p sau scene(...).");
      const sceneName = String(nameToken.value);

      const options: Record<string, ExpressionNode> = {};
      while (!this.check("RPAREN") && !this.isAtEnd()) {
        if (this.match("COMMA")) continue;
        const optKey = this.consume("IDENTIFIER", "Mong Ä‘á»£i tÃªn tham sá»‘ tÃ¹y chá»n cho scene.").value as string;
        this.consume("ASSIGN", `Mong Ä‘á»£i '=' sau tham sá»‘ ${optKey}.`);
        options[optKey] = this.parseExpression();
      }
      this.consume("RPAREN", "Mong Ä‘á»£i ')' sau khai bÃ¡o scene.");
      this.consume("COLON", "Mong Ä‘á»£i ':' á»Ÿ cuá»‘i dÃ²ng khai bÃ¡o 'with scene(...)'.");
      this.consumeNewline("Mong Ä‘á»£i xuá»‘ng dÃ²ng sau dáº¥u ':'.");

      const body = this.parseBlock();
      return {
        type: "WithSceneStatement",
        span: { start: startLoc, end: this.getLoc() },
        sceneName,
        options,
        body,
      };
    } else if (blockType === "sequence") {
      this.consume("LPAREN", "Mong Ä‘á»£i '(' sau 'sequence'.");
      this.consume("RPAREN", "Mong Ä‘á»£i ')' sau 'sequence('.");
      this.consume("COLON", "Mong Ä‘á»£i ':' á»Ÿ cuá»‘i dÃ²ng khai bÃ¡o 'with sequence():'.");
      this.consumeNewline("Mong Ä‘á»£i xuá»‘ng dÃ²ng sau dáº¥u ':'.");

      const body = this.parseBlock();
      return {
        type: "WithSequenceStatement",
        span: { start: startLoc, end: this.getLoc() },
        body,
      };
    } else if (blockType === "parallel") {
      this.consume("LPAREN", "Mong Ä‘á»£i '(' sau 'parallel'.");
      this.consume("RPAREN", "Mong Ä‘á»£i ')' sau 'parallel('.");
      this.consume("COLON", "Mong Ä‘á»£i ':' á»Ÿ cuá»‘i dÃ²ng khai bÃ¡o 'with parallel():'.");
      this.consumeNewline("Mong Ä‘á»£i xuá»‘ng dÃ²ng sau dáº¥u ':'.");

      const body = this.parseBlock();
      return {
        type: "WithParallelStatement",
        span: { start: startLoc, end: this.getLoc() },
        body,
      };
    }

    throw this.error(
      "ERR_INVALID_WITH_BLOCK",
      `Khá»‘i 'with ${blockType}' khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c há»— trá»£. Chá»‰ há»— trá»£ 'with scene(...)', 'with sequence():', hoáº·c 'with parallel():'.`,
      "Thay báº±ng 'with scene(...)', 'with sequence():' hoáº·c 'with parallel():'."
    );
  }

  private parseBlock(): StatementNode[] {
    this.consume("INDENT", "Mong Ä‘á»£i khá»‘i lá»‡nh thá»¥t lá» vÃ o.");
    const body: StatementNode[] = [];
    this.skipNewlines();

    while (!this.check("DEDENT") && !this.isAtEnd()) {
      const stmt = this.parseStatement();
      if (stmt) body.push(stmt);
      this.skipNewlines();
    }

    this.consume("DEDENT", "Mong Ä‘á»£i káº¿t thÃºc khá»‘i thá»¥t lá».");
    return body;
  }

  private parseAssignmentStatement(): AssignmentStatementNode {
    const idToken = this.consume("IDENTIFIER", "Mong Ä‘á»£i tÃªn biáº¿n.");
    const target = String(idToken.value);
    this.consume("ASSIGN", "Mong Ä‘á»£i '='.");
    const value = this.parseExpression();
    this.consumeNewlineOrEof("Mong Ä‘á»£i káº¿t thÃºc dÃ²ng sau phÃ©p gÃ¡n.");

    return {
      type: "AssignmentStatement",
      span: {
        start: { line: idToken.line, column: idToken.column, offset: idToken.offset },
        end: value.span.end,
      },
      target,
      value,
    };
  }

  private parseExpression(): ExpressionNode {
    return this.parseBinaryExpression();
  }

  private parseBinaryExpression(): ExpressionNode {
    let left = this.parsePrimary();

    while (this.match("PLUS") || this.match("MINUS")) {
      const opToken = this.previous();
      const operator = opToken.type === "PLUS" ? "+" : "-";
      const right = this.parsePrimary();
      left = {
        type: "BinaryExpression",
        span: { start: left.span.start, end: right.span.end },
        operator,
        left,
        right,
      };
    }

    return left;
  }

  private parsePrimary(): ExpressionNode {
    // Number
    if (this.check("NUMBER")) {
      const token = this.advance();
      return {
        type: "NumberLiteral",
        span: this.tokenSpan(token),
        value: Number(token.value),
      };
    }

    // String
    if (this.check("STRING")) {
      const token = this.advance();
      return {
        type: "StringLiteral",
        span: this.tokenSpan(token),
        value: String(token.value),
      };
    }

    // Boolean
    if (this.check("BOOLEAN")) {
      const token = this.advance();
      return {
        type: "BooleanLiteral",
        span: this.tokenSpan(token),
        value: Boolean(token.value),
      };
    }

    // Coordinate / List: [x, y]
    if (this.match("LBRACKET")) {
      const startLoc = this.previousLoc();
      const elements: ExpressionNode[] = [];
      if (!this.check("RBRACKET")) {
        do {
          if (this.check("RBRACKET")) break;
          elements.push(this.parseExpression());
        } while (this.match("COMMA"));
      }
      const endToken = this.consume("RBRACKET", "Mong Ä‘á»£i ']' Ä‘Ã³ng danh sÃ¡ch tá»a Ä‘á»™.");
      return {
        type: "CoordinateLiteral",
        span: { start: startLoc, end: this.tokenEndLoc(endToken) },
        elements,
      };
    }

    // Parenthesized expression: ( expr )
    if (this.match("LPAREN")) {
      const expr = this.parseExpression();
      this.consume("RPAREN", "Mong Ä‘á»£i ')' Ä‘Ã³ng ngoáº·c.");
      return expr;
    }

    // Identifier, PropertyAccess, or Function Call
    if (this.check("IDENTIFIER") || this.check("KEYWORD")) {
      const idToken = this.advance();
      let expr: ExpressionNode = {
        type: "Identifier",
        span: this.tokenSpan(idToken),
        name: String(idToken.value),
      };

      // Property access or function call loop
      while (true) {
        if (this.match("DOT")) {
          const propToken = this.consumeAny(
            ["IDENTIFIER", "KEYWORD"],
            "Mong Ä‘á»£i tÃªn thuá»™c tÃ­nh hoáº·c Ä‘iá»ƒm neo sau dáº¥u '.'."
          );
          expr = {
            type: "PropertyAccess",
            span: { start: expr.span.start, end: this.tokenEndLoc(propToken) },
            object: expr,
            property: String(propToken.value),
          };
        } else if (this.match("LPAREN")) {
          // Function call: expr must be identifier
          if (expr.type !== "Identifier") {
            throw this.error(
              "ERR_INVALID_CALL",
              "Chá»‰ cÃ³ thá»ƒ gá»i trá»±c tiáº¿p hÃ m Ä‘á»‹nh danh, khÃ´ng thá»ƒ gá»i thuá»™c tÃ­nh lá»“ng."
            );
          }
          const args = this.parseCallArguments();
          const rparenToken = this.consume("RPAREN", "Mong Ä‘á»£i ')' Ä‘Ã³ng lá»i gá»i hÃ m.");
          expr = {
            type: "CallExpression",
            span: { start: expr.span.start, end: this.tokenEndLoc(rparenToken) },
            callee: expr.name,
            args,
          };
        } else {
          break;
        }
      }

      return expr;
    }

    throw this.error(
      "ERR_UNEXPECTED_TOKEN",
      `KÃ½ hiá»‡u khÃ´ng mong Ä‘á»£i '${String(this.peek()?.value ?? "")}' (${this.peek()?.type}).`,
      "Kiá»ƒm tra láº¡i cÃº phÃ¡p biá»ƒu thá»©c."
    );
  }

  private parseCallArguments(): CallArgument[] {
    const args: CallArgument[] = [];
    if (this.check("RPAREN")) return args;

    do {
      if (this.check("RPAREN")) break;

      // Check if named argument: name = expr
      if (this.check("IDENTIFIER") && this.peekNext()?.type === "ASSIGN") {
        const nameToken = this.advance();
        this.advance(); // consume '='
        const val = this.parseExpression();
        args.push({ name: String(nameToken.value), value: val });
      } else {
        const val = this.parseExpression();
        args.push({ value: val });
      }
    } while (this.match("COMMA"));

    return args;
  }

  // --- Helper Methods ---

  private match(type: TokenType): boolean {
    if (this.check(type)) {
      this.advance();
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  private matchKeyword(kw: string): boolean {
    if (this.check("KEYWORD") && this.peek()?.value === kw) {
      this.advance();
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  private check(type: TokenType): boolean {
    if (this.isAtEnd()) return false;
    return this.peek().type === type;
  }

  private advance(): Token {
    if (!this.isAtEnd()) this.current++;
    return this.previous();
  }

  private isAtEnd(): boolean {
    return this.peek().type === "EOF";
  }

  private peek(): Token {
    return this.tokens[this.current] || { type: "EOF", value: "", line: 1, column: 1, offset: 0 };
  }

  private peekNext(): Token | undefined {
    return this.tokens[this.current + 1];
  }

  private previous(): Token {
    return this.tokens[this.current - 1];
  }

  private consume(type: TokenType, message: string): Token {
    if (this.check(type)) return this.advance();
    throw this.error(`ERR_EXPECTED_${type}`, message);
  }

  private consumeAny(types: TokenType[], message: string): Token {
    for (const t of types) {
      if (this.check(t)) return this.advance();
    }
    throw this.error("ERR_UNEXPECTED_TOKEN", message);
  }

  private consumeNewline(message: string): void {
    if (this.match("NEWLINE")) return;
    if (this.isAtEnd()) return;
    throw this.error("ERR_EXPECTED_NEWLINE", message);
  }

  private consumeNewlineOrEof(message: string): void {
    if (this.match("NEWLINE") || this.isAtEnd() || this.check("DEDENT")) return;
    throw this.error("ERR_EXPECTED_NEWLINE", message);
  }

  private skipNewlines(): void {
    while (this.match("NEWLINE")) {}
  }

  private getLoc(): SourceLocation {
    const t = this.peek();
    return { line: t.line, column: t.column, offset: t.offset };
  }

  private previousLoc(): SourceLocation {
    const t = this.previous();
    return { line: t.line, column: t.column, offset: t.offset };
  }

  private tokenSpan(t: Token): NodeSpan {
    return {
      start: { line: t.line, column: t.column, offset: t.offset },
      end: this.tokenEndLoc(t),
    };
  }

  private tokenEndLoc(t: Token): SourceLocation {
    const len = String(t.value).length;
    return {
      line: t.line,
      column: t.column + len,
      offset: t.offset + len,
    };
  }

  private error(code: string, message: string, fixSuggestion?: string): Error {
    const t = this.peek();
    this.diagnostics.push({
      code,
      message,
      line: t.line,
      column: t.column,
      severity: "error",
      fixSuggestion,
    });
    return new Error(`[${code}] ${message} táº¡i dÃ²ng ${t.line}, cá»™t ${t.column}`);
  }

  private synchronize(): void {
    this.advance();
    while (!this.isAtEnd()) {
      if (this.previous().type === "NEWLINE") return;
      if (this.check("KEYWORD") && this.peek().value === "with") return;
      this.advance();
    }
  }
}
