"use client";

import React, { useRef } from "react";
import dynamic from "next/dynamic";
import { MasterSceneGraph } from "../remotion/types/sceneGraph";
import { MainScene } from "../remotion/Composition";
import { AnimScriptScene } from "../remotion/compositions/AnimScriptScene";
import { AnimScriptIR } from "../animscript";
import { RotateCcw, RefreshCw, Sparkles, Film } from "lucide-react";

// Dynamic import with SSR disabled for client-only Remotion player
const Player = dynamic(
  () => import("@remotion/player").then((mod) => mod.Player),
  { ssr: false }
);

interface PreviewPlayerProps {
  sceneGraph?: MasterSceneGraph;
  animScriptIR?: AnimScriptIR;
  playerRefProp?: React.RefObject<any>;
  version?: number;
  onRefresh?: () => void;
}

export const PreviewPlayer: React.FC<PreviewPlayerProps> = ({
  sceneGraph,
  animScriptIR,
  playerRefProp,
  version = 0,
  onRefresh,
}) => {
  const localRef = useRef<any>(null);
  const activeRef = playerRefProp || localRef;

  // Náº¿u cÃ³ AnimScript IR, Æ°u tiÃªn hiá»ƒn thá»‹ AnimScript Engine
  const isAnimScript = Boolean(animScriptIR && animScriptIR.scenes?.length > 0);

  // TÃ­nh toÃ¡n an toÃ n tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i tá»•ng sá»‘ frame, khÃ´ng bao giá» Ä‘á»ƒ bá»‹ NaN
  const safeScenes = Array.isArray(sceneGraph?.scenes) ? sceneGraph.scenes : [];
  const calculatedTotal = safeScenes.reduce((acc, s) => {
    const start = Number(s?.startFrame) || 0;
    const dur = Number(s?.durationInFrames) || 120;
    return Math.max(acc, start + dur);
  }, 480);

  const rawTotal = isAnimScript
    ? animScriptIR?.totalDurationFrames || 360
    : Number(sceneGraph?.totalDurationInFrames) || calculatedTotal;
  const totalFrames = Math.max(60, isNaN(rawTotal) ? 480 : Math.round(rawTotal));

  const handleRewind = () => {
    if (activeRef.current) {
      activeRef.current.seekTo(0);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="relative flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-[#232326] bg-[#121215] p-3 shadow-2xl">
      {/* 1080p Preview Monitor Frame */}
      <div className="relative aspect-video w-full overflow-hidden rounded-xl bg-[#09090B] border border-[#1E1E22] shadow-inner">
        {isAnimScript ? (
          <Player
            key={`animscript-player-${totalFrames}-${version}`}
            ref={activeRef}
            component={AnimScriptScene as any}
            inputProps={{ ir: animScriptIR! }}
            durationInFrames={totalFrames}
            fps={60}
            compositionWidth={1920}
            compositionHeight={1080}
            style={{ width: "100%", height: "100%" }}
            controls
            loop
            autoPlay={false}
          />
        ) : (
          <Player
            key={`remotion-player-${sceneGraph?.title || "preview"}-${totalFrames}-${version}`}
            ref={activeRef}
            component={MainScene}
            inputProps={{ sceneGraph }}
            durationInFrames={totalFrames}
            fps={60}
            compositionWidth={1920}
            compositionHeight={1080}
            style={{
              width: "100%",
              height: "100%",
            }}
            controls
            loop
            autoPlay={false}
          />
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Control Bar & Resolution Info */}
      <div className="mt-3 flex w-full items-center justify-between px-2 text-xs text-[#8E8E93]">
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
          <span className="inline-block h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-blue-500 animate-pulse" />
          <span className="font-mono text-zinc-300">1920 x 1080 (Full HD)</span>
          <span className="text-zinc-600">|</span>
          <span className="font-mono text-zinc-300">60.0 FPS</span>
          <span className="text-zinc-600">|</span>
          <span className="font-mono text-zinc-400">
            {totalFrames} frames ({(totalFrames / 60).toFixed(1)}s)
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
          <button
            onClick={handleRewind}
            title="Quay vá» khung hÃ¬nh Ä‘áº§u tiÃªn (Frame 0)"
            className="flex items-center space-x-1 rounded-lg border border-[#27272A] bg-[#18181B] px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] text-zinc-300 hover:text-white hover:border-zinc-500 transition-colors"
          >
            <RotateCcw className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-zinc-400" />
            <span>Frame 0</span>
          </button>

          {onRefresh && (
            <button
              onClick={onRefresh}
              title="LÃ m má»›i láº¡i toÃ n bá»™ tÃ i nguyÃªn Ä‘á»“ há»a (Hot Reload Preview)"
              className="flex items-center space-x-1 rounded-lg border border-blue-500/30 bg-blue-500/10 px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] text-blue-300 hover:text-white hover:bg-blue-600/30 transition-colors"
            >
              <RefreshCw className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-blue-400" />
              <span>LÃ m má»›i Preview</span>
            </button>
          )}

          <span className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500 hidden sm:inline">GPU Vector 1080p</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

"use client";

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import {
  X,
  CloudLightning,
  Loader2,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertCircle,
  Download,
  ExternalLink,
  Clock,
  Cpu,
  Check,
  Key,
  Sparkles,
} from "lucide-react";
import { MasterSceneGraph } from "../remotion/types/sceneGraph";

interface ChunkStatus {
  name: string;
  status: string;
  conclusion: string | null;
}

interface JobsSummary {
  total: number;
  completed: number;
  inProgress: number;
  queued: number;
  totalChunks: number;
  completedChunks: number;
  inProgressChunks: number;
  isStitching: boolean;
  progressPercent: number;
  chunks: ChunkStatus[];
}

interface RenderModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onOpenSettings?: () => void;
  sceneGraph: MasterSceneGraph;
  githubToken: string;
  githubOwner: string;
  githubRepo: string;
  isScriptOutOfSync?: boolean;
  currentScript?: string;
  geminiKey?: string;
  onSceneGraphGenerated?: (graph: MasterSceneGraph) => void;
}

export const RenderModal: React.FC<RenderModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onOpenSettings,
  sceneGraph,
  githubToken,
  githubOwner,
  githubRepo,
  isScriptOutOfSync = false,
  currentScript = "",
  geminiKey = "",
  onSceneGraphGenerated,
}) => {
  const [chunkCount, setChunkCount] = useState(1);
  const [isDispatching, setIsDispatching] = useState(false);
  const [isRendering, setIsRendering] = useState(false);
  const [isUpdatingScript, setIsUpdatingScript] = useState(false);
  const [workflowStatus, setWorkflowStatus] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [runUrl, setRunUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [downloadUrl, setDownloadUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Real-time Progress & ETA States
  const [progressPercent, setProgressPercent] = useState(0);
  const [elapsedSeconds, setElapsedSeconds] = useState(0);
  const [etaSeconds, setEtaSeconds] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [jobsData, setJobsData] = useState<JobsSummary | null>(null);

  const pollIntervalRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);
  const timerIntervalRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);
  const startTimeRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const activeRunIdRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

  // Clear intervals on unmount
  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (pollIntervalRef.current) clearInterval(pollIntervalRef.current);
      if (timerIntervalRef.current) clearInterval(timerIntervalRef.current);
    };
  }, []);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const totalFrames = sceneGraph.scenes.reduce((acc, s) => {
    const start = Number(s?.startFrame) || 0;
    const dur = Number(s?.durationInFrames) || 120;
    return Math.max(acc, start + dur);
  }, 360);

  // Format giÃ¢y thÃ nh chuá»—i thÃ¢n thiá»‡n
  const formatTime = (seconds: number): string => {
    if (seconds < 60) return `${seconds}s`;
    const mins = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
    const secs = seconds % 60;
    return `${mins}m ${secs}s`;
  };

  const handleStartRender = async () => {
    setIsDispatching(true);
    setIsRendering(true);
    setErrorMessage(null);
    setWorkflowStatus("Äang gá»­i lá»‡nh Workflow Dispatch tá»›i GitHub Actions...");
    setDownloadUrl(null);
    setProgressPercent(3);
    setElapsedSeconds(0);
    setEtaSeconds(45); // Benchmark ban Ä‘áº§u ~45-60s cho 15 mÃ¡y áº£o
    setJobsData(null);
    activeRunIdRef.current = null;

    const scriptId = `story_${Date.now()}`;
    const startTime = Date.now();
    startTimeRef.current = startTime;

    // Timer Ä‘áº¿m giÃ¢y cháº¡y liÃªn tá»¥c
    if (timerIntervalRef.current) clearInterval(timerIntervalRef.current);
    timerIntervalRef.current = setInterval(() => {
      const elapsed = Math.round((Date.now() - startTime) / 1000);
      setElapsedSeconds(elapsed);
    }, 1000);

    try {
      // 1. Dispatch workflow
      const res = await fetch("/api/github-dispatch", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action: "dispatch",
          githubToken,
          owner: githubOwner,
          repo: githubRepo,
          scriptId,
          totalFrames,
          chunkCount,
          sceneGraph,
        }),
      });

      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok || !data.success) {
        throw new Error(data.error || "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ dispatch GitHub Actions");
      }

      setWorkflowStatus(`ÄÃ£ gá»­i lá»‡nh thÃ nh cÃ´ng! Äang chá» GitHub khá»Ÿi táº¡o ${chunkCount} mÃ¡y áº£o Ubuntu (16GB RAM)...`);

      // 2. Start polling for run status every 3.5s
      if (pollIntervalRef.current) clearInterval(pollIntervalRef.current);
      pollIntervalRef.current = setInterval(async () => {
        try {
          const statusRes = await fetch("/api/github-dispatch", {
            method: "POST",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              action: "status",
              githubToken,
              owner: githubOwner,
              repo: githubRepo,
              dispatchedAt: startTime,
              runId: activeRunIdRef.current,
              chunkCount,
            }),
          });
          const statusData = await statusRes.json();

          if (statusData.run) {
            if (statusData.run.id && !activeRunIdRef.current) {
              activeRunIdRef.current = statusData.run.id;
            }

            if (statusData.run.pendingCreation) {
              setWorkflowStatus(`Äang chá» GitHub Actions khá»Ÿi táº¡o ${chunkCount} mÃ¡y áº£o Ubuntu (16GB RAM)...`);
              return;
            }

            const summary: JobsSummary | undefined = statusData.run.jobsSummary;
            if (summary) {
              setJobsData(summary);
              const currentPercent = summary.progressPercent;
              setProgressPercent(currentPercent);

              // TÃ­nh toÃ¡n ETA dá»±a trÃªn thá»i gian thá»±c táº¿ Ä‘Ã£ trÃ´i qua
              const elapsed = Math.round((Date.now() - startTime) / 1000);

                const totalEstimated = Math.round((elapsed / currentPercent) * 100);
                const remaining = Math.max(3, totalEstimated - elapsed);
                setEtaSeconds(remaining);
              }

              if (summary.isStitching) {
                setWorkflowStatus("GhÃ©p ná»‘i cÃ¡c chunks báº±ng FFmpeg vÃ  Ä‘Ã³ng gÃ³i video 1080p60...");
              } else if (summary.completedChunks > 0) {

                  `ÄÃ£ hoÃ n thÃ nh ${summary.completedChunks}/${summary.totalChunks} chunks (${summary.inProgressChunks} mÃ¡y áº£o Ä‘ang render)...`
                );
              }
            }

            // HoÃ n táº¥t thÃ nh cÃ´ng
            if (statusData.run.status === "completed") {
              if (statusData.run.conclusion === "success") {
                if (pollIntervalRef.current) clearInterval(pollIntervalRef.current);
                if (timerIntervalRef.current) clearInterval(timerIntervalRef.current);
                setProgressPercent(100);
                setEtaSeconds(0);
                setWorkflowStatus("HoÃ n táº¥t 100%! Äang láº¥y Ä‘Æ°á»ng link táº£i video thÃ nh pháº©m...");

                // Láº¥y báº£n phÃ¡t hÃ nh release asset
                const releaseRes = await fetch("/api/github-dispatch", {
                  method: "POST",
                  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
                  body: JSON.stringify({
                    action: "get-release",
                    githubToken,
                    owner: githubOwner,
                    repo: githubRepo,
                    scriptId,
                  }),
                });
                const releaseData = await releaseRes.json();
                if (releaseData.downloadUrl) {
                  setDownloadUrl(releaseData.downloadUrl);
                  setWorkflowStatus("Video Full HD (1920x1080) @ 60 FPS Ä‘Ã£ sáºµn sÃ ng!");
                  setIsRendering(false);
                }
              } else {
                if (pollIntervalRef.current) clearInterval(pollIntervalRef.current);
                if (timerIntervalRef.current) clearInterval(timerIntervalRef.current);
                setErrorMessage(`Workflow káº¿t thÃºc vá»›i tráº¡ng thÃ¡i: ${statusData.run.conclusion}`);
                setIsRendering(false);
              }
            }
          }
        } catch (e) {
          // Bá» qua lá»—i táº¡m thá»i khi polling
        }
      }, 3500);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setErrorMessage(err.message || "Lá»—i khá»Ÿi cháº¡y Cloud Render");
      setIsRendering(false);
      if (timerIntervalRef.current) clearInterval(timerIntervalRef.current);

      setIsDispatching(false);
    }

                  ) : (

                  </span>

                    </span>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-5 gap-1.5">
                    {jobsData.chunks.map((chk, i) => {
                      const isDone = chk.status === "completed" && chk.conclusion === "success";
                      const isRunning = chk.status === "in_progress";
                      return (
                        <div
                          key={i}
                          className={`flex items-center justify-between rounded px-2 py-1 text-[10px] font-mono border transition-all ${
                            isDone
                              ? "bg-emerald-950/50 border-emerald-500/40 text-emerald-300"
                              : isRunning
                              ? "bg-blue-950/50 border-blue-500/40 text-blue-300 animate-pulse"
                              : "bg-[#0E0E11] border-[#27272A] text-zinc-500"
                          }`}
                        >
                          <span>{chk.name}</span>
                          {isDone ? (
                            <Check className="h-2.5 w-2.5 text-emerald-400" />
                          ) : isRunning ? (
                            <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-blue-400 animate-ping" />
                          ) : (
                            <span className="text-[9px]">chá»</span>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* GitHub Actions Link */}
              {runUrl && (
                <div className="pt-1">
                  <a
                    href={runUrl}
                    target="_blank"
                    rel="noreferrer"
                    className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 text-[11px] text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-300 hover:underline"
                  >
                    <span>Xem trá»±c tiáº¿p logs 15 mÃ¡y áº£o trÃªn GitHub Actions</span>
                    <ExternalLink className="h-3 w-3" />
                  </a>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Error */}
          {errorMessage && (
            <div className="flex flex-col space-y-2 rounded-xl bg-rose-950/40 p-3 border border-rose-500/30 text-rose-300">
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-rose-400" />
                <span>{errorMessage}</span>
              </div>
              {onOpenSettings && errorMessage.includes("GitHub Personal Access Token") && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    onClose();
                    onOpenSettings();
                  }}
                  className="self-start mt-1 flex items-center space-x-1.5 rounded-lg bg-rose-500/20 px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-rose-200 hover:bg-rose-500/30 transition-colors border border-rose-500/30"
                >
                  <Key className="h-3 w-3" />
                  <span>Báº¥m vÃ o Ä‘Ã¢y Ä‘á»ƒ má»Ÿ Cáº¥u hÃ¬nh API</span>
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Download ready button */}
          {downloadUrl && (
            <a
              href={downloadUrl}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noreferrer"
              className="flex items-center justify-center space-x-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-white shadow-lg shadow-emerald-600/30 transition-all hover:bg-emerald-500 animate-bounce"
            >
              <Download className="h-4 w-4" />
              <span>Táº£i Video ThÃ nh Pháº©m 1080p @ 60FPS (.MP4)</span>
            </a>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Footer actions */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-end space-x-3 pt-3 border-t border-[#232326]">
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="rounded-xl border border-[#27272A] bg-[#18181B] px-4 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-400 hover:text-white transition-colors"
          >
            ÄÃ³ng
          </button>

          {!downloadUrl && (
            <button
              onClick={handleStartRender}
              disabled={isDispatching || isRendering}
              className="flex items-center space-x-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white shadow-md hover:bg-blue-500 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors"
            >
              {isDispatching || isRendering ? (
                <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
              ) : (
                <CloudLightning className="h-4 w-4" />
              )}
              <span>{isRendering ? "Äang xá»­ lÃ½..." : "KÃ­ch hoáº¡t Render ÄÃ¡m mÃ¢y"}</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

        </div>
      )}

      {/* Multi-Prompt Pipeline Stepper Progress */}
      {isLoadingGemini && (
        <div className="flex flex-col space-y-2.5 rounded-xl border border-blue-500/30 bg-blue-950/20 p-3.5">

export const TimelineViewer: React.FC<TimelineViewerProps> = ({

  onSelectScene,
}) => {
  const [showJsonRaw, setShowJsonRaw] = useState(false);

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col space-y-4 rounded-2xl border border-[#232326] bg-[#121215] p-5 shadow-lg">

        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">

          <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-white">PhÃ¢n Ä‘oáº¡n Timeline & Scene Graph</h2>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => setShowJsonRaw(!showJsonRaw)}
          className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 rounded-lg border border-[#27272A] bg-[#18181B] px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] text-zinc-400 hover:text-white"
        >
          <Code2 className="h-3 w-3" />

        </button>
      </div>

      {showJsonRaw ? (
        <pre className="max-h-72 overflow-y-auto rounded-xl border border-[#27272A] bg-[#0A0A0C] p-4 text-[11px] font-mono leading-relaxed text-emerald-400">
          {JSON.stringify(sceneGraph, null, 2)}
        </pre>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col space-y-2.5 max-h-72 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
          {(sceneGraph?.scenes || []).map((scene, idx) => {

                      {idx + 1}
                    </span>
                    <h4 className="text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-200 group-hover:text-white">

                    </h4>

                    <span className="flex items-center space-x-1">

==================== FILE: C:\Users\ASUS\Downloads\TOOL_2D_ANIM_API_FIX_1\TOOL_2D_ANIM\web-studio\src\lib\pipeline\designSingleSvgAsset.ts ====================
import {generateContentCancellable} from '../geminiTransport';
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { GoogleGenerativeAI } from "@google/generative-ai";
import { executeGeminiWithRetry, sanitizeLog } from "../geminiRetry";
import { normalizePropAssetId } from "../../remotion/utils/propResolver";

export interface SvgAssetDesignResult {
  propId: string;
  cached: boolean;
  filePath: string;
  svgContent: string;
}

const SVG_ARTIST_SYSTEM_PROMPT = `You are a Principal 2D Vector Graphic Illustrator for an award-winning animation studio.
Your task is to design a single, beautiful, high-end standalone cartoon vector object as pure SVG.

MANDATORY GEOMETRIC STANDARDS (NON-NEGOTIABLE):
1. CANVAS VIEWBOX:
   viewBox="0 0 400 400" width="400" height="400"
2. BOTTOM-CENTER ORIGIN (ANCHOR SPEC):
   - The lowest point of the object MUST TOUCH EXACTLY y = 380.
   - The horizontal center of the object MUST BE ALIGNED AT x = 200.
   - This ensures 100% universal docking onto floors and tables with zero floating.
3. PREMIUM 5+ LAYER VISUAL QUALITY:
   Do NOT draw a lazy flat rectangle or basic circle. You MUST build the object using at least 5 to 8 distinct SVG elements/layers:
   - Layer 1: Ground contact shadow (<ellipse cx="200" cy="380" rx="75" ry="12" fill="#000000" opacity="0.15"/>).
   - Layer 2: Main body with rich gradients (<linearGradient> or <radialGradient> defined in <defs>).
   - Layer 3: Ambient occlusion & 3D shadow curves (darker shading paths with opacity="0.20").
   - Layer 4: Specular highlights & reflections (bright white streaks with opacity="0.45").
   - Layer 5: Accent details (textures, grooves, buttons, labels, eyes/mouth if living creature).
   - Layer 6: Clean, expressive cartoon stroke (stroke="#1A1A1A" stroke-width="10" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round").
4. STANDALONE VALID XML:
   - Output ONLY the raw <svg>...</svg> XML.
   - DO NOT wrap in markdown code fences (\`\`\`xml or \`\`\`).
   - Must include xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg".`;

/**
 * Táº¡o SVG cháº¥t lÆ°á»£ng cao theo chuáº©n 400x400 (cháº¡m Ä‘Ã¡y y=380, tÃ¢m x=200) khi offline
 */
export function createStandardProceduralSvg(propId: string): string {
  const hash = propId.split("").reduce((acc, c) => acc + c.charCodeAt(0), 0);
  const colorPalettes = [
    { main: "#F59E0B", light: "#FEF3C7", dark: "#B45309", stroke: "#1A1A1A" },
    { main: "#3B82F6", light: "#DBEAFE", dark: "#1D4ED8", stroke: "#1A1A1A" },
    { main: "#10B981", light: "#D1FAE5", dark: "#047857", stroke: "#1A1A1A" },
    { main: "#EC4899", light: "#FCE7F3", dark: "#BE185D", stroke: "#1A1A1A" },
    { main: "#8B5CF6", light: "#EDE9FE", dark: "#6D28D9", stroke: "#1A1A1A" },
    { main: "#EF4444", light: "#FEE2E2", dark: "#B91C1C", stroke: "#1A1A1A" },
  ];
  const pal = colorPalettes[hash % colorPalettes.length];
  const label = propId.replace(/[-_]/g, " ").toUpperCase();

  return `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 400 400" width="400" height="400">
  <defs>
    <radialGradient id="baseShadow_${hash}" cx="50%" cy="50%" r="50%">
      <stop offset="0%" stop-color="#000000" stop-opacity="0.30"/>
      <stop offset="100%" stop-color="#000000" stop-opacity="0"/>
    </radialGradient>
    <linearGradient id="bodyGrad_${hash}" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="100%">
      <stop offset="0%" stop-color="${pal.light}"/>
      <stop offset="50%" stop-color="${pal.main}"/>
      <stop offset="100%" stop-color="${pal.dark}"/>
    </linearGradient>
  </defs>
  <!-- Layer 1: Contact Drop Shadow at y=380 -->
  <ellipse cx="200" cy="380" rx="90" ry="14" fill="url(#baseShadow_${hash})"/>
  <!-- Layer 2: Main Body Base touching y=380 -->
  <rect x="90" y="140" width="220" height="240" rx="36" fill="url(#bodyGrad_${hash})" stroke="${pal.stroke}" stroke-width="10" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
  <!-- Layer 3: 3D Inner Shadow Curve -->
  <path d="M 100 350 Q 200 390 300 350 L 300 370 Q 200 385 100 370 Z" fill="#000000" opacity="0.22"/>
  <!-- Layer 4: Specular Glossy Reflection Streak -->
  <path d="M 120 160 L 150 160 L 115 320 L 100 320 Z" fill="#FFFFFF" opacity="0.45"/>
  <!-- Layer 5: Decorative Star Badge -->
  <circle cx="200" cy="220" r="42" fill="${pal.light}" stroke="${pal.stroke}" stroke-width="6"/>
  <path d="M 200 195 L 208 212 L 226 214 L 212 226 L 216 244 L 200 234 L 184 244 L 188 226 L 174 214 L 192 212 Z" fill="${pal.dark}"/>
  <!-- Layer 6: Label Plate -->
  <rect x="110" y="290" width="180" height="42" rx="10" fill="#1A1A1A"/>
  <text x="200" y="318" text-anchor="middle" font-size="14" font-weight="900" fill="#FFFFFF" font-family="sans-serif" letter-spacing="1.5">${label}</text>
</svg>`;
}

/**
 * BÆ°á»›c 2: Thiáº¿t káº¿ tá»«ng váº­t thá»ƒ SVG riÃªng biá»‡t (1 Prompt / 1 Object)
 * TuÃ¢n thá»§ tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i chuáº©n 400x400 cháº¡m Ä‘Ã¡y y=380, tÃ¢m x=200.
 */
export async function designSingleSvgAsset(
  propNameOrId: string,
  modelName: string = "gemini-3.1-flash-lite",
  apiKey?: string,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
  requestId?: string
): Promise<SvgAssetDesignResult> {
  const reqId = requestId || "req_local";

  if (signal?.aborted) {
    throw new DOMException("YÃªu cáº§u Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ há»§y bá»Ÿi ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng", "AbortError");
  }

  const canonicalId = normalizePropAssetId(propNameOrId);
  if (!/^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*$/.test(canonicalId)) throw new Error("Asset ID khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡");
  const assetsDir = path.join(process.cwd(), "public", "assets", "props");

  if (!fs.existsSync(assetsDir)) {
    fs.mkdirSync(assetsDir, { recursive: true });
  }

  const filePath = path.join(assetsDir, `${canonicalId}.svg`);

  // 1. Kiá»ƒm tra cache náº¿u file Ä‘Ã£ tá»“n táº¡i vÃ  Ä‘Ãºng chuáº©n
  if (fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
    const existingContent = fs.readFileSync(filePath, "utf8");
    if (existingContent.includes("<svg") && existingContent.length > 100) {
      return {
        propId: canonicalId,
        cached: true,
        filePath,
        svgContent: existingContent,
      };
    }
  }

  console.log(
    sanitizeLog(`[Pipeline][${reqId}] [BÆ°á»›c 2] Thiáº¿t káº¿ Vector Asset 400x400 cho '${canonicalId}' (Model: ${modelName})...`)
  );

  const activeKey = (apiKey || process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY || "").trim();
  let finalSvg = "";

  if (activeKey) {
    try {
      const genAI = new GoogleGenerativeAI(activeKey);
      finalSvg = await executeGeminiWithRetry(
        async () => {
          if (signal?.aborted) {
            throw new DOMException("YÃªu cáº§u Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ há»§y bá»Ÿi ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng", "AbortError");
          }

          const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({
            model: modelName,
            systemInstruction: SVG_ARTIST_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
          });
          const res = await generateContentCancellable(model,
            `Design a cartoon SVG prop: '${canonicalId}' (${propNameOrId}). Follow the 400x400 standard touching y=380 at x=200 with 5+ layers.`, signal
          );
          let raw = res.response.text().trim();
          raw = raw.replace(/^```(?:svg|xml|html)?\n?/i, "").replace(/\n?```$/i, "").trim();
          if (!raw.includes("<svg") || !raw.includes("</svg>")) {
            throw new Error(`Äáº§u ra SVG khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡ cho ${canonicalId}`);
          }
          return raw;
        },
        {
          maxRetries: 2,
          signal,
          requestId: reqId,
          step: 2,
          modelName,
        }
      );
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (err.name === "AbortError" || signal?.aborted) {
        throw err;
      }
      console.warn(
        sanitizeLog(`[Pipeline][${reqId}] [BÆ°á»›c 2] Lá»—i táº¡o vector qua AI cho '${canonicalId}': ${err.message}`)
      );
      throw err;
    }
  } else {
    throw new Error("Cáº§n API key Ä‘á»ƒ táº¡o asset cÃ²n thiáº¿u.");
  }

  // Äáº£m báº£o viewBox chuáº©n
  if (!finalSvg.includes('viewBox="0 0 400 400"')) {
    finalSvg = finalSvg.replace(/viewBox=["'][^"']+["']/, 'viewBox="0 0 400 400"');
  }

  signal?.throwIfAborted();
  fs.writeFileSync(filePath, finalSvg, "utf8");

  return {
    propId: canonicalId,
    cached: false,
    filePath,
    svgContent: finalSvg,
  };
}

import {generateContentCancellable} from '../geminiTransport';
import { GoogleGenerativeAI } from "@google/generative-ai";
import { executeGeminiWithRetry, sanitizeLog } from "../geminiRetry";
import { MicroSceneBeat } from "./splitIntoMicroBeats";
import { SceneGraphScene, PropItem, SceneKeyframe } from "../../remotion/types/sceneGraph";

const DIRECT_SCENE_SYSTEM_PROMPT = `You are an Expert Animation Scene Director directing a single animated micro-scene (3 to 7 seconds).
You are given the exact duration in frames (calculated from real voiceover audio), the spoken dialogue sentence, the required props, and the background preset.

MANDATORY DIRECTORIAL RULES:
1. SEMANTIC SLOTS (ZERO PIXEL GUESSING):
   - You MUST place characters and props using Semantic Slots only:
     * Floor: "floor.left", "floor.center", "floor.right", "floor.farLeft", "floor.farRight"
     * Desk/Tabletop: "surface.topLeft", "surface.topCenter", "surface.topRight", "desk.topLeft", "desk.topRight", "desk.seat"
     * Container: "container.inside" (for props inside a jar/box)
     * Screen close-ups: "screen.centerCloseUp", "screen.center"
2. STABLE CHARACTER STAGING (STABILITY BY DEFAULT - NO ERRATIC SLIDING):
   - By default, character stands or sits stably at their assigned slot (e.g. "floor.center", "floor.right", "desk.seat").
   - DO NOT make character walk, slide, or run across the screen unless the beat EXPLICITLY requires locomotion (e.g. "walking into the room", "running away").
   - Default keyframes: [] (stable staging). Most scenes should have empty keyframes array [].
3. CHARACTER VISIBILITY, FACING & POSES:
   - If the scene is a macro close-up of a prop or an abstract diagram ("screen.centerCloseUp" or "screen.center"): character "visible" MUST be false.
   - Poses: "standing", "sittingBehindDesk", "holdingObject", "pointing", "shrugging", "waving", "walking".
   - Only use "sittingBehindDesk" if the script explicitly mentions sitting or the slot is "desk.seat".
   - Facing: Character should face toward the primary prop of interest!
     * If prop is at "floor.left" and character is at "floor.right" or "floor.center": character "facing": "left".
     * If prop is at "floor.right" and character is at "floor.left": character "facing": "right".
4. CAMERA RIGGING:
   - For macro prop close-up: { "zoom": 1.15, "focusSlot": "screen.centerCloseUp" }
   - For diagram/infographic: { "zoom": 1.0, "focusSlot": "screen.center" }
   - For desk scene: { "zoom": 1.15, "focusSlot": "desk.seat" }
   - For wide scene: { "zoom": 1.0, "focusSlot": "floor.center" }
5. OUTPUT SCHEMA:
   Output ONLY a valid JSON object matching this schema:
{
  "character": {
    "slot": "floor.center",
    "visible": true,
    "facing": "right",
    "pose": "standing",
    "eyes": "neutral",
    "eyebrows": "neutral",
    "mouth": "smile"
  },
  "props": [
    {
      "id": "prop_id",
      "type": "kebab-case-type",
      "slot": "floor.left",
      "scale": 1.0,
      "properties": { "label": "Text if any", "parentContainerId": "container_id if inside" }
    }
  ],
  "camera": {
    "zoom": 1.0,
    "focusSlot": "floor.center"
  },
  "keyframes": []
}
Output ONLY raw JSON.`;

/**
 * BÆ°á»›c 3: Äáº¡o diá»…n chi tiáº¿t 1 Micro-Scene trong 3-5 giÃ¢y (1 Prompt / 1 Cáº£nh).
 * Náº¿u xáº£y ra lá»—i Gemini (503/429/400...), giá»¯ nguyÃªn lá»—i Ä‘á»ƒ táº§ng trÃªn xá»­ lÃ½ vÃ  cho phÃ©p Resume,
 * TUYá»†T Äá»I KHÃ”NG tá»± táº¡o ká»‹ch báº£n giáº£ láº­p (mock data) thay tháº¿ khi chÆ°a Ä‘Æ°á»£c ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng cho phÃ©p.
 */
export async function directMicroScene(
  beat: MicroSceneBeat,
  modelName: string = "gemini-3.1-flash-lite",
  apiKey?: string,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
  requestId?: string,
  sceneIndex?: number,
  totalScenes?: number
): Promise<SceneGraphScene> {
  const reqId = requestId || "req_local";

  if (signal?.aborted) {
    throw new DOMException("YÃªu cáº§u Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ há»§y bá»Ÿi ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng", "AbortError");
  }

  const activeKey = (apiKey || process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY || "").trim();
  if (!activeKey) {
    throw new Error("Cáº§n Gemini API Key Ä‘á»ƒ thá»±c hiá»‡n BÆ°á»›c 3 (directMicroScene).");
  }

  const genAI = new GoogleGenerativeAI(activeKey);

  const responseText = await executeGeminiWithRetry(
    async () => {
      if (signal?.aborted) {
        throw new DOMException("YÃªu cáº§u Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ há»§y bá»Ÿi ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng", "AbortError");
      }

      const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({
        model: modelName,
        generationConfig: { responseMimeType: "application/json" },
        systemInstruction: DIRECT_SCENE_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
      });

      const prompt = `Direct this 3-5s micro-scene:
- Scene Title: ${beat.name}
- Total Duration: ${beat.durationInFrames} frames (${beat.audioDurationSeconds.toFixed(1)} seconds @ 60fps)
- Background Preset: ${beat.backgroundPreset}
- Spoken Dialogue: "${beat.dialogue}"
- Key Action Summary: ${beat.actionSummary}
- Required Props: ${JSON.stringify(beat.requiredProps)}
- Speech Bubble: ${beat.speechBubbleText ? `"${beat.speechBubbleText}"` : "None"}`;

      const res = await generateContentCancellable(model, prompt, signal);
      let raw = res.response.text().trim();
      raw = raw.replace(/^```(?:json)?\n?/i, "").replace(/\n?```$/i, "").trim();
      if (!raw) throw new Error(`Pháº£n há»“i rá»—ng tá»« Gemini cho cáº£nh ${beat.index + 1}: "${beat.name}"`);
      return raw;
    },
    {
      maxRetries: 2,
      signal,
      requestId: reqId,
      step: 3,
      sceneIndex,
      totalScenes,
      sceneName: beat.name,
      modelName,
    }
  );

  if (signal?.aborted) {
    throw new DOMException("YÃªu cáº§u Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ há»§y bá»Ÿi ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng", "AbortError");
  }

  let directedData: any;
  try {
    directedData = JSON.parse(responseText);
  } catch (parseErr: any) {
    throw new Error(`Pháº£n há»“i JSON khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡ tá»« model ${modelName} á»Ÿ cáº£nh ${beat.index + 1}: ${parseErr.message}`);
  }

  if (!directedData || typeof directedData !== "object") {
    throw new Error(`Dá»¯ liá»‡u phÃ¢n cáº£nh khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡ tá»« model ${modelName} á»Ÿ cáº£nh ${beat.index + 1}`);
  }

  // Chuáº©n hÃ³a props
  const props: PropItem[] = Array.isArray(directedData.props)
    ? directedData.props.map((p: any, pIdx: number) => ({
        id: String(p.id || `prop_${beat.index}_${pIdx}`),
        type: String(p.type || p),
        slot: p.slot || "floor.center",
        x: Number(p.x) || 1280,
        y: Number(p.y) || 1120,
        scale: Number(p.scale) || 1.0,
        visible: p.visible !== false,
        properties: p.properties || {},
      }))
    : [];

  // Chuáº©n hÃ³a keyframes (báº£o toÃ n tham sá»‘, khÃ´ng trÃ´i dáº¡t vÃ´ cÄƒn cá»©)
  const keyframes: SceneKeyframe[] = Array.isArray(directedData.keyframes)
    ? directedData.keyframes.map((kf: any) => ({
        action: kf.action || "moveProp",
        target: kf.target || "character",
        frame: Number(kf.frame) || 0,
        params: kf.params || {},
      }))
    : [];

  // Táº¡o phá»¥ Ä‘á» chuáº©n Ä‘á»“ng bá»™ audio
  const subtitles = [
    {
      id: `sub_${beat.index}_0`,
      text: beat.dialogue,
      startFrame: 5,
      endFrame: Math.max(10, beat.durationInFrames - 5),
      words: beat.words || [],
    },
  ];

  // Táº¡o bong bÃ³ng thoáº¡i náº¿u cÃ³ lá»i thoáº¡i trÃ­ch dáº«n
  const speechBubbles = beat.speechBubbleText
    ? [
        {
          id: `bubble_${beat.index}_0`,
          text: beat.speechBubbleText,
          position: { x: 1650, y: 340 },
          tailAnchor: { x: 60, y: 40 },
          startFrame: 10,
          endFrame: Math.max(15, beat.durationInFrames - 10),
        },
      ]
    : [];

  const scene: SceneGraphScene = {
    id: `scene_${beat.index + 1}`,
    name: beat.name,
    startFrame: 0, // Sáº½ Ä‘Æ°á»£c gÃ¡n ná»‘i tiáº¿p á»Ÿ bÆ°á»›c assemble
    durationInFrames: beat.durationInFrames,
    background: {
      preset: beat.backgroundPreset,
    },
    camera: directedData.camera || { zoom: 1.0, x: 1280, y: 720 },
    character: directedData.character || {
      slot: "floor.center",
      visible: true,
      facing: "right",
      pose: "standing",
    },
    props,
    keyframes,
    subtitles,
    speechBubbles,
    environmentVfx: (beat.backgroundPreset === "gardenDay"
      ? ["cloudDrift", "sunbeams"]
      : ["cloudDrift"]) as any,
  };

  return scene;
}

import { splitIntoMicroBeats, MicroSceneBeat } from "./splitIntoMicroBeats";
import { designSingleSvgAsset } from "./designSingleSvgAsset";
import { directMicroScene } from "./directMicroScene";
import { MasterSceneGraph, SceneGraphScene } from "../../remotion/types/sceneGraph";
import { sanitizeSceneGraph } from "../../remotion/utils/sceneGraphSanitizer";
import { extractHttpStatus, sanitizeLog } from "../geminiRetry";

export interface PipelineProgressCallback {
  (step: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4, message: string, percent: number): void;
}

export interface ResumeState {
  version?: number;
  rawScript?: string;
  beats?: MicroSceneBeat[];
  directedScenes?: SceneGraphScene[];
  failedAtSceneIndex?: number;
  modelName?: string;
}

export interface PipelineOptions {
  rawScript: string;
  modelName?: string;
  apiKey?: string;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
  requestId?: string;
  resumeState?: ResumeState;
  onProgress?: PipelineProgressCallback;
  onCheckpoint?: (state: ResumeState) => void;
}

export interface PipelineExecutionResult {
  success: boolean;
  sceneGraph: MasterSceneGraph;
  beats: MicroSceneBeat[];
  autoGeneratedAssets: string[];
  totalDurationSeconds: number;
  requestId: string;
}

/**
 * Multi-Prompt Micro-Scene Pipeline Orchestrator (General-Purpose Engine)
 * - BÆ°á»›c 1: Cáº¯t nhá»‹p ká»‹ch báº£n & Æ¯á»›c tÃ­nh thá»i lÆ°á»£ng toÃ¡n há»c (Zero-TTS mode)
 * - BÆ°á»›c 2: Thiáº¿t káº¿ tá»«ng váº­t thá»ƒ SVG 400x400 cháº¡m Ä‘Ã¡y y=380
 * - BÆ°á»›c 3: Äáº¡o diá»…n tá»«ng khung hÃ¬nh 3-5s vá»›i Semantic Slots (CÃ³ há»— trá»£ Tiáº¿p tá»¥c tá»« Cáº£nh dá»Ÿ dang)
 * - BÆ°á»›c 4: KhÃ³a cá»©ng váº­t lÃ½ & GhÃ©p ná»‘i MasterSceneGraph qua sceneGraphSanitizer
 */
export async function runMultiPromptPipeline({
  rawScript,
  modelName = "gemini-3.1-flash-lite",
  apiKey,
  signal,
  requestId = `req_${Date.now()}`,
  resumeState,
  onProgress,
  onCheckpoint,
}: PipelineOptions): Promise<PipelineExecutionResult> {
  const notify = (step: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4, msg: string, pct: number) => {
    if (onProgress) onProgress(step, msg, pct);
    console.log(sanitizeLog(`[Pipeline][${requestId}] [BÆ°á»›c ${step}] (${pct}%) ${msg}`));
  };

  if (signal?.aborted) {
    throw new DOMException("YÃªu cáº§u Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ há»§y bá»Ÿi ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng", "AbortError");
  }

  if (resumeState && (resumeState.version !== 1 || resumeState.rawScript !== rawScript.trim() || resumeState.modelName !== modelName)) {
    throw Object.assign(new Error('Báº£n lÆ°u khÃ´ng khá»›p ká»‹ch báº£n/model hoáº·c thuá»™c báº£n cÅ©. HÃ£y táº¡o má»›i.'), {status:400});
  }
  const directedScenes: SceneGraphScene[] = [];
  const checkpoint = () => {
    const state = {version:1, rawScript:rawScript.trim(), modelName, beats, directedScenes:[...directedScenes], failedAtSceneIndex:directedScenes.length};
    onCheckpoint?.(state);
    return state;
  };
  // =========================================================================
  // BÆ¯á»šC 1: Cáº®T NHá»ŠP Ká»ŠCH Báº¢N & Æ¯á»šC TÃNH THá»œI LÆ¯á»¢NG (HOáº¶C TÃI Sá»¬ Dá»¤NG KHI RESUME)
  // =========================================================================
  let beats: MicroSceneBeat[] = [];

  if (resumeState?.beats && Array.isArray(resumeState.beats) && resumeState.beats.length > 0) {
    beats = resumeState.beats;
    notify(1, `TÃ¡i sá»­ dá»¥ng ${beats.length} Micro-Scenes Ä‘Ã£ phÃ¢n rÃ£ tá»« phiÃªn trÆ°á»›c...`, 20);
  } else {
    notify(1, "Äang phÃ¢n rÃ£ ká»‹ch báº£n thÃ nh cÃ¡c Micro-Scenes 3-5s vÃ  Æ°á»›c tÃ­nh thá»i lÆ°á»£ng...", 15);
    beats = await splitIntoMicroBeats(rawScript, modelName, apiKey, signal, requestId);
  }

  if (signal?.aborted) {
    throw new DOMException("YÃªu cáº§u Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ há»§y bá»Ÿi ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng", "AbortError");
  }

  if (!beats.length || beats.some((b,i) => b.index !== i || !Array.isArray(b.requiredProps) || !Number.isFinite(b.durationInFrames) || b.durationInFrames <= 0 || typeof b.dialogue !== 'string')) {
    throw Object.assign(new Error('Dá»¯ liá»‡u tÃ¡ch cáº£nh khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡.'), {status:422});
  }
  if (resumeState?.directedScenes) {
    if (!Array.isArray(resumeState.directedScenes) || resumeState.directedScenes.length > beats.length || resumeState.directedScenes.some((s,i) => !s || !Number.isFinite(s.durationInFrames) || s.durationInFrames !== beats[i].durationInFrames)) {
      throw Object.assign(new Error('Báº£n lÆ°u cáº£nh khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡.'), {status:400});
    }
    directedScenes.push(...resumeState.directedScenes);
  }
  checkpoint();

  // =========================================================================
  // BÆ¯á»šC 2: THIáº¾T Káº¾ Tá»ªNG OBJECT RIÃŠNG BIá»†T (1 PROMPT / 1 OBJECT)
  // =========================================================================
  notify(2, "Kiá»ƒm tra kho tÃ i nguyÃªn vÃ  thiáº¿t káº¿ vector SVG 400x400 cho cÃ¡c váº­t thá»ƒ...", 40);

  const allRequiredProps = Array.from(
    new Set(beats.flatMap((b) => b.requiredProps))
  );

  const autoGeneratedAssets: string[] = [];

  for (const propId of allRequiredProps) {
    if (signal?.aborted) {
      throw new DOMException("YÃªu cáº§u Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ há»§y bá»Ÿi ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng", "AbortError");
    }

    try {
      const designResult = await designSingleSvgAsset(propId, modelName, apiKey, signal, requestId);
      if (!designResult.cached) {
        autoGeneratedAssets.push(designResult.propId);
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (err.name === "AbortError" || signal?.aborted) {
        throw err;
      }
      err.failedAtStep = 2;
      err.partialState = checkpoint();
      throw err;
    }
  }

  if (signal?.aborted) {
    throw new DOMException("YÃªu cáº§u Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ há»§y bá»Ÿi ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng", "AbortError");
  }

  // =========================================================================
  // BÆ¯á»šC 3: Äáº O DIá»„N Tá»ªNG KHUNG 3-5S (1 PROMPT / 1 Cáº¢NH)
  // Há»— trá»£ tiáº¿p tá»¥c tá»« cáº£nh dá»Ÿ dang (Resumable Scene Direction)
  // =========================================================================
  const startSceneIndex = directedScenes.length;

  for (let i = startSceneIndex; i < beats.length; i++) {
    if (signal?.aborted) {
      throw new DOMException("YÃªu cáº§u Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ há»§y bá»Ÿi ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng", "AbortError");
    }

    const beat = beats[i];
    const progressPercent = 70 + Math.round((i / beats.length) * 20);
    notify(3, `Äang Ä‘áº¡o diá»…n cáº£nh ${i + 1}/${beats.length}: "${beat.name}" (Model: ${modelName})...`, progressPercent);

    try {
      const scene = await directMicroScene(beat, modelName, apiKey, signal, requestId, i, beats.length);
      directedScenes.push(scene);
      checkpoint();
    } catch (err: any) {
      // ÄÃ³ng gÃ³i tráº¡ng thÃ¡i hoÃ n thÃ nh tá»«ng pháº§n Ä‘á»ƒ táº§ng API cÃ³ thá»ƒ tráº£ vá» cho Client Resume
      const httpStatus = extractHttpStatus(err);
      const wrappedErr: any = new Error(err.message || `Lá»—i khi Ä‘áº¡o diá»…n cáº£nh ${i + 1}`);
      wrappedErr.status = httpStatus;
      wrappedErr.failedAtStep = 3;
      wrappedErr.failedAtSceneIndex = i;
      wrappedErr.failedSceneName = beat.name;
      wrappedErr.modelName = modelName;
      wrappedErr.partialState = checkpoint();
      if (signal?.aborted || err.name === "AbortError") wrappedErr.name = "AbortError";

      console.warn(
        sanitizeLog(`[Pipeline][${requestId}] Gáº·p lá»—i dá»«ng táº¡i Cáº£nh ${i + 1}/${beats.length} (HTTP: ${httpStatus}). Giá»¯ nguyÃªn ${directedScenes.length} cáº£nh Ä‘Ã£ hoÃ n táº¥t Ä‘á»ƒ cho phÃ©p tiáº¿p tá»¥c.`)
      );

      throw wrappedErr;
    }
  }

  if (signal?.aborted) {
    throw new DOMException("YÃªu cáº§u Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ há»§y bá»Ÿi ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng", "AbortError");
  }

  // =========================================================================
  // BÆ¯á»šC 4: GHÃ‰P Cáº¢NH VÃ€ KHÃ“A Cá»¨NG HÃŒNH Há»ŒC QUA SCENE GRAPH SANITIZER
  // =========================================================================
  notify(4, "KhÃ³a kiá»ƒm duyá»‡t hÃ¬nh há»c, thá»i lÆ°á»£ng Ä‘á»™ng tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i vÃ  ghÃ©p ná»‘i Master Scene Graph...", 95);

  let currentStart = 0;
  const assembledScenes: SceneGraphScene[] = directedScenes.map((s, idx) => {
    const sStart = currentStart;
    const sDur = s.durationInFrames;
    currentStart = sStart + sDur;

    // Äá»“ng bá»™ startFrame ná»‘i tiáº¿p nhau tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i
    const updatedSubs = (s.subtitles || []).map((sub) => ({
      ...sub,
      startFrame: sStart + 5,
      endFrame: sStart + sDur - 5,
    }));

    const updatedBubbles = (s.speechBubbles || []).map((b) => ({
      ...b,
      startFrame: sStart + 10,
      endFrame: sStart + sDur - 10,
    }));

    return {
      ...s,
      id: `scene_${idx + 1}`,
      startFrame: sStart,
      durationInFrames: sDur,
      subtitles: updatedSubs,
      speechBubbles: updatedBubbles,
    };
  });

  const rawMasterGraph: MasterSceneGraph = {
    version: "3.2.0",
    title: beats[0]?.name || "Micro-Scene 2D Explainer Video",
    width: 2560,
    height: 1440,
    fps: 60,
    totalDurationInFrames: currentStart,
    scenes: assembledScenes,
  };

  // KhÃ³a cá»©ng váº­t lÃ½, clipping bÃ n gháº¿, clipping váº­t chá»©a, camera bounds qua Sanitizer
  const sanitizedGraph = sanitizeSceneGraph(rawMasterGraph, rawScript);

  notify(4, "ÄÃ£ táº¡o báº£n dá»±ng. HÃ£y duyá»‡t preview trÆ°á»›c khi xuáº¥t video.", 100);

  return {
    success: true,
    sceneGraph: sanitizedGraph,
    beats,
    autoGeneratedAssets,
    totalDurationSeconds: currentStart / 60,
    requestId,
  };
}

import {generateContentCancellable} from '../geminiTransport';
import { GoogleGenerativeAI } from "@google/generative-ai";
import { executeGeminiWithRetry, sanitizeLog } from "../geminiRetry";
import { SubtitleWord, BackgroundPreset } from "../../remotion/types/sceneGraph";

export interface MicroSceneBeat {
  index: number;
  name: string;
  dialogue: string;
  actionSummary: string;
  requiredProps: string[];
  backgroundPreset: BackgroundPreset;
  speechBubbleText?: string;
  // Estimated audio & scene duration (frames & seconds)
  audioDurationSeconds: number;
  durationInFrames: number;
  words?: SubtitleWord[];
  audioBase64?: string;
}

const SPLIT_BEATS_SYSTEM_PROMPT = `You are a World-Class 2D Animation Director and Screenwriter.
Your task is to take a user script or story prompt and organize it into a sequence of tightly-paced, engaging Micro-Scene Beats.

CRITICAL DIRECTORIAL & SCRIPTWRITING RULES:
1. RESPECT USER'S EXPLICIT SCENE STRUCTURE:
   - If the user provides a script formatted with scene tags (e.g. "Cáº£nh 1:", "Cáº£nh 2:", "Scene 1:", or numbered lines):
     You MUST generate EXACTLY ONE beat per user scene! NEVER merge, split, or omit scenes.
   - If the user provides a single continuous text or rough prompt:
     Divide logically into natural narrative beats at full sentence boundaries (each ~3 to 7 seconds). NEVER break a single clause or sentence in the middle!

2. NATURAL, ENGAGING VIETNAMESE STORYTELLING (TUYá»†T Äá»I KHÃ”NG VIáº¾T VÄ‚N PHONG "Äá»š"):
   - PRESERVE AUTHENTIC USER DIALOGUE: Keep the user's authentic narrative words, tone, humor, and emotion!
   - STRIP TECHNICAL CAMERA DIRECTIONS FROM SPOKEN DIALOGUE:
     The 'dialogue' field will be read aloud by the voiceover TTS actor.
     Remove camera/directorial prefixes like "Cáº£nh 1:", "PhÃ³ng cáº­n cáº£nh vÃ o...", "Minh há»a biá»ƒu Ä‘á»“:", "GÃ³c quay tá»« trÃªn xuá»‘ng:".
     Keep the actual spoken storytelling sentence clean, natural, and complete!
   - NO MACHINE-TRANSLATE CLICHÃ‰S:
     Avoid robotic, stiff phrases (e.g. do NOT rewrite natural sentences into wooden Google Translate style).
     Keep speech natural, rhythmic, and conversational (nhÆ° ngÆ°á»i tháº­t Ä‘ang chia sáº», tÃ¢m sá»±, dáº«n dáº¯t cÃ¢u chuyá»‡n).

3. QUOTED SPEECH BUBBLES:
   - If a sentence contains a quote in single or double quotes (e.g. 'Äá»ƒ sau!' or "TÃ¡m"):
     Keep the quote in the 'dialogue' AND also extract the exact quote into 'speechBubbleText' (e.g. "Äá»ƒ sau!", "TÃ¡m") so an animated speech bubble pops up over the character!

4. REQUIRED PROPS (KEBAB-CASE):
   - Extract a clean array of physical props needed in this beat.
   - Canonical prop IDs: "glass-jar", "spider", "desk", "desk-lamp", "gauge-meter", "sofa", "laptop", "coffee-cup", "book", "car", "potted-plant", "tree", "chair".
   - If a creature/item is inside a container (e.g. spider inside jar): list both ["glass-jar", "spider"].
   - For metabolic charts, meters, or gauges: use "gauge-meter".

5. BACKGROUND PRESET:
   Choose strictly from the 9 supported presets matching context:
   - "gardenDay" (outdoor nature, meadow, grass, park, field, sky)
   - "classroom" (school, teacher, students, blackboard, lecture)
   - "office" (workplace, office desk, corporate, meeting)
   - "cityStreet" (urban road, sidewalk, traffic, cars, buildings)
   - "beachSunny" (tropical beach, sand, ocean, palm tree)
   - "nightSky" (starry night, galaxy, moon gazing, astronomy)
   - "livingRoomDay" (daytime indoor living room, home)
   - "livingRoomNight" (cozy nighttime room, bedside lamp, evening)
   - "infographicStripes" (abstract data, meters, charts, presentation)

6. OUTPUT FORMAT: Output ONLY a valid JSON object matching this schema:
{
  "beats": [
    {
      "name": "Short scene title (e.g. PhÃ¡t hiá»‡n nhá»‡n trong lá»)",
      "dialogue": "Spoken narration sentence only (no camera notes)",
      "actionSummary": "Brief description of the 1 key visual action",
      "requiredProps": ["glass-jar", "spider"],
      "backgroundPreset": "livingRoomDay",
      "speechBubbleText": "Äá»ƒ sau!"
    }
  ]
}
Output ONLY raw JSON.`;

/**
 * Æ¯á»›c tÃ­nh thá»i lÆ°á»£ng chuáº©n xÃ¡c trá»±c tiáº¿p tá»« lá»i thoáº¡i vÃ  hÃ nh Ä‘á»™ng mÃ  KHÃ”NG cáº§n gá»i TTS.
 * - Tá»‘c Ä‘á»™ Ä‘á»c tá»± nhiÃªn tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t: ~2.6 tá»«/giÃ¢y.
 * - Thá»i gian Ä‘á»‡m hÃ nh Ä‘á»™ng trá»±c quan (Ä‘á»c thÃ´ng sá»‘, di chuyá»ƒn, quan sÃ¡t): 1.0s - 2.0s.
 * - Khá»‘ng cháº¿ thá»i lÆ°á»£ng má»—i beat tá»‘i thiá»ƒu 3.0s, tá»‘i Ä‘a 12.0s.
 */
export function estimateBeatDuration(dialogue: string, actionSummary: string): { durationSeconds: number; durationInFrames: number } {
  const cleanDialogue = dialogue.trim();
  const wordCount = cleanDialogue ? cleanDialogue.split(/\s+/).length : 0;
  const speechSec = wordCount > 0 ? wordCount / 2.6 : 0;

  let actionBufferSec = 1.0;
  if (/bÆ°á»›c|Ä‘i|walk|di chuyá»ƒn|cháº¡y|ra vÆ°á»n|Ä‘áº¿n/i.test(actionSummary)) {
    actionBufferSec = 1.8;
  } else if (/phÃ³ng|cáº­n cáº£nh|biá»ƒu Ä‘á»“|quan sÃ¡t|xem xÃ©t|ngáº¯m|Ä‘o/i.test(actionSummary)) {
    actionBufferSec = 1.4;
  }

  const estimatedSec = Math.max(3.0, Math.min(12.0, speechSec + actionBufferSec));
  const durationInFrames = Math.round(estimatedSec * 60);

  return {
    durationSeconds: Number(estimatedSec.toFixed(2)),
    durationInFrames,
  };
}

/**
 * BÆ°á»›c 1: Chia ká»‹ch báº£n thÃ´ thÃ nh cÃ¡c Micro-Scene Beats 3-5s
 * vÃ  Æ°á»›c tÃ­nh thá»i lÆ°á»£ng chuáº©n xÃ¡c tá»« cÃ¢u chá»¯ & hÃ nh Ä‘á»™ng (KHÃ”NG gá»i Edge-TTS).
 */
export async function splitIntoMicroBeats(
  rawScript: string,
  modelName: string = "gemini-3.1-flash-lite",
  apiKey?: string,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
  requestId?: string
): Promise<MicroSceneBeat[]> {
  const reqId = requestId || "req_local";

  if (signal?.aborted) {
    throw new DOMException("YÃªu cáº§u Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ há»§y bá»Ÿi ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng", "AbortError");
  }

  const activeKey = (apiKey || process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY || "").trim();
  if (!activeKey) {
    throw new Error("Cáº§n Gemini API Key Ä‘á»ƒ thá»±c hiá»‡n BÆ°á»›c 1 (splitIntoMicroBeats).");
  }

  const genAI = new GoogleGenerativeAI(activeKey);

  console.log(
    sanitizeLog(`[Pipeline][${reqId}] [BÆ°á»›c 1] Äang cáº¯t nhá»‹p ká»‹ch báº£n qua model ${modelName}...`)
  );

  const responseText = await executeGeminiWithRetry(
    async () => {
      if (signal?.aborted) {
        throw new DOMException("YÃªu cáº§u Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ há»§y bá»Ÿi ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng", "AbortError");
      }

      const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel(
        {
          model: modelName,
          generationConfig: { responseMimeType: "application/json" },
          systemInstruction: SPLIT_BEATS_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
        },
        { timeout: 25000 }
      );

      const promptText = `HÃ£y phÃ¢n rÃ£ ká»‹ch báº£n sau thÃ nh cÃ¡c Micro-Scenes chuáº©n xÃ¡c.
Náº¿u ká»‹ch báº£n Ä‘Ã£ cÃ³ tá»«ng Cáº£nh (Cáº£nh 1, Cáº£nh 2...), hÃ£y giá»¯ nguyÃªn tá»«ng cáº£nh tÆ°Æ¡ng á»©ng thÃ nh 1 Micro-Scene beat, báº£o tá»“n trá»n váº¹n cÃ¢u chá»¯ vÃ  vÄƒn phong tá»± nhiÃªn cá»§a ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng (chá»‰ bÃ³c tÃ¡ch cÃ¡c chá»‰ dáº«n camera ká»¹ thuáº­t ra khá»i dialogue Ä‘á»ƒ Ä‘á»c lá»i thuyáº¿t minh):

${rawScript}`;

      const res = await generateContentCancellable(model, promptText, signal);
      const text = res.response.text();
      if (!text) throw new Error("Pháº£n há»“i cáº¯t nhá»‹p rá»—ng tá»« Gemini.");
      return text;
    },
    {
      maxRetries: 3,
      signal,
      requestId: reqId,
      step: 1,
      modelName,
    }
  );

  if (signal?.aborted) {
    throw new DOMException("YÃªu cáº§u Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ há»§y bá»Ÿi ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng", "AbortError");
  }

  let cleanJson = responseText.trim().replace(/^```(?:json)?\n?/i, "").replace(/\n?```$/i, "");
  const parsed = JSON.parse(cleanJson);
  const rawBeats = Array.isArray(parsed.beats) ? parsed.beats : [];

  if (rawBeats.length === 0) {
    throw new Error("KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ trÃ­ch xuáº¥t Micro-Scenes tá»« ká»‹ch báº£n.");
  }

  // Æ¯á»›c tÃ­nh thá»i lÆ°á»£ng trá»±c quan tá»« lá»i thoáº¡i & hÃ nh Ä‘á»™ng (KHÃ”NG Gá»ŒI EDGE-TTS)
  console.log(
    sanitizeLog(`[Pipeline][${reqId}] [BÆ°á»›c 1] Æ¯á»›c tÃ­nh thá»i lÆ°á»£ng toÃ¡n há»c cho ${rawBeats.length} Micro-Scenes (Zero-TTS mode)...`)
  );

  const microBeats: MicroSceneBeat[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < rawBeats.length; i++) {
    const b = rawBeats[i];
    const dialogueText = String(b.dialogue || rawScript).trim();
    const actionSummaryText = String(b.actionSummary || "");

    const { durationSeconds, durationInFrames } = estimateBeatDuration(dialogueText, actionSummaryText);

    microBeats.push({
      index: i,
      name: b.name || `Cáº£nh ${i + 1}`,
      dialogue: dialogueText,
      actionSummary: actionSummaryText,
      requiredProps: Array.isArray(b.requiredProps) ? b.requiredProps : [],
      backgroundPreset: b.backgroundPreset || "livingRoomDay",
      speechBubbleText: b.speechBubbleText || undefined,
      audioDurationSeconds: durationSeconds,
      durationInFrames,
      words: [],
      audioBase64: undefined,
    });
  }

  return microBeats;
}

==================== FILE: C:\Users\ASUS\Downloads\TOOL_2D_ANIM_API_FIX_1\TOOL_2D_ANIM\web-studio\src\lib\edgeTtsClient.ts ====================
import { WebSocket } from "ws";
import { SubtitleWord } from "../remotion/types/sceneGraph";

export interface EdgeTtsResult {
  audioBase64: string;
  durationMs: number;
  words: SubtitleWord[];
}

/**
 * Pure TypeScript Microsoft Edge-TTS client with full WordBoundary timestamp extraction.
 */
export async function synthesizeEdgeTts(
  text: string,
  voice: string = "vi-VN-HoaiMyNeural",
  rate: string = "+0%",
  pitch: string = "+0Hz"
): Promise<EdgeTtsResult> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const trustedToken = "6A5AAFD830364C379E0CC76104E47567";
    const endpoint = `wss://speech.platform.bing.com/consumer/speech/synthesize/readaheadedge/v1?TrustedClientToken=${trustedToken}`;

    const ws = new WebSocket(endpoint);
    const audioChunks: Buffer[] = [];
    const words: SubtitleWord[] = [];
    let isHeaderSent = false;

    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
      ws.close();
      reject(new Error("Edge-TTS WebSocket timeout sau 15 giÃ¢y"));
    }, 15000);

    ws.on("open", () => {
      // 1. Send speech config
      const date = new Date().toISOString();
      const configMessage =
        `X-Timestamp:${date}\r\n` +
        "Content-Type:application/json; charset=utf-8\r\n" +
        "Path:speech.config\r\n\r\n" +
        JSON.stringify({
          context: {
            synthesis: {
              audio: {
                metadataoptions: {
                  sentenceBoundaryEnabled: "false",
                  wordBoundaryEnabled: "true",
                },
                outputFormat: "audio-24khz-48kbitrate-mono-mp3",
              },
            },
          },
        });

      ws.send(configMessage);

      // 2. Send SSML request
      const requestId = Math.random().toString(36).substring(2);
      const ssml =
        `<speak version='1.0' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2001/10/synthesis' xml:lang='vi-VN'>` +
        `<voice name='${voice}'>` +
        `<prosody rate='${rate}' pitch='${pitch}'>${text}</prosody>` +
        `</voice></speak>`;

      const requestMessage =
        `X-RequestId:${requestId}\r\n` +
        `Content-Type:application/ssml+xml\r\n` +
        `X-Timestamp:${date}\r\n` +
        "Path:ssml\r\n\r\n" +
        ssml;

      ws.send(requestMessage);
      isHeaderSent = true;
    });

    ws.on("message", (data: any, isBinary: boolean) => {
      if (isBinary) {
        // Binary message contains header + audio bytes
        const buffer = Buffer.from(data);
        const headerEnd = buffer.indexOf("\r\n\r\n");
        if (headerEnd !== -1) {
          const audioPart = buffer.subarray(headerEnd + 4);
          audioChunks.push(audioPart);
        } else {
          audioChunks.push(buffer);
        }
      } else {
        // Text message contains metadata (WordBoundary or turn.end)
        const textMsg = data.toString();
        if (textMsg.includes("Path:audio.metadata")) {
          const bodyIndex = textMsg.indexOf("\r\n\r\n");
          if (bodyIndex !== -1) {
            try {
              const jsonBody = JSON.parse(textMsg.substring(bodyIndex + 4));
              if (jsonBody.Metadata) {
                for (const meta of jsonBody.Metadata) {
                  if (meta.Type === "WordBoundary" && meta.Data) {
                    const offsetMs = meta.Data.Offset / 10000; // 100ns ticks to ms
                    const durationMs = meta.Data.Duration / 10000;
                    words.push({
                      word: meta.Data.text.Text,
                      startMs: Math.round(offsetMs),
                      endMs: Math.round(offsetMs + durationMs),
                    });
                  }
                }
              }
            } catch (e) {
              // Ignore metadata parse error
            }
          }
        } else if (textMsg.includes("Path:turn.end")) {
          clearTimeout(timeout);
          ws.close();

          const fullAudio = Buffer.concat(audioChunks);
          const lastWord = words[words.length - 1];
          const totalDuration = lastWord ? lastWord.endMs + 300 : 2000;

          resolve({
            audioBase64: fullAudio.toString("base64"),
            durationMs: totalDuration,
            words,
          });
        }
      }
    });

    ws.on("error", (err) => {
      clearTimeout(timeout);
      reject(err);
    });
  });
}

export interface GenerateDslResult {
  script: string;
  ir: AnimScriptIR;
  diagnostics: CompilerDiagnostic[];
  correctionRounds: number;
}

export async function generateAnimScriptWithCorrection(
  prompt: string,
  apiKey: string,
  modelName = "gemini-3.1-flash-lite",
  signal?: AbortSignal,
  onProgress?: (message: string) => void
): Promise<GenerateDslResult> {
  const genAI = new GoogleGenerativeAI(apiKey);
  const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({
    model: modelName,
    systemInstruction: ANIMSCRIPT_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
  });

  onProgress?.(`Äang táº¡o mÃ£ AnimScript v0.1 tá»« ká»‹ch báº£n báº±ng model ${modelName}...`);

  let currentPrompt = `HÃ£y viáº¿t mÃ£ nguá»“n AnimScript hoÃ n chá»‰nh, chÃ­nh xÃ¡c cho ká»‹ch báº£n sau:\n${prompt}`;
  let bestScript = "";
  let bestIR: AnimScriptIR | null = null;
  let bestDiagnostics: CompilerDiagnostic[] = [];
  let rounds = 0;

  while (rounds <= 2) {
    if (signal?.aborted) {
      throw new DOMException("YÃªu cáº§u Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ há»§y bá»Ÿi ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng", "AbortError");
    }

    const response = await executeGeminiWithRetry<string>(
      async () => {
        const result = await model.generateContent(currentPrompt);
        return result.response.text();
      },
      { modelName, signal }
    );

    // Extract code block
    let cleanedCode = response.trim();
    const codeBlockMatch = cleanedCode.match(/```(?:animscript|python)?\s*([\s\S]*?)```/i);
    if (codeBlockMatch) {
      cleanedCode = codeBlockMatch[1].trim();
    }

    bestScript = cleanedCode;

    // Compile and check diagnostics
    onProgress?.(`Äang biÃªn dá»‹ch vÃ  kiá»ƒm tra tÃ­nh há»£p lá»‡ cá»§a AnimScript (Láº§n ${rounds + 1})...`);
    const { ir, diagnostics } = compileAnimScript(cleanedCode);
    bestIR = ir;
    bestDiagnostics = diagnostics;

    const errors = diagnostics.filter((d) => d.severity === "error");
    if (errors.length === 0) {
      // 100% valid!
      onProgress?.("BiÃªn dá»‹ch thÃ nh cÃ´ng! MÃ£ AnimScript há»£p lá»‡ 100%.");
      break;
    }

    rounds++;
    if (rounds > 2) {
      console.warn(`[AnimScript] ÄÃ£ thá»­ sá»­a lá»—i tá»‘i Ä‘a 2 vÃ²ng, cÃ²n ${errors.length} cáº£nh bÃ¡o/lá»—i.`);
      break;
    }

    // Auto-fix loop: send compiler diagnostics back to model
    onProgress?.(`PhÃ¡t hiá»‡n ${errors.length} lá»—i biÃªn dá»‹ch. Äang yÃªu cáº§u AI tá»± sá»­a (VÃ²ng ${rounds}/2)...`);
    const errorDetails = errors
      .map(
        (e) => `- DÃ²ng ${e.line}, Cá»™t ${e.column} [${e.code}]: ${e.message} (Gá»£i Ã½: ${e.fixSuggestion || "KhÃ´ng cÃ³"})`
      )
      .join("\n");

    currentPrompt = `MÃ£ AnimScript cá»§a báº¡n gáº·p cÃ¡c lá»—i sau khi biÃªn dá»‹ch:\n${errorDetails}\n\nMÃ£ hiá»‡n táº¡i:\n${cleanedCode}\n\nHÃ£y sá»­a láº¡i toÃ n bá»™ mÃ£ Ä‘á»ƒ kháº¯c phá»¥c triá»‡t Ä‘á»ƒ cÃ¡c lá»—i trÃªn vÃ  chá»‰ tráº£ vá» mÃ£ nguá»“n trong khá»‘i \`\`\`animscript ... \`\`\`.`;
  }

  return {
    script: bestScript,
    ir: bestIR!,
    diagnostics: bestDiagnostics,
    correctionRounds: rounds,
  };
}


==================== FILE: C:\Users\ASUS\Downloads\TOOL_2D_ANIM_API_FIX_1\TOOL_2D_ANIM\web-studio\src\lib\geminiRetry.ts ====================
import {apiContext} from './apiRequestContext';
/**
 * Gemini API Retry & Resilience Engine
 * 
 * Quy chuáº©n:
 * 1. Thá»­ láº¡i tá»‘i Ä‘a 3 láº§n sau láº§n gá»i Ä‘áº§u tiÃªn (1 initial + 3 retries = 4 láº§n gá»i).
 * 2. Thá»i gian chá» cÆ¡ sá»Ÿ: Láº§n 1 ~5s, Láº§n 2 ~15s, Láº§n 3 ~30s kÃ¨m Jitter (Â±1s Ä‘áº¿n Â±3s).
 * 3. Æ¯u tiÃªn giÃ¡ trá»‹ 'Retry-After' náº¿u server tráº£ vá» trong header hoáº·c error message.
 * 4. KhÃ´ng retry cÃ¡c lá»—i khÃ´ng thá»ƒ kháº¯c phá»¥c: 400 (Bad Request / INVALID_ARGUMENT),
 *    401/403 (API Key khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡ / PERMISSION_DENIED), 404 (NOT_FOUND).
 * 5. Há»§y chá»§ Ä‘á»™ng (AbortSignal) dá»«ng ngay láº­p tá»©c má»i vÃ²ng chá» vÃ  tÃ¡c vá»¥ ngáº§m.
 * 6. Giá»¯ nguyÃªn 100% model Ä‘Ã£ chá»n trong suá»‘t quÃ¡ trÃ¬nh thá»­ láº¡i.
 * 7. Tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i khÃ´ng ghi API key vÃ o log.
 */

export interface RetryOptions {
  maxRetries?: number;
  initialDelayMs?: number;
  onRetry?: (attempt: number, maxRetries: number, delayMs: number, error: Error) => void;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
  requestId?: string;
  step?: number;
  sceneIndex?: number;
  totalScenes?: number;
  sceneName?: string;
  modelName?: string;
}

/**
 * XÃ³a thÃ´ng tin nháº¡y cáº£m (API Key, token) khá»i log
 */
export function sanitizeLog(text: string): string {
  if (!text) return "";
  return text
    .replace(/AIza[A-Za-z0-9_-]{30,}/g, "[REDACTED_API_KEY]")
    .replace(/key=[A-Za-z0-9_-]+/gi, "key=[REDACTED]")
    .replace(/bearer\s+[A-Za-z0-9._-]+/gi, "bearer [REDACTED]");
}

/**
 * TrÃ­ch xuáº¥t mÃ£ tráº¡ng thÃ¡i HTTP chÃ­nh xÃ¡c tá»« Ä‘á»‘i tÆ°á»£ng lá»—i
 */
export function extractHttpStatus(err: any): number {
  if (!err) return 0;
  if (err.name === "AbortError") return 499;
  if (typeof err.status === "number" && err.status > 0) return err.status;
  if (typeof err.statusCode === "number" && err.statusCode > 0) return err.statusCode;
  if (typeof err.httpStatus === "number" && err.httpStatus > 0) return err.httpStatus;

  const msg = String(err.message || "");
  const match = msg.match(/\b(400|401|403|404|429|499|500|502|503|504)\b/);
  if (match) {
    return parseInt(match[1], 10);
  }

  if (msg.includes("service unavailable") || msg.includes("high demand")) return 503;
  if (msg.includes("too many requests") || msg.includes("resourceexhausted")) return 429;
  if (msg.includes("abort") || msg.includes("há»§y")) return 499;
  return 0;
}

/**
 * Kiá»ƒm tra xem má»™t lá»—i tá»« Gemini API cÃ³ pháº£i lÃ  lá»—i táº¡m thá»i (cÃ³ thá»ƒ retry) hay khÃ´ng.
 */
export function isRetryableGeminiError(err: any): boolean {
  if (!err) return false;
  if (err.name === "AbortError") return false;

  const msg = String(err.message || "").toLowerCase();
  const status = extractHttpStatus(err);

  // KhÃ´ng retry client aborts
  if (msg.includes("abort") || msg.includes("há»§y") || status === 499) {
    return false;
  }

  // Lá»—i khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c retry: Sai API key, sai tham sá»‘, cáº¥m quyá»n, model khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i
  if (
    status === 400 ||
    status === 401 ||
    status === 403 ||
    status === 404 ||
    msg.includes("api_key_invalid") ||
    msg.includes("api key not valid") ||
    msg.includes("invalid_argument") ||
    msg.includes("permission_denied") ||
    msg.includes("not found")
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  // Lá»—i Ä‘Æ°á»£c phÃ©p retry cÃ³ giá»›i háº¡n (tá»‘i Ä‘a 3 láº§n):
  // 503 (quÃ¡ táº£i), 429 (rate limit), 500, 502, 504, timeout, network reset
  if (
    status === 503 ||
    status === 429 ||
    status === 500 ||
    status === 502 ||
    status === 504 ||
    msg.includes("503") ||
    msg.includes("service unavailable") ||
    msg.includes("high demand") ||
    msg.includes("resourceexhausted") ||
    msg.includes("too many requests") ||
    msg.includes("rate limit") ||
    msg.includes("econnreset") ||
    msg.includes("etimedout") ||
    msg.includes("socket hang up") ||
    msg.includes("fetch failed")
  ) {
    return true;
  }

  return false;
}

/**
 * TrÃ­ch xuáº¥t thá»i gian chá» (giÃ¢y) tá»« Retry-After header hoáº·c ná»™i dung thÃ´ng bÃ¡o lá»—i
 */
export function extractRetryAfterSeconds(err: any): number | null {
  if (!err) return null;

  if (typeof err.retryAfter === "number" && err.retryAfter > 0) {
    return err.retryAfter;
  }

  const details = Array.isArray(err.errorDetails) ? err.errorDetails : [];
  for (const detail of details) {
    const match = String(detail.retryDelay || '').match(/^(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)s$/);
    if (match) return Number(match[1]);
  }
  const msg = String(err.message || "");

  const matchSec = msg.match(/retry(?:-|\s*)after[\s:]*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:s|sec|seconds)?/i);
  if (matchSec && matchSec[1]) {
    const val = parseFloat(matchSec[1]);
    if (!isNaN(val) && val > 0) return Math.ceil(val);
  }

  const matchIn = msg.match(/(?:try again in|wait)\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:s|sec|seconds)?/i);
  if (matchIn && matchIn[1]) {
    const val = parseFloat(matchIn[1]);
    if (!isNaN(val) && val > 0) return Math.ceil(val);
  }

  return null;
}

/**
 * TÃ­nh toÃ¡n Ä‘á»™ trá»… retry theo phÃ¢n bá»•: Láº§n 1 ~5s, Láº§n 2 ~15s, Láº§n 3 ~30s + Jitter
 */
export function computeBackoffDelay(attempt: number, retryAfterSeconds: number | null): number {
  if (retryAfterSeconds !== null && retryAfterSeconds > 0) {
    return Math.max(2000, Math.round(retryAfterSeconds * 1000));
  }

  const baseScheduleSeconds = [15, 30, 60];
  const baseSec = baseScheduleSeconds[attempt - 1] ?? 30;

  const jitterRange = attempt === 1 ? 1 : attempt === 2 ? 2 : 3;
  const jitter = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * jitterRange;
  const totalSec = Math.max(2, baseSec + jitter);

  return Math.round(totalSec * 1000);
}

/**
 * HÃ m sleep cÃ³ há»— trá»£ AbortSignal vÃ  dá»n dáº¹p listener/timer hoÃ n toÃ n
 */
export function sleep(ms: number, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    if (signal?.aborted) {
      return reject(new DOMException("YÃªu cáº§u Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ há»§y bá»Ÿi ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng", "AbortError"));
    }

    let timer: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;
    let onAbort: (() => void) | null = null;

    const cleanup = () => {
      if (timer) {
        clearTimeout(timer);
        timer = null;
      }
      if (onAbort && signal) {
        signal.removeEventListener("abort", onAbort);
        onAbort = null;
      }
    };

    onAbort = () => {
      cleanup();
      reject(new DOMException("YÃªu cáº§u Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ há»§y bá»Ÿi ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng", "AbortError"));
    };

    signal?.addEventListener("abort", onAbort, { once: true });

    timer = setTimeout(() => {
      cleanup();
      resolve();
    }, ms);
  });
}

/**
 * Bá»™ thá»±c thi tÃ¡c vá»¥ vá»›i cÆ¡ cháº¿ thá»­ láº¡i cÃ³ kiá»ƒm soÃ¡t, gáº¯n requestId, chi tiáº¿t cáº£nh vÃ  báº£o máº­t API key
 */
export async function executeGeminiWithRetry<T>(
  operation: (attempt: number) => Promise<T>,
  options: RetryOptions = {}
): Promise<T> {
  const maxRetries = Math.max(0, Math.min(3, options.maxRetries ?? 3));
  const reqId = options.requestId || "req_local";
  const modelName = options.modelName || "gemini";
  const sceneInfo = options.sceneIndex !== undefined
    ? ` (Cáº£nh ${(options.sceneIndex + 1)}${options.totalScenes ? `/${options.totalScenes}` : ""}${options.sceneName ? `: "${options.sceneName}"` : ""})`
    : "";
  const stepInfo = options.step !== undefined ? ` [BÆ°á»›c ${options.step}]` : "";

  let lastError: any = null;

  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= maxRetries + 1; attempt++) {
    if (options.signal?.aborted) {
      throw new DOMException("YÃªu cáº§u Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ há»§y bá»Ÿi ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng", "AbortError");
    }

    try {
      console.log(
        sanitizeLog(`[GeminiRetry][${reqId}]${stepInfo} Báº¯t Ä‘áº§u gá»i API (Model: ${modelName}, Láº§n thá»­: ${attempt}/${maxRetries + 1}${sceneInfo})...`)
      );

      const result = await operation(attempt);
      options.signal?.throwIfAborted();

      console.log(
        sanitizeLog(`[GeminiRetry][${reqId}]${stepInfo} ThÃ nh cÃ´ng (Model: ${modelName}, Láº§n thá»­: ${attempt}/${maxRetries + 1}, HTTP: 200${sceneInfo})`)
      );

      return result;
    } catch (err: any) {
      lastError = err;

      // Náº¿u ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng Ä‘Ã£ chá»§ Ä‘á»™ng há»§y request thÃ¬ ngá»«ng ngay
      if (err.name === "AbortError" || options.signal?.aborted) {
        console.warn(
          sanitizeLog(`[GeminiRetry][${reqId}]${stepInfo} ÄÃ£ há»§y ngay láº­p tá»©c theo yÃªu cáº§u ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng (HTTP: 499${sceneInfo}).`)
        );
        throw err;
      }

      const httpStatus = extractHttpStatus(err);
      const retryable = isRetryableGeminiError(err);
      const isLastAttempt = attempt > maxRetries;

      console.warn(
        sanitizeLog(
          `[GeminiRetry][${reqId}]${stepInfo} Gáº·p lá»—i (Model: ${modelName}, Láº§n thá»­: ${attempt}/${maxRetries + 1}, HTTP: ${httpStatus}${sceneInfo}): ${err.message}`
        )
      );

      if (!retryable || isLastAttempt) {
        console.warn(
          sanitizeLog(
            `[GeminiRetry][${reqId}]${stepInfo} Dá»«ng thá»­ láº¡i (retryable=${retryable}, isLastAttempt=${isLastAttempt}, HTTP: ${httpStatus}${sceneInfo})`
          )
        );
        throw err;
      }

      const retryAfterSec = extractRetryAfterSeconds(err);
      const delayMs = computeBackoffDelay(attempt, retryAfterSec);

      console.warn(
        sanitizeLog(
          `[GeminiRetry][${reqId}]${stepInfo} Chá» ${(delayMs / 1000).toFixed(1)}s trÆ°á»›c khi thá»­ láº¡i láº§n ${attempt + 1}/${maxRetries + 1} (HTTP: ${httpStatus}${sceneInfo})...`
        )
      );

      apiContext.getStore()?.progress(`HTTP ${httpStatus || "khÃ´ng xÃ¡c Ä‘á»‹nh"}: chá» ${Math.ceil(delayMs/1000)} giÃ¢y, thá»­ láº¡i ${attempt}/${maxRetries}.`);
      if (options.onRetry) {
        options.onRetry(attempt, maxRetries, delayMs, err);
      }

      await sleep(delayMs, options.signal);
    }
  }

  throw lastError;
}


==================== FILE: C:\Users\ASUS\Downloads\TOOL_2D_ANIM_API_FIX_1\TOOL_2D_ANIM\web-studio\src\lib\geminiTransport.ts ====================
import type {GenerativeModel} from '@google/generative-ai';
import {apiContext} from './apiRequestContext';
import {sleep} from './geminiRetry';

// All pipeline calls share a start-time gate. It persists across cancelled jobs.
let nextStart = 0;
export async function generateContentCancellable(
  model: GenerativeModel, prompt: string, signal?: AbortSignal, timeoutMs = 90000,
) {
  signal?.throwIfAborted();
  const configured = Number(process.env.GEMINI_MIN_INTERVAL_MS);
  const interval = Number.isFinite(configured) && configured >= 0 ? configured : 13000;
  while (Date.now() < nextStart) {
    const delay = nextStart - Date.now();
    apiContext.getStore()?.progress(`GiÃ£n lÆ°á»£t gá»i API: chá» ${Math.ceil(delay/1000)} giÃ¢y.`);
    await sleep(delay, signal);
  }
  signal?.throwIfAborted();
  nextStart = Date.now() + interval;
  const controller = new AbortController();
  let timedOut = false;
  const abort = () => controller.abort();
  signal?.addEventListener('abort', abort, {once:true});
  const timer = setTimeout(() => {timedOut = true; controller.abort();}, timeoutMs);
  try {
    signal?.throwIfAborted();
    const result = await model.generateContent(prompt, {signal:controller.signal, timeout:-1});
    signal?.throwIfAborted();
    return result;
  } catch (error: any) {
    if (signal?.aborted) throw new DOMException('ÄÃ£ há»§y yÃªu cáº§u', 'AbortError');
    if (timedOut) {
      throw Object.assign(new Error(`Gemini khÃ´ng hoÃ n táº¥t trong ${timeoutMs/1000} giÃ¢y.`), {name:'TimeoutError', status:504});
    }
    throw error;
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    signal?.removeEventListener('abort', abort);
  }
}

import fs from "fs";

export interface GenerateAssetResult {
  id: string;
  svgContent: string;
  dimensions: {
    width: number;
    height: number;
  };
  filePath?: string;

/**
 * Procedural Cartoon SVG Fallback
 * Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng táº¡o hÃ¬nh minh há»a vector hoáº¡t hÃ¬nh Ä‘áº¹p máº¯t, má»m máº¡i, viá»n Ä‘áº­m chuáº©n 2K
 * khi offline hoáº·c khÃ´ng káº¿t ná»‘i Ä‘Æ°á»£c Gemini/Recraft.
 */
function createProceduralCartoonSvg(id: string): string {
  const hash = id.split("").reduce((acc, c) => acc + c.charCodeAt(0), 0);
  const colorPalettes = [
    { bg: "#F59E0B", inner: "#FEF3C7", accent: "#D97706", stroke: "#141414" },
    { bg: "#3B82F6", inner: "#DBEAFE", accent: "#2563EB", stroke: "#141414" },
    { bg: "#10B981", inner: "#D1FAE5", accent: "#059669", stroke: "#141414" },
    { bg: "#EC4899", inner: "#FCE7F3", accent: "#DB2777", stroke: "#141414" },
    { bg: "#8B5CF6", inner: "#EDE9FE", accent: "#7C3AED", stroke: "#141414" },
    { bg: "#F97316", inner: "#FFEDD5", accent: "#EA580C", stroke: "#141414" },
  ];
  const pal = colorPalettes[hash % colorPalettes.length];
  const cleanLabel = id.replace(/[-_]/g, " ").toUpperCase();

  return `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="-150 -150 300 300" width="300" height="300">
  <defs>
    <radialGradient id="shadow_${hash}" cx="50%" cy="50%" r="50%">
      <stop offset="0%" stop-color="#0F172A" stop-opacity="0.3"/>
      <stop offset="100%" stop-color="#0F172A" stop-opacity="0"/>
    </radialGradient>
    <linearGradient id="grad_${hash}" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="100%">
      <stop offset="0%" stop-color="${pal.inner}"/>
      <stop offset="100%" stop-color="${pal.bg}"/>
    </linearGradient>
  </defs>
  <!-- Drop Shadow -->
  <ellipse cx="0" cy="125" rx="110" ry="18" fill="url(#shadow_${hash})"/>
  <!-- Main Cartoon Badge -->
  <rect x="-110" y="-110" width="220" height="220" rx="42" fill="url(#grad_${hash})" stroke="${pal.stroke}" stroke-width="12" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
  <!-- Inner Rim -->
  <rect x="-90" y="-90" width="180" height="180" rx="28" fill="none" stroke="${pal.accent}" stroke-width="6" opacity="0.6"/>
  <!-- Shiny reflection stripe -->
  <path d="M-75 -85 L-20 -85 L-65 40 L-90 40 Z" fill="#FFFFFF" opacity="0.45"/>
  <!-- Cartoon Star / Sparkle Icon -->
  <path d="M0 -45 Q0 -15 30 -15 Q0 -15 0 15 Q0 -15 -30 -15 Q0 -15 0 -45 Z" fill="${pal.accent}" stroke="${pal.stroke}" stroke-width="6" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
  <!-- Label with Be Vietnam Pro font -->
  <rect x="-85" y="45" width="170" height="42" rx="12" fill="#141414"/>
  <text x="0" y="72" text-anchor="middle" font-size="15" font-weight="900" fill="#FFFFFF" font-family="sans-serif" letter-spacing="1">${cleanLabel}</text>
</svg>`;
}

/**
 * Auto AI SVG Generator (Gemini Vector Engine / Procedural Fallback)
 * Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng táº¡o asset vector SVG khi Scene Graph yÃªu cáº§u má»™t Ä‘áº¡o cá»¥ chÆ°a cÃ³ trong Asset Bank.
 */
export async function generateVectorAsset(
  prompt: string,
  propId?: string,
  provider: "recraft" | "gemini" = "gemini",
  explicitApiKey?: string
): Promise<GenerateAssetResult> {
  const safeId = (propId || `ai_${Date.now()}`).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]/g, "_");
  const geminiApiKey = explicitApiKey || process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY;

  let svgContent = "";
  let width = 300;
  let height = 300;

  if (geminiApiKey) {

      const genAI = new GoogleGenerativeAI(geminiApiKey);

      const systemPrompt = `You are a Principal 2D Vector Graphic Illustrator for an animation studio producing high quality 1080p stickman videos.
Generate a valid, beautiful, clean standalone SVG representing the object described.
Strict Rules:
- Output ONLY the raw <svg>...</svg> XML markup. Do NOT use markdown code blocks (\`\`\`xml or \`\`\`).
- Visual Style: Cartoon 2D animation style with thick smooth black outline (#141414, 10-14px width, stroke-linecap="round", stroke-linejoin="round").
- Colors: Soft, vibrant flat colors with cute specular highlights or soft shading.
- Viewport: Perfectly centered around (0, 0), with viewBox="-150 -150 300 300", width="300", height="300".
- Must include xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg".`;

      svgContent = await executeGeminiWithRetry(
        async (attempt) => {

          const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({ model: targetModel });
          const result = await model.generateContent(`${systemPrompt}\n\nObject description: ${prompt} (${safeId})`);
