            return rawText.substring(startIdx, endIdx);
          }
          throw new Error("KhÃ´ng nháº­n diá»‡n Ä‘Æ°á»£c tháº» <svg> há»£p lá»‡ trong pháº£n há»“i.");
        },
        {
          maxRetries: 3,
          onRetry: (attempt, maxRetries, delayMs, err) => {
            console.warn(
              `[VectorAssetGenerator] Model ${targetModel} tháº¥t báº¡i (${err.message}). Äang chá» ${(delayMs / 1000).toFixed(1)}s Ä‘á»ƒ thá»­ láº¡i láº§n ${attempt}/${maxRetries}...`
            );
          },
        }
      );
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.warn(`[VectorAssetGenerator] Gemini ${targetModel} tháº¥t báº¡i sau 3 láº§n thá»­ láº¡i cho '${safeId}':`, err.message);
    }
  }

  // 2. PROCEDURAL CARTOON FALLBACK Náº¾U KHÃ”NG CÃ“ Káº¾T Ná»I API
  if (!svgContent) {
    svgContent = createProceduralCartoonSvg(safeId);
  }

  // 3. Tá»° Äá»˜NG GHI FILE VÃ€O KHO TÃ€I NGUYÃŠN public/assets/props/
  try {
    const assetsDir = path.join(process.cwd(), "public", "assets", "props");
    if (!fs.existsSync(assetsDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(assetsDir, { recursive: true });
    }
    const targetFile = path.join(assetsDir, `${safeId}.svg`);
    fs.writeFileSync(targetFile, svgContent, "utf8");

    return {
      id: safeId,
      svgContent,
      dimensions: { width, height },
      filePath: `/assets/props/${safeId}.svg`,
    };
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.warn("[VectorAssetGenerator] KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ ghi file vÃ o public folder:", err.message);
    return {
      id: safeId,
      svgContent,
      dimensions: { width, height },
    };
  }
}

import React from "react";
import { staticFile } from "remotion";
import { BackgroundPreset } from "../../types/sceneGraph";

export interface DynamicBackgroundProps {
  preset: BackgroundPreset | string;
  customAssetFileName?: string;
}

/**
 * DynamicBackground: Bá»™ Náº¡p Bá»‘i Cáº£nh Äá»™ng 2K tá»« Kho Asset Bank
 * Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng Ã¡nh xáº¡ má»i preset vÃ  alias (camelCase, kebab-case, tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t) sang file SVG tháº­t:
 * 1. living-room-day (PhÃ²ng khÃ¡ch ban ngÃ y)
 * 2. living-room-night (PhÃ²ng khÃ¡ch ban Ä‘Ãªm)
 * 3. garden-day (Äá»“ng cá» xanh, báº§u trá»i mÃ¢y tráº¯ng náº¯ng vÃ ng)
 * 4. infographic-stripes (Sá»c infographic trá»«u tÆ°á»£ng)
 * 5. classroom (Lá»›p há»c, báº£ng Ä‘en lá»›n)
 * 6. office (VÄƒn phÃ²ng cao á»‘c, view thÃ nh phá»‘)
 * 7. city-street (ÄÆ°á»ng phá»‘ Ä‘Ã´ thá»‹, vá»‰a hÃ¨)
 * 8. beach-sunny (BÃ£i biá»ƒn nhiá»‡t Ä‘á»›i, cÃ¡t vÃ ng, ráº·ng dá»«a)
 * 9. night-sky (Báº§u trá»i Ä‘Ãªm Ä‘áº§y sao láº¥p lÃ¡nh, váº§ng trÄƒng khuyáº¿t)
 */
export const DynamicBackground: React.FC<DynamicBackgroundProps> = ({
  preset,
  customAssetFileName,
}) => {
  const fileNameMap: Record<string, string> = {
    // 1. PhÃ²ng khÃ¡ch ban ngÃ y
    livingRoomDay: "living-room-day",
    "living-room-day": "living-room-day",
    livingroom: "living-room-day",
    indoor: "living-room-day",

    // 2. PhÃ²ng khÃ¡ch ban Ä‘Ãªm
    livingRoomNight: "living-room-night",
    "living-room-night": "living-room-night",

    // 3. Äá»“ng cá» / SÃ¢n vÆ°á»n
    gardenDay: "garden-day",
    "garden-day": "garden-day",
    garden: "garden-day",
    meadow: "garden-day",
    grass: "garden-day",
    outdoor: "garden-day",

    // 4. Infographic
    infographicStripes: "infographic-stripes",
    "infographic-stripes": "infographic-stripes",
    infographic: "infographic-stripes",

    // 5. Lá»›p há»c
    classroom: "classroom",
    school: "classroom",

    // 6. VÄƒn phÃ²ng
    office: "office",
    workplace: "office",

    // 7. ÄÆ°á»ng phá»‘
    cityStreet: "city-street",
    "city-street": "city-street",
    city: "city-street",
    street: "city-street",

    // 8. BÃ£i biá»ƒn
    beachSunny: "beach-sunny",
    "beach-sunny": "beach-sunny",
    beach: "beach-sunny",
    sea: "beach-sunny",

    // 9. Báº§u trá»i Ä‘Ãªm
    nightSky: "night-sky",
    "night-sky": "night-sky",
    night: "night-sky",
    stars: "night-sky",
  };

  const resolveBackgroundFileName = (rawPreset: string | undefined): string => {
    if (!rawPreset) return "living-room-day";
    const raw = String(rawPreset).trim().toLowerCase();
    if (fileNameMap[rawPreset] || fileNameMap[raw]) return fileNameMap[rawPreset] || fileNameMap[raw];

    if (/meadow|garden|grass|lawn|field|park|forest|nature|outdoor|dong-co|thao-nguyen|vuon|cay-xanh/i.test(raw)) {
      return "garden-day";
    }
    if (/class|school|lecture|teacher|student|lop-hoc|truong-hoc|giang-duong|bang-den/i.test(raw)) {
      return "classroom";
    }
    if (/office|workplace|desk|corp|meeting|business|van-phong|cong-ty|cong-so/i.test(raw)) {
      return "office";
    }
    if (/street|city|road|urban|traffic|car|duong-pho|pho-xa|via-he/i.test(raw)) {
      return "city-street";
    }
    if (/beach|sea|ocean|sand|summer|coast|bai-bien|bien|bo-cat/i.test(raw)) {
      return "beach-sunny";
    }
    if (/night-sky|night_sky|star|galaxy|space|cosmos|ngan-ha|vu-tru|sao/i.test(raw)) {
      return "night-sky";
    }
    if (/night|dark|evening|moon|khuya|toi|dem|trang/i.test(raw)) {
      return "living-room-night";
    }
    if (/info|graph|chart|metric|stripes|bieu-do|infographic/i.test(raw)) {
      return "infographic-stripes";
    }
    return "living-room-day";
  };

  const assetFileName = customAssetFileName || resolveBackgroundFileName(preset as string);
  const baseAssetPath = staticFile(`assets/backgrounds/${assetFileName}.svg`);
  const assetUrl = `${baseAssetPath}?v=20261108_2k`;

  return (
    <g id={`dynamic-background-${preset}`} data-preset={preset}>
      <image
        href={assetUrl}
        xlinkHref={assetUrl}
        x={0}
        y={0}
        width={2560}
        height={1440}
        preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice"
      />
    </g>
  );
};

==================== FILE: C:\Users\ASUS\Downloads\TOOL_2D_ANIM_API_FIX_1\TOOL_2D_ANIM\web-studio\src\remotion\components\character\FacialExpressions.tsx ====================
import React from "react";
import { EyeExpression, EyebrowExpression, MouthExpression, Point2D } from "../../types/character";

interface FacialExpressionsProps {
  headRadius: number;
  facing?: "left" | "right";
  eyes?: EyeExpression;
  eyebrows?: EyebrowExpression;
  mouth?: MouthExpression;
  pupilGaze?: Point2D;
  blush?: boolean;
  sweatDrop?: boolean;
  strokeColor?: string;
  useDotEyes?: boolean; // Cho phÃ©p chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»•i linh hoáº¡t náº¿u cáº§n
}

export const FacialExpressions: React.FC<FacialExpressionsProps> = ({
  headRadius = 90,
  facing = "right",
  eyes = "neutral",
  eyebrows = "neutral",
  mouth = "smile",
  pupilGaze = { x: 0, y: 0 },
  blush = false,
  sweatDrop = false,
  strokeColor = "#18181B",
  useDotEyes = false,
}) => {
  // Tá»‰ lá»‡ hÃ¬nh há»c khuÃ´n máº·t theo áº£nh máº«u 02_MAU_NGUOI_DOI_MU.png
  const eyeDistance = headRadius * 0.38;
  const eyeY = -headRadius * 0.08;
  const eyeRx = headRadius * 0.22;
  const eyeRy = headRadius * 0.28;
  const pupilRadius = headRadius * 0.12;

  // LÃ´ng mÃ y: 2 nÃ©t ngáº¯n hÆ¡i xáº¿ch lÃªn phÃ­a trÃªn máº¯t
  const browY = eyeY - eyeRy - headRadius * 0.08;
  const browHalfW = headRadius * 0.16;
  const browStrokeWidth = Math.max(4, headRadius * 0.075);

  // Gaze offset: con ngÆ°Æ¡i Ä‘en di chuyá»ƒn trong lÃ²ng tráº¯ng theo gaze
  const clampedGazeX = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, pupilGaze.x || 0));
  const clampedGazeY = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, pupilGaze.y || 0));
  const maxPupilDx = eyeRx - pupilRadius - 2;
  const maxPupilDy = eyeRy - pupilRadius - 2;
  const gazeDx = clampedGazeX * maxPupilDx;
  const gazeDy = clampedGazeY * maxPupilDy;

  const leftEyeCenter = { x: -eyeDistance, y: eyeY };
  const rightEyeCenter = { x: eyeDistance, y: eyeY };

  // Tá»a Ä‘á»™ 2 lÃ´ng mÃ y Ä‘á»™c láº­p
  const leftBrowX1 = -eyeDistance - browHalfW;
  const leftBrowX2 = -eyeDistance + browHalfW;
  const rightBrowX1 = eyeDistance - browHalfW;
  const rightBrowX2 = eyeDistance + browHalfW;

  return (
    <g className="stickman-face">
      {/* 1. NÃ‰T XÃM Cáº NH MÃ (3 nÃ©t xÃ¡m ngáº¯n má»—i bÃªn theo áº£nh 02_MAU_NGUOI_DOI_MU.png) */}
      <g stroke="#94A3B8" strokeWidth={Math.max(3, headRadius * 0.055)} strokeLinecap="round">
        {/* MÃ¡ trÃ¡i */}
        <line x1={-headRadius + 2} y1={-headRadius * 0.08} x2={-headRadius - 16} y2={-headRadius * 0.14} />
        <line x1={-headRadius} y1={0} x2={-headRadius - 18} y2={0} />
        <line x1={-headRadius + 2} y1={headRadius * 0.08} x2={-headRadius - 16} y2={headRadius * 0.14} />
        {/* MÃ¡ pháº£i */}
        <line x1={headRadius - 2} y1={-headRadius * 0.08} x2={headRadius + 16} y2={-headRadius * 0.14} />
        <line x1={headRadius} y1={0} x2={headRadius + 18} y2={0} />
        <line x1={headRadius - 2} y1={headRadius * 0.08} x2={headRadius + 16} y2={headRadius * 0.14} />
      </g>

      {/* Blush cheeks */}
      {blush && (
        <g opacity={0.65}>
          <ellipse cx={-eyeDistance - 10} cy={eyeY + 36} rx={16} ry={9} fill="#FF8BA7" />
          <ellipse cx={eyeDistance + 10} cy={eyeY + 36} rx={16} ry={9} fill="#FF8BA7" />
        </g>
      )}

      {/* 2. HAI LÃ”NG MÃ€Y NGáº®N HÆ I Xáº¾CH Äá»˜C Láº¬P PHÃA TRÃŠN Máº®T */}
      <g stroke={strokeColor} strokeWidth={browStrokeWidth} strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" fill="none">
        {eyebrows === "neutral" && (
          <>
            <line x1={leftBrowX1} y1={browY + 2} x2={leftBrowX2} y2={browY - 3} />
            <line x1={rightBrowX1} y1={browY - 3} x2={rightBrowX2} y2={browY + 2} />
          </>
        )}
        {eyebrows === "raised" && (
          <>
            <line x1={leftBrowX1} y1={browY - 10} x2={leftBrowX2} y2={browY - 14} />
            <line x1={rightBrowX1} y1={browY - 14} x2={rightBrowX2} y2={browY - 10} />
          </>
        )}
        {eyebrows === "inquisitive" && (
          <>
            <line x1={leftBrowX1} y1={browY} x2={leftBrowX2} y2={browY} />
            <line x1={rightBrowX1} y1={browY - 8} x2={rightBrowX2} y2={browY - 2} />
          </>
        )}
        {eyebrows === "worried" && (
          <>
            <line x1={leftBrowX1} y1={browY - 6} x2={leftBrowX2} y2={browY + 4} />
            <line x1={rightBrowX1} y1={browY + 4} x2={rightBrowX2} y2={browY - 6} />
          </>
        )}
        {eyebrows === "furrowed" && (
          <>
            <line x1={leftBrowX1} y1={browY + 5} x2={leftBrowX2} y2={browY - 5} />
            <line x1={rightBrowX1} y1={browY - 5} x2={rightBrowX2} y2={browY + 5} />
          </>
        )}
      </g>

      {/* 3. Máº®T: HAI Máº®T OVAL CÃ“ VIá»€N, LÃ’NG TRáº®NG VÃ€ CON NGÆ¯Æ I ÄEN THEO GAZE (CHá»ˆNH Sá»¬A V4) */}
      {useDotEyes ? (
        // Cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ Dot Eyes cÅ© (náº¿u gá»i rÃµ)
        <g>
          <circle cx={leftEyeCenter.x + gazeDx} cy={leftEyeCenter.y + gazeDy} r={headRadius * 0.08} fill={strokeColor} />
          <circle cx={rightEyeCenter.x + gazeDx} cy={rightEyeCenter.y + gazeDy} r={headRadius * 0.08} fill={strokeColor} />
        </g>
      ) : eyes === "closed" ? (
        <g stroke={strokeColor} strokeWidth={Math.max(4, headRadius * 0.075)} strokeLinecap="round" fill="none">
          <line x1={leftEyeCenter.x - eyeRx} y1={leftEyeCenter.y} x2={leftEyeCenter.x + eyeRx} y2={leftEyeCenter.y} />
          <line x1={rightEyeCenter.x - eyeRx} y1={rightEyeCenter.y} x2={rightEyeCenter.x + eyeRx} y2={rightEyeCenter.y} />
        </g>
      ) : eyes === "happy" ? (
        <g stroke={strokeColor} strokeWidth={Math.max(4, headRadius * 0.075)} strokeLinecap="round" fill="none">
          <path d={`M ${leftEyeCenter.x - eyeRx} ${eyeY + 4} Q ${leftEyeCenter.x} ${eyeY - eyeRy} ${leftEyeCenter.x + eyeRx} ${eyeY + 4}`} />
          <path d={`M ${rightEyeCenter.x - eyeRx} ${eyeY + 4} Q ${rightEyeCenter.x} ${eyeY - eyeRy} ${rightEyeCenter.x + eyeRx} ${eyeY + 4}`} />
        </g>
      ) : (
        <g id="stickman-oval-eyes">
          {/* Máº¯t trÃ¡i: viá»n oval + lÃ²ng tráº¯ng */}
          <ellipse
            cx={leftEyeCenter.x}
            cy={leftEyeCenter.y}
            rx={eyeRx}
            ry={eyeRy}
            fill="#FFFFFF"
            stroke={strokeColor}
            strokeWidth={Math.max(4, headRadius * 0.075)}
          />
          {/* Con ngÆ°Æ¡i Ä‘en máº¯t trÃ¡i */}
          <circle
            cx={leftEyeCenter.x + gazeDx}
            cy={leftEyeCenter.y + gazeDy}
            r={pupilRadius}
            fill={strokeColor}
          />

          {/* Máº¯t pháº£i: viá»n oval + lÃ²ng tráº¯ng */}
          <ellipse
            cx={rightEyeCenter.x}
            cy={rightEyeCenter.y}
            rx={eyeRx}
            ry={eyeRy}
            fill="#FFFFFF"
            stroke={strokeColor}
            strokeWidth={Math.max(4, headRadius * 0.075)}
          />
          {/* Con ngÆ°Æ¡i Ä‘en máº¯t pháº£i */}
          <circle
            cx={rightEyeCenter.x + gazeDx}
            cy={rightEyeCenter.y + gazeDy}
            r={pupilRadius}
            fill={strokeColor}
          />
        </g>
      )}

      {/* 4. MIá»†NG CÆ¯á»œI Má»˜T NÃ‰T (A single curved smile) */}
      <g stroke={strokeColor} strokeWidth={Math.max(5, headRadius * 0.08)} strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" fill="none">
        {mouth === "smile" && (
          <path d={`M ${-headRadius * 0.28} ${headRadius * 0.38} Q 0 ${headRadius * 0.54} ${headRadius * 0.28} ${headRadius * 0.38}`} />
        )}
        {mouth === "circleOpen" && (
          <circle cx={0} cy={headRadius * 0.44} r={headRadius * 0.16} fill={strokeColor} />
        )}
        {mouth === "line" && (
          <line x1={-headRadius * 0.24} y1={headRadius * 0.42} x2={headRadius * 0.24} y2={headRadius * 0.42} />
        )}
        {mouth === "talking" && (
          <path d={`M ${-headRadius * 0.26} ${headRadius * 0.36} Q 0 ${headRadius * 0.65} ${headRadius * 0.26} ${headRadius * 0.36} Z`} fill="#881337" strokeWidth={Math.max(4, headRadius * 0.07)} />
        )}
        {mouth === "gasp" && (
          <ellipse cx={0} cy={headRadius * 0.46} rx={headRadius * 0.18} ry={headRadius * 0.26} fill="#141414" />
        )}
      </g>

      {/* 5. Vá»†T XÃM NHáº¸ DÆ¯á»šI MIá»†NG (Chin shadow theo áº£nh máº«u) */}
      <path
        d={`M ${-headRadius * 0.26} ${headRadius * 0.56} Q 0 ${headRadius * 0.68} ${headRadius * 0.26} ${headRadius * 0.56}`}
        fill="none"
        stroke="#CBD5E1"
        strokeWidth={Math.max(4, headRadius * 0.065)}
        strokeLinecap="round"
        opacity={0.8}
      />

      {/* Sweat drop for nervous moment */}
      {sweatDrop && (
        <path
          d={`M ${headRadius - 16} ${-headRadius / 2} C ${headRadius - 6} ${-headRadius / 2 - 14}, ${headRadius + 8} ${-headRadius / 2}, ${headRadius - 4} ${-headRadius / 2 + 18} C ${headRadius - 12} ${-headRadius / 2 + 10}, ${headRadius - 20} ${-headRadius / 2 - 4}, ${headRadius - 16} ${-headRadius / 2} Z`}
          fill="#4FC3F7"
          stroke={strokeColor}
          strokeWidth={4}
        />
      )}
    </g>
  );
};

/**
 * HandInteraction: Component bÃ n tay tÆ°Æ¡ng tÃ¡c váº­t lÃ½ (DÃ¡n nhÃ£n, cháº¡m, cáº§m náº¯m)
 * - Tiáº¿p cáº­n má»¥c tiÃªu Ä‘Ãºng tá»a Ä‘á»™ tÆ°Æ¡ng tÃ¡c
 * - KhÃ´ng kÃ©o giÃ£n xÆ°Æ¡ng phi tá»± nhiÃªn (duy trÃ¬ tá»· lá»‡ báº¯p tay vÃ  cáº³ng tay)
 * - Ã‰p nhÃ£n vÃ o bá» máº·t vá»›i micro-press, sau Ä‘Ã³ rÃºt tay vá» dá»©t khoÃ¡t
 * - NhÃ£n dÃ¡n lÆ°u láº¡i vÄ©nh viá»…n trÃªn váº­t thá»ƒ sau khi dÃ¡n
 */
export const HandInteraction: React.FC<HandInteractionProps> = ({
  frame,
  fps = 60,
  targetPos,
  originPos = { x: 2150, y: 520 },
  labelText = "TÃ¡m",
  startFrame = 25,
  applyFrame = 70,
  retractFrame = 105,
  endFrame = 150,
}) => {
  // Tráº¡ng thÃ¡i nhÃ£n dÃ¡n: CÃ³ hiá»ƒn thá»‹ trÃªn lá» khÃ´ng? (Hiá»ƒn thá»‹ ngay tá»« khi cháº¡m applyFrame trá»Ÿ Ä‘i)
  const isLabelAdhered = frame >= applyFrame;

  // Náº¿u chÆ°a Ä‘áº¿n thá»i Ä‘iá»ƒm báº¯t Ä‘áº§u hÃ nh Ä‘á»™ng, chá»‰ render nhÃ£n náº¿u Ä‘Ã£ dÃ¡n
  const isHandActive = frame >= startFrame && frame <= endFrame;

  // TÃ­nh vá»‹ trÃ­ bÃ n tay theo thá»i gian
  let handX = originPos.x;
  let handY = originPos.y;
  let handRotation = 15; // Äá»™ nghiÃªng cáº§m nhÃ£n
  let handScale = 1.0;

  if (frame < startFrame) {
    handX = originPos.x;
    handY = originPos.y;
  } else if (frame < applyFrame) {
    // 1. Pha Ä‘Æ°a nhÃ£n tá»›i (Approach)
    const p = interpolate(frame, [startFrame, applyFrame], [0, 1], {
      extrapolateLeft: "clamp",
      extrapolateRight: "clamp",
      easing: Easing.bezier(0.25, 0.1, 0.25, 1),
    });
    handX = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [originPos.x, targetPos.x]);
    handY = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [originPos.y, targetPos.y]);
    handRotation = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [25, 0]);
  } else if (frame < retractFrame) {
    // 2. Pha dÃ¡n nhÃ£n & miáº¿t nháº¹ (Contact & Press)
    const pressProgress = interpolate(frame, [applyFrame, applyFrame + 15, retractFrame], [0, 1, 0], {
      extrapolateLeft: "clamp",
      extrapolateRight: "clamp",
    });
    handX = targetPos.x;
    handY = targetPos.y + pressProgress * 3; // Nháº¹ nhÃ ng miáº¿t vÃ o kÃ­nh
    handRotation = 0;
    handScale = 1.0 + pressProgress * 0.02;
  } else if (frame <= endFrame) {
    // 3. Pha rÃºt tay vá» (Retract)
    const r = interpolate(frame, [retractFrame, endFrame], [0, 1], {
      extrapolateLeft: "clamp",
      extrapolateRight: "clamp",
      easing: Easing.bezier(0.25, 0.1, 0.25, 1),
    });
    handX = interpolate(r, [0, 1], [targetPos.x, originPos.x + 80]);
    handY = interpolate(r, [0, 1], [targetPos.y, originPos.y + 40]);
    handRotation = interpolate(r, [0, 1], [0, 20]);
  } else {
    // Tay Ä‘Ã£ rÃºt hoÃ n toÃ n ra khá»i khung hÃ¬nh
    handX = originPos.x + 200;
  }

  // KÃ­ch thÆ°á»›c nhÃ£n dÃ¡n
  const labelW = 200;
  const labelH = 80;

  return (
    <g id="hand-interaction-layer">
      {/* 1. NHÃƒN DÃN ÄÃƒ DÃNH TRÃŠN Bá»€ Máº¶T Lá»Œ (LÆ°u láº¡i vÄ©nh viá»…n trÃªn kÃ­nh) */}
      {isLabelAdhered && (
        <g id="adhered-label-sticker" transform={`translate(${targetPos.x}, ${targetPos.y})`}>
          {/* BÃ³ng Ä‘á»• nháº¹ cá»§a tem dÃ¡n trÃªn máº·t kÃ­nh */}
          <rect
            x={-labelW / 2 + 3}
            y={-labelH / 2 + 5}
            width={labelW}
            height={labelH}
            rx={14}
            fill="#0284C7"
            opacity={0.3}
          />
          {/* ThÃ¢n tem dÃ¡n tráº¯ng viá»n Ä‘á» sáº¯c nÃ©t */}
          <rect
            x={-labelW / 2}
            y={-labelH / 2}
            width={labelW}
            height={labelH}
            rx={14}
            fill="#FFFFFF"
            stroke="#141414"
            strokeWidth={7}
          />
          {/* Viá»n chá»‰ trang trÃ­ bÃªn trong tem */}
          <rect
            x={-labelW / 2 + 8}
            y={-labelH / 2 + 8}
            width={labelW - 16}
            height={labelH - 16}
            rx={8}
            fill="none"
            stroke="#E11D48"
            strokeWidth={3}
            strokeDasharray="8 4"
          />
          {/* Chá»¯ trÃªn nhÃ£n: "TÃ¡m" (Chá»¯ hoa Ä‘áº­m, rÃµ rÃ ng, cÄƒn giá»¯a) */}
          <text
            x={0}
            y={15}
            textAnchor="middle"
            fill="#E11D48"
            fontSize={44}
            fontWeight="900"
            fontFamily="var(--font-be-vietnam-pro), sans-serif"
            letterSpacing="2px"
          >
            {labelText}
          </text>
        </g>
      )}

      {/* 2. CÃNH TAY & BÃ€N TAY Cáº¦M NHÃƒN Tá»šI DÃN */}
      {isHandActive && (
        <g
          id="active-hand-rig"
          transform={`translate(${handX}, ${handY}) rotate(${handRotation}) scale(${handScale})`}
        >
          {/* NhÃ£n dÃ¡n ÄANG ÄÆ¯á»¢C Cáº¦M TRÃŠN TAY (chá»‰ hiá»ƒn thá»‹ trÆ°á»›c khi apply) */}
          {!isLabelAdhered && (
            <g id="carried-label-sticker">
              <rect
                x={-labelW / 2}
                y={-labelH / 2}
                width={labelW}
                height={labelH}
                rx={14}
                fill="#FFFFFF"
                stroke="#141414"
                strokeWidth={7}
              />
              <rect
                x={-labelW / 2 + 8}
                y={-labelH / 2 + 8}
                width={labelW - 16}
                height={labelH - 16}
                rx={8}
                fill="none"
                stroke="#E11D48"
                strokeWidth={3}
                strokeDasharray="8 4"
              />
              <text
                x={0}
                y={15}
                textAnchor="middle"
                fill="#E11D48"
                fontSize={44}
                fontWeight="900"
                fontFamily="var(--font-be-vietnam-pro), sans-serif"
                letterSpacing="2px"
              >
                {labelText}
              </text>
            </g>
          )}

          {/* Cáº²NG TAY VÃ€ BÃ€N TAY NGÆ¯á»œI QUE Vá»šI KHá»šP KHUá»¶U TAY VÃ€ NGÃ“N TAY RÃ• RÃ€NG */}
          <g id="forearm-and-hand" stroke="#141414" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round">
            {/* Cáº³ng tay ná»‘i tá»« khuá»·u tay Ä‘áº¿n cá»• tay */}
            <line x1={35} y1={-15} x2={260} y2={-90} strokeWidth={18} />

            {/* Báº¯p tay ná»‘i tá»« ngoÃ i khung hÃ¬nh vÃ o khuá»·u tay */}
            <line x1={260} y1={-90} x2={620} y2={-190} strokeWidth={20} />

            {/* Khá»›p khuá»·u tay */}
            <circle cx={260} cy={-90} r={11} fill="#141414" strokeWidth={0} />

            {/* Khá»›p cá»• tay */}
            <circle cx={35} cy={-15} r={10} fill="#141414" strokeWidth={0} />

            {/* LÃ²ng bÃ n tay */}
            <ellipse cx={18} cy={-8} rx={22} ry={16} fill="#FFFFFF" strokeWidth={12} />

            {/* NgÃ³n cÃ¡i káº¹p trÃªn mÃ©p nhÃ£n */}
            <path
              d="M 12,-18 C 2,-28 -10,-18 -16,-6"
              fill="none"
              strokeWidth={13}
            />

            {/* NgÃ³n trá» miáº¿t máº·t nhÃ£n */}
            <path
              d="M 22,2 C 10,14 -4,8 -12,-2"
              fill="none"
              strokeWidth={13}
            />

            {/* CÃ¡c ngÃ³n cÃ²n láº¡i co nháº¹ tá»± nhiÃªn */}
            <path
              d="M 30,8 C 20,20 6,14 0,4"
              fill="none"
              strokeWidth={12}
            />
          </g>
        </g>
      )}
    </g>
  );
};

import { Point2D } from "../../types/character";

/**
 * Calculates 2-bone Inverse Kinematics (IK) for stickman arms and legs.
 * Given root (shoulder/hip) and target (hand/foot), finds intermediate joint (elbow/knee).
 *
 * @param root Joint start position (Shoulder or Hip)
 * @param target End effector position (Hand or Foot)
 * @param length1 Upper segment length
 * @param length2 Lower segment length
 * @param bendPositive Direction of bend (true = outwards/downwards, false = inwards/upwards)
 */
export function solveTwoBoneIK(
  root: Point2D,
  target: Point2D,
  length1: number,
  length2: number,
  bendPositive: boolean = true
): { joint: Point2D; reachedTarget: Point2D } {
  const dx = target.x - root.x;
  const dy = target.y - root.y;
  const distance = Math.hypot(dx, dy);

  // Maximum reach limit
  const maxReach = length1 + length2;
  const minReach = Math.abs(length1 - length2);

  // Clamp distance if target is out of reach
  const clampedDist = Math.max(minReach + 0.1, Math.min(distance, maxReach - 0.1));

  // Base angle from root to target
  const baseAngle = Math.atan2(dy, dx);

  // Law of Cosines to find angle at root
  // c^2 = a^2 + b^2 - 2ab * cos(C)
  const cosAngle1 =
    (length1 * length1 + clampedDist * clampedDist - length2 * length2) /
    (2 * length1 * clampedDist);
  const clampedCos1 = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, cosAngle1));
  const angle1 = Math.acos(clampedCos1);

  // Determine actual angle of upper segment
  const jointAngle = bendPositive ? baseAngle + angle1 : baseAngle - angle1;

  const joint: Point2D = {
    x: root.x + Math.cos(jointAngle) * length1,
    y: root.y + Math.sin(jointAngle) * length1,
  };

  // Reached target position (handles clamped distance if out of reach)
  let reachedTarget: Point2D = target;
  if (distance > maxReach) {
    reachedTarget = {
      x: root.x + (dx / distance) * (maxReach - 0.1),
      y: root.y + (dy / distance) * (maxReach - 0.1),
    };
  }

  return { joint, reachedTarget };
}

/**
 * Procedural walk cycle calculator returning leg angles and foot positions.
 * Connected topology: Both legs branch from the exact same hip pivot (0, 0).
 */
export function calculateWalkCycle(
  hip: Point2D,
  progress: number,
  strideWidth: number = 65,
  stepHeight: number = 22
): {
  leftFoot: Point2D;
  rightFoot: Point2D;
  leftKnee: Point2D;
  rightKnee: Point2D;
} {
  const phase = (progress * Math.PI * 2) % (Math.PI * 2);
  const legLength = 350;
  const halfLeg = legLength / 2;

  // Chu ká»³ bÆ°á»›c Ä‘i tá»± nhiÃªn 2 pha (KhÃ´ng moonwalk):
  // Pha vung chÃ¢n (Swing): ChÃ¢n nháº¥c lÃªn khÃ´ng trung (sin > 0) vÃ  vung tá»« sau ra trÆ°á»›c (-cos tÄƒng tá»« -strideWidth sang +strideWidth)
  // Pha chá»‘ng chÃ¢n (Stance): ChÃ¢n cháº¡m Ä‘áº¥t (y = 0) vÃ  Ä‘áº¡p lÃ¹i tá»« trÆ°á»›c vá» sau (-cos giáº£m tá»« +strideWidth sang -strideWidth)
  const leftPhase = phase;
  const leftYOffset = Math.max(0, Math.sin(leftPhase)) * stepHeight;
  const leftXOffset = -Math.cos(leftPhase) * strideWidth;

  // ChÃ¢n pháº£i lá»‡ch pha 180 Ä‘á»™ (PI radian)
  const rightPhase = (phase + Math.PI) % (Math.PI * 2);
  const rightYOffset = Math.max(0, Math.sin(rightPhase)) * stepHeight;
  const rightXOffset = -Math.cos(rightPhase) * strideWidth;

  const leftFootTarget: Point2D = {
    x: hip.x + leftXOffset,
    y: hip.y + legLength - leftYOffset,
  };

  const rightFootTarget: Point2D = {
    x: hip.x + rightXOffset,
    y: hip.y + legLength - rightYOffset,
  };

  // Connected topology: both leg IK roots converge at single hip pivot
  const leftIK = solveTwoBoneIK(
    hip,
    leftFootTarget,
    halfLeg,
    halfLeg,
    false
  );

  const rightIK = solveTwoBoneIK(
    hip,
    rightFootTarget,
    halfLeg,
    halfLeg,
    false
  );

  return {
    leftFoot: leftIK.reachedTarget,
    rightFoot: rightIK.reachedTarget,
    leftKnee: leftIK.joint,
    rightKnee: rightIK.joint,
  };
}

/**
 * Calculates the orientation angle (in degrees) of a bone segment from start joint to end joint.
 * @param start Pivot point (e.g. Shoulder or Hip)
 * @param end End point (e.g. Elbow or Knee)
 * @param defaultOrientation 'downwards' (0 deg is pointing along +Y, standard for character limbs) or 'rightwards' (0 deg is +X)
 */
export function calculateSegmentAngle(
  start: Point2D,
  end: Point2D,
  defaultOrientation: "downwards" | "rightwards" = "downwards"
): number {
  const dx = end.x - start.x;
  const dy = end.y - start.y;
  const angleDeg = Math.atan2(dy, dx) * (180 / Math.PI);
  return defaultOrientation === "downwards" ? angleDeg - 90 : angleDeg;
}

/**
 * Calculates the child's local rotation angle inside parent's rotated coordinate frame.
 * Normalized to [-180, 180] degrees.
 */
export function calculateLocalAngle(
  parentGlobalAngleDeg: number,
  childGlobalAngleDeg: number
): number {
  let diff = (childGlobalAngleDeg - parentGlobalAngleDeg) % 360;
  if (diff > 180) diff -= 360;
  if (diff < -180) diff += 360;
  return diff;
}

export interface HierarchicalTwoBoneResult {
  upperAngleDeg: number;       // Global rotation of upper bone (0 deg = +Y downwards)
  lowerLocalAngleDeg: number;  // Local rotation of lower bone relative to upper bone
  lowerGlobalAngleDeg: number; // Global rotation of lower bone
  joint: Point2D;              // Joint (elbow/knee) position in root coordinates
  reachedTarget: Point2D;      // End effector (hand/foot) position in root coordinates
}

/**
 * Solves 2-bone Inverse Kinematics returning both global and local angles
 * for hierarchical nested SVG groups (<g> within <g>).
 */
export function solveTwoBoneIKHierarchical(
  root: Point2D,
  target: Point2D,
  length1: number,
  length2: number,
  bendPositive: boolean = true
): HierarchicalTwoBoneResult {
  const { joint, reachedTarget } = solveTwoBoneIK(root, target, length1, length2, bendPositive);
  const upperAngleDeg = calculateSegmentAngle(root, joint, "downwards");
  const lowerGlobalAngleDeg = calculateSegmentAngle(joint, reachedTarget, "downwards");
  const lowerLocalAngleDeg = calculateLocalAngle(upperAngleDeg, lowerGlobalAngleDeg);

  return {
    upperAngleDeg,
    lowerLocalAngleDeg,
    lowerGlobalAngleDeg,
    joint,
    reachedTarget,
  };
}

export interface HierarchicalWalkCycleResult {
  leftThighAngle: number;
  leftCalfLocalAngle: number;
  leftFootLocalAngle: number;
  rightThighAngle: number;
  rightCalfLocalAngle: number;
  rightFootLocalAngle: number;
  leftKnee: Point2D;
  rightKnee: Point2D;
  leftFoot: Point2D;
  rightFoot: Point2D;
}

/**
 * Calculates procedural walk cycle angles for hierarchical nested leg groups.
 * Both legs branch from the exact same hip pivot (0, 0), and knees/feet are nested children.
 */
export function calculateWalkCycleHierarchical(
  hip: Point2D,
  progress: number,
  strideWidth: number = 65,
  stepHeight: number = 22,
  thighLength: number = 175,
  calfLength: number = 175
): HierarchicalWalkCycleResult {
  const { leftFoot, rightFoot, leftKnee, rightKnee } = calculateWalkCycle(
    hip,
    progress,
    strideWidth,
    stepHeight
  );

  const leftIK = solveTwoBoneIKHierarchical(hip, leftFoot, thighLength, calfLength, false);
  const rightIK = solveTwoBoneIKHierarchical(hip, rightFoot, thighLength, calfLength, false);

  // BÃ n chÃ¢n tiáº¿p Ä‘áº¥t tá»± nhiÃªn (hÆ¡i cháº¿ch gÃ³t khi cháº¡m Ä‘áº¥t, tháº³ng khi Ä‘áº¡p lÃ¹i)
  const leftFootGlobalAngle = 0;
  const leftFootLocalAngle = calculateLocalAngle(leftIK.lowerGlobalAngleDeg, leftFootGlobalAngle);

  const rightFootGlobalAngle = 0;
  const rightFootLocalAngle = calculateLocalAngle(rightIK.lowerGlobalAngleDeg, rightFootGlobalAngle);

  return {
    leftThighAngle: leftIK.upperAngleDeg,
    leftCalfLocalAngle: leftIK.lowerLocalAngleDeg,
    leftFootLocalAngle,
    rightThighAngle: rightIK.upperAngleDeg,
    rightCalfLocalAngle: rightIK.lowerLocalAngleDeg,
    rightFootLocalAngle,
    leftKnee,
    rightKnee,
    leftFoot,
    rightFoot,
  };
}

import React from "react";
import { StickmanState, Point2D, StickmanAnchors } from "../../types/character";
import {
  solveTwoBoneIKHierarchical,
  calculateWalkCycleHierarchical,
  calculateLocalAngle,
} from "./Kinematics";
import { FacialExpressions } from "./FacialExpressions";
import { getHumanoidBones } from "../../../animscript/registry/assetRegistry";

export interface StickmanBodyParts {
  head?: React.ReactNode;
  torso?: React.ReactNode;
  leftUpperArm?: React.ReactNode;
  leftLowerArm?: React.ReactNode;
  leftHand?: React.ReactNode;
  rightUpperArm?: React.ReactNode;
  rightLowerArm?: React.ReactNode;
  rightHand?: React.ReactNode;
  leftThigh?: React.ReactNode;
  leftCalf?: React.ReactNode;
  leftFoot?: React.ReactNode;
  rightThigh?: React.ReactNode;
  rightCalf?: React.ReactNode;
  rightFoot?: React.ReactNode;
}

export interface StickmanRigProps {
  state: StickmanState;
  renderPart?: "all" | "bodyOnly" | "armsOnly" | "legsOnly";
  parts?: StickmanBodyParts;
  breathScale?: number;
  breathScaleY?: number;
  showDebugMarkers?: boolean;
  onAnchorsComputed?: (anchors: StickmanAnchors) => void;
}

/**
 * HÃ m tÃ­nh toÃ¡n Äá»™ng há»c Thuáº­n (Forward Kinematics) dÃ¹ng chung 100% giá»¯a Renderer, HUD vÃ  Validator
 * Äáº£m báº£o má»i Ä‘iá»ƒm neo khá»›p xÆ°Æ¡ng, thÆ°á»›c Ä‘o vÃ  tá»a Ä‘á»™ tháº¿ giá»›i Ä‘á»u xuáº¥t phÃ¡t tá»« cÃ¹ng má»™t thuáº­t toÃ¡n duy nháº¥t.
 */
export function computeStickmanFK(
  state: StickmanState,
  breathScale: number = 1.0
): StickmanAnchors {
  const {
    x,
    y,
    scale = 1,
    facing = "right",
    pose = "standing",
    limbAngles,
  } = state;

  const bones = getHumanoidBones(600);
  const spineLength = bones.torsoLength;
  const upperArmLength = bones.upperArmLength;
  const forearmLength = bones.forearmLength;
  const thighLength = bones.thighLength;
  const calfLength = bones.shinLength;
  const headRadius = bones.headRadius;
  const neckLength = bones.neckLength;
  const standingLegLength = thighLength + calfLength;

  const effectiveSpineLength = spineLength * breathScale;
  const isSitting = pose === "sittingBehindDesk";
  const defaultHipY = isSitting ? -180 : -standingLegLength;
  let hipYVal = defaultHipY;
  if (state.seatedHipY !== undefined) {
    if (state.seatedHipY <= -220 && state.seatedHipY >= -350) {
      const sittingRatio = (state.seatedHipY - (-350)) / 130;
      hipYVal = -standingLegLength + sittingRatio * (standingLegLength - 180);
    } else {
      hipYVal = state.seatedHipY;
    }
  }
  const hip: Point2D = { x: 0, y: hipYVal };

  const shoulderHalfWidth = bones.shoulderHalfWidth; // 5
  const pelvicHalfWidth = bones.pelvicHalfWidth; // 16
  const torsoAngle = limbAngles?.torso ?? 0;
  const torsoRad = (torsoAngle * Math.PI) / 180;
  const cosTorso = Math.cos(torsoRad);
  const sinTorso = Math.sin(torsoRad);

  const neckUpper: Point2D = { x: 0, y: -effectiveSpineLength };
  const headCenterUpper: Point2D = { x: 0, y: -effectiveSpineLength - neckLength - headRadius };
  const leftShoulderUpper: Point2D = { x: -shoulderHalfWidth, y: -effectiveSpineLength + 4 };
  const rightShoulderUpper: Point2D = { x: shoulderHalfWidth, y: -effectiveSpineLength + 4 };

  const rotateUpperPoint = (p: Point2D): Point2D => ({
    x: p.x * cosTorso - p.y * sinTorso,
    y: hip.y + p.x * sinTorso + p.y * cosTorso,
  });

  const neck = rotateUpperPoint(neckUpper);
  const headCenter = rotateUpperPoint(headCenterUpper);
  const leftShoulder = rotateUpperPoint(leftShoulderUpper);
  const rightShoulder = rotateUpperPoint(rightShoulderUpper);

  const leftHipJoint: Point2D = { x: -pelvicHalfWidth, y: hip.y };
  const rightHipJoint: Point2D = { x: pelvicHalfWidth, y: hip.y };

  const flipSign = facing === "left" ? -1 : 1;

  let leftUpperArmAngle = limbAngles?.leftUpperArm ?? -14;
  let leftLowerArmLocalAngle = limbAngles?.leftLowerArm ?? -12;
  let rightUpperArmAngle = limbAngles?.rightUpperArm ?? -18;
  let rightLowerArmLocalAngle = limbAngles?.rightLowerArm ?? -16;

  const leftUpperRad = (leftUpperArmAngle * Math.PI) / 180;
  const leftLowerGlobalRad = ((leftUpperArmAngle + leftLowerArmLocalAngle) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const leftElbowUpper: Point2D = {
    x: leftShoulderUpper.x - Math.sin(leftUpperRad) * upperArmLength,
    y: leftShoulderUpper.y + Math.cos(leftUpperRad) * upperArmLength,
  };
  const leftWristUpper: Point2D = {
    x: leftElbowUpper.x - Math.sin(leftLowerGlobalRad) * forearmLength,
    y: leftElbowUpper.y + Math.cos(leftLowerGlobalRad) * forearmLength,
  };
  const leftElbow = rotateUpperPoint(leftElbowUpper);
  const leftWrist = rotateUpperPoint(leftWristUpper);

  const rightUpperRad = (rightUpperArmAngle * Math.PI) / 180;
  const rightLowerGlobalRad = ((rightUpperArmAngle + rightLowerArmLocalAngle) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const rightElbowUpper: Point2D = {
    x: rightShoulderUpper.x - Math.sin(rightUpperRad) * upperArmLength,
    y: rightShoulderUpper.y + Math.cos(rightUpperRad) * upperArmLength,
  };
  const rightWristUpper: Point2D = {
    x: rightElbowUpper.x - Math.sin(rightLowerGlobalRad) * forearmLength,
    y: rightElbowUpper.y + Math.cos(rightLowerGlobalRad) * forearmLength,
  };
  const rightElbow = rotateUpperPoint(rightElbowUpper);
  const rightWrist = rotateUpperPoint(rightWristUpper);

  let leftThighAngle = limbAngles?.leftThigh ?? 0;
  let leftCalfLocalAngle = limbAngles?.leftCalf ?? 0;
  let rightThighAngle = limbAngles?.rightThigh ?? 0;
  let rightCalfLocalAngle = limbAngles?.rightCalf ?? 0;

  const leftThighRad = (leftThighAngle * Math.PI) / 180;
  const leftCalfGlobalRad = ((leftThighAngle + leftCalfLocalAngle) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const leftKnee: Point2D = {
    x: leftHipJoint.x - Math.sin(leftThighRad) * thighLength,
    y: leftHipJoint.y + Math.cos(leftThighRad) * thighLength,
  };
  const leftFoot: Point2D = {
    x: leftKnee.x - Math.sin(leftCalfGlobalRad) * calfLength,
    y: leftKnee.y + Math.cos(leftCalfGlobalRad) * calfLength,
  };

  const rightThighRad = (rightThighAngle * Math.PI) / 180;
  const rightCalfGlobalRad = ((rightThighAngle + rightCalfLocalAngle) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const rightKnee: Point2D = {
    x: rightHipJoint.x - Math.sin(rightThighRad) * thighLength,
    y: rightHipJoint.y + Math.cos(rightThighRad) * thighLength,
  };
  const rightFoot: Point2D = {
    x: rightKnee.x - Math.sin(rightCalfGlobalRad) * calfLength,
    y: rightKnee.y + Math.cos(rightCalfGlobalRad) * calfLength,
  };

  return {
    root: { x, y },
    headCenter: { x: x + headCenter.x * scale * flipSign, y: y + headCenter.y * scale },
    mouth: { x: x + headCenter.x * scale * flipSign, y: y + (headCenter.y + 35) * scale },
    neck: { x: x + neck.x * scale * flipSign, y: y + neck.y * scale },
    hip: { x: x + hip.x * scale * flipSign, y: y + hip.y * scale },
    leftShoulder: { x: x + leftShoulder.x * scale * flipSign, y: y + leftShoulder.y * scale },
    rightShoulder: { x: x + rightShoulder.x * scale * flipSign, y: y + rightShoulder.y * scale },
    leftElbow: { x: x + leftElbow.x * scale * flipSign, y: y + leftElbow.y * scale },
    rightElbow: { x: x + rightElbow.x * scale * flipSign, y: y + rightElbow.y * scale },
    leftWrist: { x: x + leftWrist.x * scale * flipSign, y: y + leftWrist.y * scale },
    rightWrist: { x: x + rightWrist.x * scale * flipSign, y: y + rightWrist.y * scale },
    leftHand: { x: x + leftWrist.x * scale * flipSign, y: y + leftWrist.y * scale },
    rightHand: { x: x + rightWrist.x * scale * flipSign, y: y + rightWrist.y * scale },
    leftHip: { x: x + leftHipJoint.x * scale * flipSign, y: y + hip.y * scale },
    rightHip: { x: x + rightHipJoint.x * scale * flipSign, y: y + hip.y * scale },
    leftKnee: { x: x + leftKnee.x * scale * flipSign, y: y + leftKnee.y * scale },
    rightKnee: { x: x + rightKnee.x * scale * flipSign, y: y + rightKnee.y * scale },
    leftFoot: { x: x + leftFoot.x * scale * flipSign, y: y + leftFoot.y * scale },
    rightFoot: { x: x + rightFoot.x * scale * flipSign, y: y + rightFoot.y * scale },
  };
}

/**
 * StickmanRig: Khung XÆ°Æ¡ng Pháº£ Há»‡ Cha - Con Äá»™c Láº­p 2K (Hierarchical Nested Rigging)
 * 
 * NGUYÃŠN Táº®C Báº¤T DI Báº¤T Dá»ŠCH:
 * 1. Má»i bá»™ pháº­n ná»‘i tiáº¿p nhau PHáº¢I Ä‘Æ°á»£c lá»“ng trá»±c tiáº¿p vÃ o trong tháº» <g> cá»§a bá»™ pháº­n cha.
 *    - Khá»›p Vai -> Báº¯p tay -> Khá»›p CÃ¹i chá» (Elbow) -> Cáº³ng tay -> Khá»›p Cá»• tay -> BÃ n tay.
 *    - Khá»›p HÃ´ng -> ÄÃ¹i -> Khá»›p Äáº§u gá»‘i (Knee) -> Cáº³ng chÃ¢n -> Khá»›p Cá»• chÃ¢n -> BÃ n chÃ¢n.
 * 2. Tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i khÃ´ng dÃ¹ng tá»a Ä‘á»™ rá»i ráº¡c (KHÃ”NG BAO GIá»œ Äá»¨T KHá»šP).
 * 3. Há»— trá»£ thay tháº¿ tá»«ng bá»™ pháº­n Ä‘á»™c láº­p qua props `parts` (Custom Skins / Assets).
 */
export const StickmanRig: React.FC<StickmanRigProps> = ({
  state,
  renderPart = "all",
  parts,
  breathScale = 1.0,
  breathScaleY = 1.0,
  showDebugMarkers = false,
  onAnchorsComputed,
}) => {
  const {
    x,
    y,
    scale = 1,
    facing = "right",
    pose = "standing",
    holdingTarget,
    pointingTarget,
    walkCycleProgress = 0,
    limbAngles,
  } = state;

  const isDebug = Boolean(showDebugMarkers || state.showDebugMarkers);

  // KÃ­ch thÆ°á»›c chuáº©n xÆ°Æ¡ng que theo tá»· lá»‡ AnimScript (Standard Minimalist Stickman, height=600 scale)
  const bones = getHumanoidBones(600);
  const spineLength = bones.torsoLength; // 190
  const upperArmLength = bones.upperArmLength; // 115
  const forearmLength = bones.forearmLength; // 110
  const handLength = bones.handLength; // 15
  const thighLength = bones.thighLength; // 155
  const calfLength = bones.shinLength; // 155
  const headRadius = bones.headRadius; // 46
  const neckLength = bones.neckLength; // 16
  const standingLegLength = thighLength + calfLength; // 310

  // HÆ¡i thá»Ÿ tÃ¡c Ä‘á»™ng nháº¹ lÃªn chiá»u dÃ i sá»‘ng lÆ°ng
  const effectiveSpineLength = spineLength * breathScale;

  // =========================================================================
  // Gá»C TOÃ€N Cá»¤C KHá»šP XÆ¯Æ NG (CONNECTED TOPOLOGY - Há»˜I Tá»¤ 100% VÃ€O TRá»¤C THÃ‚N)
  // =========================================================================
  const isSitting = pose === "sittingBehindDesk";
  const defaultHipY = isSitting ? -180 : -standingLegLength; // -310 khi Ä‘á»©ng
  let hipYVal = defaultHipY;
  if (state.seatedHipY !== undefined) {
    if (state.seatedHipY <= -220 && state.seatedHipY >= -350) {
      const sittingRatio = (state.seatedHipY - (-350)) / 130;
      hipYVal = -standingLegLength + sittingRatio * (standingLegLength - 180);
    } else {
      hipYVal = state.seatedHipY;
    }
  }
  const hip: Point2D = { x: 0, y: hipYVal };

  // Khá»›p vai: Äáº·t sÃ¡t hai bÃªn Ä‘áº§u trÃªn cá»§a thÃ¢n (neck.y + 4), dÃ¡ng ngÆ°á»i que vai háº¹p
  const shoulderHalfWidth = bones.shoulderHalfWidth; // 5
  const pelvicHalfWidth = bones.pelvicHalfWidth; // 3
  const torsoAngle = state.limbAngles?.torso ?? 0;
  const torsoRad = (torsoAngle * Math.PI) / 180;
  const cosTorso = Math.cos(torsoRad);
  const sinTorso = Math.sin(torsoRad);

  // Äiá»ƒm trong há»‡ tá»a Ä‘á»™ cá»§a upper body (gá»‘c lÃ  hip, y hÆ°á»›ng lÃªn lÃ  Ã¢m)
  const neckUpper: Point2D = { x: 0, y: -effectiveSpineLength };
  const headCenterUpper: Point2D = { x: 0, y: -effectiveSpineLength - neckLength - headRadius };
  const leftShoulderUpper: Point2D = { x: -shoulderHalfWidth, y: -effectiveSpineLength + 4 };
  const rightShoulderUpper: Point2D = { x: shoulderHalfWidth, y: -effectiveSpineLength + 4 };

  // HÃ m chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»•i tá»« há»‡ tá»a Ä‘á»™ upper body sang tá»a Ä‘á»™ thÃ¢n cá»¥c bá»™
  const rotateUpperPoint = (p: Point2D): Point2D => ({
    x: p.x * cosTorso - p.y * sinTorso,
    y: hip.y + p.x * sinTorso + p.y * cosTorso,
  });

  const neck: Point2D = rotateUpperPoint(neckUpper);
  const headCenter: Point2D = rotateUpperPoint(headCenterUpper);
  const leftShoulder: Point2D = rotateUpperPoint(leftShoulderUpper);
  const rightShoulder: Point2D = rotateUpperPoint(rightShoulderUpper);

  // Khá»›p hÃ´ng:
  const leftHipJoint: Point2D = { x: -pelvicHalfWidth, y: hip.y };
  const rightHipJoint: Point2D = { x: pelvicHalfWidth, y: hip.y };

  const flipSign = facing === "left" ? -1 : 1;

  // Trá»ng sá»‘ bÆ°á»›c Ä‘i vÃ  tráº¡ng thÃ¡i Ä‘ang bÆ°á»›c Ä‘i
  const weight = state.walkWeight !== undefined ? state.walkWeight : 1.0;
  const isWalking = (pose === "walking" || walkCycleProgress > 0) && weight > 0.02;

  // =========================================================================
  // 1. TÃNH TOÃN GÃ“C XOAY KHá»šP TAY (HIERARCHICAL ARM ANGLES)
  // Gá»“m: upperArmAngle (GÃ³c toÃ n cá»¥c) vÃ  lowerArmLocalAngle (GÃ³c cá»¥c bá»™ bÃªn trong báº¯p tay)
  // =========================================================================
  // GÃ³c nghá»‰ tá»± nhiÃªn khi Ä‘á»©ng: Hai tay buÃ´ng xuÃ´i má»m máº¡i, hÆ¡i gáº­p nháº¹ á»Ÿ cÃ¹i chá»
  let leftUpperArmAngle = -14;
  let leftLowerArmLocalAngle = -12;
  let leftHandLocalAngle = 0;

  let rightUpperArmAngle = -18;
  let rightLowerArmLocalAngle = -16;
  let rightHandLocalAngle = 0;

  if (isWalking && pose !== "holdingObject" && !holdingTarget) {
    // Vung tay tá»± nhiÃªn Ä‘á»‘i xá»©ng vá»›i bÆ°á»›c chÃ¢n khi thá»±c sá»± di chuyá»ƒn
    const armSwing = Math.sin(walkCycleProgress * Math.PI * 2) * (32 * weight);
    leftUpperArmAngle = -14 - armSwing;
    leftLowerArmLocalAngle = -12 + Math.abs(armSwing) * 0.25;

    rightUpperArmAngle = -18 + armSwing;
    rightLowerArmLocalAngle = -16 + Math.abs(armSwing) * 0.25;
  } else if (pose === "waving") {
    // Tay trÃ¡i á»Ÿ tÆ° tháº¿ nghá»‰ tá»± nhiÃªn
    leftUpperArmAngle = -14;
    leftLowerArmLocalAngle = -12;

    // Tay pháº£i giÆ¡ cao váº«y tay nhá»‹p nhÃ ng qua khá»›p cÃ¹i chá»
    rightUpperArmAngle = -135; // GiÆ¡ xÃ©o lÃªn trÃªn vá» phÃ­a trÆ°á»›c
    const waveSway = Math.sin((walkCycleProgress || 0) * Math.PI * 8) * 25;
    rightLowerArmLocalAngle = -35 + waveSway;
    rightHandLocalAngle = 12;
  } else if (pose === "holdingObject" && holdingTarget) {
    const targetLeftLocal: Point2D = {
      x: ((holdingTarget.leftGrip.x - x) / scale) * flipSign,
      y: (holdingTarget.leftGrip.y - y) / scale,
    };
    const targetRightLocal: Point2D = {
      x: ((holdingTarget.rightGrip.x - x) / scale) * flipSign,
      y: (holdingTarget.rightGrip.y - y) / scale,
    };

    const leftIK = solveTwoBoneIKHierarchical(leftShoulder, targetLeftLocal, upperArmLength, forearmLength, false);
    const rightIK = solveTwoBoneIKHierarchical(rightShoulder, targetRightLocal, upperArmLength, forearmLength, true);

    leftUpperArmAngle = leftIK.upperAngleDeg;
    leftLowerArmLocalAngle = leftIK.lowerLocalAngleDeg;
    rightUpperArmAngle = rightIK.upperAngleDeg;
    rightLowerArmLocalAngle = rightIK.lowerLocalAngleDeg;
  } else if (pose === "holdingObject") {
    // GiÆ¡ hai tay ra phÃ­a trÆ°á»›c Ã´m/cáº§m Ä‘á»“ váº­t tá»± nhiÃªn
    leftUpperArmAngle = -35;
    leftLowerArmLocalAngle = -50;
    rightUpperArmAngle = -40;
    rightLowerArmLocalAngle = -45;
  } else if (pose === "sittingBehindDesk") {
    // Ngá»“i lÃ m viá»‡c trÆ°á»›c bÃ n: Hai tay vÆ°Æ¡n vá» phÃ­a trÆ°á»›c vÃ  Ä‘áº·t bÃ n tay chuáº©n xÃ¡c lÃªn máº·t bÃ n / bÃ n phÃ­m laptop
    const typeOffset = state.typingProgress !== undefined ? Math.sin(state.typingProgress * Math.PI * 6) * 3 : 0;
    leftUpperArmAngle = -34 + typeOffset;
    leftLowerArmLocalAngle = -48 - typeOffset;
    rightUpperArmAngle = -38 - typeOffset;
    rightLowerArmLocalAngle = -44 + typeOffset;
  } else if (pose === "pointing" && pointingTarget) {
    const targetLocal: Point2D = {
      x: ((pointingTarget.x - x) / scale) * flipSign,
      y: (pointingTarget.y - y) / scale,
    };
    const pointIK = solveTwoBoneIKHierarchical(rightShoulder, targetLocal, upperArmLength, forearmLength, true);
    rightUpperArmAngle = pointIK.upperAngleDeg;
    rightLowerArmLocalAngle = pointIK.lowerLocalAngleDeg;

    leftUpperArmAngle = -14;
    leftLowerArmLocalAngle = -12;
  } else if (pose === "pointing") {
    // Chá»‰ tay dá»©t khoÃ¡t vá» phÃ­a trÆ°á»›c/trÃªn
    rightUpperArmAngle = -70;
    rightLowerArmLocalAngle = -8;
    leftUpperArmAngle = -14;
    leftLowerArmLocalAngle = -12;
  } else if (pose === "shrugging") {
    // NhÃºn vai biá»ƒu cáº£m: Hai cÃ¹i chá» khÃ©p sÃ¡t sÆ°á»n, hai cáº³ng tay ngá»­a lÃªn phÃ­a trÆ°á»›c
    leftUpperArmAngle = -18;
    leftLowerArmLocalAngle = -65;
    rightUpperArmAngle = -22;
    rightLowerArmLocalAngle = -70;
  }

  // Ãp dá»¥ng ghi Ä‘Ã¨ gÃ³c chi tiáº¿t (náº¿u cÃ³ tá»« Animation Keyframes)
  const limbAnglesAny = limbAngles as any;
  if (limbAngles?.leftUpperArm !== undefined) leftUpperArmAngle = limbAngles.leftUpperArm;
  if (limbAngles?.leftLowerArm !== undefined) {
    leftLowerArmLocalAngle = limbAngles.leftLowerArm;
  } else if (limbAnglesAny?.leftForearm !== undefined) {
    leftLowerArmLocalAngle = limbAnglesAny.leftForearm - leftUpperArmAngle;
  }
  if (limbAngles?.leftHand !== undefined) leftHandLocalAngle = limbAngles.leftHand;

  if (limbAngles?.rightUpperArm !== undefined) rightUpperArmAngle = limbAngles.rightUpperArm;
  if (limbAngles?.rightLowerArm !== undefined) {
    rightLowerArmLocalAngle = limbAngles.rightLowerArm;
  } else if (limbAnglesAny?.rightForearm !== undefined) {
    rightLowerArmLocalAngle = limbAnglesAny.rightForearm - rightUpperArmAngle;
  }
  if (limbAngles?.rightHand !== undefined) rightHandLocalAngle = limbAngles.rightHand;

  // =========================================================================
  // 2. TÃNH TOÃN GÃ“C XOAY KHá»šP CHÃ‚N (HIERARCHICAL LEG ANGLES)
  // Gá»“m: thighAngle (ToÃ n cá»¥c), calfLocalAngle (Cá»¥c bá»™ Ä‘Ã¹i) vÃ  footLocalAngle (Cá»¥c bá»™ cáº³ng chÃ¢n)
  // =========================================================================
  // Äá»©ng tá»± nhiÃªn vá»¯ng chÃ£i: ChÃ¢n sau lÃ¹i nháº¹, chÃ¢n trÆ°á»›c tiáº¿n nháº¹, cáº£ 2 bÃ n chÃ¢n pháº³ng trÃªn sÃ n
  // Tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i khÃ´ng cháº­p Ä‘Ã¨ lÃªn nhau thÃ nh má»™t que
  let leftThighAngle = 8;
  let leftCalfLocalAngle = -4;
  let leftFootLocalAngle = -4;

  let rightThighAngle = -6;
  let rightCalfLocalAngle = 6;
  let rightFootLocalAngle = 0;

  if (isWalking) {
    const stride = 65 * weight;
    const stepH = 22 * weight;
    const walkData = calculateWalkCycleHierarchical(
      hip,
      walkCycleProgress,
      stride,
      stepH,
      thighLength,
      calfLength
    );
    leftThighAngle = walkData.leftThighAngle;
    leftCalfLocalAngle = walkData.leftCalfLocalAngle;
    leftFootLocalAngle = walkData.leftFootLocalAngle;
    rightThighAngle = walkData.rightThighAngle;
    rightCalfLocalAngle = walkData.rightCalfLocalAngle;
    rightFootLocalAngle = walkData.rightFootLocalAngle;
  } else if (pose === "sittingBehindDesk") {
    // Cáº£ hai Ä‘Ã¹i hÆ°á»›ng vá» phÃ­a trÆ°á»›c dÆ°á»›i bÃ n (gáº§n náº±m ngang), hai cáº³ng chÃ¢n vuÃ´ng gÃ³c xuá»‘ng Ä‘áº¥t cháº¡m sÃ n
    leftThighAngle = -85;
    leftCalfLocalAngle = 85;
    leftFootLocalAngle = 0;
    rightThighAngle = -88;
    rightCalfLocalAngle = 88;
    rightFootLocalAngle = 0;
  }

  if (limbAngles?.leftThigh !== undefined) leftThighAngle = limbAngles.leftThigh;
  if (limbAngles?.leftCalf !== undefined) {
    leftCalfLocalAngle = limbAngles.leftCalf;
  } else if (limbAnglesAny?.leftShin !== undefined) {
    leftCalfLocalAngle = limbAnglesAny.leftShin - leftThighAngle;
  }
  if (limbAngles?.leftFoot !== undefined) leftFootLocalAngle = limbAngles.leftFoot;

  if (limbAngles?.rightThigh !== undefined) rightThighAngle = limbAngles.rightThigh;
  if (limbAngles?.rightCalf !== undefined) {
    rightCalfLocalAngle = limbAngles.rightCalf;
  } else if (limbAnglesAny?.rightShin !== undefined) {
    rightCalfLocalAngle = limbAnglesAny.rightShin - rightThighAngle;
  }
  if (limbAngles?.rightFoot !== undefined) rightFootLocalAngle = limbAngles.rightFoot;

  const headAngle = limbAngles?.head ?? 0;

  // =========================================================================
  // 3. TÃNH TOÃN Tá»ŒA Äá»˜ TIÃŠU ÄIá»‚M (FORWARD KINEMATICS FOR ANCHORS)
  // Trong há»‡ tá»a Ä‘á»™ SVG: Äiá»ƒm (0, L) quay gÃ³c theta sáº½ cÃ³ x = -L * sin(theta), y = L * cos(theta)
  // =========================================================================
  const leftUpperRad = (leftUpperArmAngle * Math.PI) / 180;
  const leftLowerGlobalRad = ((leftUpperArmAngle + leftLowerArmLocalAngle) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const leftElbowUpper: Point2D = {
    x: leftShoulderUpper.x - Math.sin(leftUpperRad) * upperArmLength,
    y: leftShoulderUpper.y + Math.cos(leftUpperRad) * upperArmLength,
  };
  // Sá»¬A Lá»–I 2: Äáº¦U Cáº²NG TAY VÃ€ BÃ€N TAY Káº¾T THÃšC ÄÃšNG Táº I wristWorld (KHÃ”NG Há»ž KHá»šP)
  const leftWristUpper: Point2D = {
    x: leftElbowUpper.x - Math.sin(leftLowerGlobalRad) * forearmLength,
    y: leftElbowUpper.y + Math.cos(leftLowerGlobalRad) * forearmLength,
  };
  const leftElbow: Point2D = rotateUpperPoint(leftElbowUpper);
  const leftWrist: Point2D = rotateUpperPoint(leftWristUpper);
  const leftHand: Point2D = leftWrist; // Renderer vÃ  há»‡ anchor dÃ¹ng chung 100% wristWorld

  const rightUpperRad = (rightUpperArmAngle * Math.PI) / 180;
  const rightLowerGlobalRad = ((rightUpperArmAngle + rightLowerArmLocalAngle) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const rightElbowUpper: Point2D = {
    x: rightShoulderUpper.x - Math.sin(rightUpperRad) * upperArmLength,
    y: rightShoulderUpper.y + Math.cos(rightUpperRad) * upperArmLength,
  };
  const rightWristUpper: Point2D = {
    x: rightElbowUpper.x - Math.sin(rightLowerGlobalRad) * forearmLength,
    y: rightElbowUpper.y + Math.cos(rightLowerGlobalRad) * forearmLength,
  };
  const rightElbow: Point2D = rotateUpperPoint(rightElbowUpper);
  const rightWrist: Point2D = rotateUpperPoint(rightWristUpper);
  const rightHand: Point2D = rightWrist; // Renderer vÃ  há»‡ anchor dÃ¹ng chung 100% wristWorld

  const leftThighRad = (leftThighAngle * Math.PI) / 180;
  const leftCalfGlobalRad = ((leftThighAngle + leftCalfLocalAngle) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const leftKnee: Point2D = {
    x: leftHipJoint.x - Math.sin(leftThighRad) * thighLength,
    y: leftHipJoint.y + Math.cos(leftThighRad) * thighLength,
  };
  const leftFoot: Point2D = {
    x: leftKnee.x - Math.sin(leftCalfGlobalRad) * calfLength,
    y: leftKnee.y + Math.cos(leftCalfGlobalRad) * calfLength,
  };

  const rightThighRad = (rightThighAngle * Math.PI) / 180;
  const rightCalfGlobalRad = ((rightThighAngle + rightCalfLocalAngle) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const rightKnee: Point2D = {
    x: rightHipJoint.x - Math.sin(rightThighRad) * thighLength,
    y: rightHipJoint.y + Math.cos(rightThighRad) * thighLength,
  };
  const rightFoot: Point2D = {
    x: rightKnee.x - Math.sin(rightCalfGlobalRad) * calfLength,
    y: rightKnee.y + Math.cos(rightCalfGlobalRad) * calfLength,
  };

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (onAnchorsComputed) {
      const fkAnchors = computeStickmanFK(state, breathScale);
      onAnchorsComputed(fkAnchors);
    }
  }, [state, breathScale, onAnchorsComputed]);

  const flipTransform = facing === "left" ? "scale(-1, 1)" : "";

  // Khá»›p hÃ¡ng hiá»ƒn thá»‹: Äá»“ng nháº¥t 100% giá»¯a thÆ°á»ng vÃ  debug (pelvicHalfWidth = 3px chuáº©n inverted V)
  const hipJointLeftX = leftHipJoint.x;
  const hipJointRightX = rightHipJoint.x;

  return (
    <g
      id="stickman-rig-wrapper"
      transform={`translate(${x}, ${y}) scale(${scale}) ${flipTransform} scale(1, ${breathScaleY})`}
      data-character-pose={pose}
    >
      {/* BÃ“NG Äá»” TIáº¾P XÃšC Máº¶T SÃ€N / CÃT (Theo áº£nh 02_MAU_NGUOI_DOI_MU.png) */}
      {(renderPart === "all" || renderPart === "bodyOnly" || renderPart === "legsOnly") && (
        <ellipse cx={0} cy={0} rx={headRadius * 0.95} ry={11} fill="rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.20)" />
      )}

      {/* =========================================================================
          LAYER A: CHÃ‚N (HIERARCHICAL NESTED LEGS: HIP -> THIGH -> KNEE -> CALF -> FOOT)
          Báº£o toÃ n trá»n váº¹n 2 Ä‘oáº¡n xÆ°Æ¡ng vÃ  Ä‘áº§y Ä‘á»§ khá»›p ná»‘i ká»ƒ cáº£ khi ngá»“i hoáº·c bá»‹ che khuáº¥t
         ========================================================================= */}
      {(renderPart === "all" || renderPart === "bodyOnly" || renderPart === "legsOnly") && (
        <g id="layer-character-legs">
          {/* Cáº¦U Ná»I KHá»šP HÃ”NG & ÄÃY THÃ‚N: Ná»‘i liá»n nÃ©t tá»« cuá»‘i thÃ¢n qua hÃ´ng Ä‘áº¿n 2 chÃ¢n, khÃ´ng há»Ÿ */}
          <line
            x1={leftHipJoint.x}
            y1={hip.y}
            x2={rightHipJoint.x}
            y2={hip.y}
            stroke="#18181B"
            strokeWidth={10}
            strokeLinecap="round"
          />
          <circle cx={0} cy={hip.y} r={5} fill="#18181B" />

          {/* XÆ¯Æ NG CHáº¬U Ká»¸ THUáº¬T (CHá»ˆ HIá»‚N THá»Š TRONG DEBUG MODE) */}
          {isDebug && (
            <line
              x1={leftHipJoint.x}
              y1={hip.y}
              x2={rightHipJoint.x}
              y2={hip.y}
              stroke="#EF4444"
              strokeWidth={4}
              strokeDasharray="4 2"
              strokeLinecap="round"
            />
          )}

          {/* CHÃ‚N TRÃI */}
          <g id="left-hip-cluster" transform={`translate(${hipJointLeftX}, ${leftHipJoint.y})`}>
            {/* ÄÃ¹i trÃ¡i: Xoay quanh khá»›p hÃ´ng (0, 0) */}
            <g id="bone-left-thigh" transform={`rotate(${leftThighAngle})`}>
              {parts?.leftThigh ? (
                parts.leftThigh
              ) : (
                <g id="default-left-thigh">
                  {isDebug && <circle cx={0} cy={0} r={6} fill="#EF4444" />}
                  <line
                    x1={0}
                    y1={0}
                    x2={0}
                    y2={thighLength}
                    stroke="#18181B"
                    strokeWidth={10}
                    strokeLinecap="round"
                    strokeLinejoin="round"
                  />
                </g>
              )}

              {/* Khá»›p Ä‘áº§u gá»‘i (Left Knee Joint): Náº±m chÃ­nh xÃ¡c táº¡i Ä‘áº§u mÃºt Ä‘Ã¹i (0, thighLength) */}
              <g id="left-knee-cluster" transform={`translate(0, ${thighLength})`}>
                {/* Cáº³ng chÃ¢n trÃ¡i: Xoay cá»¥c bá»™ quanh Ä‘áº§u gá»‘i */}
                <g id="bone-left-calf" transform={`rotate(${leftCalfLocalAngle})`}>

import React from "react";
import { useCurrentFrame, useVideoConfig, spring, interpolate } from "remotion";
import { Point2D } from "../../types/character";
import { DynamicLineStyle } from "../../types/sceneGraph";

export interface DynamicLineProps {
  /**
   * Äáº§u mÃºt gá»‘c (tá»a Ä‘á»™ tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i trÃªn canvas 2K).
   */
  start: Point2D;
  /**
   * Äáº§u mÃºt Ä‘Ã­ch.
   */
  end: Point2D;
  /**
   * Kiá»ƒu hÃ¬nh thÃ¡i Ä‘Æ°á»ng line.
   */
  style: DynamicLineStyle;
  /**
   * MÃ u sáº¯c chÃ­nh.
   */
  color?: string;
  strokeWidth?: number;
  /**
   * CÃ³ dÃ¹ng spring animation Ä‘á»ƒ tháº£ dáº§n xuá»‘ng khÃ´ng (cho dÃ¢y tÆ¡ nhá»‡n).
   */
  animated?: boolean;
  /**
   * Frame báº¯t Ä‘áº§u animation (relative to composition).
   */
  animationDelay?: number;
  /**
   * Cho kiá»ƒu "light": gÃ³c má»Ÿ hÃ¬nh chÃ³p.
   */
  coneAngle?: number;
  /**
   * Cho kiá»ƒu "light": chiá»u dÃ i hÃ¬nh chÃ³p.
   */
  coneLength?: number;
}

/**
 * DynamicLine: ÄÆ°á»ng line SVG Ä‘á»™ng vá»›i Ä‘áº§u mÃºt trá»±c tiáº¿p bÃ¡m theo tá»a Ä‘á»™ anchor.
 *
 * Há»— trá»£ 5 kiá»ƒu hiá»ƒn thá»‹:
 * 1. "silk"    â€” Sá»£i tÆ¡ nhá»‡n (catenary curve, spring dangle)
 * 2. "light"   â€” HÃ¬nh chÃ³p sÃ¡ng tá»« Ä‘Ã¨n bÃ n
 * 3. "pointer" â€” ÄÆ°á»ng chá»‰ dáº«n tÃªn (tá»« nhÃ£n Ä‘áº¿n váº­t thá»ƒ)
 * 4. "dashed"  â€” ÄÆ°á»ng nÃ©t Ä‘á»©t
 * 5. "glow"    â€” ÄÆ°á»ng phÃ¡t sÃ¡ng neon
 */
export const DynamicLine: React.FC<DynamicLineProps> = ({
  start,
  end,
  style = "pointer",
  color,
  strokeWidth,
  animated = false,
  animationDelay = 0,
  coneAngle = 40,
  coneLength = 300,
}) => {
  const frame = useCurrentFrame();
  const { fps } = useVideoConfig();

  // =========================================================================
  // SPRING ANIMATION cho Ä‘áº§u mÃºt Ä‘Ã­ch (tháº£ dÃ¢y tÆ¡ nhá»‡n)
  // =========================================================================
  let effectiveEnd = end;
  if (animated) {
    const progress = spring({
      frame: Math.max(0, frame - animationDelay),
      fps,
      config: {
        damping: 6,
        stiffness: 40,
        mass: 1.2,
      },
    });
    effectiveEnd = {
      x: interpolate(progress, [0, 1], [start.x, end.x]),
      y: interpolate(progress, [0, 1], [start.y, end.y]),
    };
  }

  switch (style) {
    case "silk":
      return <SpiderSilkLine start={start} end={effectiveEnd} color={color} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} />;
    case "light":
      return <LightCone origin={start} coneAngle={coneAngle} coneLength={coneLength} />;
    case "pointer":
      return <PointerLine start={start} end={effectiveEnd} color={color} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} />;
    case "dashed":
      return <DashedLine start={start} end={effectiveEnd} color={color} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} />;
    case "glow":
      return <GlowLine start={start} end={effectiveEnd} color={color} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} />;
    default:
      return <PointerLine start={start} end={effectiveEnd} color={color} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} />;
  }
};

// =========================================================================
// 1. Sá»¢I TÆ  NHá»†N (Catenary Curve tá»« tráº§n nhÃ  xuá»‘ng váº­t thá»ƒ)
// =========================================================================
const SpiderSilkLine: React.FC<{
  start: Point2D;
  end: Point2D;
  color?: string;
  strokeWidth?: number;
}> = ({ start, end, color = "#888888", strokeWidth = 3 }) => {
  // Catenary sag dá»±a trÃªn khoáº£ng cÃ¡ch dá»c
  const sagAmount = Math.abs(end.y - start.y) * 0.06;
  const midX = (start.x + end.x) / 2 + sagAmount * 0.3;
  const midY = (start.y + end.y) / 2;

  const d = `M ${start.x} ${start.y} Q ${midX} ${midY} ${end.x} ${end.y}`;

  return (
    <g id="spider-silk-line">
      <path
        d={d}
        fill="none"
        stroke={color}
        strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
        strokeLinecap="round"
        opacity={0.75}
      />
      {/* NÃºt nhá» á»Ÿ Ä‘áº§u treo */}
      <circle cx={start.x} cy={start.y} r={4} fill={color} opacity={0.6} />
    </g>
  );
};

// =========================================================================
// 2. HÃŒNH CHÃ“P SÃNG Tá»ª ÄÃˆN BÃ€N (Triangular light cone)
// =========================================================================
const LightCone: React.FC<{
  origin: Point2D;
  coneAngle?: number;
  coneLength?: number;
}> = ({ origin, coneAngle = 40, coneLength = 300 }) => {
  // Tia sÃ¡ng chiáº¿u xuá»‘ng (angle = 90 deg = tháº³ng xuá»‘ng)
  const halfAngleRad = ((coneAngle / 2) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const baseAngleRad = (90 * Math.PI) / 180; // chiáº¿u tháº³ng xuá»‘ng

  const leftX = origin.x + Math.cos(baseAngleRad - halfAngleRad) * coneLength;
  const leftY = origin.y + Math.sin(baseAngleRad - halfAngleRad) * coneLength;
  const rightX = origin.x + Math.cos(baseAngleRad + halfAngleRad) * coneLength;
  const rightY = origin.y + Math.sin(baseAngleRad + halfAngleRad) * coneLength;

  return (
    <g id="light-cone">
      <defs>
        <linearGradient id="light-cone-gradient" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="0%" y2="100%">
          <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#FFEAA7" stopOpacity={0.7} />
          <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#FFEAA7" stopOpacity={0.0} />
        </linearGradient>
      </defs>
      <polygon
        points={`${origin.x},${origin.y} ${leftX},${leftY} ${rightX},${rightY}`}
        fill="url(#light-cone-gradient)"
        opacity={0.5}
      />
    </g>
  );
};

// =========================================================================
// 3. ÄÆ¯á»œNG CHá»ˆ DáºªN TÃŠN (Pointer line tá»« nhÃ£n Ä‘áº¿n váº­t thá»ƒ)
// =========================================================================
const PointerLine: React.FC<{
  start: Point2D;
  end: Point2D;
  color?: string;
  strokeWidth?: number;
}> = ({ start, end, color = "#141414", strokeWidth = 5 }) => {
  return (
    <g id="pointer-line">
      <line
        x1={start.x}
        y1={start.y}
        x2={end.x}
        y2={end.y}
        stroke={color}
        strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
        strokeLinecap="round"
      />
      {/* NÃºt trÃ²n á»Ÿ Ä‘iá»ƒm gáº¯n (pivot) */}
      <circle cx={end.x} cy={end.y} r={7} fill="#FFFFFF" stroke={color} strokeWidth={4} />
    </g>
  );
};

// =========================================================================
// 4. ÄÆ¯á»œNG NÃ‰T Äá»¨T (Dashed line)
// =========================================================================
const DashedLine: React.FC<{
  start: Point2D;
  end: Point2D;
  color?: string;
  strokeWidth?: number;
}> = ({ start, end, color = "#666666", strokeWidth = 4 }) => {
  return (
    <line
      x1={start.x}
      y1={start.y}
      x2={end.x}
      y2={end.y}
      stroke={color}
      strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
      strokeDasharray="12 8"
      strokeLinecap="round"
    />
  );
};

// =========================================================================
// 5. ÄÆ¯á»œNG PHÃT SÃNG NEON (Glow line)
// =========================================================================
const GlowLine: React.FC<{
  start: Point2D;
  end: Point2D;
  color?: string;
  strokeWidth?: number;
}> = ({ start, end, color = "#00E5FF", strokeWidth = 4 }) => {
  const glowId = `glow-filter-${Math.round(start.x)}-${Math.round(start.y)}`;
  return (
    <g id="glow-line">
      <defs>
        <filter id={glowId}>
          <feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="6" result="blur" />
          <feMerge>
            <feMergeNode in="blur" />
            <feMergeNode in="SourceGraphic" />
          </feMerge>
        </filter>
      </defs>
      <line
        x1={start.x}
        y1={start.y}
        x2={end.x}
        y2={end.y}
        stroke={color}
        strokeWidth={strokeWidth + 4}
        strokeLinecap="round"
        filter={`url(#${glowId})`}
        opacity={0.6}
      />
      <line
        x1={start.x}
        y1={start.y}
        x2={end.x}
        y2={end.y}
        stroke={color}
        strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
        strokeLinecap="round"
      />
    </g>
  );
};

// =========================================================================
// UTILITY: Giáº£i quyáº¿t tham chiáº¿u anchor thÃ nh tá»a Ä‘á»™ tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i
// =========================================================================

export interface AnchorResolverContext {
  propAnchors: Record<string, { x: number; y: number; leftGrip: Point2D; rightGrip: Point2D; surfaceDecal: Point2D }>;
  characterAnchors: Record<string, Point2D>;
  canvasHeight: number;
}

/**
 * Giáº£i quyáº¿t chuá»—i tham chiáº¿u anchor thÃ nh tá»a Ä‘á»™ Point2D.
 * Format: "prop:{propId}:{port}" | "character:{anchorName}" | "ceiling:{x}" | "floor:{x}"
 */
export function resolveAnchorRef(
  ref: string,
  context: AnchorResolverContext
): Point2D {
  const parts = ref.split(":");

  if (parts[0] === "prop" && parts.length >= 3) {
    const propId = parts[1];
    const port = parts[2];
    const propData = context.propAnchors[propId];
    if (!propData) return { x: 0, y: 0 };
    switch (port) {
      case "leftGrip":
        return propData.leftGrip;
      case "rightGrip":
        return propData.rightGrip;
      case "surfaceDecal":
        return propData.surfaceDecal;
      case "center":
        return { x: propData.x, y: propData.y };
      default:
        return { x: propData.x, y: propData.y };
    }
  }

  if (parts[0] === "character" && parts.length >= 2) {
    const anchorName = parts[1];
    return context.characterAnchors[anchorName] || { x: 0, y: 0 };
  }

  if (parts[0] === "ceiling") {
    const xPos = parts.length >= 2 ? parseFloat(parts[1]) : 1280;
    return { x: xPos, y: 0 };
  }

  if (parts[0] === "floor") {
    const xPos = parts.length >= 2 ? parseFloat(parts[1]) : 1280;
    return { x: xPos, y: context.canvasHeight };
  }

  return { x: 0, y: 0 };
}

  frontArtwork?: React.ReactNode;

/**
 * Desk Wrapper Component (Figma-to-React Standard)
 * Äáº£m báº£o luáº­t Z-Index: tÃ¡ch lá»›p chÃ¢n sau (backLegs) vÃ  máº·t bÃ n + chÃ¢n trÆ°á»›c (tableTopAndFrontLegs).
 */
export const Desk: React.FC<DeskProps> = ({
  x,
  y,
  width = 720,
  height = 240,
  scale = 1,
  layerPart = "all",
  onSurfaceAnchorComputed,
  backLegsArtwork,
  frontArtwork,
}) => {
  const deskTopY = -height;

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (onSurfaceAnchorComputed) {
      onSurfaceAnchorComputed({
        x,
        y: y + deskTopY * scale,
      });
    }
  }, [x, y, scale, height]);

  return (
    <g
      id="desk-wrapper"
      transform={`translate(${x}, ${y}) scale(${scale})`}
      data-prop-type="desk"
    >
      {/* =========================================================================

      {(layerPart === "all" || layerPart === "backLegs") && (
        <g id="desk-back-legs">
          {backLegsArtwork ? (
            backLegsArtwork
          ) : (
            /* Báº N HÃƒY PASTE MÃƒ SVG CHÃ‚N SAU Cá»¦A BÃ€N Tá»ª FIGMA VÃ€O ÄÃ‚Y */
            /* <!-- PASTE REAL SVG HERE --> */

          2. Máº¶T BÃ€N & CHÃ‚N TRÆ¯á»šC (Render á»Ÿ MidgroundFrontLayer - Z-Index ÄÃˆ LÃŠN nhÃ¢n váº­t)
         ========================================================================= */}
      {(layerPart === "all" || layerPart === "tableTopAndFrontLegs") && (
        <g id="desk-tabletop-and-front-legs">
          {frontArtwork ? (
            frontArtwork
          ) : (
            /* Báº N HÃƒY PASTE MÃƒ SVG Máº¶T BÃ€N VÃ€ CHÃ‚N TRÆ¯á»šC Tá»ª FIGMA VÃ€O ÄÃ‚Y */
            /* <!-- PASTE REAL SVG HERE --> */

  );
};
