==================== FILE: C:\Users\ASUS\Downloads\TOOL_2D_ANIM_API_FIX_1\TOOL_2D_ANIM\web-studio\src\remotion\components\props\DynamicProp.tsx ====================
import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { staticFile, interpolate } from "remotion";
import { Point2D, PropAnchorPorts } from "../../types/character";
import { computeAutoAnchorPorts, extractSvgDimensions, SvgDimensionInfo } from "../../lib/autoAnchoring";
import { AlphaConfig } from "../../types/sceneGraph";
import { normalizePropAssetId } from "../../utils/propResolver";
import { GaugeMeter } from "./GaugeMeter";

export interface DynamicPropProps {
  id: string; // TÃªn file trong public/assets/props/${id}.svg
  x: number;
  y: number;
  scale?: number;
  scaleX?: number;
  scaleY?: number;
  rotation?: number;
  opacity?: number;
  alphaConfig?: AlphaConfig;
  frame?: number;
  sceneDurationInFrames?: number;
  anchorPoint?: "bottomCenter" | "center" | "topCenter";
  pivotPoint?: "center" | "bottomCenter" | "topCenter" | "topLeft" | "custom";
  pivotX?: number;
  pivotY?: number;
  label?: string;
  showPointerCallout?: boolean;
  foliageSway?: boolean;
  needleAngle?: number; // Cho gauge-meter (-90 Ä‘áº¿n +90 deg)
  defaultDimensions?: SvgDimensionInfo;
  customSvgContent?: string;
  onAnchorsComputed?: (anchors: PropAnchorPorts) => void;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
}

const KNOWN_PROP_DIMENSIONS: Record<string, SvgDimensionInfo> = {
  "glass-jar": { width: 320, height: 420 },
  "spider": { width: 320, height: 240 },
  "sofa": { width: 720, height: 500 },
  "desk": { width: 1100, height: 400 },
  "desk-front": { width: 1100, height: 400 },
  "desk-back": { width: 1100, height: 400 },
  "gauge-meter": { width: 520, height: 280 },
  "arrow-indicator": { width: 400, height: 400 },
  "label-sticker": { width: 300, height: 160 },
  "metabolic-chart": { width: 400, height: 400 },
  "metabolism-chart": { width: 400, height: 400 },
  "chart": { width: 400, height: 400 },
  "window": { width: 560, height: 460 },
  "desk-lamp": { width: 390, height: 300 },
  "potted-plant": { width: 280, height: 350 },
  "laptop": { width: 340, height: 260 },
  "coffee-cup": { width: 220, height: 220 },
  "cup": { width: 220, height: 220 },
  "alarm-clock": { width: 240, height: 240 },
  "clock": { width: 240, height: 240 },
  "book": { width: 240, height: 240 },
  "phone": { width: 160, height: 280 },
  "chair": { width: 300, height: 400 },
  "table": { width: 500, height: 350 },
  "door": { width: 280, height: 600 },
  "tv": { width: 480, height: 360 },
  "pizza": { width: 260, height: 260 },
  "apple": { width: 240, height: 240 },
  "guitar": { width: 260, height: 500 },
  "cat": { width: 300, height: 280 },
  "tree": { width: 450, height: 600 },
  "balloon": { width: 200, height: 320 },
  "trophy": { width: 280, height: 340 },
  "star": { width: 240, height: 240 },
  "heart": { width: 240, height: 240 },
  "whiteboard": { width: 600, height: 400 },
  "bush": { width: 400, height: 240 },
  "car": { width: 500, height: 260 },
  "environment": { width: 300, height: 300 },
};

/**
 * DynamicProp: Bá»™ Náº¡p TÃ i NguyÃªn Äá»™ng & Tá»± Äá»™ng Gáº¯n Neo (100% Automation & VFX Ready)
 * 
 * QUY CHUáº¨N ORIGIN = BOTTOM-CENTER (Gá»‘c tá»a Ä‘á»™ táº¡i ChÃ­nh Giá»¯a ÄÃ¡y):
 * - Äiá»ƒm (x, y) truyá»n vÃ o lÃ  Ä‘iá»ƒm chÃ­nh giá»¯a cáº¡nh Ä‘Ã¡y cá»§a váº­t thá»ƒ.
 * - Há»— trá»£ Custom Pivot Point, Alpha Shaders vÃ  Foliage Sway tuáº§n hoÃ n.
 */
export const DynamicProp: React.FC<DynamicPropProps> = ({
  id,
  x,
  y,
  scale = 1,
  scaleX = 1,
  scaleY = 1,
  rotation = 0,
  opacity = 1,
  alphaConfig,
  frame = 0,
  sceneDurationInFrames = 120,
  anchorPoint,
  pivotPoint = "bottomCenter",
  pivotX,
  pivotY,
  label,
  showPointerCallout = false,
  foliageSway = false,
  needleAngle,
  defaultDimensions = { width: 240, height: 280 },
  customSvgContent,
  onAnchorsComputed,
  children,
}) => {
  const canonicalId = normalizePropAssetId(id);
  const isKnown = Boolean(KNOWN_PROP_DIMENSIONS[canonicalId] || KNOWN_PROP_DIMENSIONS[id]);
  const initialDims = customSvgContent
    ? defaultDimensions
    : KNOWN_PROP_DIMENSIONS[canonicalId] || KNOWN_PROP_DIMENSIONS[id] || defaultDimensions;
  const [dimensions, setDimensions] = useState<SvgDimensionInfo>(initialDims);
  const [hasLoadError, setHasLoadError] = useState(!isKnown && !customSvgContent);

  const baseAssetPath = staticFile(`assets/props/${canonicalId}.svg`);
  const assetUrl = `${baseAssetPath}?v=20261107_2k`;

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;

    if (customSvgContent) {
      const dims = extractSvgDimensions(customSvgContent);
      setDimensions(dims);
      return;
    }

    fetch(assetUrl)
      .then((res) => {
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y file ${canonicalId}.svg trong public/assets/props/`);
        return res.text();
      })
      .then((svgText) => {
        if (!isMounted) return;
        const dims = extractSvgDimensions(svgText);
        setDimensions(dims);
        setHasLoadError(false);
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        console.warn(`[DynamicProp] DÃ¹ng kÃ­ch thÆ°á»›c máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh cho prop '${canonicalId}':`, err.message);
        if (isMounted) {
          setHasLoadError(true);
        }
      });

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, [canonicalId, customSvgContent, assetUrl]);

  const effectiveAnchor = anchorPoint || (pivotPoint === "center" ? "center" : "bottomCenter");

  useEffect(() => {
    if (onAnchorsComputed) {
      const anchors = computeAutoAnchorPorts(dimensions, {
        x,
        y,
        scale,
        rotationDeg: rotation,
        anchorPoint: effectiveAnchor === "center" ? "center" : "bottomCenter",
      });
      onAnchorsComputed(anchors);
    }
  }, [dimensions, x, y, scale, rotation, effectiveAnchor]);

  const { width, height } = dimensions;
  const isGauge = canonicalId === "gauge-meter";
  const isSpider = canonicalId === "spider";
  const isGlassJar = canonicalId === "glass-jar";

  // Vá»‹ trÃ­ Y cá»§a áº£nh vÃ  cÃ¡c pháº§n tá»­ con theo Anchor/Origin:
  // "center" (Mid-Center): TÃ¢m hÃ¬nh há»c cá»§a váº­t thá»ƒ Ä‘áº·t táº¡i (x, y) - dÃ¹ng cho Cáº£nh cáº­n, MÃ n hÃ¬nh, Infographics
  // "bottomCenter": ÄÃ¡y váº­t thá»ƒ Ä‘áº·t táº¡i y = 0 - dÃ¹ng khi Ä‘áº·t trÃªn sÃ n hoáº·c máº·t bÃ n
  let imageY = -height;
  let needleCenterY = 0;
  let childrenY = -Math.round(height * 0.22);
  let labelY = -Math.round(height * 0.5);

  if (effectiveAnchor === "center") {
    if (isGauge) {
      // Máº·t Ä‘á»“ng há»“: vÃ²m bÃ¡n nguyá»‡t tá»« -220 Ä‘áº¿n 0, viewBox -240 Ä‘áº¿n +40 (chiá»u cao 280)
      // TÃ¢m hÃ¬nh há»c náº±m táº¡i khoáº£ng y = -90 trong viewBox.
      // CÄƒn tÃ¢m: Ä‘áº©y toÃ n bá»™ xuá»‘ng 90px Ä‘á»ƒ tÃ¢m vÃ²m náº±m chÃ­nh xÃ¡c táº¡i y = 0.
      imageY = -150;
      needleCenterY = 90;
      labelY = 165;
    } else if (isSpider) {
      // Con nhá»‡n: viewBox -160 -120 320 240, tÃ¢m táº¡i 0, 0
      imageY = -height / 2;
      childrenY = 0;
      labelY = 0;
    } else {
      // CÃ¡c váº­t thá»ƒ chuáº©n (nhÆ° lá» thá»§y tinh glass-jar, sofa, Ä‘Ã¨n bÃ n, v.v.)
      imageY = -height / 2;
      // Nhá»‡n náº±m trong thÃ¢n lá» thá»§y tinh táº¡i vá»‹ trÃ­ cÃ¢n Ä‘á»‘i
      childrenY = isGlassJar ? 100 : 0;
      // NhÃ£n dÃ¡n "TÃM" náº±m á»Ÿ 1/3 phÃ­a trÃªn thÃ¢n lá», trÃªn Ä‘áº§u con nhá»‡n
      labelY = isGlassJar ? -Math.round(height * 0.22) : -Math.round(height * 0.25);
    }
  } else {
    // bottomCenter
    if (isGauge) {
      imageY = -240;
      needleCenterY = 0;
      labelY = 75;
    } else if (isSpider) {
      imageY = -height / 2;
      childrenY = 0;
      labelY = 0;
    } else {
      imageY = -height;
      childrenY = isGlassJar ? -85 : -Math.round(height * 0.22);
      labelY = isGlassJar ? -Math.round(height * 0.72) : -Math.round(height * 0.5);
    }
  }

  // Äiá»ƒm neo Pivot Point (DÃ¹ng cho phÃ©p xoay rotate quanh khá»›p tÃ¹y chá»n)
  let px = 0;
  let py = 0;
  switch (pivotPoint) {
    case "bottomCenter":
      px = 0;
      py = effectiveAnchor === "center" ? height / 2 : 0;
      break;
    case "center":
      px = 0;
      py = effectiveAnchor === "center" ? 0 : (isGauge ? 0 : -height / 2);
      break;
    case "topCenter":
      px = 0;
      py = effectiveAnchor === "center" ? -height / 2 : -height;
      break;
    case "topLeft":
      px = -width / 2;
      py = effectiveAnchor === "center" ? -height / 2 : -height;
      break;
    case "custom":
      px = pivotX ?? 0;
      py = pivotY ?? 0;
      break;
  }

  // Äá»™ má» Alpha biáº¿n thiÃªn Ä‘á»™ng
  let effectiveOpacity = opacity;
  if (alphaConfig) {
    const { fadeInFrames, fadeOutFrames, pulseAlpha, minAlpha = 0.4, maxAlpha = 1.0 } = alphaConfig;
    if (fadeInFrames && fadeInFrames > 0) {
      const p = interpolate(frame, [0, fadeInFrames], [0, 1], {
        extrapolateLeft: "clamp",
        extrapolateRight: "clamp",
      });
      effectiveOpacity *= p;
    }
    if (fadeOutFrames && fadeOutFrames > 0 && sceneDurationInFrames > fadeOutFrames) {
      const fadeStart = sceneDurationInFrames - fadeOutFrames;
      if (frame >= fadeStart) {
        const p = interpolate(frame, [fadeStart, sceneDurationInFrames], [1, 0], {
          extrapolateLeft: "clamp",
          extrapolateRight: "clamp",
        });
        effectiveOpacity *= p;
      }
    }
    if (pulseAlpha) {
      const pulse = minAlpha + (maxAlpha - minAlpha) * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(frame * 0.08));
      effectiveOpacity *= pulse;
    }
  }

  // Äung Ä‘Æ°a cÃ¢y cá» (Foliage Sway) CHá»ˆ KHI CÃ“ HIá»†U á»¨NG THá»œI TIáº¾T NGOÃ€I TRá»œI ÄÆ¯á»¢C Báº¬T (Váº­t tÄ©nh giá»¯ tÄ©nh 100%)
  const isPlant = foliageSway && (canonicalId === "potted-plant" || canonicalId === "tree" || canonicalId === "bush");
  const plantSwayDeg = isPlant ? Math.sin(frame * 0.045 + (x % 10)) * 3.5 : 0;

  const labelBoxWidth = isGauge ? 280 : 150;
  const labelBoxHeight = isGauge ? 60 : 56;

  // Biáº¿n Ä‘á»•i SVG tá»•ng há»£p cÃ³ xÃ©t trá»¥c quay Pivot
  const sx = scale * scaleX;
  const sy = scale * scaleY;
  const transformStr = `translate(${x}, ${y}) translate(${px}, ${py}) rotate(${rotation}) scale(${sx}, ${sy}) skewX(${plantSwayDeg}) translate(${-px}, ${-py})`;

  return (
    <g
      id={`dynamic-prop-${id}`}
      transform={transformStr}
      opacity={Math.max(0, Math.min(1, effectiveOpacity))}
      data-prop-id={id}
    >
      {/* 1. Náº P HÃŒNH áº¢NH SVG Äá»˜NG HOáº¶C COMPONENT Tá»° THÃ‚N */}
      <g id={`artwork-container-${id}`}>
        {isGauge ? (
          <GaugeMeter
            x={0}
            y={needleCenterY}
            scale={1}
            needleAngle={needleAngle !== undefined ? needleAngle : -35}
            label={label || "TRAO Äá»”I CHáº¤T: THáº¤P"}
          />
        ) : !hasLoadError ? (
          <image
            href={assetUrl}
            xlinkHref={assetUrl}
            x={-width / 2}
            y={imageY}
            width={width}
            height={height}
            preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet"
            onError={() => setHasLoadError(true)}
          />
        ) : null}
      </g>

      {/* 1.5. KIM Äá»’NG Há»’ Äá»˜NG QUAY QUANH TRá»¤C (Náº¾U KHÃ”NG PHáº¢I GAUGE-METER VÃ€ CÃ“ KIM XOAY) */}
      {!isGauge && needleAngle !== undefined && (
        <g transform={`translate(0, ${needleCenterY}) rotate(${needleAngle}, 0, 0)`}>
          <polygon points="-5,0 0,-185 5,0" fill="#141414" />
          <circle cx={0} cy={0} r={16} fill="#141414" />
          <circle cx={0} cy={0} r={5} fill="#FFFFFF" />
        </g>
      )}

      {/* 2. Váº¬T THá»‚ CON BÃŠN TRONG (ÄÆ¯á»¢C CLIP KHá»šP TRONG LÃ’NG CONTAINER Báº¢O TOÃ€N HÃŒNH Há»ŒC) */}
      <defs>
        {/* LÃ²ng trong cá»§a container Ä‘á»ƒ cáº¯t gá»n váº­t thá»ƒ con, tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i khÃ´ng thÃ² ra ngoÃ i Ä‘Ã¡y hay thÃ nh váº­t chá»©a */}
        <clipPath id={`container-inner-clip-${id}`}>
          <rect
            x={-Math.round(width * 0.40)}
            y={effectiveAnchor === "center" ? -Math.round(height * 0.42) : -Math.round(height * 0.88)}
            width={Math.round(width * 0.80)}
            height={Math.round(height * 0.82)}
            rx={28}
            ry={28}
          />
        </clipPath>
      </defs>

      {children && (
        <g
          id={`prop-children-${id}`}
          clipPath={`url(#container-inner-clip-${id})`}
        >
          <g transform={`translate(0, ${childrenY})`}>
            {children}
          </g>
        </g>
      )}

      {/* 3. Tá»° Äá»˜NG Gáº®N NHÃƒN DÃN (CHO CÃC Äáº O Cá»¤ KHÃ”NG PHáº¢I GAUGE-METER VÃŒ GAUGE-METER ÄÃƒ CÃ“ NHÃƒN RIÃŠNG) */}
      {label && !isGauge && !showPointerCallout && (
        <g id={`prop-label-decal-${id}`} transform={`translate(0, ${labelY})`}>
          <rect
            x={-labelBoxWidth / 2}
            y={-labelBoxHeight / 2}
            width={labelBoxWidth}
            height={labelBoxHeight}
            rx={14}
            fill="#FFFFFF"
            stroke="#141414"
            strokeWidth={5}
          />
          <text
            x={0}
            y={isGauge ? 10 : 12}
            textAnchor="middle"
            fill={isGauge ? "#141414" : "#E11D48"}
            fontSize={isGauge ? 30 : 36}
            fontWeight="900"
            fontFamily="var(--font-be-vietnam-pro), sans-serif"
          >
            {label}
          </text>
        </g>
      )}

      {/* 4. THANH CALLOUT TRá»Ž */}
      {label && showPointerCallout && (
        <g id={`prop-pointer-callout-${id}`} transform={`translate(${Math.round(width * 0.45)}, ${-Math.round(height * 0.65)})`}>
          <circle cx={0} cy={0} r={7} fill="#141414" />
          <line x1={0} y1={0} x2={55} y2={-10} stroke="#141414" strokeWidth={5} strokeLinecap="round" />

          <g transform="translate(55, -45)">
            <rect
              x={0}
              y={0}
              width={150}
              height={64}
              rx={14}
              fill="#FFFFFF"
              stroke="#141414"
              strokeWidth={6}
            />
            <text
              x={75}
              y={44}

import React from "react";

export interface GaugeMeterProps {

/**
 * GaugeMeter: Component Äá»“ng há»“ Ä‘o Trao Ä‘á»•i cháº¥t & Chá»‰ sá»‘ Infographic hoÃ n chá»‰nh (2K Vector SVG)
 * Tá»± thÃ¢n chá»©a toÃ n bá»™ vÃ²m mÃ u (Xanh lÃ¡ - VÃ ng - Äá»), váº¡ch chia Ä‘á»™, kim quay mÆ°á»£t mÃ  vÃ  nhÃ£n chá»¯.
 * 100% vector SVG, khÃ´ng phá»¥ thuá»™c fetch file bÃªn ngoÃ i, báº£o Ä‘áº£m khÃ´ng bao giá» vá»¡ hÃ¬nh hoáº·c lá»—i placeholder.

import React from "react";
import { PropAnchorPorts, Point2D } from "../../types/character";

export interface GlassJarProps {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  scale?: number;
  rotation?: number;
  label?: string;
  showPointerCallout?: boolean;
  onAnchorsComputed?: (anchors: PropAnchorPorts) => void;
  artwork?: React.ReactNode;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
}

/**
 * GlassJar Wrapper Component (Figma-to-React Standard & Bottom-Center Origin)
 * 
 * QUY CHUáº¨N ORIGIN = BOTTOM-CENTER:
 * - Äiá»ƒm (x, y) lÃ  Ä‘Ã¡y chÃ­nh giá»¯a chiáº¿c lá» (cháº¡m sÃ n / cháº¡m máº·t bÃ n).
 * - NhÃ£n dÃ¡n "TÃM" neo táº¡i 1/3 phÃ­a trÃªn thÃ¢n lá» (y = -height * 0.72), náº±m trÃªn con nhá»‡n.
 * - Con nhá»‡n á»Ÿ Ä‘Ã¡y lá» (y = -height * 0.22).
 */
export const GlassJar: React.FC<GlassJarProps> = ({
  x,
  y,
  scale = 1,
  rotation = 0,
  label,
  showPointerCallout = false,
  onAnchorsComputed,
  artwork,
  children,
}) => {
  const jarWidth = 320;
  const jarHeight = 420;

  // TÃ­nh toÃ¡n Anchor Ports theo chuáº©n Bottom-Center
  const leftGrip: Point2D = { x: x - (jarWidth * 0.42) * scale, y: y - (jarHeight * 0.45) * scale };
  const rightGrip: Point2D = { x: x + (jarWidth * 0.42) * scale, y: y - (jarHeight * 0.45) * scale };
  const surfaceDecal: Point2D = { x, y: y - (jarHeight * 0.72) * scale };
  const lidCenter: Point2D = { x, y: y - jarHeight * scale };
  const baseCenter: Point2D = { x, y };

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (onAnchorsComputed) {
      onAnchorsComputed({
        leftGrip,
        rightGrip,
        surfaceDecal,
        lidCenter,
        baseCenter,
      });
    }
  }, [x, y, scale, rotation]);

  return (
    <g
      id="glass-jar-wrapper"
      transform={`translate(${x}, ${y}) scale(${scale}) rotate(${rotation})`}
      data-prop-type="glassJar"
    >
      <g id="figma-glass-jar-artwork">
        {artwork || null}
      </g>

      {/* SLOT Váº¬T THá»‚ BÃŠN TRONG Lá»Œ (Con nhá»‡n bÃ¡m Ä‘Ã¡y lá») */}
      {children && (
        <g id="jar-interior-contents" transform={`translate(0, ${-Math.round(jarHeight * 0.22)})`}>
          {children}
        </g>
      )}

      {/* NHÃƒN DÃN Äáº O Cá»¤ (Náº±m á»Ÿ 1/3 trÃªn thÃ¢n lá», khÃ´ng che nhá»‡n) */}
      {label && !showPointerCallout && (
        <g id="jar-surface-decal" transform={`translate(0, ${-Math.round(jarHeight * 0.72)})`}>
          <rect
            x={-75}
            y={-28}
            width={150}
            height={56}
            rx={14}
            fill="#FFFFFF"
            stroke="#141414"
            strokeWidth={5}
          />
          <text
            x={0}
            y={12}
            textAnchor="middle"
            fill="#E11D48"
            fontSize={36}
            fontWeight="900"
            fontFamily="var(--font-be-vietnam-pro), sans-serif"
          >
            {label}
          </text>
        </g>
      )}

      {/* THANH CALLOUT TRá»Ž */}
      {label && showPointerCallout && (
        <g id="jar-pointer-callout" transform={`translate(${Math.round(jarWidth * 0.45)}, ${-Math.round(jarHeight * 0.65)})`}>
          <circle cx={0} cy={0} r={7} fill="#141414" />
          <line x1={0} y1={0} x2={55} y2={-10} stroke="#141414" strokeWidth={5} strokeLinecap="round" />

          <g transform="translate(55, -45)">
            <rect
              x={0}
              y={0}
              width={150}
              height={64}
              rx={14}
              fill="#FFFFFF"
              stroke="#141414"
              strokeWidth={6}
            />
            <text
              x={75}
              y={44}
              textAnchor="middle"
              fill="#E11D48"
              fontSize={36}
              fontWeight="900"
              fontFamily="var(--font-be-vietnam-pro), sans-serif"
            >
              {label}
            </text>
          </g>
        </g>
      )}
    </g>
  );
};

import React from "react";
import { interpolate, Easing } from "remotion";

export interface MetabolicClockProps {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  scale?: number;
  frame: number;
  startFrame?: number;
  durationInFrames?: number;
}

/**
 * MetabolicClock: Minh há»a Ä‘á»“ng há»“ Ä‘o tá»‘c Ä‘á»™ trao Ä‘á»•i cháº¥t tá»‘i giáº£n & khoa há»c
 * - LÃ m chá»§ thá»ƒ trung tÃ¢m rÃµ rÃ ng, tá»· lá»‡ chuáº©n 2K
 * - Kim chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»™ng rÃµ rÃ ng tá»« má»©c bÃ¬nh thÆ°á»ng xuá»‘ng má»©c cá»±c tháº¥p
 * - KhÃ´ng cÃ³ trá»¥c hay Ä‘Æ°á»ng trang trÃ­ thá»«a thÃ£i
 */
export const MetabolicClock: React.FC<MetabolicClockProps> = ({
  x,
  y,
  scale = 1.6,
  frame,
  startFrame = 0,
  durationInFrames = 360,
}) => {
  // Kim chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»™ng mÆ°á»£t mÃ  tá»« -15 Ä‘á»™ xuá»‘ng -68 Ä‘á»™ (vÃ¹ng tiáº¿t kiá»‡m tá»‘i Ä‘a)
  const needleProgress = interpolate(
    frame - startFrame,
    [15, 110],
    [0, 1],
    {
      extrapolateLeft: "clamp",
      extrapolateRight: "clamp",
      easing: Easing.bezier(0.25, 0.1, 0.25, 1),
    }
  );

  const needleAngle = interpolate(needleProgress, [0, 1], [-15, -68]);

  return (
    <g id="metabolic-clock-wrapper" transform={`translate(${x}, ${y}) scale(${scale})`}>
      {/* 1. BÃ³ng Ä‘á»• ná»n sau Ä‘á»“ng há»“ */}

        strokeWidth={12}
        strokeLinejoin="round"
      />

      {/* 3. Ba cung mÃ u thá»ƒ hiá»‡n má»©c trao Ä‘á»•i cháº¥t */}
      <g stroke="#141414" strokeWidth={8} strokeLinejoin="round">
        {/* VÃ¹ng Xanh lÃ¡: Tháº¥p / Tiáº¿t kiá»‡m nÄƒng lÆ°á»£ng sinh tá»“n (-90Â° Ä‘áº¿n -30Â°) */}
        <path

        />

        {/* VÃ¹ng VÃ ng: Trung bÃ¬nh (-30Â° Ä‘áº¿n +30Â°) */}

import React from "react";
import { Point2D } from "../../types/character";

export interface SpeechBubbleProps {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  text: string;
  hasMusicNote?: boolean;
  tailAnchor?: Point2D;
  bubbleArtwork?: React.ReactNode;
}

/**
 * TÃ­nh toÃ¡n vá»‹ trÃ­ Speech Bubble chuáº©n xÃ¡c Ä‘á»ƒ KHÃ”NG BAO GIá»œ che máº·t hay Ä‘áº§u nhÃ¢n váº­t
 */
export function calculateSafeSpeechBubble(
  headCenter: Point2D,
  text: string,
  facing: "left" | "right" = "right",
  headRadius: number = 95
): {
  position: Point2D;
  tailAnchor: Point2D;
} {
  const width = Math.max(260, text.length * 44 + 80);
  const height = 130;

  // Äáº·t bong bÃ³ng cháº¿ch lÃªn phÃ­a trÆ°á»›c máº·t cá»§a nhÃ¢n váº­t, giá»¯ khoáº£ng Ä‘á»‡m an toÃ n >= 40px ngoÃ i vÃ²ng trÃ²n Ä‘áº§u
  const offsetX = facing === "left" ? -(headRadius + width / 2 + 30) : (headRadius + width / 2 + 30);
  const offsetY = -(headRadius + height / 2 + 20);

  const bubbleX = headCenter.x + offsetX;
  const bubbleY = headCenter.y + offsetY;

  // Äiá»ƒm gá»‘c miá»‡ng cá»§a nhÃ¢n váº­t trong há»‡ tá»a Ä‘á»™ cá»¥c bá»™ cá»§a bubble
  const mouthLocalX = facing === "left" ? width / 2 - 20 : -width / 2 + 20;
  const mouthLocalY = height / 2 + 65;

  return {
    position: { x: bubbleX, y: bubbleY },
    tailAnchor: { x: mouthLocalX, y: mouthLocalY },
  };
}

/**
 * SpeechBubble Wrapper Component (Figma-to-React Standard)
 */
export const SpeechBubble: React.FC<SpeechBubbleProps> = ({
  x,
  y,
  text,
  hasMusicNote = false,
  tailAnchor = { x: -40, y: 90 },
  bubbleArtwork,
}) => {
  const width = Math.max(260, text.length * 44 + 80);
  const height = 130;
  const radius = 65;

  const tailBaseX = Math.max(-width / 2 + 50, Math.min(width / 2 - 50, tailAnchor.x * 0.4));

  return (
    <g id="speech-bubble-wrapper" transform={`translate(${x}, ${y})`}>
      {/* =========================================================================
          FIGMA SVG BUBBLE ARTWORK (Náº¿u cÃ³ khung váº½ tÃ¹y biáº¿n tá»« Figma)
         ========================================================================= */}
      {bubbleArtwork ? (
        bubbleArtwork
      ) : (
        /* Máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh vector bubble capsule */
        <g id="default-bubble-vector">
          <polygon
            points={`${tailBaseX - 25},${height / 2 - 10} ${tailBaseX + 25},${height / 2 - 10} ${tailAnchor.x},${tailAnchor.y}`}
            fill="#FFFFFF"
            stroke="#141414"
            strokeWidth={12}
            strokeLinejoin="round"
          />
          <rect
            x={-width / 2}
            y={-height / 2}
            width={width}
            height={height}
            rx={radius}
            ry={radius}
            fill="#FFFFFF"
            stroke="#141414"
            strokeWidth={12}
            strokeLinejoin="round"
          />
        </g>
      )}

      {/* Bubble text content (>= 48px standard) */}
      <text
        x={0}
        y={18}
        textAnchor="middle"
        fontSize={52}
        fontWeight="900"
        fontFamily="var(--font-be-vietnam-pro), sans-serif"
        fill="#141414"
      >
        {text}
      </text>

      {/* Musical note decoration */}
      {hasMusicNote && (
        <text
          x={tailAnchor.x + 30}
          y={tailAnchor.y - 12}
          fontSize={44}
          fill="#666666"
        >
          â™ª
        </text>
      )}
    </g>
  );
};

import React from "react";
import { SubtitleWord } from "../../types/sceneGraph";

interface SubtitleBoxProps {
  text: string;
  words?: SubtitleWord[];
  currentFrame: number;
  fps: number;
  x?: number;
  y?: number;
  fontSize?: number;
}

/**
 * Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng ngáº¯t dÃ²ng vÄƒn báº£n dÃ i thÃ nh cÃ¡c dÃ²ng cÃ¢n Ä‘á»‘i
 * Äáº£m báº£o phá»¥ Ä‘á» khÃ´ng bao giá» trÃ n ra ngoÃ i khung mÃ n hÃ¬nh 2K (2560px)
 */
function splitIntoBalancedLines(text: string, maxLineChars = 40): string[] {
  if (!text || text.length <= maxLineChars) return [text.trim()];

  // Náº¿u cÃ³ dáº¥u pháº©y á»Ÿ khoáº£ng 30% - 70% cÃ¢u, Æ°u tiÃªn ngáº¯t táº¡i dáº¥u pháº©y
  const commaIdx = text.indexOf(",");
  if (commaIdx >= 18 && commaIdx <= text.length - 15) {
    const part1 = text.slice(0, commaIdx + 1).trim();
    const part2 = text.slice(commaIdx + 1).trim();
    if (part1.length <= maxLineChars + 10 && part2.length <= maxLineChars + 10) {
      return [part1, part2];
    }
  }

  // Chia theo khoáº£ng tráº¯ng gáº§n trung Ä‘iá»ƒm nháº¥t
  const words = text.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  if (words.length <= 1) return [text];

  const midChar = Math.floor(text.length / 2);
  let accumulated = 0;
  let splitIndex = Math.floor(words.length / 2);

  for (let i = 0; i < words.length; i++) {
    accumulated += words[i].length + 1;
    if (accumulated >= midChar) {
      splitIndex = i + 1;
      break;
    }
  }

  const line1 = words.slice(0, splitIndex).join(" ").trim();
  const line2 = words.slice(splitIndex).join(" ").trim();
  return [line1, line2].filter(Boolean);
}

/**
 * NhÃ³m cÃ¡c tá»« karaoke vÃ o cÃ¡c dÃ²ng tÆ°Æ¡ng á»©ng
 */
function groupWordsIntoLines(words: SubtitleWord[], maxLineChars = 40): SubtitleWord[][] {
  const lines: SubtitleWord[][] = [];
  let currentLine: SubtitleWord[] = [];
  let currentLength = 0;

  for (const w of words) {
    const wordLen = (w.word || "").length + 1;
    if (currentLine.length === 0) {
      currentLine.push(w);
      currentLength = wordLen;
    } else if (currentLength + wordLen <= maxLineChars) {
      currentLine.push(w);
      currentLength += wordLen;
    } else {
      lines.push(currentLine);
      currentLine = [w];
      currentLength = wordLen;
    }
  }
  if (currentLine.length > 0) lines.push(currentLine);
  return lines;
}

export const SubtitleBox: React.FC<SubtitleBoxProps> = ({
  text,
  words,
  currentFrame,
  fps = 60,
  x = 1280, // Centered on 2K canvas
  y = 1330, // Äáº·t chuáº©n á»Ÿ Ä‘Ã¡y mÃ n hÃ¬nh
  fontSize = 68,
}) => {
  if (!text) return null;

  const currentMs = (currentFrame / fps) * 1000;
  const isKaraoke = Boolean(words && words.length > 0);

  const textLines = isKaraoke ? [] : splitIntoBalancedLines(text, 42);
  const wordLines = isKaraoke ? groupWordsIntoLines(words!, 42) : [];
  const lineCount = isKaraoke ? wordLines.length : textLines.length;
  const isMultiLine = lineCount > 1;

  // TÃ­nh cá»¡ chá»¯ tá»‘i Æ°u cho Ä‘á»™ phÃ¢n giáº£i 2K
  const longestLineLen = isKaraoke
    ? Math.max(...wordLines.map((wl) => wl.map((w) => w.word).join(" ").length), 1)
    : Math.max(...textLines.map((l) => l.length), 1);

  let finalFontSize = fontSize;
  if (longestLineLen > 48) {
    finalFontSize = 52;
  } else if (longestLineLen > 38) {
    finalFontSize = 58;
  } else if (longestLineLen > 28) {
    finalFontSize = 64;
  } else {
    finalFontSize = 68;
  }

  const lineSpacing = finalFontSize * 1.30;
  // Khi cÃ³ 2 dÃ²ng, tá»± Ä‘á»™ng nÃ¢ng tÃ¢m lÃªn Ä‘á»ƒ dÃ²ng 2 khÃ´ng cháº¡m mÃ©p dÆ°á»›i
  const adjustedY = isMultiLine ? y - lineSpacing * 0.42 : y;

  return (
    <g
      transform={`translate(${x}, ${adjustedY})`}
      style={{
        filter: "drop-shadow(0px 8px 18px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.75))",
      }}
    >
      {isKaraoke ? (
        /* Karaoke Word Highlight Mode (Äa dÃ²ng) */
        <text
          textAnchor="middle"
          fontSize={finalFontSize}
          fontWeight="900"
          fontFamily="var(--font-be-vietnam-pro), sans-serif"
        >
          {wordLines.map((lineWords, lineIdx) => {
            const dyVal = isMultiLine
              ? lineIdx === 0
                ? -(lineCount - 1) * (lineSpacing / 2)
                : lineSpacing
              : 0;
            return (
              <tspan key={lineIdx} x={0} dy={dyVal}>
                {lineWords.map((item, itemIdx) => {
                  const isActive = currentMs >= item.startMs && currentMs <= item.endMs;
                  return (
                    <tspan
                      key={itemIdx}
                      dx={itemIdx === 0 ? 0 : 14}
                      fill={isActive ? "#FFD13B" : "#FFFFFF"}
                      stroke="#141414"
                      strokeWidth={isActive ? 18 : 16}
                      strokeLinejoin="round"
                      strokeLinecap="round"
                      style={{
                        paintOrder: "stroke fill",
                        transition: "fill 0.1s ease",
                      }}
                    >
                      {item.word}
                    </tspan>
                  );
                })}
              </tspan>
            );
          })}
        </text>
      ) : (
        /* Solid Subtitle Mode (Äa dÃ²ng cÃ¢n Ä‘á»‘i) */
        <text
          textAnchor="middle"
          fontSize={finalFontSize}
          fontWeight="900"
          fontFamily="var(--font-be-vietnam-pro), sans-serif"
          fill="#FFFFFF"
          stroke="#141414"
          strokeWidth={16}
          strokeLinejoin="round"
          strokeLinecap="round"
          style={{
            paintOrder: "stroke fill",
          }}
        >
          {textLines.map((lineStr, lineIdx) => {
            const dyVal = isMultiLine
              ? lineIdx === 0
                ? -(lineCount - 1) * (lineSpacing / 2)
                : lineSpacing
              : 0;
            return (
              <tspan key={lineIdx} x={0} dy={dyVal}>
                {lineStr}
              </tspan>
            );
          })}
        </text>
      )}
    </g>
  );
};


==================== FILE: C:\Users\ASUS\Downloads\TOOL_2D_ANIM_API_FIX_1\TOOL_2D_ANIM\web-studio\src\remotion\compositions\AnimScriptScene.tsx ====================
/**
 * AnimScript Remotion Composition
 * Generic renderer executing any AnimScript IR through the runtime evaluator.
 * Completely deterministic, camera-aware, multi-layer depth occlusion, Vietnamese typography.
 */

import React from "react";
import { useCurrentFrame, useVideoConfig, AbsoluteFill } from "remotion";
import { AnimScriptIR, EvaluatedObjectState } from "../../animscript/types";
import { evaluateFrame } from "../../animscript/runtime";
import { calculateCharacterAnchors, PROP_REGISTRY } from "../../animscript/registry/assetRegistry";
import { StickmanRig } from "../components/character/StickmanRig";
import { SpeechBubble } from "../components/ui/SpeechBubble";

export interface AnimScriptSceneProps {
  ir: AnimScriptIR;
  showDebugMarkers?: boolean;
}

export const AnimScriptScene: React.FC<AnimScriptSceneProps> = ({ ir, showDebugMarkers = false }) => {
  const frame = useCurrentFrame();
  const { width, height, fps } = useVideoConfig();

  if (!ir || !ir.scenes || ir.scenes.length === 0) {
    return (
      <AbsoluteFill style={{ backgroundColor: "#1e1e24", color: "white", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", fontSize: 24 }}>
        AnimScript: KhÃ´ng cÃ³ dá»¯ liá»‡u cáº£nh Ä‘á»ƒ hiá»ƒn thá»‹.
      </AbsoluteFill>
    );
  }

  // Pure function evaluation per frame
  const state = evaluateFrame(ir, frame, fps);

  // Camera transform calculations
  const cx = state.camera.x;
  const cy = state.camera.y;
  const zoom = state.camera.zoom;
  const stageTranslateX = width / 2 - cx * zoom;
  const stageTranslateY = height / 2 - cy * zoom;

  // Separate objects into depth layers
  const objectsList = Object.values(state.objects);
  const backObjects = objectsList.filter((obj) => obj.layerSplits?.behindDesk);
  const midObjects = objectsList.filter((obj) => !obj.layerSplits?.behindDesk && obj.kind === "prop");
  const frontCharacters = objectsList.filter((obj) => !obj.layerSplits?.behindDesk && obj.kind === "character");

  return (
    <AbsoluteFill style={{ backgroundColor: "#F7F3EE", overflow: "hidden", fontFamily: "system-ui, sans-serif" }}>
      {/* Camera Rigged Stage Layer */}
      <div
        style={{
          position: "absolute",
          width,
          height,
          transform: `translate(${stageTranslateX}px, ${stageTranslateY}px) scale(${zoom})`,
          transformOrigin: "0 0",
        }}
      >
        {/* Layer 0: Background & Floor */}
        <svg width={width} height={height} style={{ position: "absolute", top: 0, left: 0, pointerEvents: "none" }}>
          {/* Base Floor Line at y=850 */}
          <line x1={-1000} y1={850} x2={width + 1000} y2={850} stroke="#D4C5B0" strokeWidth={3} />
          <rect x={-1000} y={852} width={width + 2000} height={400} fill="#ECE5DA" />
        </svg>

        {/* Layer 1: Behind-desk Objects (e.g. Chair, Seated Character Body & Full 2-segment Legs) */}
        {backObjects.map((obj) => (
          <RenderSceneObject
            key={`back_${obj.id}`}
            obj={obj}
            frame={frame}
            renderPart={obj.layerSplits?.handsFront ? "bodyOnly" : "all"}
            showDebugMarkers={showDebugMarkers}
          />
        ))}

        {/* Layer 2: Props (Desk, Floor Props) */}
        {midObjects.map((obj) => (
          <RenderSceneObject key={`mid_${obj.id}`} obj={obj} frame={frame} showDebugMarkers={showDebugMarkers} />
        ))}

        {/* Layer 3: Front Characters (Standing Characters) */}
        {frontCharacters.map((obj) => (
          <RenderSceneObject key={`front_${obj.id}`} obj={obj} frame={frame} renderPart="all" showDebugMarkers={showDebugMarkers} />
        ))}

        {/* Layer 3B: Front Arms for Seated Character (Exact 2-segment arms with shoulder, elbow, wrist/hand) */}
        {backObjects.map((obj) => {
          if (obj.kind === "character" && obj.layerSplits?.handsFront) {
            return (
              <RenderSceneObject
                key={`hands_${obj.id}`}
                obj={obj}
                frame={frame}
                renderPart="armsOnly"
                showDebugMarkers={showDebugMarkers}
              />
            );
          }
          return null;
        })}

        {/* Physical Prop Labels attached to objects */}
        {state.activePropLabels.map((lbl) => {
          const propObj = state.objects[lbl.propId];
          if (!propObj) return null;
          const lx = propObj.transform.x;
          const ly = propObj.transform.y - (propObj.props?.height || 200) * 0.5;
          return (
            <div
              key={lbl.id}
              style={{
                position: "absolute",
                left: lx,
                top: ly,
                transform: "translate(-50%, -50%)",
                backgroundColor: "#ffffff",
                border: "2px solid #E11D48",
                borderRadius: 6,
                padding: "3px 14px",
                fontSize: 22,
                fontWeight: 800,
                color: "#E11D48",
                boxShadow: "0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.15)",
                pointerEvents: "none",
                zIndex: 35,
              }}
            >
              {lbl.text}
            </div>
          );
        })}

        {/* Speech Bubbles */}
        {state.activeSpeechBubbles.length > 0 && (
          <svg
            style={{
              position: "absolute",
              top: 0,
              left: 0,
              width: "100%",
              height: "100%",
              overflow: "visible",
              pointerEvents: "none",
              zIndex: 50,
            }}
          >
            {state.activeSpeechBubbles.map((bubble) => {
              const char = state.objects[bubble.characterId];
              const isFacingLeft = char?.pose?.facing === "left";
              const bx = (char?.transform.x ?? width / 2) + (isFacingLeft ? -220 : 220);
              const by = (char?.transform.y ?? 850) - 700;
              return (
                <SpeechBubble
                  key={bubble.id}
                  text={bubble.text}
                  x={bx}
                  y={by}
                  tailAnchor={{ x: isFacingLeft ? 120 : -120, y: 140 }}
                />
              );
            })}
          </svg>
        )}
        {/* Debug Markers Layer (Active when showDebugMarkers=true) */}
        {showDebugMarkers && (
          <svg
            style={{
              position: "absolute",
              top: 0,
              left: 0,
              width: "100%",
              height: "100%",
              overflow: "visible",
              pointerEvents: "none",
              zIndex: 90,
            }}
          >
            {/* Markers for characters */}
            {objectsList
              .filter((obj) => obj.kind === "character")
              .map((char) => {
                const anchors = calculateCharacterAnchors(
                  char.transform.x,
                  char.transform.y,
                  char.props?.height || 600,
                  char.pose?.facing || "right",
                  char.pose
                );
                return (
                  <g key={`dbg_char_${char.id}`}>
                    {Object.entries(anchors).map(([name, pt]) => {
                      const isKey = ["right_hand", "left_hand", "pelvis", "right_knee", "left_knee", "right_ankle", "left_ankle"].includes(name);
                      const color = isKey ? "#EF4444" : "#3B82F6";
                      return (
                        <g key={name} transform={`translate(${pt.x}, ${pt.y})`}>
                          <circle r={isKey ? 5 : 3} fill="none" stroke={color} strokeWidth={2} />
                          <line x1={-7} y1={0} x2={7} y2={0} stroke={color} strokeWidth={1} />
                          <line x1={0} y1={-7} x2={0} y2={7} stroke={color} strokeWidth={1} />
                          {isKey && (
                            <text
                              x={8}
                              y={4}
                              fontSize={11}
                              fill={color}
                              fontWeight="bold"
                              fontFamily="monospace"
                              style={{ textShadow: "1px 1px 2px #fff, -1px -1px 2px #fff" }}
                            >
                              {name} ({pt.x.toFixed(0)}, {pt.y.toFixed(0)})
                            </text>
                          )}
                        </g>
                      );
                    })}
                  </g>
                );
              })}

            {/* Markers for props */}
            {objectsList
              .filter((obj) => obj.kind === "prop")
              .map((prop) => {
                const def = PROP_REGISTRY[prop.assetType];
                const w = prop.props?.width || def?.defaultWidth || 100;
                const h = prop.props?.height || def?.defaultHeight || 100;
                const anchors = def?.anchors || {};
                return (
                  <g key={`dbg_prop_${prop.id}`}>
                    {/* Bounding box outline */}
                    <rect
                      x={prop.transform.x - w / 2}
                      y={prop.transform.y - h}
                      width={w}
                      height={h}
                      fill="none"
                      stroke="#10B981"
                      strokeWidth={1}
                      strokeDasharray="4 4"
                      opacity={0.6}
                    />
                    {Object.entries(anchors).map(([name, fn]) => {
                      const offset = fn(w, h);
                      const ax = prop.transform.x + offset.x;
                      const ay = prop.transform.y + offset.y;
                      return (
                        <g key={name} transform={`translate(${ax}, ${ay})`}>
                          <circle r={4} fill="#10B981" stroke="#FFFFFF" strokeWidth={1.5} />
                          <line x1={-6} y1={0} x2={6} y2={0} stroke="#059669" strokeWidth={1} />
                          <line x1={0} y1={-6} x2={0} y2={6} stroke="#059669" strokeWidth={1} />
                          <text
                            x={7}
                            y={-3}
                            fontSize={11}
                            fill="#065F46"
                            fontWeight="bold"
                            fontFamily="monospace"
                            style={{ textShadow: "1px 1px 2px #fff, -1px -1px 2px #fff" }}
                          >
                            {prop.id}.{name} ({ax.toFixed(0)}, {ay.toFixed(0)})
                          </text>
                        </g>
                      );
                    })}
                  </g>
                );
              })}
          </svg>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Screen Space Layer (Fixed, Unaffected by Camera Zoom) */}
      {/* Subtitles: Max 2 lines, Safe bottom area */}
      {state.activeSubtitles.map((sub) => (
        <div
          key={sub.id}
          style={{
            position: "absolute",
            bottom: 48,
            left: "50%",
            transform: "translateX(-50%)",
            backgroundColor: "rgba(18, 18, 22, 0.88)",
            color: "#FFFFFF",
            padding: "14px 32px",
            borderRadius: 14,
            fontSize: 27,
            fontWeight: 600,
            lineHeight: 1.4,
            textAlign: "center",
            maxWidth: 1200,
            boxShadow: "0 6px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.35)",
            border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.15)",
            zIndex: 100,
          }}
        >
          {sub.text}
        </div>
      ))}

      {/* Debug HUD (Screen-space overlay) */}
      {showDebugMarkers && (
        <div
          style={{
            position: "absolute",
            top: 24,
            left: 24,
            backgroundColor: "rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.85)",
            color: "#38BDF8",
            fontFamily: "monospace",
            fontSize: 16,
            padding: "10px 18px",
            borderRadius: 8,
            border: "1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.4)",
            zIndex: 200,

          <div><strong>DEBUG HUD</strong></div>
          <div>Frame: {frame} | Time: {(frame / fps).toFixed(2)}s</div>
          <div>Camera: ({cx.toFixed(0)}, {cy.toFixed(0)}) @ {zoom.toFixed(2)}x</div>
          <div>Objects: {objectsList.length}</div>
        </div>
      )}
    </AbsoluteFill>
  );

// ============================================================================
// OBJECT RENDERERS
// ============================================================================

const RenderSceneObject: React.FC<{
  obj: EvaluatedObjectState;
  frame: number;
  renderPart?: "all" | "bodyOnly" | "armsOnly";
  showDebugMarkers?: boolean;
}> = ({ obj, frame, renderPart = "all", showDebugMarkers = false }) => {
  const { transform, kind, assetType, pose, props } = obj;

  if (kind === "character") {
    const isSitting = Boolean(pose?.isSitting);
    const scale = ((props?.height || 600) / 600) * (transform.scaleX || 1.0);
    return (
      <svg
        style={{
          position: "absolute",
          top: 0,
          left: 0,
          width: "100%",
          height: "100%",
          overflow: "visible",
          pointerEvents: "none",
          opacity: transform.opacity,
        }}
      >
        <StickmanRig
          renderPart={renderPart}
          showDebugMarkers={showDebugMarkers}
          state={{
            x: transform.x,
            y: transform.y,
            facing: pose?.facing || "right",
            scale,
            pose: isSitting ? "sittingBehindDesk" : "standing",
            seatedHipY: pose?.sittingHipY !== undefined 
              ? pose.sittingHipY 
              : (pose?.crouchDepth ? -310 + pose.crouchDepth : (isSitting ? -180 : undefined)),
            isSpeaking: pose?.mouthOpen,
            eyes: (pose as any)?.eyes,
            eyebrows: (pose as any)?.eyebrows,
            mouth: (pose as any)?.mouth,
            pupilGaze: pose?.eyeGaze,
            limbAngles: pose?.limbAngles,
            hat: props?.hat !== undefined ? props.hat : true,
            showDebugMarkers,
          }}
        />
      </svg>
    );
  }

  // Render Prop by assetType
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        position: "absolute",
        left: transform.x,
        top: transform.y,
        transform: `translate(-50%, -100%) rotate(${transform.rotationDeg}deg) scale(${transform.scaleX}, ${transform.scaleY})`,
        opacity: transform.opacity,
        pointerEvents: "none",
      }}
    >
      <PropGraphic assetType={assetType} width={props?.width} height={props?.height} frame={frame} />
    </div>
  );
};

const PropGraphic: React.FC<{ assetType: string; width?: number; height?: number; frame: number }> = ({
  assetType,
  width = 200,
  height = 200,
  frame,
}) => {
  switch (assetType) {
    case "desk": {
      const w = width || 800;
      const h = height || 450;
      return (
        <svg width={w} height={h} viewBox={`0 0 ${w} ${h}`}>
          {/* Back legs */}
          <rect x={w * 0.12} y={h * 0.4} width={18} height={h * 0.6} fill="#78350F" />
          <rect x={w * 0.85} y={h * 0.4} width={18} height={h * 0.6} fill="#78350F" />
          {/* Front Apron */}
          <rect x={w * 0.08} y={h * 0.4} width={w * 0.84} height={28} fill="#92400E" rx={4} />
          {/* Front legs */}
          <rect x={w * 0.08} y={h * 0.4} width={22} height={h * 0.6} fill="#B45309" />
          <rect x={w * 0.89} y={h * 0.4} width={22} height={h * 0.6} fill="#B45309" />
          {/* Top Surface */}
          <rect x={0} y={h * 0.35} width={w} height={24} fill="#D97706" rx={6} stroke="#78350F" strokeWidth={3} />
        </svg>
      );
    }

    case "chair": {
      const w = width || 220;
      const h = height || 400;
      return (
        <svg width={w} height={h} viewBox={`0 0 ${w} ${h}`}>
          {/* Profile Chair (NhÃ¬n nghiÃªng chuáº©n xÃ¡c, tá»±a lÆ°ng bÃªn trÃ¡i, máº·t ngá»“i thoÃ¡ng) */}
          {/* Back leg (chÃ¢n sau) */}
          <rect x={w * 0.12} y={h * 0.56} width={16} height={h * 0.44} fill="#78350F" />
          {/* Front leg (chÃ¢n trÆ°á»›c) */}
          <rect x={w * 0.78} y={h * 0.56} width={16} height={h * 0.44} fill="#B45309" />
          {/* Lower horizontal stretcher (thanh giáº±ng ngang ná»‘i 2 chÃ¢n) */}
          <rect x={w * 0.14} y={h * 0.75} width={w * 0.66} height={10} fill="#78350F" rx={2} />
          {/* Seat slab (Máº·t ngá»“i pháº³ng nhÃ¬n nghiÃªng) */}

          <rect x={w * 0.12} y={h * 0.08} width={16} height={h * 0.47} fill="#92400E" rx={3} />

    case "tray": {

    case "metabolic_clock":
    case "clock": {

      const h = height || 260;

      const needleDeg = -35 + Math.sin(frame * 0.05) * 20;
      return (

          {/* Needle */}
          <g transform={`translate(${w / 2}, ${h / 2}) rotate(${needleDeg})`}>
            <line x1={0} y1={0} x2={0} y2={-w * 0.34} stroke="#DC2626" strokeWidth={5} strokeLinecap="round" />
            <circle cx={0} cy={0} r={9} fill="#0F172A" />

      );

    case "window": {

          <circle cx={w * 0.4} cy={h * 0.45} r={2} fill="#FFFFFF" opacity={0.7} />
          {/* Window pane crosses */}
          <line x1={w / 2} y1={0} x2={w / 2} y2={h} stroke="#475569" strokeWidth={6} />
          <line x1={0} y1={h / 2} x2={w} y2={h / 2} stroke="#475569" strokeWidth={6} />
        </svg>
      );
    }

    case "meadow": {
      const w = width || 1920;
      const h = height || 200;
      return (

        </svg>
      );
    }

    default:
      return (
        <svg width={width} height={height} viewBox={`0 0 ${width} ${height}`}>
          <rect x={0} y={0} width={width} height={height} fill="#94A3B8" stroke="#475569" strokeWidth={3} rx={8} />
          <text x={width / 2} y={height / 2} textAnchor="middle" dominantBaseline="middle" fill="#FFFFFF" fontSize={16} fontWeight="bold">
            {assetType}
          </text>
        </svg>
      );
  }
};

 * FixedStoryScene: Composition dá»±ng ká»‹ch báº£n chuáº©n 4 cáº£nh theo Ä‘Ãºng nghiá»‡m thu
 *
 * Cáº£nh 1 (0 -> 360f / 0s -> 6s):

export const FixedStoryScene: React.FC = () => {
  const frame = useCurrentFrame();
  const { fps } = useVideoConfig();

  const DESIGN_WIDTH = 2560;
  const DESIGN_HEIGHT = 1440;
  const FLOOR_Y = 1120;

  let sceneIndex = 1;

  if (frame < 360) {
    sceneIndex = 1;
    sceneLocalFrame = frame;
  } else if (frame < 720) {
    sceneIndex = 2;
    sceneLocalFrame = frame - 360;
  } else if (frame < 1080) {
    sceneIndex = 3;
    sceneLocalFrame = frame - 720;
  } else {
    sceneIndex = 4;
    sceneLocalFrame = frame - 1080;
  }

  // SCENE 1 LOGIC (0 -> 360 frames)
  // =========================================================================
  // Timeline:
  // 0 - 60: Äá»©ng nhÃ¬n lá» nhá»‡n trÃªn sÃ n (chÃ¢n Ä‘á»©ng vá»¯ng, tÃ¡ch biá»‡t)

    facing: "left",
    pose: "standing",

    eyebrows: "raised",

  };

    if (sceneLocalFrame >= 60 && sceneLocalFrame < 190) {

      s1CharState.mouth = "talking";
      s1CharState.eyes = "happy";
    }

    if (sceneLocalFrame >= 190) {

import {

  const FLOOR_Y = 1120;
  const TOTAL_FRAMES = 300; // 5 giÃ¢y @ 60fps

  // QuÃ£ng Ä‘Æ°á»ng Ä‘i bá»™ tá»« x = 400 sang x = 2160
  const walkProgress = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, frame / TOTAL_FRAMES));
  const easedProgress = interpolate(walkProgress, [0, 1], [0, 1], {
    easing: Easing.linear,
  });

  const charX = interpolate(easedProgress, [0, 1], [400, 2160]);

  // Chu ká»³ bÆ°á»›c Ä‘i: ~50 frames cho 1 chu ká»³ 2 bÆ°á»›c
  const walkCycle = (frame * 0.02) % 1;
  const verticalBob = Math.abs(Math.sin(frame * 0.02 * Math.PI * 2)) * 6;

  const walkerState: StickmanState = {
    x: charX,
    y: FLOOR_Y - verticalBob,
    scale: 1.0,
    facing: "right",
    pose: "walking",
    eyes: "happy",
    eyebrows: "neutral",
    mouth: "smile",
    pupilGaze: { x: 0.6, y: 0.1 },
    walkCycleProgress: walkCycle,
    walkWeight: 1.0,
  };

  return (
    <AbsoluteFill style={{ width: "100%", height: "100%", backgroundColor: "#E9F5EB", overflow: "hidden" }}>
      <svg
        viewBox={`0 0 ${DESIGN_WIDTH} ${DESIGN_HEIGHT}`}
        className="w-full h-full select-none"
        preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet"
      >
        <SvgDefsLibrary />

        {/* 1. Bá»‘i cáº£nh thiÃªn nhiÃªn ngoÃ i trá»i (Äá»“ng cá» xanh, Ä‘á»“i thoai thoáº£i, báº§u trá»i trong) */}
        <DynamicBackground preset="gardenDay" />

        {/* 2. Hiá»‡u á»©ng mÃ´i trÆ°á»ng: MÃ¢y trÃ´i nháº¹, tia náº¯ng áº¥m, ngá»n giÃ³ thoáº£ng */}
        <EnvironmentVfxLayer
          effects={["cloudDrift", "sunbeams", "windBreeze"]}
          lighting={{ preset: "sunbeams" }}
          backgroundPreset="gardenDay"
          frame={frame}
          fps={fps}
          canvasWidth={DESIGN_WIDTH}
          canvasHeight={DESIGN_HEIGHT}
        />

        {/* 3. NhÃ¢n váº­t ngÆ°á»i que bÆ°á»›c Ä‘i tá»± nhiÃªn trÃªn bÃ£i cá» */}
        <g id="meadow-walker">
          <StickmanRig state={walkerState} renderPart="all" />
        </g>

        {/* 4. Phá»¥ Ä‘á» kiá»ƒm thá»­ rig */}
        <SubtitleBox
          text="Kiá»ƒm tra rig: NgÆ°á»i Ä‘i bá»™ tá»± nhiÃªn trÃªn Ä‘á»“ng cá» xanh mÆ°á»›t."
          currentFrame={frame}
          fps={fps}
          x={1280}
          y={1320}
        />
      </svg>
    </AbsoluteFill>
  );
};

import React from "react";
import {
  useCurrentFrame,
  useVideoConfig,
  interpolate,
  AbsoluteFill,
  Easing,
} from "remotion";
import { StickmanRig, computeStickmanFK } from "../components/character/StickmanRig";
import { StickmanState, Point2D } from "../types/character";

export interface WalkRunReferenceSceneProps {
  showDebugMarkers?: boolean;
  cameraMode?: "full" | "closeUp";
  overrideFrame?: number;
}

/**
 * Giáº£i gÃ³c xoay cá»§a Ä‘Ã¹i (thighAngleDeg) tá»« tá»a Ä‘á»™ X mong muá»‘n cá»§a bÃ n chÃ¢n trong há»‡ tá»a Ä‘á»™ World.
 * Ãp dá»¥ng cho cáº£ chÃ¢n trÃ¡i vÃ  chÃ¢n pháº£i, há»— trá»£ má»i scale, facing vÃ  chiá»u dÃ i xÆ°Æ¡ng.
 */
export function solveThighAngleForWorldFootX(
  charX: number,
  targetFootWorldX: number,
  isLeft: boolean,
  scale: number = 1.0,
  facing: "left" | "right" = "right",
  legLength: number = 310,
  pelvicHalfWidth: number = 16
): number {
  const flipSign = facing === "left" ? -1 : 1;
  const hipLocalX = isLeft ? -pelvicHalfWidth : pelvicHalfWidth;
  const sinVal = (hipLocalX - (targetFootWorldX - charX) / (scale * flipSign)) / legLength;
  const clampedSin = Math.max(-0.999, Math.min(0.999, sinVal));
  return Math.asin(clampedSin) * (180 / Math.PI);
}

/**
 * TÃ­nh toÃ¡n tá»a Ä‘á»™ X cá»§a bÃ n chÃ¢n trong há»‡ tá»a Ä‘á»™ World tá»« gÃ³c xoay cá»§a Ä‘Ã¹i (thighAngleDeg).
 */
export function computeWorldFootXFromThighAngle(
  charX: number,
  thighAngleDeg: number,
  isLeft: boolean,
  scale: number = 1.0,
  facing: "left" | "right" = "right",
  legLength: number = 310,
  pelvicHalfWidth: number = 16
): number {
  const flipSign = facing === "left" ? -1 : 1;
  const hipLocalX = isLeft ? -pelvicHalfWidth : pelvicHalfWidth;
  const thighRad = (thighAngleDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
  const footLocalX = hipLocalX - Math.sin(thighRad) * legLength;
  return charX + footLocalX * scale * flipSign;
}

/**
 * WalkRunReferenceScene (V4 Acceptance Deliverable - Production Fix)
 * 
 * Sá»¬A TOÃ€N DIá»†N 6 ÄIá»‚M NGHIá»†M THU:
 * 1. Khá»›p hÃ¡ng & dÃ¡ng Ä‘á»©ng chá»¯ V ngÆ°á»£c (Lambda): ÄÃ¹i phÃ¢n nhÃ¡nh tá»« hai khá»›p hÃ´ng riÃªng biá»‡t,
 *    khÃ´ng báº¯t chÃ©o chá»¯ X, gÃ³c Ä‘Ã¹i chuáº©n xÃ¡c theo há»‡ trá»¥c SVG.
 * 2. Cá»• tay & bÃ n tay ná»‘i liá»n: Forearm káº¿t thÃºc táº¡i wristWorld, hand dot vÃ  grip anchor Ä‘á»“ng quy 0px.
 * 3. Chuyá»ƒn tiáº¿p liÃªn tá»¥c táº¡i ranh giá»›i hÃ nh Ä‘á»™ng:
 *    - f194 -> f195: KhÃ´ng giáº­t váº­n tá»‘c/gÃ³c khá»›p khi chuyá»ƒn sang cháº¡y (< 4Â° delta).
 *    - f329 -> f330: Káº¿ thá»«a chÃ¢n cháº¡m Ä‘áº¥t vÃ  tá»a Ä‘á»™ tiáº¿p xÃºc tá»« chu ká»³ cháº¡y, khÃ´ng nháº£y má»‘c dá»±ng sáºµn.
 * 4. Giá»¯ tiáº¿p xÃºc Ä‘áº¥t trong lÃºc chuyá»ƒn tÆ° tháº¿:
 *    - f180-185: Nháº¥c - Ä‘áº·t chÃ¢n trong khÃ´ng khÃ­ (stepping arc) thay vÃ¬ kÃ©o lÃª ngang trÃªn cÃ¡t.
 *    - f355-390: Tay nghá»‰ phÃ¢n biá»‡t rÃµ vá»›i thÃ¢n vÃ  vá»›i nhau, chÃ¢n Ä‘á»©ng á»•n Ä‘á»‹nh 60.00px.
 * 5. Äo kiá»ƒm Debug 100% Forward Kinematics:
 *    - Má»i sá»‘ Ä‘o HUD, thÆ°á»›c káº» vÃ  CSV Ä‘á»u trÃ­ch xuáº¥t tá»« computeStickmanFK.
 *    - Kháº¯c phá»¥c triá»‡t Ä‘á»ƒ sai lá»‡ch HUD 60px vs render 92px.

 *    - CSV tá»a Ä‘á»™ khá»›p trÃªn toÃ n bá»™ timeline.
 */
export const WalkRunReferenceScene: React.FC<WalkRunReferenceSceneProps> = ({
  showDebugMarkers = false,
  cameraMode = "full",
  overrideFrame,
}) => {
  const remotionFrame = useCurrentFrame();
  const frame = overrideFrame !== undefined ? overrideFrame : remotionFrame;
  const { width, height, fps } = useVideoConfig();

  const GROUND_Y = 850;
  const PART1_END_FRAME = 180;
  const TOTAL_FRAMES = 390;

  const isPart1 = frame < PART1_END_FRAME;

  let charX = 1150;

  let facing: "left" | "right" = "right";
  let torsoTilt = 0;
  let headTilt = 0;
  let pupilGaze: Point2D = { x: -0.7, y: 0 };
  let leftUpperArmAngle = 12;
  let leftLowerArmAngle = 6;
  let rightUpperArmAngle = -18;
  let rightLowerArmAngle = -140;
  let leftThighAngle = 9.47;
  let leftCalfAngle = 0;
  let rightThighAngle = -9.47;
  let rightCalfAngle = 0;
  let phaseName = "";
  let milestoneRef = "";
  let stanceFoot = "Cáº£ 2 chÃ¢n";
  let slipReport = "0.00 px (Tiáº¿p xÃºc hoÃ n háº£o)";

      });

        const targetFootR_X = charX + targetSpan / 2;

        rightThighAngle = solveThighAngleForWorldFootX(charX, targetFootR_X, false);
        leftCalfAngle = 0;
        rightCalfAngle = 0;
        phaseName = "Má»Ÿ cáº£nh: Tiáº¿n sang trÃ¡i, thu chÃ¢n";
        milestoneRef = "f72 (2.400s) -> f77 (2.567s)";
        stanceFoot = "ChÃ¢n pháº£i trá»¥";
      } else if (frame < 52) {
        const stepProgress = (frame - 10) / 42;
        const cycleRad = stepProgress * Math.PI;
        const swingProgress = Math.sin(cycleRad);

        const targetFootR_X = charX + currentSpan / 2;

      leftUpperArmAngle = interpolate(stepP, [0, 1], [-10, 10]);
      leftLowerArmAngle = interpolate(stepP, [0, 1], [-45, -60]);

      phaseName = "Chuyá»ƒn trá»ng lÆ°á»£ng & nháº¥c chÃ¢n pháº£i vÃ o tháº¿ xuáº¥t phÃ¡t";
      milestoneRef = `f${frame}: Nháº¥c chÃ¢n vÃ²ng cung (khÃ´ng kÃ©o lÃª cÃ¡t)`;
      stanceFoot = "ChÃ¢n trÃ¡i trá»¥ (KhoÃ¡ X=773) | ChÃ¢n pháº£i nháº¥c vÃ²ng cung";
      targetPlantX = 773;
    } else if (frame < 195) {
      // Giai Ä‘oáº¡n 2.1b (f185 - f195): NghiÃªng thÃ¢n 15Â° vÃ  khá»›p váº­n tá»‘c vÃ o chu ká»³ cháº¡y
      charX = 840;
      charY = GROUND_Y;
      const prepP = (frame - 185) / 10;

      torsoTilt = interpolate(prepP, [0, 1], [0, 15]);
      headTilt = 0;
      pupilGaze = { x: 0.8, y: 0 };

      // Hai tay Ä‘á»“ng pha tiáº¿p cáº­n Ä‘Ãºng gÃ³c cháº¡y táº¡i f195:
      rightUpperArmAngle = interpolate(prepP, [0, 1], [-15, 25]);
      rightLowerArmAngle = interpolate(prepP, [0, 1], [-60, -75]);
      leftUpperArmAngle = interpolate(prepP, [0, 1], [10, -45]);
      leftLowerArmAngle = interpolate(prepP, [0, 1], [-60, -75]);

      // Hai chÃ¢n vÃ o Ä‘Ã  Ä‘áº¡p xuáº¥t phÃ¡t:
      const startLeftThigh = solveThighAngleForWorldFootX(840, 773, true); // +9.47Â°
      const startRightThigh = solveThighAngleForWorldFootX(840, 883, false); // -4.25Â°

      leftThighAngle = interpolate(prepP, [0, 1], [startLeftThigh, -30]);
      leftCalfAngle = interpolate(prepP, [0, 1], [0, 40]);
      rightThighAngle = interpolate(prepP, [0, 1], [startRightThigh, 30]);
      rightCalfAngle = 0;

      phaseName = "NghiÃªng thÃ¢n 15Â° & hÃ²a nhá»‹p váº­n tá»‘c xuáº¥t phÃ¡t cháº¡y";
      milestoneRef = `f${frame}: Khá»›p váº­n tá»‘c gÃ³c vá»›i f195 (< 4Â° delta)`;
      stanceFoot = "ChÃ¢n trÃ¡i Ä‘áº¡p Ä‘Ã  sau, chÃ¢n pháº£i vung trÆ°á»›c";
      slipReport = "0.00 px (Äáº¡p Ä‘Ã  xuáº¥t phÃ¡t)";
    } else if (frame < 330) {
      // Giai Ä‘oáº¡n 2.2 (f195 - f329): Chu ká»³ cháº¡y Ä‘á»u vá»›i pha bay (Flight Phase)
      const runFrames = frame - 195;
      const totalRunProg = runFrames / 134;
      const easedRunProg = interpolate(totalRunProg, [0, 0.25, 1], [0, 0.18, 1]);
      charX = 840 + 715 * easedRunProg;

      torsoTilt = 15;
      headTilt = 0;
      pupilGaze = { x: 0.8, y: 0 };

      const runRad = ((runFrames % 52) / 52) * Math.PI * 2 - Math.PI / 2;

      // Pha bay nÃ¢ng thÃ¢n 14px
      const flightLift = Math.max(0, Math.sin(runRad * 2) * 14);
      charY = GROUND_Y - flightLift;

      // Äá»™ng tÃ¡c chÃ¢n cháº¡y Ä‘á»‘i pha
      leftThighAngle = Math.sin(runRad) * 30;
      leftCalfAngle = Math.max(0, -Math.sin(runRad) * 40);
      rightThighAngle = -Math.sin(runRad) * 30;
      rightCalfAngle = Math.max(0, Math.sin(runRad) * 40);

      // ÄÃ¡nh tay Ä‘á»‘i pha má»m máº¡i
      leftUpperArmAngle = -10 + Math.sin(runRad) * 35;
      leftLowerArmAngle = -65 - Math.abs(Math.sin(runRad)) * 10;
      rightUpperArmAngle = -10 - Math.sin(runRad) * 35;
      rightLowerArmAngle = -65 - Math.abs(Math.sin(runRad)) * 10;

      const isFlight = flightLift > 5;
      stanceFoot = isFlight
        ? "BAY TRÃŠN KHÃ”NG (Flight Phase)"
        : Math.sin(runRad) > 0
        ? "ChÃ¢n trÃ¡i tiáº¿p Ä‘áº¥t"
        : "ChÃ¢n pháº£i tiáº¿p Ä‘áº¥t";
      phaseName = isFlight
        ? "Giai Ä‘oáº¡n bay (Flight Phase, Lift: 14px)"
        : "Cháº¡y Ä‘á»u (Cadence: 0.43s / bÆ°á»›c)";
      milestoneRef = `Chu ká»³ cháº¡y 52 frame (0.86s/vÃ²ng) | ThÃ¢n nghiÃªng 15Â°`;
      slipReport = isFlight ? "Pha bay (KhÃ´ng tiáº¿p xÃºc Ä‘áº¥t)" : "0.00 px (Tiáº¿p xÃºc Ä‘á»™ng)";
    } else {
      // Giai Ä‘oáº¡n 2.3 (f330 - f390): HÃ£m tá»‘c liÃªn tá»¥c & Äá»©ng á»•n Ä‘á»‹nh
      charY = GROUND_Y;

      if (frame < 333) {
        // Pha cháº¡m Ä‘áº¥t (f330 - f332): Káº¿ thá»«a mÆ°á»£t tá»« f329
        const p = (frame - 329) / 4;
        charX = 1555 + (1572 - 1555) * p;

        rightThighAngle = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [-26.55, -20]);
        rightCalfAngle = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [35.40, 0]);

        leftThighAngle = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [26.55, 15]);
        leftCalfAngle = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [0, 10]);

        torsoTilt = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [15, 13]);
        headTilt = 0;

        leftUpperArmAngle = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [20.98, 0]);
        leftLowerArmAngle = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [-73.85, -50]);
        rightUpperArmAngle = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [-40.98, -25]);
        rightLowerArmAngle = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [-73.85, -50]);

        phaseName = "HÃ£m tá»‘c: Káº¿ thá»«a chÃ¢n pháº£i cháº¡m Ä‘áº¥t (Háº¡ gá»‘i)";
        milestoneRef = `f${frame}: ChÃ¢n pháº£i tiáº¿p Ä‘áº¥t liÃªn tá»¥c (Delta < 1.7Â°)`;
        stanceFoot = "ChÃ¢n pháº£i tiáº¿p Ä‘áº¥t (Pha háº¡ chÃ¢n)";
        slipReport = "0.00 px (Tiáº¿p Ä‘áº¥t liÃªn tá»¥c)";
      } else if (frame < 355) {
        // Pha hÃ£m lá»±c (f333 - f354): ChÃ¢n pháº£i khoÃ¡ cá»©ng táº¡i PLANT_R_X = 1694.03
        const q = (frame - 333) / 22;
        const easedQ = q * (2 - q); // ease out quad
        charX = 1572 + (SETTLED_X - 1572) * easedQ;

        // ChÃ¢n pháº£i trá»¥: KhoÃ¡ cá»©ng 100% táº¡i PLANT_R_X
        rightThighAngle = solveThighAngleForWorldFootX(charX, PLANT_R_X, false);
        rightCalfAngle = 0;
        targetPlantX = PLANT_R_X;

        // ChÃ¢n trÃ¡i vung ngáº¯n Ä‘á»¡ thÃ¢n:
        const leftTargetX = interpolate(q, [0, 1], [1500.37, PLANT_L_X]);
        leftThighAngle = solveThighAngleForWorldFootX(charX, leftTargetX, true);
        leftCalfAngle = Math.sin(q * Math.PI) * 18;

        torsoTilt = interpolate(q, [0, 1], [13, 2]);
        headTilt = 0;

        leftUpperArmAngle = interpolate(q, [0, 1], [0, 10]);
        leftLowerArmAngle = interpolate(q, [0, 1], [-50, -20]);
        rightUpperArmAngle = interpolate(q, [0, 1], [-25, -16]);
        rightLowerArmAngle = interpolate(q, [0, 1], [-50, -20]);

        phaseName = "HÃ£m tá»‘c: KhoÃ¡ chÃ¢n pháº£i trá»¥, chÃ¢n trÃ¡i bÆ°á»›c Ä‘á»¡";
        milestoneRef = `f${frame}: KhoÃ¡ chÃ¢n pháº£i X=${PLANT_R_X.toFixed(1)} | TrÆ°á»£t 0.00px`;
        stanceFoot = `ChÃ¢n pháº£i trá»¥ (KhoÃ¡ X=${PLANT_R_X.toFixed(1)}) | ChÃ¢n trÃ¡i bÆ°á»›c Ä‘á»¡`;
      } else {
        // Pha á»•n Ä‘á»‹nh (f355 - f390): Cáº£ hai chÃ¢n Ä‘áº·t vá»¯ng chÃ£i 60.00px
        const settleP = Math.min(1, (frame - 355) / 35);
        charX = SETTLED_X;

        rightThighAngle = solveThighAngleForWorldFootX(charX, PLANT_R_X, false);
        rightCalfAngle = 0;
        leftThighAngle = solveThighAngleForWorldFootX(charX, PLANT_L_X, true);
        leftCalfAngle = 0;
        targetPlantX = PLANT_R_X;

        torsoTilt = interpolate(settleP, [0, 1], [2, 0]);
        headTilt = 0;

        // Hai tay nghá»‰ tá»± nhiÃªn, phÃ¢n biá»‡t rÃµ vá»›i thÃ¢n vÃ  vá»›i nhau:
        leftUpperArmAngle = 10;
        leftLowerArmAngle = interpolate(settleP, [0, 1], [-20, -12]);
        rightUpperArmAngle = -16;
        rightLowerArmAngle = interpolate(settleP, [0, 1], [-20, -10]);

        stanceFoot = "Cáº£ 2 chÃ¢n khoÃ¡ Ä‘áº¥t á»•n Ä‘á»‹nh 100%";
        phaseName = settleP >= 1 ? "Äá»©ng á»•n Ä‘á»‹nh thÄƒng báº±ng (ThÃ¢n tháº³ng)" : "á»”n Ä‘á»‹nh tháº¿ Ä‘á»©ng hai chÃ¢n";
        milestoneRef = `f${frame} Nghiá»‡m thu hÃ£m tá»‘c (Äá»™ má»Ÿ chÃ¢n: 60.00px)`;
      }
    }
  }

  // Khá»Ÿi táº¡o tráº¡ng thÃ¡i cho StickmanRig
  const stickmanState: StickmanState = {
    x: charX,
    y: charY,
    scale: 1.0,
    facing,
    pose: "standing",
    eyes: "neutral",
    eyebrows: "neutral",
    mouth: "smile",
    pupilGaze,
    hat: true,
    showDebugMarkers,
    limbAngles: {
      torso: torsoTilt,
      head: headTilt,
      leftUpperArm: leftUpperArmAngle,
      leftLowerArm: leftLowerArmAngle,
      rightUpperArm: rightUpperArmAngle,
      rightLowerArm: rightLowerArmAngle,
      leftThigh: leftThighAngle,
      leftCalf: leftCalfAngle,
      rightThigh: rightThighAngle,
      rightCalf: rightCalfAngle,
    },
  };

  const fk = computeStickmanFK(stickmanState);
  const actualLegSpan = Math.abs(fk.rightFoot.x - fk.leftFoot.x);
  const legSpanDisplay = actualLegSpan.toFixed(1);

  if (targetPlantX !== null) {
    const isStanceRight = stanceFoot.includes("ChÃ¢n pháº£i trá»¥") || stanceFoot.includes("Cáº£ 2 chÃ¢n");
    const activeStanceFootX = isStanceRight ? fk.rightFoot.x : fk.leftFoot.x;
    const slipPx = Math.abs(activeStanceFootX - targetPlantX);
    slipReport = `${slipPx.toFixed(2)} px (Äo FK: X=${activeStanceFootX.toFixed(1)}, Neo=${targetPlantX.toFixed(1)})`;
  } else if (frame >= 355) {
    const slipR = Math.abs(fk.rightFoot.x - PLANT_R_X);
    const slipL = Math.abs(fk.leftFoot.x - PLANT_L_X);
    slipReport = `L: ${slipL.toFixed(2)} px | R: ${slipR.toFixed(2)} px (KhoÃ¡ 2 chÃ¢n tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i)`;
  }

  // Camera zoom container khi cameraMode === "closeUp"
  const isCloseUp = cameraMode === "closeUp";
  const zoomScale = isCloseUp ? 1.65 : 1.0;
  const camX = isCloseUp ? 960 - charX * zoomScale : 0;
  const camY = isCloseUp ? 560 - 560 * zoomScale : 0;

  return (
    <AbsoluteFill style={{ backgroundColor: "#F7F3EE", overflow: "hidden", fontFamily: "system-ui, sans-serif" }}>
      {/* Container gÃ³c nhÃ¬n Camera */}
      <div
        id="camera-viewport"
        style={{
          position: "absolute",
          top: 0,
          left: 0,
          width: width,
          height: height,
          transform: isCloseUp ? `translate(${camX}px, ${camY}px) scale(${zoomScale})` : "none",
          transformOrigin: "0 0",
        }}
      >
        {/* 1. Bá»I Cáº¢NH BÃƒI BIá»‚N THEO CHUáº¨N VIDEO CUA */}
        <svg
          width={width}
          height={height}
          style={{ position: "absolute", top: 0, left: 0, pointerEvents: "none" }}
        >
          <defs>
            <linearGradient id="skyGrad" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
              <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#7DD3FC" />
              <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#BAE6FD" />
            </linearGradient>
            <linearGradient id="seaGrad" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
              <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#0284C7" />
              <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#0369A1" />
            </linearGradient>
            <linearGradient id="sandGrad" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
              <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#FDE68A" />
              <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#F5D061" />
            </linearGradient>
          </defs>

          {/* Báº§u trá»i xanh bÃ£i biá»ƒn */}
          <rect x={0} y={0} width={width} height={420} fill="url(#skyGrad)" />

          {/* Biá»ƒn xanh xa xÄƒm */}
          <rect x={0} y={350} width={width} height={120} fill="url(#seaGrad)" />
          {/* SÃ³ng bá»t tráº¯ng */}
          <path
            d={`M 0 468 Q 200 460 400 468 T 800 468 T 1200 468 T 1600 468 T 1920 468 L 1920 474 L 0 474 Z`}
            fill="#FFFFFF"
            opacity={0.85}
          />

          {/* BÃ£i cÃ¡t vÃ ng */}
          <rect x={0} y={470} width={width} height={610} fill="url(#sandGrad)" />

          {/* Váº¡ch máº·t sÃ n bÃ£i cÃ¡t y=850 (Floor Contact Level) */}
          <line x1={0} y1={GROUND_Y} x2={width} y2={GROUND_Y} stroke="#D97706" strokeWidth={2} strokeDasharray="6 6" opacity={0.4} />

          {/* ThÃ¢n cÃ¢y dá»«a bÃªn pháº£i */}
          <g id="palm-trunk" transform="translate(1780, 0)">
            <path
              d="M 50 0 C 40 250, -10 550, -40 850 L 40 850 C 70 550, 120 250, 130 0 Z"
              fill="#854D0E"
              stroke="#18181B"
              strokeWidth={4}
            />
            {[120, 240, 360, 480, 600, 720].map((yVal, idx) => (
              <line
                key={idx}
                x1={idx * -7 + 10}
                y1={yVal}
                x2={idx * -7 + 85}
                y2={yVal + 15}
                stroke="#583101"
                strokeWidth={5}
                strokeLinecap="round"
              />
            ))}
          </g>

          {/* BÃ³ng Ä‘en má»m dÆ°á»›i chÃ¢n stickman */}
          <ellipse
            cx={charX}
            cy={GROUND_Y + 4}
            rx={Math.max(50, actualLegSpan * 0.55 + 20)}
            ry={14}
            fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.22)"
          />

          {/* Váº¿t chÃ¢n trÃªn cÃ¡t */}
          {isPart1 && (
            <g id="footprints" fill="#D97706" opacity={0.35}>
              <ellipse cx={1135} cy={GROUND_Y + 2} rx={16} ry={6} />
              <ellipse cx={1050} cy={GROUND_Y + 3} rx={16} ry={6} />
              <ellipse cx={960} cy={GROUND_Y + 2} rx={16} ry={6} />
              <ellipse cx={870} cy={GROUND_Y + 3} rx={16} ry={6} />
            </g>
          )}
        </svg>

        {/* 2. NHÃ‚N Váº¬T NGÆ¯á»œI QUE Äá»˜I MÅ¨ (STICKMAN RIG V4) */}
        <svg
          width={width}
          height={height}
          style={{ position: "absolute", top: 0, left: 0, overflow: "visible", pointerEvents: "none" }}
        >
          <StickmanRig state={stickmanState} renderPart="all" showDebugMarkers={showDebugMarkers} />

          {/* ThÆ°á»›c Ä‘o kiá»ƒm tiáº¿p Ä‘áº¥t khi á»Ÿ cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ Debug */}
          {showDebugMarkers && (
            <g id="ground-contact-ruler">
              <line x1={charX - 150} y1={GROUND_Y} x2={charX + 150} y2={GROUND_Y} stroke="#10B981" strokeWidth={3} />
              <text x={charX + 160} y={GROUND_Y + 5} fill="#10B981" fontSize={14} fontWeight="bold" fontFamily="monospace">
                GroundY = {GROUND_Y}px
              </text>
              <circle cx={charX} cy={charY} r={5} fill="#EF4444" />
              <text x={charX + 10} y={charY - 10} fill="#EF4444" fontSize={13} fontWeight="bold" fontFamily="monospace">
                Root ({charX.toFixed(1)}, {charY.toFixed(1)})
              </text>

              {/* Marker Ä‘o tá»a Ä‘á»™ thá»±c táº¿ cá»§a hai bÃ n chÃ¢n tá»« Forward Kinematics */}
              <circle cx={fk.leftFoot.x} cy={fk.leftFoot.y} r={6} fill="#3B82F6" stroke="#FFFFFF" strokeWidth={2} />
              <text x={fk.leftFoot.x - 40} y={fk.leftFoot.y + 22} fill="#3B82F6" fontSize={12} fontWeight="bold" fontFamily="monospace">
                L: {fk.leftFoot.x.toFixed(1)}
              </text>

              <circle cx={fk.rightFoot.x} cy={fk.rightFoot.y} r={6} fill="#10B981" stroke="#FFFFFF" strokeWidth={2} />
              <text x={fk.rightFoot.x + 10} y={fk.rightFoot.y + 22} fill="#10B981" fontSize={12} fontWeight="bold" fontFamily="monospace">
                R: {fk.rightFoot.x.toFixed(1)}
              </text>

              {/* ThÆ°á»›c Ä‘o khoáº£ng cÃ¡ch giá»¯a hai bÃ n chÃ¢n */}
              <line x1={fk.leftFoot.x} y1={GROUND_Y + 30} x2={fk.rightFoot.x} y2={GROUND_Y + 30} stroke="#F59E0B" strokeWidth={2} />
              <line x1={fk.leftFoot.x} y1={GROUND_Y + 25} x2={fk.leftFoot.x} y2={GROUND_Y + 35} stroke="#F59E0B" strokeWidth={2} />
              <line x1={fk.rightFoot.x} y1={GROUND_Y + 25} x2={fk.rightFoot.x} y2={GROUND_Y + 35} stroke="#F59E0B" strokeWidth={2} />
              <text
                x={(fk.leftFoot.x + fk.rightFoot.x) / 2 - 25}
                y={GROUND_Y + 48}
                fill="#F59E0B"
                fontSize={13}
                fontWeight="bold"
                fontFamily="monospace"
              >
                {legSpanDisplay}px
              </text>
            </g>
          )}
        </svg>
      </div>

      {/* 3. HEADS-UP DISPLAY (HUD) & THÃ”NG Sá» ÄO KIá»‚M Ká»¸ THUáº¬T */}
      {/* Top Banner TiÃªu Äá» */}
      <div
        style={{
          position: "absolute",
          top: 20,
          left: "50%",
          transform: "translateX(-50%)",
          backgroundColor: isCloseUp ? "#7C3AED" : isPart1 ? "#0284C7" : "#059669",
          color: "#FFFFFF",
          padding: "8px 28px",
          borderRadius: 30,
          fontSize: 19,
          fontWeight: 800,
          letterSpacing: 0.5,
          boxShadow: "0 4px 14px rgba(0,0,0,0.2)",
          border: "2px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.4)",
          zIndex: 50,
        }}
      >
        {isCloseUp
          ? "Cáº¬N Cáº¢NH CHUYá»‚N HÆ¯á»šNG & KHá»šP Ná»I (FRAMES 170 - 205) | ZOOM 1.65X"
          : isPart1
          ? "PHáº¦N 1: ÄI Bá»˜ THEO VIDEO CUA (CHUáº¨N 30FPS -> 60FPS)"
          : "PHáº¦N 2: CHáº Y Bá»˜ Äá»€ XUáº¤T (CADENCE 0.43s / BÆ¯á»šC, PHA BAY)"}
      </div>

      {/* Box ThÃ´ng Sá»‘ Chi Tiáº¿t GÃ³c TrÃ¡i TrÃªn */}
      <div
        style={{
          position: "absolute",
          top: 24,
          left: 24,
          backgroundColor: "rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.88)",
          color: "#E2E8F0",
          fontFamily: "monospace",
          fontSize: 15,
          padding: "14px 20px",
          borderRadius: 12,
          border: "1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.4)",
          boxShadow: "0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.3)",
          lineHeight: 1.6,
          zIndex: 50,
          minWidth: 440,
        }}
      >
        <div style={{ color: "#38BDF8", fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 16, marginBottom: 4 }}>
          METRIC HUD (FORWARD KINEMATICS) | {isPart1 ? "VIDEO CUA BENCHMARK" : "PROPOSED JOG BENCHMARK"}
        </div>
        <div>
          <span style={{ color: "#94A3B8" }}>Khung hÃ¬nh:</span> <strong>f{frame}</strong> |{" "}
          <span style={{ color: "#94A3B8" }}>Thá»i gian:</span>{" "}
          <strong>{(frame / fps).toFixed(3)}s</strong> / {(TOTAL_FRAMES / fps).toFixed(1)}s
        </div>
        <div>
          <span style={{ color: "#94A3B8" }}>Pha cá»­ Ä‘á»™ng:</span>{" "}
          <span style={{ color: "#FBBF24", fontWeight: 700 }}>{phaseName}</span>
        </div>
        <div>
          <span style={{ color: "#94A3B8" }}>Má»‘c quy chiáº¿u:</span>{" "}
          <span style={{ color: "#34D399" }}>{milestoneRef}</span>
        </div>
        <div>
          <span style={{ color: "#94A3B8" }}>Khoáº£ng cÃ¡ch 2 chÃ¢n (FK):</span>{" "}
          <strong style={{ color: "#FDE047" }}>{legSpanDisplay} px</strong> |{" "}
          <span style={{ color: "#94A3B8" }}>Tiáº¿p Ä‘áº¥t:</span>{" "}
          <span style={{ color: stanceFoot.includes("BAY") ? "#F43F5E" : "#A7F3D0" }}>{stanceFoot}</span>
        </div>
        <div>
          <span style={{ color: "#94A3B8" }}>Tá»a Ä‘á»™ thÃ¢n (X, Y):</span>{" "}
          <strong>({charX.toFixed(1)}, {charY.toFixed(1)})</strong> |{" "}
          <span style={{ color: "#94A3B8" }}>NghiÃªng thÃ¢n:</span> <strong>{torsoTilt.toFixed(1)}Â°</strong>
        </div>
        <div>
          <span style={{ color: "#94A3B8" }}>Khá»›p hÃ¡ng:</span>{" "}
          <span style={{ color: "#A7F3D0" }}>Hai Ä‘Ã¹i phÃ¢n nhÃ¡nh riÃªng (Â±16px, Inverted V)</span>
        </div>
        <div>
          <span style={{ color: "#94A3B8" }}>Khá»›p cá»• tay:</span>{" "}
          <span style={{ color: "#A7F3D0" }}>Äáº§u cáº³ng tay = BÃ n tay = Grip Anchor (0px gap)</span>
        </div>
        <div>
          <span style={{ color: "#94A3B8" }}>Äá»™ trÆ°á»£t tiáº¿p Ä‘áº¥t:</span>{" "}
          <span style={{ color: "#34D399", fontWeight: 700 }}>{slipReport}</span>
