==================== FILE: C:\Users\ASUS\Downloads\TOOL_2D_ANIM_API_FIX_1\TOOL_2D_ANIM\web-studio\src\remotion\lib\autoAnchoring.ts ====================
import { Point2D, PropAnchorPorts } from "../types/character";

export interface SvgDimensionInfo {
  width: number;
  height: number;
  minX?: number;
  minY?: number;
}

/**
 * PhÃ¢n tÃ­ch cÃº phÃ¡p chuá»—i SVG Ä‘á»ƒ tá»± Ä‘á»™ng bÃ³c tÃ¡ch viewBox hoáº·c thuá»™c tÃ­nh width/height.
 */
export function extractSvgDimensions(svgText: string): SvgDimensionInfo {
  let width = 240;
  let height = 240;
  let minX = 0;
  let minY = 0;

  // 1. TÃ¬m viewBox="minX minY width height"
  const viewBoxMatch = svgText.match(/viewBox=["']\s*([-\d.]+)\s+([-\d.]+)\s+([\d.]+)\s+([\d.]+)\s*["']/i);
  if (viewBoxMatch) {
    minX = parseFloat(viewBoxMatch[1]);
    minY = parseFloat(viewBoxMatch[2]);
    width = parseFloat(viewBoxMatch[3]);
    height = parseFloat(viewBoxMatch[4]);
    return { width, height, minX, minY };
  }

  // 2. TÃ¬m width="..." vÃ  height="..."
  const widthMatch = svgText.match(/width=["']([\d.]+)(?:px)?["']/i);
  const heightMatch = svgText.match(/height=["']([\d.]+)(?:px)?["']/i);
  if (widthMatch) width = parseFloat(widthMatch[1]);
  if (heightMatch) height = parseFloat(heightMatch[1]);

  return { width, height, minX, minY };
}

/**
 * Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng tÃ­nh toÃ¡n cÃ¡c cá»•ng neo toÃ¡n há»c (Auto-Anchoring) dá»±a trÃªn Bounding Box thá»±c táº¿ cá»§a SVG.
 * 
 * QUY CHUáº¨N ORIGIN = BOTTOM-CENTER (Gá»‘c tá»a Ä‘á»™ táº¡i ChÃ­nh Giá»¯a ÄÃ¡y):
 * - Äiá»ƒm (0, 0) lÃ  Ä‘Ã¡y chÃ­nh giá»¯a cá»§a váº­t thá»ƒ (cháº¡m sÃ n / cháº¡m máº·t bÃ n).
 * - baseCenter / bottomAnchor: (0, 0)
 * - topAnchor / lidCenter: Äá»‰nh trÃªn (0, -height)
 * - surfaceDecal: TÃ¢m máº·t trÆ°á»›c (0, -height * 0.5)
 * - leftGrip: Cáº¡nh trÃ¡i cáº§m náº¯m (-width * 0.42, -height * 0.45)
 * - rightGrip: Cáº¡nh pháº£i cáº§m náº¯m (+width * 0.42, -height * 0.45)
 */
export function computeAutoAnchorPorts(
  dimensions: SvgDimensionInfo,
  transform: { x: number; y: number; scale?: number; rotationDeg?: number; anchorPoint?: "bottomCenter" | "center" }
): PropAnchorPorts {
  const { width, height } = dimensions;
  const scale = transform.scale ?? 1;
  const rotationDeg = transform.rotationDeg ?? 0;
  const isCentered = transform.anchorPoint === "center";
  const rad = (rotationDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
  const cos = Math.cos(rad);
  const sin = Math.sin(rad);

  // Helper xoay Ä‘iá»ƒm cá»¥c bá»™ vÃ  cá»™ng offset toÃ n cá»¥c (x, y)
  const toGlobal = (localX: number, localY: number): Point2D => {
    const scaledX = localX * scale;
    const scaledY = localY * scale;
    return {
      x: transform.x + (scaledX * cos - scaledY * sin),
      y: transform.y + (scaledX * sin + scaledY * cos),
    };
  };

  if (isCentered) {
    return {
      leftGrip: toGlobal(-width * 0.42, 0),
      rightGrip: toGlobal(width * 0.42, 0),
      surfaceDecal: toGlobal(0, -height * 0.22),
      lidCenter: toGlobal(0, -height / 2),
      baseCenter: toGlobal(0, height / 2),
    };
  }

  return {
    leftGrip: toGlobal(-width * 0.42, -height * 0.45),
    rightGrip: toGlobal(width * 0.42, -height * 0.45),
    surfaceDecal: toGlobal(0, -height * 0.5),
    lidCenter: toGlobal(0, -height),
    baseCenter: toGlobal(0, 0),
  };
}


==================== FILE: C:\Users\ASUS\Downloads\TOOL_2D_ANIM_API_FIX_1\TOOL_2D_ANIM\web-studio\src\remotion\lib\motionEngine.ts ====================
import { spring, interpolate } from "remotion";

/**
 * Remotion Spring Physics Motion Engine
 * Cung cáº¥p cÃ¡c hÃ m chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»™ng há»¯u cÆ¡ (organic motion) thay tháº¿ hoÃ n toÃ n linear interpolation.
 */

export interface SpringConfig {
  damping?: number;
  stiffness?: number;
  mass?: number;
  overshootClamping?: boolean;
}

const SOFT_SPRING: SpringConfig = { damping: 14, stiffness: 80, mass: 0.8 };
const BOUNCY_SPRING: SpringConfig = { damping: 10, stiffness: 120, mass: 0.6 };
const SNAPPY_SPRING: SpringConfig = { damping: 20, stiffness: 200, mass: 0.5 };
const GENTLE_SPRING: SpringConfig = { damping: 18, stiffness: 60, mass: 1.0 };

export const springPresets = {
  soft: SOFT_SPRING,
  bouncy: BOUNCY_SPRING,
  snappy: SNAPPY_SPRING,
  gentle: GENTLE_SPRING,
} as const;

/**
 * Táº¡o giÃ¡ trá»‹ spring 0â†’1 báº¯t Ä‘áº§u táº¡i má»™t frame cá»¥ thá»ƒ.
 */
export function springEnter(
  frame: number,
  fps: number,
  delay: number = 0,
  config: SpringConfig = SOFT_SPRING
): number {
  return spring({
    frame: Math.max(0, frame - delay),
    fps,
    config: {
      damping: config.damping ?? 14,
      stiffness: config.stiffness ?? 80,
      mass: config.mass ?? 0.8,
      overshootClamping: config.overshootClamping ?? false,
    },
  });
}

/**
 * Spring scale cho hiá»‡u á»©ng xuáº¥t hiá»‡n "pop-in" (0â†’1 vá»›i overshoot nháº¹).
 */
export function springScale(
  frame: number,
  fps: number,
  delay: number = 0,
  config: SpringConfig = BOUNCY_SPRING
): number {
  return springEnter(frame, fps, delay, config);
}

/**
 * Spring dá»‹ch chuyá»ƒn tá»« vá»‹ trÃ­ A sang vá»‹ trÃ­ B.
 */
export function springMove(
  frame: number,
  fps: number,
  from: number,
  to: number,
  delay: number = 0,
  config: SpringConfig = SOFT_SPRING
): number {
  const progress = springEnter(frame, fps, delay, config);
  return interpolate(progress, [0, 1], [from, to]);
}

// =========================================================================
// MICRO-ANIMATIONS (Chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»™ng vi mÃ´ cho nhÃ¢n váº­t sá»‘ng Ä‘á»™ng)
// =========================================================================

/**
 * Hiá»‡u á»©ng thá»Ÿ pháº­p phá»“ng cho lá»“ng ngá»±c (Sine wave oscillation).
 * Tráº£ vá» giÃ¡ trá»‹ scale Y dao Ä‘á»™ng trong khoáº£ng [1 - amplitude, 1 + amplitude].
 */
export function breathingOscillation(
  frame: number,
  fps: number,
  cycleSeconds: number = 3.0,
  amplitude: number = 0.038
): number {
  const phase = (frame / fps) * (Math.PI * 2) / cycleSeconds;
  return 1.0 + Math.sin(phase) * amplitude;
}

/**
 * Nhá»‹p nhÃ¡y máº¯t ngáº«u nhiÃªn (blink) tráº£ vá» boolean.
 * NhÃ¡y khoáº£ng 3-5 láº§n má»—i 60 giÃ¢y, má»—i láº§n nhÃ¡y kÃ©o dÃ i ~6 frames.
 */
export function shouldBlink(frame: number, fps: number, seed: number = 42): boolean {
  const blinkDuration = 6; // frames
  // Sá»­ dá»¥ng hÃ m toÃ¡n chia pháº§n dÆ° Ä‘á»ƒ táº¡o blink pattern giáº£ ngáº«u nhiÃªn
  const blinkIntervals = [
    Math.floor(1.2 * fps),
    Math.floor(3.7 * fps),
    Math.floor(5.1 * fps),
    Math.floor(7.8 * fps),
    Math.floor(9.4 * fps),
    Math.floor(12.0 * fps),
    Math.floor(14.5 * fps),
    Math.floor(16.3 * fps),
  ];

  for (const interval of blinkIntervals) {
    const offset = ((interval + seed * 7) % (Math.floor(fps * 4))) + Math.floor(fps * 2);
    const cycleFrame = frame % offset;
    if (cycleFrame >= 0 && cycleFrame < blinkDuration) {
      return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

/**
 * Hiá»‡u á»©ng nÃ³i miá»‡ng má»Ÿ-Ä‘Ã³ng nhá»‹p nhÃ ng (lip-sync Ä‘Æ¡n giáº£n).
 * Khoáº£ng 4-5 láº§n má»Ÿ miá»‡ng má»—i giÃ¢y.
 */
export function talkingMouthCycle(frame: number, fps: number): boolean {
  const frequency = 5; // syllables per second
  const phase = (frame / fps) * frequency * Math.PI * 2;
  return Math.sin(phase) > 0.2;
}

/**
 * Hiá»‡u á»©ng láº¯c lÆ° nháº¹ (idle sway) theo trá»¥c X cho nhÃ¢n váº­t Ä‘á»©ng yÃªn.
 */
export function idleSway(
  frame: number,
  fps: number,
  amplitudeX: number = 14,
  cycleSeconds: number = 3.6
): number {
  const phase = (frame / fps) * (Math.PI * 2) / cycleSeconds;
  return Math.sin(phase) * amplitudeX;
}

/**
 * Hiá»‡u á»©ng dao Ä‘á»™ng láº¯c lÆ° cho con nhá»‡n trong lá».
 */
export function spiderSway(
  frame: number,
  fps: number,
  amplitudeX: number = 6,
  amplitudeY: number = 3
): { offsetX: number; offsetY: number; rotation: number } {
  const phaseX = (frame / fps) * Math.PI * 2 / 2.8; // ~2.8s cycle
  const phaseY = (frame / fps) * Math.PI * 2 / 1.6; // ~1.6s cycle
  return {
    offsetX: Math.sin(phaseX) * amplitudeX,
    offsetY: Math.sin(phaseY) * amplitudeY,
    rotation: Math.sin(phaseX * 0.7) * 5, // subtle rotation Â±5 degrees
  };
}

/**
 * Hiá»‡u á»©ng Squash & Stretch (NÃ©n/GiÃ£n) khi váº­t thá»ƒ rÆ¡i cháº¡m Ä‘áº¥t.
 * @param frame Frame hiá»‡n táº¡i (tÆ°Æ¡ng Ä‘á»‘i so vá»›i thá»i Ä‘iá»ƒm cháº¡m Ä‘áº¥t)
 * @param fps FPS
 * @param impactFrame Frame xáº£y ra va cháº¡m (tÆ°Æ¡ng Ä‘á»‘i)
 * @returns {{ scaleX: number; scaleY: number }}
 */
export function squashStretch(
  frame: number,
  fps: number,
  impactFrame: number = 0
): { scaleX: number; scaleY: number } {
  const elapsed = frame - impactFrame;
  if (elapsed < 0) {
    // Pre-impact: Stretch khi rÆ¡i xuá»‘ng
    const stretchFactor = 1.0 + Math.min(0.15, Math.abs(elapsed) / fps * 0.4);
    return { scaleX: 1.0 / stretchFactor, scaleY: stretchFactor };
  }

  // Post-impact: Squash rá»“i náº£y láº¡i
  const recovery = spring({
    frame: elapsed,
    fps,
    config: { damping: 8, stiffness: 180, mass: 0.5 },
  });

  const squashPeak = 0.25;
  const squashAmount = interpolate(recovery, [0, 1], [squashPeak, 0]);
  return {
    scaleX: 1.0 + squashAmount,
    scaleY: 1.0 - squashAmount,
  };
}

/**
 * Hiá»‡u á»©ng sá»£i tÆ¡ nhá»‡n tháº£ tá»« tráº§n nhÃ  xuá»‘ng (spring dangle).
 * Tráº£ vá» tá»a Ä‘á»™ Y Ä‘Ã¡y dÃ¢y tÆ¡.
 */
export function spiderSilkDangle(
  frame: number,
  fps: number,
  ceilingY: number,
  targetY: number,
  delay: number = 0
): number {
  const progress = springEnter(frame, fps, delay, {
    damping: 6,
    stiffness: 40,
    mass: 1.2,
  });
  return interpolate(progress, [0, 1], [ceilingY, targetY]);
}

/**
 * Loáº¡i bá» dáº¥u tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t (NFD Unicode normalization)
 */
export function removeVietnameseAccents(str: string): string {
  return str
    .normalize("NFD")
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
    .replace(/Ä‘/g, "d")
    .replace(/Ä/g, "D");
}

/**
 * Chuáº©n hÃ³a chuá»—i Ä‘áº§u vÃ o thÃ nh dáº¡ng kebab-case khÃ´ng dáº¥u
 */
export function toKebabKey(raw: string): string {
  return removeVietnameseAccents(String(raw || ""))
    .trim()
    .replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, "$1-$2") // camelCase to kebab
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-") // thay tháº¿ kÃ½ tá»± láº¡, gáº¡ch dÆ°á»›i, khoáº£ng tráº¯ng báº±ng '-'
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, ""); // xoÃ¡ dáº¥u gáº¡ch á»Ÿ Ä‘áº§u vÃ  cuá»‘i
}

/**
 * Danh sÃ¡ch Token ghÃ©p biÃªn Ä‘á»™ Æ°u tiÃªn cao khi chuá»—i cÃ³ tiá»n tá»‘/háº­u tá»‘.
 * Sá»­ dá»¥ng Regex biÃªn tá»« cháº·t cháº½ (^|-)token(-|$) Ä‘á»ƒ trÃ¡nh báº¯t nháº§m chá»¯ cÃ¡i lá»t trong tá»« khÃ¡c!
 */
const TOKEN_BOUNDARIES: Array<{ regex: RegExp; canonicalId: CanonicalPropId }> = [
  // CÃ  phÃª / Cá»‘c nÆ°á»›c (trÃ¡nh dÃ­nh co -> bush)
  { regex: /(?:^|-)(?:coffee-cup|coffee|teacup|tach-ca-phe|ly-ca-phe|coc-ca-phe)(?:-|$)/i, canonicalId: "coffee-cup" },
  // CÃºp vÃ ng (trÃ¡nh cup -> coffee-cup)
  { regex: /(?:^|-)(?:trophy|cup-vang|giai-thuong)(?:-|$)/i, canonicalId: "trophy" },
  // Laptop / MÃ¡y tÃ­nh (trÃ¡nh computer -> bush)
  { regex: /(?:^|-)(?:laptop|computer|may-tinh|may-tinh-xach-tay)(?:-|$)/i, canonicalId: "laptop" },
  // MÃ¨o (trÃ¡nh con meo -> bush)
  { regex: /(?:^|-)(?:cat|meo|con-meo|chu-meo)(?:-|$)/i, canonicalId: "cat" },
  // CÃ¢y to (trÃ¡nh cay xanh -> potted-plant)
  { regex: /(?:^|-)(?:tree|cay-xanh|cay-to|cay-lon|cay-co-thu)(?:-|$)/i, canonicalId: "tree" },
  // BÃ n Äƒn (trÃ¡nh ban an -> desk)
  { regex: /(?:^|-)(?:table|ban-an|ban-tron|dining-table)(?:-|$)/i, canonicalId: "table" },
  // ÄÃ¨n bÃ n (Æ¯u tiÃªn cao trÆ°á»›c bÃ n há»c desk Ä‘á»ƒ trÃ¡nh desk-lamp dÃ­nh sang desk)
  { regex: /(?:^|-)(?:desk-lamp|desklamp|den-ban|den-hoc|reading-lamp)(?:-|$)/i, canonicalId: "desk-lamp" },
  // BÃ n há»c
  { regex: /(?:^|-)(?:desk-front|desk-back)(?:-|$)/i, canonicalId: "desk" },
  { regex: /(?:^|-)(?:desk|ban-hoc|ban-lam-viec)(?:-|$)/i, canonicalId: "desk" },
  // Cháº­u cÃ¢y
  { regex: /(?:^|-)(?:potted-plant|chau-cay|chau-hoa|chau-canh|cay-canh)(?:-|$)/i, canonicalId: "potted-plant" },
  // Bá»¥i cÃ¢y / Tháº£m cá» (chá»‰ khá»›p Ä‘Ãºng token Ä‘á»™c láº­p 'bush', 'bui-cay', 'bui-co', 'tham-co', 'hedge', 'shrub')
  { regex: /(?:^|-)(?:bush|bui-cay|bui-co|tham-co|hedge|shrub)(?:-|$)/i, canonicalId: "bush" },
  // Lá» thá»§y tinh
  { regex: /(?:^|-)(?:glass-jar|jar|thuy-tinh|lo-thuy|chiec-lo)(?:-|$)/i, canonicalId: "glass-jar" },
  // Nhá»‡n
  { regex: /(?:^|-)(?:spider|nhen|con-nhen|tam)(?:-|$)/i, canonicalId: "spider" },
  // ÄÃ¨n bÃ n
  { regex: /(?:^|-)(?:desk-lamp|den-ban|den-hoc)(?:-|$)/i, canonicalId: "desk-lamp" },
  // Äá»“ng há»“ Ä‘o / Tá»‘c Ä‘á»™ / Trao Ä‘á»•i cháº¥t (Táº¥t cáº£ quy vá» gauge-meter chuáº©n)
  { regex: /(?:^|-)(?:gauge-meter|gauge|speed-meter|speedometer|speedmeter|speed-gauge|dong-ho-do|trao-doi-chat|metabolic|metabolism)(?:-|$)/i, canonicalId: "gauge-meter" },
  // MÅ©i tÃªn / Chá»‰ dáº«n
  { regex: /(?:^|-)(?:arrow-indicator|arrow|pointer-arrow|pointer|mui-ten|chi-dan)(?:-|$)/i, canonicalId: "arrow-indicator" },
  // NhÃ£n dÃ¡n
  { regex: /(?:^|-)(?:label-sticker|label|sticker|nhan-dan)(?:-|$)/i, canonicalId: "label-sticker" },
  // Äá»“ng há»“ bÃ¡o thá»©c
  { regex: /(?:^|-)(?:alarm-clock|dong-ho-bao-thuc)(?:-|$)/i, canonicalId: "alarm-clock" },
  // Äá»“ng há»“
  { regex: /(?:^|-)(?:clock|dong-ho)(?:-|$)/i, canonicalId: "clock" },
  // Ã” tÃ´
  { regex: /(?:^|-)(?:car|o-to|xe-hoi|xe-o-to)(?:-|$)/i, canonicalId: "car" },
];

export interface PropResolutionResult {
  canonicalId: string;
  isResolved: boolean;
  status: "canonical" | "alias" | "token" | "unknown";
  raw: string;
}

/**
 * PhÃ¢n giáº£i chi tiáº¿t kÃ¨m tráº¡ng thÃ¡i
 */
export function resolvePropAsset(rawType?: string): PropResolutionResult {
  if (!rawType) {
    return {
      canonicalId: "unknown-prop",
      isResolved: false,
      status: "unknown",
      raw: "",
    };
  }

  const key = toKebabKey(rawType);

  // 1. Kiá»ƒm tra trá»±c tiáº¿p xem Ä‘Ã£ lÃ  ID chuáº©n chÆ°a
  if (CANONICAL_SET.has(key)) {
    return {
      canonicalId: key as CanonicalPropId,
      isResolved: true,
      status: "canonical",
      raw: rawType,
    };
  }

  // 2. Tra cá»©u báº£ng Alias chÃ­nh xÃ¡c
  if (EXACT_PROP_ALIASES[key]) {
    return {
      canonicalId: EXACT_PROP_ALIASES[key],
      isResolved: true,
      status: "alias",
      raw: rawType,
    };
  }

  // 3. Khá»›p tá»« khÃ³a cÃ³ ranh giá»›i tá»« cháº·t cháº½ (Token boundaries)
  for (const { regex, canonicalId } of TOKEN_BOUNDARIES) {
    if (regex.test(key)) {
      return {
        canonicalId,
        isResolved: true,
        status: "token",
        raw: rawType,
      };
    }
  }

  // 4. Fallback: Báº£o toÃ n Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng kebab cá»§a prop thay vÃ¬ Ã¢m tháº§m Ä‘á»•i sang Ä‘á»“ váº­t khÃ¡c!
  return {
    canonicalId: key || "prop",
    isResolved: false,
    status: "unknown",
    raw: rawType,
  };
}

/**
 * HÃ m phÃ¢n giáº£i chÃ­nh (Drop-in replacement cho code cÅ©)
 * LuÃ´n tráº£ vá» tÃªn file SVG chuáº©n tÆ°Æ¡ng á»©ng
 */
export function normalizePropAssetId(rawType?: string): string {
  return resolvePropAsset(rawType).canonicalId;
}


==================== FILE: C:\Users\ASUS\Downloads\TOOL_2D_ANIM_API_FIX_1\TOOL_2D_ANIM\web-studio\src\remotion\utils\sceneGraphSanitizer.ts ====================
import {
  MasterSceneGraph,
  SceneGraphScene,
  EnvironmentVfxType,
  BackgroundPreset,
  SceneKeyframe,
  PropItem,
} from "../types/sceneGraph";
import { normalizePropAssetId } from "./propResolver";

/**
 * Trá»£ thá»§ báº£o toÃ n giÃ¡ trá»‹ sá»‘ (Báº£o toÃ n sá»‘ 0, khÃ´ng coi 0 lÃ  falsy)
 */
export function numOrDef(val: any, defaultVal: number): number {
  if (val !== undefined && val !== null && !isNaN(Number(val))) {
    return Number(val);
  }
  return defaultVal;
}

/**
 * Trá»£ thá»§ báº£o toÃ n giÃ¡ trá»‹ sá»‘ tÃ¹y chá»n (tráº£ vá» undefined náº¿u khÃ´ng cÃ³)
 */
export function numOrUndef(val: any): number | undefined {
  if (val !== undefined && val !== null && !isNaN(Number(val))) {
    return Number(val);
  }
  return undefined;
}

/**
 * TÃ¡ch bá» chá»‰ dáº«n hÃ¬nh áº£nh / mÃ¡y quay khá»i lá»i Ä‘á»c (Narration vs Visual Direction Separation)
 * VÃ­ dá»¥: "PhÃ³ng cáº­n cáº£nh vÃ o chiáº¿c lá» thá»§y tinh, tÃ´i dÃ¡n nhÃ£n Ä‘áº·t tÃªn cho nÃ³ lÃ  'TÃ¡m'."
 *        -> "TÃ´i dÃ¡n nhÃ£n Ä‘áº·t tÃªn cho nÃ³ lÃ  'TÃ¡m'."
 */
export function stripDirectorialInstructions(text: string): string {
  if (!text) return "";
  let clean = String(text).trim();

  // Loáº¡i bá» nhÃ£n cáº£nh (Cáº£nh 1:, Scene 2...)
  clean = clean.replace(/^(?:Cáº£nh|Scene)\s*\d+[\s:.-]*/i, "");

  // Loáº¡i bá» cÃ¡c chá»‰ dáº«n gÃ³c mÃ¡y, bá»‘ cá»¥c, minh há»a Ä‘á»©ng á»Ÿ Ä‘áº§u cÃ¢u cÃ³ dáº¥u ngáº¯t
  clean = clean.replace(
    /^(?:PhÃ³ng cáº­n cáº£nh|Cáº­n cáº£nh|ToÃ n cáº£nh|Trung cáº£nh|Minh há»a biá»ƒu Ä‘á»“|Minh há»a|GÃ³c quay|HÃ¬nh áº£nh|Quay cáº­n|Cáº­n cáº£nh vÃ o|PhÃ³ng to vÃ o|PhÃ³ng to|Chuyá»ƒn cáº£nh sang)\s*(?:vÃ o|vá»|chiáº¿c|con|ngÆ°á»i|hÃ¬nh|Ã´|khung|mÃ n hÃ¬nh|phÃ²ng)?\s*[^,.:\n]{0,60}[,.:-]\s*/i,
    ""
  );

  // Loáº¡i bá» cÃ¡c cá»¥m chá»‰ dáº«n gÃ³c mÃ¡y á»Ÿ Ä‘áº§u cÃ¢u ká»ƒ cáº£ khi khÃ´ng cÃ³ dáº¥u pháº©y ngÄƒn cÃ¡ch
  clean = clean.replace(
    /^(?:PhÃ³ng cáº­n cáº£nh|Cáº­n cáº£nh|ToÃ n cáº£nh|Trung cáº£nh|Quay cáº­n|PhÃ³ng to vÃ o|PhÃ³ng to|Chuyá»ƒn cáº£nh sang)\s+(?:vÃ o|vá»)\s+/i,
    ""
  );
  clean = clean.replace(/^(?:Minh há»a biá»ƒu Ä‘á»“|Minh há»a)\s+/i, "");

  // Viáº¿t hoa láº¡i chá»¯ cÃ¡i Ä‘áº§u
  if (clean.length > 0) {
    clean = clean.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + clean.slice(1);
  }

  return clean.trim() || text.trim();
}

/**
 * Chuáº©n hÃ³a má»™t Keyframe dá»±a trÃªn Action phÃ¢n biá»‡t (Discriminated Action Keyframe Sanitizer)
 * Giá»¯ nguyÃªn 100% tham sá»‘ Ä‘áº·c thÃ¹ cá»§a tá»«ng loáº¡i action (eyes, mouth, fromScale, toScale, fromDeg, toDeg...)
 */
export function sanitizeKeyframe(kf: any, defaultFrame: number = 0): SceneKeyframe {
  const action = String(kf?.action || kf?.type || "changeExpression") as SceneKeyframe["action"];
  const target = String(kf?.target || kf?.targetId || (action === "moveCharacter" || action === "changeExpression" ? "character" : "prop"));
  const frame = numOrDef(kf?.frame !== undefined ? kf.frame : kf?.startFrame, defaultFrame);

  const rawParams = kf?.params && typeof kf.params === "object" ? { ...kf.params } : { ...kf };

  let sanitizedParams: Record<string, any> = {};

  switch (action) {
    case "changeExpression": {
      if (rawParams.eyes) sanitizedParams.eyes = String(rawParams.eyes);
      if (rawParams.mouth) sanitizedParams.mouth = String(rawParams.mouth);
      if (rawParams.eyebrows) sanitizedParams.eyebrows = String(rawParams.eyebrows);
      if (rawParams.blush !== undefined) sanitizedParams.blush = Boolean(rawParams.blush);
      if (rawParams.sweatDrop !== undefined) sanitizedParams.sweatDrop = Boolean(rawParams.sweatDrop);
      if (rawParams.pupilGaze && typeof rawParams.pupilGaze === "object") {
        sanitizedParams.pupilGaze = {
          x: numOrDef(rawParams.pupilGaze.x, 0),
          y: numOrDef(rawParams.pupilGaze.y, 0),
        };
      }
      break;
    }

    case "scaleProp": {
      sanitizedParams.fromScale = numOrDef(rawParams.fromScale, 1.0);
      sanitizedParams.toScale = numOrDef(rawParams.toScale, 1.5);
      if (rawParams.target || rawParams.targetId) {
        sanitizedParams.targetId = String(rawParams.target || rawParams.targetId);
      }
      break;
    }

    case "rotateProp": {
      sanitizedParams.fromDeg = numOrDef(rawParams.fromDeg, 0);
      sanitizedParams.toDeg = numOrDef(rawParams.toDeg, 45);
      if (rawParams.target || rawParams.targetId) {
        sanitizedParams.targetId = String(rawParams.target || rawParams.targetId);
      }
      break;
    }

    case "moveCharacter":
    case "moveProp": {
      if (rawParams.fromSlot) sanitizedParams.fromSlot = String(rawParams.fromSlot);
      if (rawParams.toSlot) sanitizedParams.toSlot = String(rawParams.toSlot);
      if (rawParams.fromX !== undefined) sanitizedParams.fromX = Number(rawParams.fromX);
      if (rawParams.toX !== undefined) sanitizedParams.toX = Number(rawParams.toX);
      if (rawParams.fromY !== undefined) sanitizedParams.fromY = Number(rawParams.fromY);
      if (rawParams.toY !== undefined) sanitizedParams.toY = Number(rawParams.toY);
      if (rawParams.durationFrames !== undefined) sanitizedParams.durationFrames = Number(rawParams.durationFrames);
      break;
    }

    case "squashImpact": {
      sanitizedParams.impactFrame = numOrDef(rawParams.impactFrame, frame);
      break;
    }

    case "cameraZoom": {
      sanitizedParams.zoomFrom = numOrDef(rawParams.zoomFrom, 1.0);
      sanitizedParams.zoomTo = numOrDef(rawParams.zoomTo, 1.5);
      if (rawParams.durationFrames !== undefined) {
        sanitizedParams.durationFrames = numOrDef(rawParams.durationFrames, 30);
      }
      break;
    }

    case "cameraPan": {
      if (rawParams.fromX !== undefined) sanitizedParams.fromX = Number(rawParams.fromX);
      if (rawParams.toX !== undefined) sanitizedParams.toX = Number(rawParams.toX);
      if (rawParams.fromY !== undefined) sanitizedParams.fromY = Number(rawParams.fromY);
      if (rawParams.toY !== undefined) sanitizedParams.toY = Number(rawParams.toY);
      if (rawParams.durationFrames !== undefined) {
        sanitizedParams.durationFrames = numOrDef(rawParams.durationFrames, 30);
      }
      break;
    }

    default: {
      for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(rawParams)) {
        if (k !== "action" && k !== "type" && k !== "target" && k !== "frame" && k !== "startFrame") {
          sanitizedParams[k] = v;
        }
      }
      break;
    }
  }

  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(rawParams)) {
    if (sanitizedParams[k] === undefined && k !== "action" && k !== "type" && k !== "target" && k !== "frame" && k !== "startFrame") {
      sanitizedParams[k] = v;
    }
  }

  return {
    action,
    target,
    frame,
    params: sanitizedParams,
    springConfig: kf?.springConfig || undefined,
  };
}

/**
 * Chuáº©n hÃ³a vÃ  báº£o toÃ n toÃ n bá»™ dá»¯ liá»‡u MasterSceneGraph
 */
export function sanitizeSceneGraph(
  raw: any,
  userPrompt?: string
): MasterSceneGraph & { sanitizerAuditLog?: string[] } {
  const scenesRaw = Array.isArray(raw?.scenes) ? raw.scenes : [];
  const auditLog: string[] = [];

  let currentStart = 0;

  // TrÃ­ch xuáº¥t cÃ¡c cÃ¢u thoáº¡i tá»« prompt
  const promptSceneTexts: string[] = [];
  if (userPrompt && typeof userPrompt === "string") {
    const lines = userPrompt.split(/\r?\n/).map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    for (const line of lines) {
      const match = line.match(/^(?:cáº£nh|scene)\s*\d+[:.-]?\s*(.*)$/i);
      if (match && match[1].trim()) {
        promptSceneTexts.push(match[1].trim());
      } else if (line.length > 5 && !line.startsWith("#")) {
        promptSceneTexts.push(line);
      }
    }
  }

  const cleanScenes: SceneGraphScene[] = scenesRaw.map((s: any, idx: number) => {
    // 1. Thu tháº­p phá»¥ Ä‘á»: há»— trá»£ cáº£ Array s.subtitles, Object s.subtitles, hoáº·c s.subtitle
    let rawSubs: any[] = [];
    if (Array.isArray(s?.subtitles)) {
      rawSubs = s.subtitles;
    } else if (s?.subtitles && typeof s.subtitles === "object") {
      rawSubs = [s.subtitles];
    } else if (s?.subtitle && typeof s.subtitle === "object") {
      rawSubs = [s.subtitle];
    }

    const existingSubs = rawSubs.filter(
      (sub: any) => sub?.text && String(sub.text).trim().length > 0
    );

    let rawSubText = "";
    if (existingSubs.length > 0) {
      rawSubText = existingSubs.map((sub: any) => sub.text).join(" ");
    } else {
      rawSubText =
        promptSceneTexts[idx] ||
        (s?.name && !/^cáº£nh\s*\d+$/i.test(s.name) ? s.name : "") ||
        s?.description ||
        "";
    }

    // TÃ¡ch sáº¡ch chá»‰ dáº«n hÃ¬nh áº£nh/camera khá»i phá»¥ Ä‘á» spoken narration
    const cleanSpokenText = stripDirectorialInstructions(rawSubText);
    const wordCount = cleanSpokenText ? cleanSpokenText.split(/\s+/).length : 0;
    const estDialogueFrames = wordCount > 0 ? Math.round((wordCount / 2.8) * 60) + 30 : 120;

    let dur = Math.max(60, Math.round(Number(s?.durationInFrames || s?.durationFrames || s?.duration || estDialogueFrames)));
    if (wordCount > 0 && dur > estDialogueFrames + 60) {
      dur = estDialogueFrames + 30;
    }

    const start = currentStart;
    currentStart = start + dur;

    const sceneCombinedText = `${s?.name || ""} ${rawSubText} ${promptSceneTexts[idx] || ""}`.toLowerCase();

    // 2. PhÃ¢n giáº£i bá»‘i cáº£nh Background Preset chÃ­nh xÃ¡c (Há»— trá»£ 9 Bá»‘i cáº£nh Äá»™ng 2K)
    let preset: BackgroundPreset = "livingRoomDay";
    if (s?.background?.preset) {
      const rawP = String(s.background.preset).trim().toLowerCase();
      if (/meadow|garden|grass|lawn|field|park|forest|nature|outdoor|sky/i.test(rawP)) {
        preset = "gardenDay";
      } else if (/class|school|lecture|teacher/i.test(rawP)) {
        preset = "classroom";
      } else if (/office|workplace|desk|corp|meeting/i.test(rawP)) {
        preset = "office";
      } else if (/street|city|road|urban|traffic/i.test(rawP)) {
        preset = "cityStreet";
      } else if (/beach|sea|ocean|sand/i.test(rawP)) {
        preset = "beachSunny";
      } else if (/night-sky|night_sky|star|galaxy|space/i.test(rawP)) {
        preset = "nightSky";
      } else if (/night|dark|evening|moon/i.test(rawP)) {
        preset = "livingRoomNight";
      } else if (/info|graph|chart|metric|stripes/i.test(rawP)) {
        preset = "infographicStripes";
      } else if (["livingRoomDay", "livingRoomNight", "gardenDay", "infographicStripes", "classroom", "office", "cityStreet", "beachSunny", "nightSky"].includes(s.background.preset)) {
        preset = s.background.preset;
      }
    }

    if (preset === "livingRoomDay") {
      if (/lá»›p há»c|trÆ°á»ng há»c|giáº£ng Ä‘Æ°á»ng|bÃ i giáº£ng|tháº§y|cÃ´ giÃ¡o|báº£ng Ä‘en|classroom|school/i.test(sceneCombinedText)) {
        preset = "classroom";
      } else if (/vÄƒn phÃ²ng|cÃ´ng ty|cÃ´ng sá»Ÿ|há»p|doanh nghiá»‡p|office|workplace/i.test(sceneCombinedText)) {
        preset = "office";
      } else if (/Ä‘Æ°á»ng phá»‘|phá»‘ xÃ¡|vá»‰a hÃ¨|thÃ nh phá»‘|Ä‘Ã´ thá»‹|street|city/i.test(sceneCombinedText)) {
        preset = "cityStreet";
      } else if (/bÃ£i biá»ƒn|biá»ƒn|bá» cÃ¡t|sÃ³ng biá»ƒn|mÃ¹a hÃ¨|cÃ¢y dá»«a|beach|sea/i.test(sceneCombinedText)) {
        preset = "beachSunny";
      } else if (/báº§u trá»i Ä‘Ãªm|ngáº¯m sao|Ä‘Ãªm Ä‘áº§y sao|vÅ© trá»¥|dáº£i ngÃ¢n hÃ |night sky|starry/i.test(sceneCombinedText)) {
        preset = "nightSky";
      } else if (
        /Ä‘á»“ng cá»|bÃ£i cá»|cá» xanh|tháº£o nguyÃªn|vÆ°á»n|sÃ¢n vÆ°á»n|cÃ´ng viÃªn|ngoÃ i trá»i|thiÃªn nhiÃªn|báº§u trá»i|mÃ¢y tráº¯ng|náº¯ng vÃ ng|cÃ¢y xanh|rá»«ng|garden|park|meadow|grass|lawn|outdoor|sky/i.test(sceneCombinedText) &&
        !/trong nhÃ |phÃ²ng khÃ¡ch|bÃ n há»c|gháº¿ sofa|indoor|living\s*room/i.test(sceneCombinedText)
      ) {
        preset = "gardenDay";
      } else if (/ban Ä‘Ãªm|tá»‘i|khuya|Ä‘Ãªm|trÄƒng|sao|night|moonlight/i.test(sceneCombinedText)) {
        preset = "livingRoomNight";
      } else if (/biá»ƒu Ä‘á»“|sá»‘ liá»‡u|chá»‰ sá»‘|infographic|thuyáº¿t trÃ¬nh/i.test(sceneCombinedText)) {
        preset = "infographicStripes";
      }
    }

    // Báº£o toÃ n mÃ u ná»n tÃ¹y chá»‰nh (customColors)
    const customColors =
      s?.background?.customColors && typeof s.background.customColors === "object"
        ? { ...s.background.customColors }
        : undefined;

    // 3. PhÃ¢n tÃ­ch Ä‘á»‘i tÆ°á»£ng nhÃ¢n váº­t (Há»— trá»£ cáº£ s.character láº«n máº£ng s.characters)
    const rawChar =
      s?.character ||
      (Array.isArray(s?.characters) && s.characters.length > 0 ? s.characters[0] : undefined);

    const isCloseUpScene =
      /cáº­n cáº£nh|phÃ³ng cáº­n|zoom|macro|closeup|close_up/i.test(sceneCombinedText) ||
      s?.camera?.type === "macro_closeup" ||
      s?.camera?.focusSlot === "screen.centerCloseUp";

    const isDiagramScene =
      /biá»ƒu Ä‘á»“|chá»‰ sá»‘|infographic|trao Ä‘á»•i cháº¥t|metabolism/i.test(sceneCombinedText) ||
      preset === "infographicStripes";

    // XÃ¡c Ä‘á»‹nh visibility:
    // Náº¿u pose: "offscreen" / "none", hoáº·c lÃ  cáº£nh cáº­n/biá»ƒu Ä‘á»“ khÃ´ng cÃ³ nhÃ¢n váº­t tham gia: áº¨N HOÃ€N TOÃ€N NHÃ‚N Váº¬T
    let charVisible = true;
    if (rawChar?.pose === "offscreen" || rawChar?.pose === "none") {
      charVisible = false;
    } else if (isCloseUpScene || isDiagramScene) {
      // á»ž cáº£nh cáº­n cáº£nh váº­t thá»ƒ (screen.centerCloseUp) hoáº·c biá»ƒu Ä‘á»“ infographic (screen.center):
      // Báº®T BUá»˜C áº¨N NHÃ‚N Váº¬T Ä‘á»ƒ tiÃªu Ä‘iá»ƒm Ä‘áº¡o cá»¥/biá»ƒu Ä‘á»“ hiá»ƒn thá»‹ trá»n váº¹n 100%, khÃ´ng bá»‹ nhÃ¢n váº­t che khuáº¥t
      charVisible = false;
    } else if (rawChar?.visible !== undefined) {
      charVisible = Boolean(rawChar.visible);
    }

    // PhÃ¢n giáº£i tÆ° tháº¿ Pose vÃ  HÆ°á»›ng nhÃ¬n
    let characterPose = "standing";
    const rawPose = String(rawChar?.pose || "").toLowerCase();
    const hasWalkKeywords = /(?:bÆ°á»›c Ä‘i tá»«|Ä‘i bá»™ tá»«|cháº¡y tá»«|walking across|walks from)/i.test(sceneCombinedText);

    // QUY Táº®C TÆ¯ THáº¾ NGá»’I:
    // Tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i khÃ´ng dÃ¹ng 'working' hay 'writing' Ä‘Æ¡n láº» Ä‘á»ƒ Ã©p tÆ° tháº¿ ngá»“i.
    // Chá»‰ kÃ­ch hoáº¡t 'sittingBehindDesk' khi cÃ³ tá»« khÃ³a ngá»“i rÃµ rÃ ng ('ngá»“i', 'sit', 'sitting')
    // hoáº·c nhÃ¢n váº­t Ä‘Æ°á»£c chá»‰ Ä‘á»‹nh rÃµ rÃ ng vÃ o slot gháº¿ bÃ n há»c 'desk.seat'.
    const isExplicitSitting =
      /sit|ngá»“i/i.test(rawPose) ||
      rawChar?.slot === "desk.seat" ||
      s?.character?.slot === "desk.seat" ||
      /ngá»“i\s*(?:sau|vÃ o|bÃªn|á»Ÿ)?\s*(?:bÃ n|gháº¿)?/i.test(sceneCombinedText);

    if (isExplicitSitting) {
      characterPose = "sittingBehindDesk";
    } else if (/^walk/i.test(rawPose) || hasWalkKeywords) {
      characterPose = "walking";
    } else if (/hold|cáº§m|mang|xÃ¡ch/i.test(rawPose) || /cáº§m|bÃª|mang|xÃ¡ch|giá»¯|nÃ¢ng|holding|carry/i.test(sceneCombinedText)) {
      characterPose = "holdingObject";
    } else if (/point|chá»‰/i.test(rawPose)) {
      characterPose = "pointing";
    } else if (/shrug/i.test(rawPose)) {
      characterPose = "shrugging";
    } else if (/wave/i.test(rawPose)) {
      characterPose = "waving";
    } else if (rawChar?.pose && typeof rawChar.pose === "string") {
      characterPose = rawChar.pose;
    }

    let charSlot = rawChar?.slot || s?.character?.slot;
    if (!charSlot) {
      if (characterPose === "sittingBehindDesk") {
        charSlot = "desk.seat";
      } else if (rawChar?.x !== undefined) {
        charSlot = undefined;
      } else {
        charSlot = "floor.center";
      }
    }

    const charX = numOrDef(rawChar?.x, 1280);
    const charY = numOrDef(rawChar?.y, 1120);
    const charScale = numOrDef(rawChar?.scale, 1.0);

    const character = {
      slot: charSlot,
      visible: charVisible,
      x: charX,
      y: charY,
      scale: charScale,
      facing: rawChar?.facing === "left" ? ("left" as const) : ("right" as const),
      pose: characterPose,
      eyes: rawChar?.eyes || rawChar?.expression || "neutral",
      eyebrows: rawChar?.eyebrows || "neutral",
      mouth: rawChar?.mouth || "smile",
      blush: rawChar?.blush !== undefined ? Boolean(rawChar.blush) : undefined,
      sweatDrop: rawChar?.sweatDrop !== undefined ? Boolean(rawChar.sweatDrop) : undefined,
      pupilGaze: characterPose === "walking" ? { x: 0.6, y: 0 } : (rawChar?.pupilGaze || { x: 0, y: 0 }),
    };

    // 4. Chuáº©n hÃ³a camera
    // CHá»NG PHÃ“NG Äáº I QUÃ Má»¨C (Anti-Overzoom):
    // Äá»‘i vá»›i cáº£nh cáº­n (screen.centerCloseUp), prop Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c scale 1.7x; zoom camera chá»‰ nÃªn tá»« 1.0 Ä‘áº¿n 1.15 Ä‘á»ƒ khÃ´ng cáº¯t mÃ©p
    let rawZoom = numOrDef(s?.camera?.zoom, 1.0);
    if (isCloseUpScene && rawZoom > 1.25) {
      rawZoom = 1.15;
    }

    const cameraFocusSlot =
      s?.camera?.focusSlot ||
      s?.camera?.focalPoint ||
      (isCloseUpScene ? "screen.centerCloseUp" : isDiagramScene ? "screen.center" : characterPose === "sittingBehindDesk" ? "desk.seat" : undefined);

    const camera = {
      x: numOrDef(s?.camera?.x, 1280),
      y: numOrDef(s?.camera?.y, 720),
      zoom: rawZoom,
      zoomFrom: numOrUndef(s?.camera?.zoomFrom),
      zoomTo: isCloseUpScene && s?.camera?.zoomTo && s.camera.zoomTo > 1.25 ? 1.15 : numOrUndef(s?.camera?.zoomTo),
      focusSlot: cameraFocusSlot,
      focusTarget: s?.camera?.focusTarget || undefined,
      easing: s?.camera?.easing || undefined,
      panDurationFrames: numOrUndef(s?.camera?.panDurationFrames),
    };

    // 5. Chuáº©n hÃ³a Keyframes cÃ³ phÃ¢n biá»‡t Action (Báº¢O TOÃ€N TRá»ŒN Váº¸N PARAMS, khÃ´ng tá»± Ã½ chÃ¨n trÆ°á»£t lÆ°á»›t 600 -> 1850)
    let finalKeyframes: SceneKeyframe[] = Array.isArray(s?.keyframes) && s.keyframes.length > 0
      ? s.keyframes.map((kf: any) => sanitizeKeyframe(kf, 0))
      : [];

    // 6. Chuáº©n hÃ³a hiá»‡u á»©ng mÃ´i trÆ°á»ng & Ä‘áº¡o cá»¥ Props (Báº¢O TOÃ€N x=0, y=0, scale tÃ¹y chá»‰nh, visible, containment)
    let envVfx: EnvironmentVfxType[] = Array.isArray(s?.environmentVfx) ? [...s.environmentVfx] : [];

    const rawPropsList = Array.isArray(s?.props)
      ? s.props
      : Array.isArray(s?.assets)
      ? s.assets.filter((a: any) => a.type !== "character")
      : [];

    // Chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»•i má»i Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng (ká»ƒ cáº£ máº£ng string do Gemini tráº£ vá») thÃ nh object chuáº©n
    const rawProps = rawPropsList.map((p: any, pIdx: number) => {
      if (typeof p === "string") {
        return { id: `prop_${idx}_${pIdx}`, type: p };
      }
      return p;
    });

    const props: PropItem[] = rawProps
      .map((p: any, pIdx: number) => {
        const rawTypeStr = String(p?.type || "");
        const rawIdStr = String(p?.id || "");

        let canonicalType = normalizePropAssetId(rawTypeStr);

        // PHÃ‚N GIáº¢I NGá»® NGHÄ¨A CÃ“ ÄIá»€U KIá»†N (KhÃ´ng Ã¡nh xáº¡ chung chung náº¿u thiáº¿u thÃ´ng tin):
        // Náº¿u type rá»—ng, unknown-prop hoáº·c tá»« khÃ³a chung chung, chá»‰ phÃ¢n giáº£i khi cÃ³ Ä‘á»§ thÃ´ng tin tá»« ID, properties hoáº·c ngá»¯ cáº£nh
        if (
          !rawTypeStr ||
          canonicalType === "unknown-prop" ||
          canonicalType === "prop" ||
          rawTypeStr === "container" ||
          rawTypeStr === "furniture" ||
          rawTypeStr === "light_source" ||
          rawTypeStr === "light-source" ||
          rawTypeStr === "infographic_meter" ||
          rawTypeStr === "infographic-meter"
        ) {
          const idResolved = normalizePropAssetId(rawIdStr);
          if (idResolved !== "unknown-prop" && idResolved !== "prop") {
            canonicalType = idResolved;
          } else {
            const propContext = `${rawIdStr} ${p?.properties?.title || ""} ${p?.properties?.label || ""} ${p?.name || ""}`.toLowerCase();

            if (rawTypeStr === "container") {
              // Chá»‰ Ã¡nh xáº¡ thÃ nh glass-jar náº¿u cÃ³ thÃ´ng tin rÃµ rÃ ng vá» lá»/hÅ©/thá»§y tinh
              if (/jar|lo|hu|thuy-tinh|thuy\s*tinh|glass/i.test(propContext) || /lá»|hÅ©|thá»§y tinh|chiáº¿c lá»/i.test(sceneCombinedText)) {
                canonicalType = "glass-jar";
              } else if (/box|hop|thung|carton|giay/i.test(propContext) || /há»™p|thÃ¹ng/i.test(sceneCombinedText)) {
                canonicalType = "cardboard-box";
              }
              // Náº¿u khÃ´ng Ä‘á»§ thÃ´ng tin, giá»¯ nguyÃªn type mÃ  khÃ´ng Ã©p sang glass-jar
            } else if (rawTypeStr === "furniture") {
              // Chá»‰ Ã¡nh xáº¡ thÃ nh bÃ n há»c desk náº¿u cÃ³ ngá»¯ cáº£nh bÃ n há»c/lÃ m viá»‡c
              if (/desk|ban-hoc|ban-lam-viec|study/i.test(propContext) || /bÃ n há»c|bÃ n lÃ m viá»‡c/i.test(sceneCombinedText)) {
                canonicalType = "desk";
              } else if (/table|ban-an|dining/i.test(propContext) || /bÃ n Äƒn|bÃ n trÃ /i.test(sceneCombinedText)) {
                canonicalType = "table";
              } else if (/sofa|couch|ghe-dai/i.test(propContext) || /sofa|gháº¿ dÃ i/i.test(sceneCombinedText)) {
                canonicalType = "sofa";
              } else if (/chair|ghe-don/i.test(propContext) || /gháº¿ Ä‘Æ¡n|gháº¿/i.test(sceneCombinedText)) {
                canonicalType = "chair";
              }
              // Náº¿u khÃ´ng Ä‘á»§ thÃ´ng tin, giá»¯ nguyÃªn furniture
            } else if (rawTypeStr === "light_source" || rawTypeStr === "light-source") {
              // Chá»‰ Ã¡nh xáº¡ thÃ nh desk-lamp náº¿u cÃ³ ngá»¯ cáº£nh Ä‘Ã¨n bÃ n/Ä‘Ã¨n há»c
              if (/desk-lamp|lamp|den-ban|den-hoc|study/i.test(propContext) || /Ä‘Ã¨n bÃ n|Ä‘Ã¨n há»c/i.test(sceneCombinedText)) {
                canonicalType = "desk-lamp";
              }
              // Náº¿u khÃ´ng Ä‘á»§ thÃ´ng tin, giá»¯ nguyÃªn light-source
            } else if (rawTypeStr === "infographic_meter" || rawTypeStr === "infographic-meter") {
              canonicalType = "gauge-meter";
            }
          }
        }

        // 1. CHUYá»‚N Äá»”I HIá»†U á»¨NG THá»œI TIáº¾T KHAI BÃO NHáº¦M THÃ€NH PROP (WIND, BREEZE, RAIN, SUNBEAMS)
        // Tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i khÃ´ng render thÃ nh prop há»™p chá»¯ trÃªn sÃ n, mÃ  bá»• sung vÃ o environmentVfx
        if (
          /^(?:wind|breeze|lan-gio|con-gio|gio-thoang|wind-breeze|windbreeze)$/i.test(canonicalType) ||
          /^(?:wind|breeze|gio)$/i.test(rawTypeStr) ||
          /wind|breeze/i.test(rawIdStr)
        ) {
          if (!envVfx.includes("windBreeze")) envVfx.push("windBreeze");
          return null;
        }
        if (
          /^(?:rain|mua|rain-loop|rainloop)$/i.test(canonicalType) ||
          /^(?:rain|mua)$/i.test(rawTypeStr)
        ) {
          if (!envVfx.includes("rainLoop")) envVfx.push("rainLoop");
          return null;
        }
        if (/^(?:sunbeams|tia-nang|sun-rays|godrays)$/i.test(canonicalType)) {
          if (!envVfx.includes("sunbeams")) envVfx.push("sunbeams");
          return null;
        }
        if (/^(?:clouds?|may-troi|cloud-drift|clouddrift)$/i.test(canonicalType)) {
          if (!envVfx.includes("cloudDrift")) envVfx.push("cloudDrift");
          return null;
        }

        // 2. Táº¤T Cáº¢ BIáº¾N THá»‚ BIá»‚U Äá»’, Äá»’NG Há»’ ÄO Tá»C Äá»˜ / TRAO Äá»”I CHáº¤T -> GAUGE-METER HOÃ€N CHá»ˆNH
        if (
          /speed|meter|gauge|metabol|trao-doi-chat|dong-ho-do/i.test(canonicalType) ||
          /speed|meter|gauge|metabol|trao-doi/i.test(rawTypeStr) ||
          /speed|meter|gauge|trao_doi/i.test(rawIdStr) ||
          (isDiagramScene && (canonicalType === "chart" || canonicalType === "unknown-prop" || canonicalType === "prop"))
        ) {
          canonicalType = "gauge-meter";
        }

        // 3. Táº¤T Cáº¢ BIáº¾N THá»‚ MÅ¨I TÃŠN CHá»ˆ DáºªN -> ARROW-INDICATOR
        if (
          /pointer|arrow|mui-ten|chi-dan/i.test(canonicalType) ||
          /pointer|arrow/i.test(rawTypeStr) ||
          /pointer|arrow/i.test(rawIdStr)
        ) {
          canonicalType = "arrow-indicator";
        }

        // 4. Báº¢O Vá»† CHá»NG DUPLICATE SPIDER TRONG CÃ™NG Váº¬T CHá»¨A:
        // Khi má»™t sinh váº­t/Ä‘áº¡o cá»¥ (nhÆ° con nhá»‡n spider) Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c xáº¿p náº±m BÃŠN TRONG váº­t chá»©a (glass-jar cÃ³ hasSpiderInside: true),
        // há»‡ thá»‘ng pháº£i Tá»° Äá»˜NG XÃ“A Bá»Ž instance thá»«a cá»§a con nhá»‡n Ä‘Ã³ á»Ÿ bÃªn ngoÃ i sÃ n nhÃ !
        const jarProp = rawProps.find(
          (other: any) =>
            other !== p &&
            other?.properties?.hasSpiderInside &&
            (/glass-jar|jar|thuy-tinh/i.test(other?.type || "") || /glass_jar|jar/i.test(other?.id || ""))
        );

        if (canonicalType === "spider" && jarProp) {
          return null; // Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng xÃ³a bá» instance thá»«a cá»§a con nhá»‡n á»Ÿ ngoÃ i sÃ n nhÃ !
        }

        // Loáº¡i bá» ná»™i tháº¥t trong nhÃ  khi á»Ÿ cáº£nh ngoÃ i trá»i gardenDay
        if (preset === "gardenDay" && ["sofa", "desk", "desk-lamp", "desk-front", "desk-back"].includes(canonicalType)) {
          if (!/sofa|bÃ n há»c|gháº¿|Ä‘Ã¨n bÃ n/i.test(sceneCombinedText)) {
            return null;
          }
        }

        const isScreenSlot = Boolean(
          (p?.slot && String(p.slot).startsWith("screen.")) || isCloseUpScene || isDiagramScene
        );

        let propScale: number;
        if (p?.scale !== undefined && !isNaN(Number(p.scale))) {
          propScale = Number(p.scale);
        } else {
          if (canonicalType === "glass-jar") {
            if (isScreenSlot) {
              propScale = 1.7; // Chuáº©n 850px-900px cáº£nh cáº­n 2K
            } else if (p?.slot === "desk.topRight" || p?.slot === "desk.topLeft" || characterPose === "sittingBehindDesk") {
              propScale = 0.35; // ~280px trÃªn máº·t bÃ n
            } else {
              propScale = 0.38; // ~310px trÃªn sÃ n nhÃ 
            }
          } else if (canonicalType === "gauge-meter") {
            propScale = isScreenSlot ? 1.6 : 1.0;
          } else if (canonicalType === "desk-lamp") {
            propScale = 1.15;
          } else {
            propScale = 1.0;
          }
        }

        // TrÃ­ch xuáº¥t nhÃ£n dÃ¡n thÃ´ng minh (label / labelText / title)
        const labelText =
          p?.properties?.label ||
          p?.properties?.labelText ||
          p?.properties?.title ||
          p?.label ||
          p?.labelText ||
          (/Ä‘áº·t tÃªn.*lÃ \s*['"â€œ]?([a-zA-Z0-9Ã€-á»¹]+)['"â€]?/i.exec(sceneCombinedText)?.[1]) ||
          (isCloseUpScene && /tÃ¡m/i.test(sceneCombinedText) ? "TÃM" : undefined);

        const hasSpider =
          canonicalType === "glass-jar" &&
          (/nhá»‡n|spider|tÃ¡m/i.test(sceneCombinedText) ||
            p?.properties?.hasSpiderInside === true);

        // PhÃ¢n giáº£i slot chuáº©n
        let propSlot = p?.slot;
        if (!propSlot) {
          if (isCloseUpScene && canonicalType === "glass-jar") propSlot = "screen.centerCloseUp";
          else if (isDiagramScene && canonicalType === "gauge-meter") propSlot = "screen.center";
          else if (canonicalType === "desk-lamp") propSlot = "desk.topLeft";
          else if (canonicalType === "glass-jar" && characterPose === "sittingBehindDesk") propSlot = "desk.topRight";
        }

        const propItem: PropItem = {
          id: String(p?.id || `prop_${idx}_${pIdx}`),
          type: canonicalType,
          slot: propSlot || undefined,
          layer: p?.layer === "midgroundBackLayer" ? ("midgroundBackLayer" as const) : ("midgroundFrontLayer" as const),
          x: numOrDef(p?.x, 1280),
          y: numOrDef(p?.y, 1120),
          scale: propScale,
          scaleX: numOrDef(p?.scaleX, 1),
          scaleY: numOrDef(p?.scaleY, 1),
          rotation: numOrDef(p?.rotation, 0),
          opacity: numOrDef(p?.opacity, 1),
          visible: p?.visible !== undefined ? Boolean(p.visible) : true,
          alphaConfig: p?.alphaConfig || undefined,
          anchorPoint: p?.anchorPoint || (isScreenSlot ? ("center" as const) : undefined),
          pivotPoint: p?.pivotPoint || (isScreenSlot ? ("center" as const) : undefined),
          pivotX: numOrUndef(p?.pivotX),
          pivotY: numOrUndef(p?.pivotY),
          properties: {
            ...p?.properties,
            hasSpiderInside: hasSpider ? true : undefined,
            label: labelText,
          },
          entrance: p?.entrance || undefined,
        };

        return propItem;
      })
      .filter(Boolean) as PropItem[];

    // 7. Environment VFX & Lighting
    const isNight = preset === "livingRoomNight" || preset === "nightSky";
    if (preset === "gardenDay" || preset === "beachSunny") {
      if (!envVfx.includes("cloudDrift")) envVfx.push("cloudDrift");
      if (!envVfx.includes("sunbeams")) envVfx.push("sunbeams");
      if (!envVfx.includes("windBreeze")) envVfx.push("windBreeze");
      if (!envVfx.includes("foliageSway")) envVfx.push("foliageSway");
    } else if (isNight && !envVfx.includes("cloudDrift") && !isCloseUpScene && !isDiagramScene) {
      envVfx.push("cloudDrift");
    }

    // Náº¿u cáº£nh ban Ä‘Ãªm cÃ³ Ä‘Ã¨n bÃ n (desk-lamp), Báº®T BUá»˜C lighting lÃ  warmNightLamp
    const hasDeskLamp = props.some((p) => p.type === "desk-lamp");
    let lightingPreset = "neutral";
    if (hasDeskLamp || /Ã¡nh Ä‘Ã¨n|Ä‘Ã¨n bÃ n|Ä‘Ã¨n áº¥m|lamp/i.test(sceneCombinedText)) {
      lightingPreset = "warmNightLamp";
    } else if (preset === "gardenDay" || preset === "beachSunny") {
      lightingPreset = "sunbeams";
    } else if (isNight) {
      lightingPreset = "moonlight";
    }

    const lighting = typeof s?.lighting === "object" ? { ...s.lighting, preset: lightingPreset } : { preset: lightingPreset };

    // Subtitles chuáº©n xÃ¡c, Ä‘Ã£ bÃ³c tÃ¡ch sáº¡ch camera notes
    const subtitles = [
      {
        id: `sub_${idx}_0`,
        text: cleanSpokenText,
        startFrame: start + 5,
        endFrame: start + dur - 5,
        words: Array.isArray(existingSubs[0]?.words) ? existingSubs[0].words : [],
      },
    ];

    return {
      id: String(s?.id || s?.sceneId || `scene_${idx + 1}`),
      name: String(s?.name || `Cáº£nh ${idx + 1}`),
      startFrame: start,
      durationInFrames: dur,
      background: {
        preset,
        customColors,
      },
      camera,
      character,
      props,
      subtitles,
      speechBubbles: Array.isArray(s?.speechBubbles) ? s.speechBubbles : [],
      keyframes: finalKeyframes,
      lines: Array.isArray(s?.lines) ? s.lines : [],
      environmentVfx: envVfx,
      lighting,
      propAnimations: Array.isArray(s?.propAnimations) ? s.propAnimations : [],
    };
  });

  return {
    version: String(raw?.version || "3.1.0"),
    title: String(raw?.title || "2D Animation Story"),
    width: 2560,
    height: 1440,
    fps: 60,
    totalDurationInFrames: Math.max(60, currentStart),
    audioTrack: raw?.audioTrack || undefined,
    scenes: cleanScenes,
    sanitizerAuditLog: auditLog,
  };
}

import { Point2D } from "../types/character";

export const STAGE_CONFIG = {
  canvasWidth: 2560,
  canvasHeight: 1440,
  floorY: 1120, // 2K Floor Baseline
  deskX: 1150,
  deskY: 1120,
  deskSurfaceY: 760, // Top surface of the desk
  screenCenterX: 1280,
  screenCenterY: 720,
};

/**
 * SEMANTIC SLOTS (Chuáº©n hÃ³a tá»a Ä‘á»™ ngá»¯ nghÄ©a cho AI Scriptwriting)
 * GiÃºp AI (Gemini) khÃ´ng bao giá» pháº£i Ä‘oÃ¡n pixel (x, y).
 * Táº¥t cáº£ cÃ¡c tá»a Ä‘á»™ Ä‘á»u tuÃ¢n thá»§ nguyÃªn táº¯c ORIGIN = BOTTOM-CENTER.
 */
export const SEMANTIC_SLOTS: Record<string, Point2D> = {
  // --- 1. SLOTS TRÃŠN SÃ€N NHÃ€ (floorY = 1120) ---
  "floor.farLeft": { x: 380, y: STAGE_CONFIG.floorY },
  "floor.left": { x: 780, y: STAGE_CONFIG.floorY },
  "floor.centerLeft": { x: 1050, y: STAGE_CONFIG.floorY },
  "floor.center": { x: 1280, y: STAGE_CONFIG.floorY },
  "floor.centerRight": { x: 1510, y: STAGE_CONFIG.floorY },
  "floor.right": { x: 1850, y: STAGE_CONFIG.floorY },
  "floor.farRight": { x: 2200, y: STAGE_CONFIG.floorY },

  // --- 2. SLOTS BÃ€N Há»ŒC & LÃ€M VIá»†C (Desk at x=1150, tabletop at y=760) ---
  // Vá»‹ trÃ­ ngÆ°á»i que ngá»“i chuáº©n sau bÃ n
  "desk.seat": { x: STAGE_CONFIG.deskX, y: STAGE_CONFIG.floorY },
  // ÄÃ¨n bÃ n Ä‘áº·t sÃ¡t máº·t pháº³ng bÃ n bÃªn trÃ¡i
  "desk.topLeft": { x: 800, y: STAGE_CONFIG.deskSurfaceY },
  // Laptop / Äáº¡o cá»¥ giá»¯a bÃ n
  "desk.topCenter": { x: STAGE_CONFIG.deskX, y: STAGE_CONFIG.deskSurfaceY },
  // Lá» thá»§y tinh / Cá»‘c cÃ  phÃª Ä‘áº·t trÃªn bÃ n bÃªn pháº£i
  "desk.topRight": { x: 1480, y: STAGE_CONFIG.deskSurfaceY },

  // --- 3. SLOTS Cáº¢NH Cáº¬N & INFOGRAPHIC (Giá»¯a mÃ n hÃ¬nh) ---
  "screen.center": { x: STAGE_CONFIG.screenCenterX, y: STAGE_CONFIG.screenCenterY },
  "screen.centerCloseUp": { x: STAGE_CONFIG.screenCenterX, y: STAGE_CONFIG.screenCenterY },
  "screen.gaugeCenter": { x: STAGE_CONFIG.screenCenterX, y: 720 },

  // --- 4. SLOTS SOFA PHÃ’NG KHÃCH ---
  "sofa.seat": { x: 2050, y: STAGE_CONFIG.floorY },
  "sofa.left": { x: 1850, y: STAGE_CONFIG.floorY },
  "sofa.right": { x: 2250, y: STAGE_CONFIG.floorY },
};

/**
 * PhÃ¢n giáº£i slot ngá»¯ nghÄ©a thÃ nh tá»a Ä‘á»™ Point2D { x, y }
 * Náº¿u khÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y slot, fallback vá» tá»a Ä‘á»™ thá»§ cÃ´ng hoáº·c tÃ¢m mÃ n hÃ¬nh.
 */
export function resolveSemanticSlot(
  slot?: string,
  fallback?: { x?: number; y?: number }
): Point2D {
  if (slot && SEMANTIC_SLOTS[slot]) {
    return SEMANTIC_SLOTS[slot];
  }

  // Thá»­ tra cá»©u linh hoáº¡t (bá» khoáº£ng tráº¯ng, case-insensitive)
  if (slot) {
    const normalized = slot.trim().toLowerCase();
    for (const [key, val] of Object.entries(SEMANTIC_SLOTS)) {
      if (key.toLowerCase() === normalized) {
        return val;
      }
    }
  }

  return {
    x: fallback?.x ?? STAGE_CONFIG.screenCenterX,
    y: fallback?.y ?? STAGE_CONFIG.floorY,
  };
}


==================== FILE: C:\Users\ASUS\Downloads\TOOL_2D_ANIM_API_FIX_1\TOOL_2D_ANIM\web-studio\src\remotion\vfx\AnimatedTransform.tsx ====================
import React from "react";
import { interpolate } from "remotion";
import { AlphaConfig } from "../types/sceneGraph";

export interface AnimatedTransformProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  frame?: number;
  fps?: number;
  // Position
  x?: number;
  y?: number;
  // Scale & Squash/Stretch
  scale?: number;
  scaleX?: number;
  scaleY?: number;
  squashStretch?: {
    enabled?: boolean;
    impactFrame?: number;
    factor?: number;
  };
  // Rotation & Pivot
  rotation?: number;
  pivotPoint?: "center" | "bottomCenter" | "topCenter" | "topLeft" | "custom";
  pivotX?: number;
  pivotY?: number;
  objectWidth?: number;
  objectHeight?: number;
  // Alpha & Fade
  opacity?: number;
  alphaConfig?: AlphaConfig;
  sceneDurationInFrames?: number;
  id?: string;
  className?: string;
}

/**
 * AnimatedTransform: Bá»™ Äiá»u Khiá»ƒn Biáº¿n Äá»•i Váº­t Thá»ƒ & Trá»¥c Quay TÃ¹y Biáº¿n (Custom Pivot & Shaders)
 * 
 * 1. Há»— trá»£ xoay quanh trá»¥c neo tÃ¹y chá»‰nh (kim Ä‘á»“ng há»“ quay quanh tÃ¢m, kim GaugeMeter quay quanh trá»¥c Ä‘Ã¡y).
 * 2. PhÃ³ng to/thu nhá» mÆ°á»£t mÃ  kÃ¨m Squash & Stretch khi rÆ¡i cháº¡m Ä‘áº¥t.
 * 3. Fade-in, Fade-out Ä‘a táº§ng alpha vÃ  pulseAlpha pháº­p phá»“ng Ã¡nh sÃ¡ng.
 */
export const AnimatedTransform: React.FC<AnimatedTransformProps> = ({
  children,
  frame = 0,
  x = 0,
  y = 0,
  scale = 1,
  scaleX = 1,
  scaleY = 1,
  squashStretch,
  rotation = 0,
  pivotPoint = "bottomCenter",
  pivotX,
  pivotY,
  objectWidth = 240,
  objectHeight = 280,
  opacity = 1,
  alphaConfig,
  sceneDurationInFrames = 120,
  id,
  className,
}) => {
  // =========================================================================
  // 1. TÃNH TOÃN ÄIá»‚M NEO TRá»¤C QUAY (PIVOT POINT ANCHOR)
  // Há»‡ tá»a Ä‘á»™ chuáº©n Bottom-Center: (0, 0) lÃ  Ä‘Ã¡y chÃ­nh giá»¯a
  // =========================================================================
  let px = 0;
  let py = 0;

  switch (pivotPoint) {
    case "bottomCenter":
      px = 0;
      py = 0;
      break;
    case "center":
      px = 0;
      py = -objectHeight / 2;
      break;
    case "topCenter":
      px = 0;
      py = -objectHeight;
      break;
    case "topLeft":
      px = -objectWidth / 2;
      py = -objectHeight;
      break;
    case "custom":
      px = pivotX ?? 0;
      py = pivotY ?? 0;
      break;
  }

  // =========================================================================
  // 2. TÃNH TOÃN SQUASH & STRETCH (NÃ‰N GIÃƒN KHI CHáº M Äáº¤T)
  // =========================================================================
  let effectiveScaleX = scale * scaleX;
  let effectiveScaleY = scale * scaleY;

  if (squashStretch?.enabled) {
    const impactF = squashStretch.impactFrame ?? 0;
    const factor = squashStretch.factor ?? 0.25;
    if (frame >= impactF && frame <= impactF + 25) {
      const diff = frame - impactF;
      const osc = Math.sin(diff * 0.4) * Math.exp(-diff * 0.14);
      effectiveScaleX *= 1 + osc * factor;
      effectiveScaleY *= 1 - osc * factor;
    }
  }

  // =========================================================================
  // 3. TÃNH TOÃN ÄA Táº¦NG ALPHA (FADE-IN / FADE-OUT / PULSE ALPHA)
  // =========================================================================
  let effectiveOpacity = opacity;

  if (alphaConfig) {
    const { fadeInFrames, fadeOutFrames, pulseAlpha, minAlpha = 0.4, maxAlpha = 1.0 } = alphaConfig;

    if (fadeInFrames && fadeInFrames > 0) {
      const fadeProgress = interpolate(frame, [0, fadeInFrames], [0, 1], {
        extrapolateLeft: "clamp",
        extrapolateRight: "clamp",
      });
      effectiveOpacity *= fadeProgress;
    }

    if (fadeOutFrames && fadeOutFrames > 0 && sceneDurationInFrames > fadeOutFrames) {
      const fadeOutStart = sceneDurationInFrames - fadeOutFrames;
      if (frame >= fadeOutStart) {
        const fadeOutProgress = interpolate(frame, [fadeOutStart, sceneDurationInFrames], [1, 0], {
          extrapolateLeft: "clamp",
          extrapolateRight: "clamp",
        });
        effectiveOpacity *= fadeOutProgress;
      }
    }

    if (pulseAlpha) {
      const pulse = minAlpha + (maxAlpha - minAlpha) * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(frame * 0.08));
      effectiveOpacity *= pulse;
    }
  }

  // Chuá»—i biáº¿n Ä‘á»•i SVG vá»›i Pivot chuáº©n xÃ¡c
  const transformString = `translate(${x}, ${y}) translate(${px}, ${py}) rotate(${rotation}) scale(${effectiveScaleX}, ${effectiveScaleY}) translate(${-px}, ${-py})`;

  return (
    <g
      id={id ? `animated-transform-${id}` : undefined}
      className={className}
      transform={transformString}
      opacity={Math.max(0, Math.min(1, effectiveOpacity))}
    >
      {children}
    </g>
  );
};


==================== FILE: C:\Users\ASUS\Downloads\TOOL_2D_ANIM_API_FIX_1\TOOL_2D_ANIM\web-studio\src\remotion\vfx\CameraRig.tsx ====================
import React from "react";
import { interpolate, Easing } from "remotion";
import { CameraConfig, PropItem } from "../types/sceneGraph";
import { StickmanState, Point2D } from "../types/character";
import { resolveSemanticSlot, STAGE_CONFIG } from "../utils/slotResolver";

export interface CameraRigProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  camera?: CameraConfig;
  sceneLocalFrame: number;
  durationInFrames: number;
  character?: StickmanState;
  propsList?: PropItem[];
  canvasWidth?: number;
  canvasHeight?: number;
}

export interface ClampedCameraTransform {
  zoom: number;
  focalX: number;
  focalY: number;
  transformString: string;
}

/**
 * TÃ­nh toÃ¡n Camera Framing tá»‘i Æ°u theo Bounding Box cá»§a chá»§ thá»ƒ:
 * - TrÃ¡nh hoÃ n toÃ n vÃ¹ng phá»¥ Ä‘á» phÃ­a Ä‘Ã¡y (subtitleReservedHeight)
 * - ThÃªm khoáº£ng Ä‘á»‡m an toÃ n padding xung quanh
 * - Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng tÃ­nh toÃ¡n má»©c zoom vÃ  tÃ¢m tiÃªu cá»± focal point
 */
export function calculateBoundingBoxFraming(
  box: { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number },
  canvasWidth: number = 2560,
  canvasHeight: number = 1440,
  subtitleReservedHeight: number = 220,
  padding: number = 120
): { zoom: number; focalX: number; focalY: number } {
  const usableWidth = canvasWidth - padding * 2;
  const usableHeight = canvasHeight - subtitleReservedHeight - padding * 2;

  const targetW = Math.max(100, box.width);
  const targetH = Math.max(100, box.height);

  const zoomX = usableWidth / targetW;
  const zoomY = usableHeight / targetH;
  const zoom = Math.max(1.0, Math.min(2.5, Math.min(zoomX, zoomY)));

  const focalX = box.x + box.width / 2;
  const verticalShiftInViewport = subtitleReservedHeight / (2 * zoom);
  const focalY = box.y + box.height / 2 + verticalShiftInViewport;

  return { zoom, focalX, focalY };
}

/**
 * HÃ m toÃ¡n há»c clampCameraTransform khÃ³a cá»©ng khÃ´ng gian tháº¿ giá»›i vÃ o viewport 2K:
 * - á»ž má»i má»©c zoom, mÃ©p khung hÃ¬nh khÃ´ng bao giá» bá»‹ há»Ÿ mÃ©p ngoÃ i cá»§a Background (0 <= left, right <= 2560, 0 <= top, bottom <= 1440).
 * - KhÃ´ng bao giá» cáº¯t máº¥t pháº§n Ä‘áº§u cá»§a nhÃ¢n váº­t chÃ­nh (Ä‘á»‰nh Ä‘áº§u luÃ´n >= 150px trong viewport).
 */
export function clampCameraTransform(
  rawZoom: number,
  rawFocalX: number,
  rawFocalY: number,
  canvasWidth: number = 2560,
  canvasHeight: number = 1440,
  characterHeadY?: number
): ClampedCameraTransform {
  const centerX = canvasWidth / 2;
  const centerY = canvasHeight / 2;

  let zoom = Math.max(1.0, rawZoom);
  let focalX = rawFocalX;
  let focalY = rawFocalY;

  if (zoom <= 1.001) {
    zoom = 1.0;
    focalX = centerX;
    focalY = centerY;
  } else {
    const halfVisibleW = centerX / zoom;
    const halfVisibleH = centerY / zoom;

    // Káº¹p biÃªn trá»¥c X: KhÃ´ng há»Ÿ khoáº£ng trá»‘ng bÃªn trÃ¡i hoáº·c pháº£i canvas
    focalX = Math.max(halfVisibleW, Math.min(canvasWidth - halfVisibleW, focalX));

    // Káº¹p biÃªn trá»¥c Y: KhÃ´ng há»Ÿ trÃªn (y=0) vÃ  khÃ´ng lá»™ Ä‘Ã¡y sÃ n dÆ°á»›i (y=1440)
    const minFocalY = halfVisibleH;
    const maxFocalY = canvasHeight - halfVisibleH;
    focalY = Math.max(minFocalY, Math.min(maxFocalY, focalY));

    // Äáº£m báº£o Ä‘á»‰nh Ä‘áº§u nhÃ¢n váº­t luÃ´n >= 150px trong viewport
    if (characterHeadY !== undefined) {
      const charHeadViewportY = (characterHeadY - focalY) * zoom + centerY;
      if (charHeadViewportY < 150) {
        const neededFocalY = characterHeadY - (150 - centerY) / zoom;
        focalY = Math.max(minFocalY, Math.min(maxFocalY, neededFocalY));
      }
    }
  }

  const transformString = `translate(${centerX}, ${centerY}) scale(${zoom}) translate(${-focalX}, ${-focalY})`;
  return { zoom, focalX, focalY, transformString };
}

/**
 * CameraRig: Há»‡ thá»‘ng Camera Äiá»‡n áº£nh 2K (Cinematography Camera Engine)
 * 
 * 1. Zoom In / Zoom Out mÆ°á»£t mÃ  theo hÃ m Easing.bezier(0.25, 0.1, 0.25, 1).
 * 2. Pan & Focal Tracking: BÃ¡m theo slot ngá»¯ nghÄ©a ('focusSlot') hoáº·c ID Ä‘á»‘i tÆ°á»£ng ('focusTarget').
 * 3. Äáº£m báº£o SubtitleBox vÃ  UI Overlay náº±m ngoÃ i CameraRig Ä‘á»ƒ luÃ´n sáº¯c nÃ©t, khÃ´ng bá»‹ phÃ³ng to theo cáº£nh.
 */
export const CameraRig: React.FC<CameraRigProps> = ({
  children,
  camera,
  sceneLocalFrame,
  durationInFrames,
  character,
  propsList = [],
  canvasWidth = 2560,
  canvasHeight = 1440,
}) => {
  const centerX = canvasWidth / 2;
  const centerY = canvasHeight / 2;

  // 1. TÃNH TOÃN ÄIá»‚M FOCAL TARGET (TÃ‚M TIÃŠU Cá»° Cá»¦A CAMERA)
  let targetPoint: Point2D = {
    x: camera?.x ?? centerX,
    y: camera?.y ?? centerY,
  };

  if ((camera as any)?.focusBoundingBox) {
    const framing = calculateBoundingBoxFraming(
      (camera as any).focusBoundingBox,
      canvasWidth,
      canvasHeight
    );
    targetPoint = { x: framing.focalX, y: framing.focalY };
  } else if (camera?.focusSlot) {
    const slotPoint = resolveSemanticSlot(camera.focusSlot, targetPoint);
    if (
      camera.focusSlot === "desk.seat" ||
      camera.focusSlot.startsWith("floor.") ||
      camera.focusSlot.startsWith("sofa.")
    ) {
      targetPoint = {
        x: slotPoint.x,
        y: Math.min(centerY, slotPoint.y - 420),
      };
    } else {
      targetPoint = slotPoint;
    }
  } else if (camera?.focusTarget) {
    if (camera.focusTarget === "character" && character && character.visible !== false) {
      targetPoint = {
        x: character.x,
        y: character.y - 420,
      };
    } else {
      const foundProp = propsList.find(
        (p) => p.id === camera.focusTarget || p.type === camera.focusTarget
      );
      if (foundProp) {
        const propPos = resolveSemanticSlot(foundProp.slot, { x: foundProp.x, y: foundProp.y });
        targetPoint = {
          x: propPos.x,
          y: propPos.y - (foundProp.anchorPoint === "center" ? 0 : 150),
        };
      }
    }
  }

  // 2. TÃNH TOÃN ZOOM Dá»°A TRÃŠN ZOOMFROM -> ZOOMTO HOáº¶C ZOOM TÄ¨NH
  const isCloseUpFocus =
    camera?.focusSlot === "screen.centerCloseUp" ||
    camera?.focusSlot === "screen.center" ||
    propsList.some((p) => p.slot === "screen.centerCloseUp" || p.slot === "screen.center");

  let rawZoomFrom = camera?.zoomFrom ?? (camera?.zoom ?? 1.0);
  let rawZoomTo = camera?.zoomTo ?? (camera?.zoom ?? 1.0);

  // CHá»NG PHÃ“NG Äáº I QUÃ Má»¨C (Anti-Overzoom): Cáº£nh cáº­n Ä‘áº¡o cá»¥ Ä‘Ã£ scale 1.7x nÃªn zoom camera tá»‘i Ä‘a 1.15x
  if (isCloseUpFocus) {
    if (rawZoomFrom > 1.25) rawZoomFrom = 1.15;
    if (rawZoomTo > 1.25) rawZoomTo = 1.15;
  }

  const zoomFrom = rawZoomFrom;
  const zoomTo = rawZoomTo;
  const isZooming = camera?.zoomFrom !== undefined && camera?.zoomTo !== undefined;

  let currentZoom = zoomTo;
  let currentFocalX = targetPoint.x;
  let currentFocalY = targetPoint.y;

  if (isZooming) {
    const panDuration = camera?.panDurationFrames ?? Math.min(60, durationInFrames);
    const progress = interpolate(sceneLocalFrame, [0, panDuration], [0, 1], {
      extrapolateLeft: "clamp",
      extrapolateRight: "clamp",
      easing: Easing.bezier(0.25, 0.1, 0.25, 1),
    });

    currentZoom = interpolate(progress, [0, 1], [zoomFrom, zoomTo]);
    currentFocalX = interpolate(progress, [0, 1], [centerX, targetPoint.x]);
    currentFocalY = interpolate(progress, [0, 1], [centerY, targetPoint.y]);
  }

  // 3. Káº¸P BIÃŠN CAMERA Báº°NG HÃ€M TOÃN Há»ŒC CHUáº¨N
  const charHeadY =
    character && character.visible !== false && character.x > -9000
      ? character.y - 755
      : undefined;

  const clamped = clampCameraTransform(
    currentZoom,
    currentFocalX,
    currentFocalY,
    canvasWidth,
    canvasHeight,
    charHeadY
  );

  return (
    <g id="camera-rig-viewport" transform={clamped.transformString}>
      {children}
    </g>
  );
};

import React from "react";

export interface EnvironmentVfxLayerProps {
  effects?: EnvironmentVfxType[];
  lighting?: LightingConfig;
  backgroundPreset?: string;
  frame: number;
  fps?: number;
  canvasWidth?: number;
  canvasHeight?: number;
}

// Máº£ng háº¡t mÆ°a cá»‘ Ä‘á»‹nh theo thuáº­t toÃ¡n PRNG (Ä‘áº£m báº£o deterministic vÃ  mÆ°á»£t mÃ  60fps)
const RAIN_DROPS = Array.from({ length: 70 }, (_, i) => ({
  id: i,
  seedX: (i * 137.5) % 2560,
  seedY: (i * 83.3) % 1440,
  speed: 24 + (i % 5) * 4,
  length: 28 + (i % 4) * 6,
  opacity: 0.25 + (i % 4) * 0.12,
  strokeWidth: 2.5 + (i % 3) * 0.8,
}));

// Máº£ng gá»£n sÃ³ng nÆ°á»›c khi mÆ°a cháº¡m sÃ n
const RAIN_SPLASHES = Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => ({

  y: STAGE_CONFIG.floorY,
  period: 30 + (i % 4) * 5,
  phase: i * 7,

// ÄÃ¡m mÃ¢y trÃ´i ngang qua báº§u trá»i
const CLOUDS = [

  { id: 4, seedX: 2350, y: 260, width: 380, height: 150, speed: 0.9, opacity: 0.65 },
];

/**
 * EnvironmentVfxLayer: Há»‡ thá»‘ng MÃ´i trÆ°á»ng Chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»™ng Láº·p Tá»± Äá»™ng (Procedural Environment Loops)
 * 
 * 1. rainLoop: MÆ°a rÆ¡i nghiÃªng theo chiá»u sÃ¢u (Parallax) kÃ¨m gá»£n nÆ°á»›c lÄƒn tÄƒn trÃªn máº·t sÃ n.
 * 2. cloudDrift: MÃ¢y trÃ´i ngang liÃªn tá»¥c báº±ng phÃ©p chia láº¥y dÆ° khÃ´ng giáº­t khá»±ng.
 * 3. windBreeze: LÃ n giÃ³ uá»‘n lÆ°á»£n má»m máº¡i chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»™ng báº±ng strokeDashoffset.
 * 4. sunbeams & warmNightLamp: Luá»“ng sÃ¡ng Volumetric Light Falloff Ä‘iá»‡n áº£nh.
 */
export const EnvironmentVfxLayer: React.FC<EnvironmentVfxLayerProps> = ({
  effects = [],
  lighting,
  backgroundPreset,
  frame,
  canvasWidth = 2560,
  canvasHeight = 1440,
}) => {
  const hasRain = effects.includes("rainLoop");
  const hasClouds = effects.includes("cloudDrift");
  const hasWind = effects.includes("windBreeze");
  const hasSunbeams = effects.includes("sunbeams") || lighting?.preset === "sunbeams" || lighting?.sunbeams;
  const hasWarmNightLamp = lighting?.preset === "warmNightLamp";

  // Máº·t trá»i dáº¡ng thiÃªn thá»ƒ (Sun Orb) CHá»ˆ hiá»ƒn thá»‹ á»Ÿ ngoáº¡i cáº£nh ngoÃ i trá»i (nhÆ° gardenDay).

  const windowY = 160;
  const windowW = 560;
  const windowH = 460;

    { id: "ic1", seedX: 0, y: 220, speed: 0.45, width: 220, height: 75, opacity: 0.9 },
    { id: "ic2", seedX: 320, y: 310, speed: 0.35, width: 190, height: 65, opacity: 0.8 },
  ];

  return (

        {/* ClipPath chuáº©n khá»›p chÃ­nh xÃ¡c 100% vá»›i lÃ²ng kÃ­nh cá»­a sá»• phÃ²ng khÃ¡ch (2K) */}
        <clipPath id="window-glass-clip">

              const currentX =

              const currentY = (drop.seedY + frame * drop.speed) % canvasHeight;
              const currentX =
                (drop.seedX - (currentY / canvasHeight) * 220 + canvasWidth) % canvasWidth;
              return (
                <line
                  key={drop.id}
                  x1={currentX}
                  y1={currentY}
                  x2={currentX - 7}
                  y2={currentY + drop.length}
                  strokeWidth={drop.strokeWidth}
                  opacity={drop.opacity}
                />
              );
            })}
          </g>

          {/* Gá»£n nÆ°á»›c lÄƒn tÄƒn trÃªn máº·t sÃ n (chá»‰ ngoÃ i trá»i, khÃ´ng bao giá» xuáº¥t hiá»‡n trÃªn sÃ n phÃ²ng khÃ¡ch) */}
          {!isIndoor && (
            <g id="vfx-rain-splashes" stroke="#93C5FD" fill="none">
              {RAIN_SPLASHES.map((splash) => {
                const life = (frame + splash.phase) % splash.period;
                const progress = life / splash.period;
                const rx = progress * 24;
                const ry = rx * 0.35;
                const splashOpacity = (1 - progress) * 0.65;
                return (
                  <ellipse
                    key={splash.id}
                    cx={splash.x}
                    cy={splash.y}
                    rx={rx}
                    ry={ry}
                    strokeWidth={2}
                    opacity={splashOpacity}
                  />
                );
              })}
            </g>
          )}
        </g>
      )}
    </g>
  );
};
