export const MainScene: React.FC<MainCompositionProps> = (props) => {
  const frame = useCurrentFrame();
  const { fps, width, height } = useVideoConfig();

  // XÃ¡c Ä‘á»‹nh Scene Graph linh hoáº¡t (há»— trá»£ cáº£ props.sceneGraph láº«n root props)
  const effectiveGraph: MasterSceneGraph = useMemo(() => {
    if (props.sceneGraph && props.sceneGraph !== defaultMockSceneGraph) {
      return props.sceneGraph;
    }
    if (props.scenes && Array.isArray(props.scenes) && props.scenes.length > 0) {
      return {
        version: "3.1.0",
        title: "Stickman 2D Explainer Video",
        width: 2560,
        height: 1440,
        fps: 60,
        totalDurationInFrames: props.totalFrames || 480,
        scenes: props.scenes,
      } as MasterSceneGraph;
    }
    return props.sceneGraph || defaultMockSceneGraph;
  }, [props.sceneGraph, props.scenes, props.totalFrames]);

  // LÆ°u trá»¯ cá»•ng neo cá»§a Ä‘áº¡o cá»¥ Ä‘Æ°á»£c active Ä‘á»ƒ phá»¥c vá»¥ Auto-Anchoring IK
  const [activePropAnchors, setActivePropAnchors] = useState<
    Record<string, PropAnchorPorts>
  >({});

  // =========================================================================
  // TÃŒM SCENE HIá»†N Táº I (Äáº£m báº£o an toÃ n 100% khÃ´ng bá»‹ undefined/NaN)
  // =========================================================================
  const safeScenes = Array.isArray(effectiveGraph?.scenes) && effectiveGraph.scenes.length > 0
    ? effectiveGraph.scenes
    : sampleScriptSceneGraph.scenes;

  const currentScene: SceneGraphScene =
    safeScenes.find((s) => {
      const sStart = Number(s?.startFrame) || 0;
      const sDur = Number(s?.durationInFrames) || 120;
      return frame >= sStart && frame < sStart + sDur;
    }) || safeScenes[safeScenes.length - 1];

  const sceneStartFrame = Number(currentScene?.startFrame) || 0;
  const sceneLocalFrame = frame - sceneStartFrame;

  const safeSubtitles = Array.isArray(currentScene?.subtitles) ? currentScene.subtitles : [];
  const safeSpeechBubbles = Array.isArray(currentScene?.speechBubbles) ? currentScene.speechBubbles : [];
  const safeKeyframes = Array.isArray(currentScene?.keyframes) ? currentScene.keyframes : [];

  // =========================================================================
  // TÃŒM SUBTITLE & SPEECH BUBBLE ACTIVE
  // =========================================================================
  const activeSubtitle = safeSubtitles.find((sub) => {
    const s = Number(sub?.startFrame) || 0;
    const e = Number(sub?.endFrame) || 0;
    return frame >= s && frame <= e;
  });

  const activeSpeechBubbles = safeSpeechBubbles.filter((b) => {
    const s = Number(b?.startFrame) || 0;
    const e = Number(b?.endFrame) || 0;
    return frame >= s && frame <= e;
  });

  // =========================================================================
  // GIáº¢I QUYáº¾T KEYFRAMES â†’ Cáº­p nháº­t props/character state theo thá»i gian
  // =========================================================================
  const activeKeyframes = useMemo(
    () => safeKeyframes.filter((kf) => sceneLocalFrame >= (Number(kf?.frame) || 0)),
    [safeKeyframes, sceneLocalFrame]
  );

  // =========================================================================
  // TÃNH TOÃN TRáº NG THÃI NHÃ‚N Váº¬T Vá»šI MICRO-ANIMATIONS & DYNAMIC MOTION
  // =========================================================================
  const sceneText = `${currentScene.name || ""} ${safeSubtitles.map((s) => s.text).join(" ")}`.toLowerCase();
  const hasHoldAction = /cáº§m|bÃª|mang|xÃ¡ch|giá»¯|nÃ¢ng|holding|carry/i.test(sceneText);

  const isSitting = currentScene.character.pose === "sittingBehindDesk";
  const isHolding =
    currentScene.character.pose === "holdingObject" ||
    (hasHoldAction && currentScene.props.length > 0 && !isSitting);
  const isCharacterVisible =
    currentScene.character.visible !== false && currentScene.character.x > -9000;

  // PhÃ¢n giáº£i vá»‹ trÃ­ chuáº©n qua Semantic Slot (Zero-Guessing Pixel Engine)
  const baseCharPos = resolveSemanticSlot(currentScene.character.slot, {
    x: currentScene.character.x,
    y: currentScene.character.y ?? STAGE_CONFIG.floorY,
  });

  // Báº¯t Ä‘áº§u vá»›i state ban Ä‘áº§u
  const dynamicCharacterState: StickmanState = {
    ...currentScene.character,
    x: baseCharPos.x,
    y: baseCharPos.y,
  };
  if (isHolding) {
    dynamicCharacterState.pose = "holdingObject";
  }

  // --- Ãp dá»¥ng keyframe "moveCharacter" Náº¾U VÃ€ CHá»ˆ Náº¾U cÃ³ keyframe rÃµ rÃ ng ---
  // TÃNH TOÃN Váº¬N Tá»C THá»°C Táº¾ & CHá»ˆ BÆ¯á»šC ÄI KHI ÄANG DI CHUYá»‚N
  const isWalkingPose = dynamicCharacterState.pose === "walking";
  const moveCharKf: any = activeKeyframes.find(
    (kf: any) =>
      (kf.action === "moveCharacter" || kf.type === "moveCharacter") &&
      (kf.target === "character" || kf.targetId === "stickman_main" || !kf.target)
  );
  let charVelocity = 0;

  if (isCharacterVisible) {
    if (moveCharKf) {
      const rawParams = moveCharKf.params || moveCharKf;
      const resolvedFrom = rawParams.fromSlot ? resolveSemanticSlot(rawParams.fromSlot, baseCharPos) : undefined;
      const resolvedTo = rawParams.toSlot ? resolveSemanticSlot(rawParams.toSlot, baseCharPos) : undefined;

      const fromX = rawParams.fromX !== undefined ? Number(rawParams.fromX) : (resolvedFrom?.x ?? baseCharPos.x);
      const toX = rawParams.toX !== undefined ? Number(rawParams.toX) : (resolvedTo?.x ?? baseCharPos.x);

      // Náº¿u Ä‘iá»ƒm Ä‘áº¿n khÃ¡c Ä‘iá»ƒm Ä‘i: Di chuyá»ƒn mÆ°á»£t mÃ  vÃ  kÃ­ch hoáº¡t bÆ°á»›c Ä‘i
      if (Math.abs(toX - fromX) > 10) {
        const walkStartFrame = Number(moveCharKf?.frame ?? moveCharKf?.startFrame ?? 0);
        const walkDuration = Math.max(60, Math.min(Number(rawParams.durationFrames) || (currentScene.durationInFrames * 0.8), 300));
        const walkLocalProgress = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (sceneLocalFrame - walkStartFrame) / walkDuration));

        const easedProgress = interpolate(
          walkLocalProgress,
          [0, 1],
          [0, 1],
          {
            extrapolateLeft: "clamp",
            extrapolateRight: "clamp",
            easing: Easing.bezier(0.25, 0.1, 0.25, 1),
          }
        );

        dynamicCharacterState.x = interpolate(easedProgress, [0, 1], [fromX, toX]);

        if (sceneLocalFrame >= walkStartFrame && sceneLocalFrame < walkStartFrame + walkDuration) {
          charVelocity = Math.abs(toX - fromX) / (walkDuration * 0.5);
          dynamicCharacterState.pose = "walking";
        } else {
          charVelocity = 0;
        }
      } else {
        // Tá»a Ä‘á»™ giá»¯ nguyÃªn
        dynamicCharacterState.x = baseCharPos.x;
        charVelocity = 0;
      }

      if (rawParams.fromY !== undefined && rawParams.toY !== undefined) {
        dynamicCharacterState.y = springMove(
          sceneLocalFrame,
          fps,
          Number(rawParams.fromY),
          Number(rawParams.toY),
          Number(moveCharKf.frame ?? 0),
          { damping: 18, stiffness: 70, mass: 0.8, overshootClamping: true }
        );
      }
    } else {
      // KhÃ´ng cÃ³ keyframe di chuyá»ƒn: Giá»¯ nguyÃªn vá»¯ng cháº¯c táº¡i vá»‹ trÃ­ Semantic Slot, tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i khÃ´ng trÃ´i dáº¡t
      dynamicCharacterState.x = baseCharPos.x;
      charVelocity = 0;
    }
  }

  // --- Hiá»‡u á»©ng bÆ°á»›c chÃ¢n (Walk Cycle Animation) KHI VÃ€ CHá»ˆ KHI ÄANG DI CHUYá»‚N THá»°C Sá»° ---
  const isWalkingActive = (isWalkingPose || charVelocity > 0.1) && charVelocity > 0.1;
  const walkWeight = isWalkingActive ? 1.0 : 0;
  dynamicCharacterState.walkWeight = walkWeight;

  if (isWalkingActive) {
    // Tá»‘c Ä‘á»™ bÆ°á»›c tá»± nhiÃªn: ~0.95s cho 1 chu ká»³ 2 bÆ°á»›c (~55 frames @ 60 FPS)
    dynamicCharacterState.walkCycleProgress = (sceneLocalFrame * 0.018) % 1;
    // Nháº¥p nhÃ´ hÃ´ng tá»± nhiÃªn theo bÆ°á»›c Ä‘i
    const walkBobY = Math.abs(Math.sin(sceneLocalFrame * 0.018 * Math.PI * 2)) * 6.0;
    dynamicCharacterState.y = (dynamicCharacterState.y || STAGE_CONFIG.floorY) - walkBobY;
  } else {
    // Khi Ä‘á»©ng yÃªn: CHÃ‚N Äá»¨NG IM CHáº M Äáº¤T 100%, khÃ´ng Ä‘áº¡p chÃ¢n, khÃ´ng trÆ°á»£t chÃ¢n
    dynamicCharacterState.walkCycleProgress = 0;
    dynamicCharacterState.walkWeight = 0;
  }

  // --- Ãp dá»¥ng keyframe "changeExpression" ---
  const exprKeyframes = activeKeyframes.filter(
    (kf) => kf.action === "changeExpression" && kf.target === "character"
  );
  if (exprKeyframes.length > 0) {
    const latest = exprKeyframes[exprKeyframes.length - 1];
    if (latest.params.eyes) dynamicCharacterState.eyes = latest.params.eyes;
    if (latest.params.mouth) dynamicCharacterState.mouth = latest.params.mouth;
    if (latest.params.eyebrows) dynamicCharacterState.eyebrows = latest.params.eyebrows;
    if (latest.params.blush !== undefined) dynamicCharacterState.blush = latest.params.blush;
    if (latest.params.sweatDrop !== undefined) dynamicCharacterState.sweatDrop = latest.params.sweatDrop;
    if (latest.params.pupilGaze) dynamicCharacterState.pupilGaze = latest.params.pupilGaze;
  }

  // --- Micro-Animations (breathing, blink, talk, conversational gesturing, idle sway) ---
  if (isCharacterVisible) {
    if (shouldBlink(frame, fps)) {
      dynamicCharacterState.eyes = "closed";
    }

    // CHá»ˆ NÃ“I KHI NHÃ‚N Váº¬T THá»°C Sá»° ÄANG NÃ“I (CÃ“ BONG BÃ“NG THOáº I HOáº¶C isSpeaking = true)
    // TUYá»†T Äá»I KHÃ”NG coi lá»i dáº«n phá»¥ Ä‘á» (narration voiceover) lÃ  nhÃ¢n váº­t Ä‘ang nÃ³i!
    const isCharacterSpeaking = Boolean(
      (dynamicCharacterState.isSpeaking || activeSpeechBubbles.length > 0) &&
      dynamicCharacterState.mouth !== "gasp"
    );
    if (isCharacterSpeaking) {
      if (talkingMouthCycle(frame, fps)) {
        dynamicCharacterState.mouth = "talking";
      }
    }

    // Dynamic Conversational Gesturing:
    // CHá»ˆ cá»­ Ä‘á»™ng tay khi nhÃ¢n váº­t thá»±c sá»± nÃ³i VÃ€ khÃ´ng cÃ³ limbAngles Ä‘Æ°á»£c chá»‰ Ä‘á»‹nh tá»« trÆ°á»›c
    if (dynamicCharacterState.pose === "standing" && isCharacterSpeaking) {
      const hasCustomRightArm =
        currentScene.character.limbAngles?.rightUpperArm !== undefined;
      if (!hasCustomRightArm) {
        const speechCadence = Math.sin(sceneLocalFrame * 0.14);
        const armGestureLift = Math.max(0, speechCadence) * 12;
        dynamicCharacterState.limbAngles = {
          ...dynamicCharacterState.limbAngles,
          rightUpperArm: (dynamicCharacterState.limbAngles?.rightUpperArm ?? -18) - armGestureLift * 0.4,
          rightLowerArm: (dynamicCharacterState.limbAngles?.rightLowerArm ?? -16) - armGestureLift * 0.5,
          head: (dynamicCharacterState.limbAngles?.head ?? 0) + Math.sin(sceneLocalFrame * 0.08) * 2,
        };
      }
    } else if (dynamicCharacterState.pose === "sittingBehindDesk") {
      // Khi ngá»“i sau bÃ n lÃ m viá»‡c: Äáº§u hÆ¡i cÃºi táº­p trung, tay cá»­ Ä‘á»™ng nháº¹ nhÃ ng trÃªn máº·t bÃ n
      const deskCadence = Math.sin(sceneLocalFrame * 0.16) * 3.5;
      dynamicCharacterState.limbAngles = {
        ...dynamicCharacterState.limbAngles,
        head: -7 + Math.sin(sceneLocalFrame * 0.07) * 2,
        rightLowerArm: -38 + deskCadence,
        leftLowerArm: -42 - deskCadence * 0.5,
      };
    }
  }

  // --- Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng gÃ¡n holdingTarget tá»« Auto-Anchoring IK (Æ¯u tiÃªn váº­t thá»ƒ cáº§m tay di Ä‘á»™ng) ---
  const primaryProp = currentScene.props.find((p) => {
    const id = normalizePropAssetId(p.type);
    return !["sofa", "desk", "desk-front", "desk-back", "tree", "bush", "door", "whiteboard", "tv", "table"].includes(id);
  }) || currentScene.props[0];

  if (isHolding && primaryProp) {
    const propHoldingX = dynamicCharacterState.x + (dynamicCharacterState.facing === "left" ? -140 : 140);
    const propHoldingY = (dynamicCharacterState.y || STAGE_CONFIG.floorY) - 170;

    if (activePropAnchors[primaryProp.id]) {
      const anchors = activePropAnchors[primaryProp.id];
      dynamicCharacterState.holdingTarget = {
        leftGrip: anchors.leftGrip,
        rightGrip: anchors.rightGrip,
      };
    } else {
      dynamicCharacterState.holdingTarget = {
        leftGrip: { x: propHoldingX - 110, y: propHoldingY },
        rightGrip: { x: propHoldingX + 110, y: propHoldingY },
      };
    }
  }

  // =========================================================================
  // ÃNH Xáº  PROP ASSET ID (Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng chuáº©n hÃ³a camelCase & alias sang file vector 2K tháº­t)
  // =========================================================================
  const resolvePropAssetId = (type: string) => normalizePropAssetId(type);

  // =========================================================================
  // TÃNH TOÃN Vá»Š TRÃ PROP Vá»šI ENTRANCE ANIMATION + KEYFRAMES + SEMANTIC SLOTS + VFX
  // =========================================================================
  const computePropTransform = (
    prop: typeof currentScene.props[0]
  ): {
    x: number;
    y: number;
    scaleVal: number;
    rotation: number;
    squash: { scaleX: number; scaleY: number };
    needleAngle?: number;
  } => {
    const basePos = resolveSemanticSlot(prop.slot, { x: prop.x, y: prop.y });
    let px = basePos.x;
    let py = basePos.y;
    let ps = prop.scale ?? 1;
    let pr = prop.rotation ?? 0;
    let sq = { scaleX: 1, scaleY: 1 };
    let needleAngleVal: number | undefined = undefined;

    // --- Entrance Animation ---
    if (prop.entrance) {
      const { type, delay = 0, from, springConfig: cfg } = prop.entrance;
      const enterProgress = springEnter(sceneLocalFrame, fps, delay, cfg);

      switch (type) {
        case "spring":
        case "slide": {
          const offsetDist = 800;
          if (from === "left") px = interpolate(enterProgress, [0, 1], [basePos.x - offsetDist, basePos.x]);
          else if (from === "right") px = interpolate(enterProgress, [0, 1], [basePos.x + offsetDist, basePos.x]);
          else if (from === "top") py = interpolate(enterProgress, [0, 1], [basePos.y - offsetDist, basePos.y]);
          else if (from === "bottom") py = interpolate(enterProgress, [0, 1], [basePos.y + offsetDist, basePos.y]);
          break;
        }
        case "pop": {
          ps = interpolate(springScale(sceneLocalFrame, fps, delay, cfg), [0, 1], [0, prop.scale ?? 1]);
          break;
        }
        case "drop": {
          const startY = from === "top" ? -200 : basePos.y - 600;
          py = interpolate(enterProgress, [0, 1], [startY, basePos.y]);
          break;
        }
        case "fadeIn": {
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    // --- Keyframe overrides (moveProp, scaleProp, squashImpact, rotateProp) ---
    const propKeyframes = activeKeyframes.filter((kf) => kf.target === prop.id);
    for (const kf of propKeyframes) {
      switch (kf.action) {
        case "moveProp": {
          if (kf.params.fromY !== undefined && kf.params.toY !== undefined) {
            py = springMove(sceneLocalFrame, fps, kf.params.fromY, kf.params.toY, kf.frame, kf.springConfig);
          }
          if (kf.params.fromX !== undefined && kf.params.toX !== undefined) {
            px = springMove(sceneLocalFrame, fps, kf.params.fromX, kf.params.toX, kf.frame, kf.springConfig);
          }
          break;
        }
        case "scaleProp": {
          if (kf.params.fromScale !== undefined && kf.params.toScale !== undefined) {
            ps = springMove(sceneLocalFrame, fps, kf.params.fromScale, kf.params.toScale, kf.frame, kf.springConfig);
          }
          break;
        }
        case "squashImpact": {
          const impactF = kf.params.impactFrame ?? kf.frame;
          sq = squashStretch(sceneLocalFrame, fps, impactF);
          break;
        }
        case "rotateProp": {
          if (kf.params.fromDeg !== undefined && kf.params.toDeg !== undefined) {
            pr = springMove(sceneLocalFrame, fps, kf.params.fromDeg, kf.params.toDeg, kf.frame, kf.springConfig);
          }
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    // --- Prop Animations tá»« VFX Engine (rotateNeedle, scaleBounce) ---
    if (currentScene.propAnimations) {
      const anim = currentScene.propAnimations.find((a) => a.targetId === prop.id);
      if (anim) {
        if (anim.type === "rotateNeedle" && anim.fromDeg !== undefined && anim.toDeg !== undefined) {
          const needleProgress = interpolate(
            sceneLocalFrame,
            [0, Math.min(60, currentScene.durationInFrames)],
            [0, 1],
            {
              extrapolateLeft: "clamp",
              extrapolateRight: "clamp",
              easing: Easing.bezier(0.25, 0.1, 0.25, 1),
            }
          );
          needleAngleVal = interpolate(needleProgress, [0, 1], [anim.fromDeg, anim.toDeg]);
        }
        if (anim.type === "scaleBounce" && anim.fromScale !== undefined && anim.toScale !== undefined) {
          const bounceProgress = springScale(sceneLocalFrame, fps, 0, { damping: 12, stiffness: 140, mass: 0.5 });
          ps = interpolate(bounceProgress, [0, 1], [anim.fromScale, anim.toScale]);
        }
      }
    }

    // Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng xoay kim sinh Ä‘á»™ng cho gauge-meter náº¿u chÆ°a cÃ³ keyframe cá»¥ thá»ƒ
    if (resolvePropAssetId(prop.type) === "gauge-meter" && needleAngleVal === undefined) {
      const progress = interpolate(sceneLocalFrame, [10, 75], [0, 1], {
        extrapolateLeft: "clamp",
        extrapolateRight: "clamp",
        easing: Easing.bezier(0.25, 0.1, 0.25, 1),
      });
      needleAngleVal = interpolate(progress, [0, 1], [-70, -25]);
    }

    // Náº¿u nhÃ¢n váº­t Ä‘ang cáº§m Ä‘áº¡o cá»¥ nÃ y, dÃ­nh cháº·t theo tay nhÃ¢n váº­t
    if (isHolding && primaryProp && prop.id === primaryProp.id) {
      px = dynamicCharacterState.x + (dynamicCharacterState.facing === "left" ? -140 : 140);
      py = (dynamicCharacterState.y || STAGE_CONFIG.floorY) - 170;
    }

    return { x: px, y: py, scaleVal: ps, rotation: pr, squash: sq, needleAngle: needleAngleVal };
  };

  // =========================================================================
  // ENTRANCE OPACITY (cho fadeIn)
  // =========================================================================
  const computeEntranceOpacity = (prop: typeof currentScene.props[0]): number => {
    if (prop.entrance?.type === "fadeIn") {
      return springEnter(sceneLocalFrame, fps, prop.entrance.delay ?? 0, prop.entrance.springConfig);
    }
    return prop.opacity ?? 1;
  };

  // =========================================================================
  // TÃNH TOÃN Vá»Š TRÃ SPIDER SWAY
  // =========================================================================
  const spiderOffset = spiderSway(frame, fps);

  // =========================================================================
  // BREATHING & IDLE CHU Ká»² CON NGÆ¯á»œI (ORIGIN = BOTTOM-MID, SCALE Y Tá»ª 0.98 Äáº¾N 1.03)
  // Chu ká»³ thá»Ÿ: 1.75s (~105 frames @ 60 FPS), chÃ¢n cá»‘ Ä‘á»‹nh 100% trÃªn sÃ n, KHÃ”NG trÆ°á»£t ngang
  // =========================================================================
  const breathPhase = (frame * Math.PI * 2) / 105;
  const humanBreathScaleY = isCharacterVisible
    ? 1.005 + Math.sin(breathPhase) * 0.025 // Dao Ä‘á»™ng chuáº©n tá»« 0.98 Ä‘áº¿n 1.03
    : 1.0;

  // =========================================================================
  // DYNAMIC LINES: Giáº£i quyáº¿t anchor refs thÃ nh tá»a Ä‘á»™
  // =========================================================================
  const activeLines = (currentScene.lines || []).filter(
    (line) => frame >= line.startFrame && frame <= line.endFrame
  );

  // Há»‡ tá»a Ä‘á»™ thiáº¿t káº¿ chuáº©n 2K (2560x1440), tá»± Ä‘á»™ng scale Ä‘á»“ng nháº¥t sang Full HD 1080p (1920x1080) khi render
  const DESIGN_WIDTH = 2560;
  const DESIGN_HEIGHT = 1440;

  const anchorContext: AnchorResolverContext = {
    propAnchors: Object.fromEntries(
      Object.entries(activePropAnchors).map(([id, ports]) => [
        id,
        {
          x: currentScene.props.find((p) => p.id === id)?.x ?? 0,
          y: currentScene.props.find((p) => p.id === id)?.y ?? 0,
          leftGrip: ports.leftGrip,
          rightGrip: ports.rightGrip,
          surfaceDecal: ports.surfaceDecal,
        },
      ])
    ),
    characterAnchors: {},
    canvasHeight: DESIGN_HEIGHT,
  };

  const resolveLineEndpoints = (
    line: DynamicLineItem
  ): { start: Point2D; end: Point2D } => {
    const start = line.startFixed
      ? line.startFixed
      : line.startAnchorRef
        ? resolveAnchorRef(line.startAnchorRef, anchorContext)
        : { x: 0, y: 0 };
    const end = line.endFixed
      ? line.endFixed
      : line.endAnchorRef
        ? resolveAnchorRef(line.endAnchorRef, anchorContext)
        : { x: 0, y: 0 };
    return { start, end };
  };

  // Kiá»ƒm tra xem scene cÃ³ hiá»‡u á»©ng Ä‘ung Ä‘Æ°a cá» cÃ¢y khÃ´ng
  const hasFoliageSway = currentScene.environmentVfx?.includes("foliageSway");

  // =========================================================================
  // RENDER THEO CHIá»€U SÃ‚U & Há»† THá»NG CAMERA RIG + VFX SHADERS
  // =========================================================================
  return (
    <AbsoluteFill style={{ width: "100%", height: "100%", backgroundColor: "#F8EFE3", overflow: "hidden" }}>
      <svg
        viewBox={`0 0 ${DESIGN_WIDTH} ${DESIGN_HEIGHT}`}
        className="w-full h-full select-none"
        style={{ width: "100%", height: "100%" }}
        preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet"
      >
        {/* ===============================================================
            1. THÆ¯ VIá»†N SHADER & GRADIENT ÄIá»†N áº¢NH (SVG DEFS LIBRARY)
        =============================================================== */}
        <SvgDefsLibrary />

        {/* ===============================================================
            2. CAMERA RIG VIEWPORT (ÄIá»€U KHIá»‚N ZOOM & TARGET TRACKING)
        =============================================================== */}
        <CameraRig
          camera={currentScene.camera}
          sceneLocalFrame={sceneLocalFrame}
          durationInFrames={currentScene.durationInFrames}
          character={dynamicCharacterState}
          propsList={currentScene.props}
          canvasWidth={DESIGN_WIDTH}
          canvasHeight={DESIGN_HEIGHT}
        >
          {/* LAYER 10: DYNAMIC BACKGROUND */}
          <g id="layer-background">
            <DynamicBackground preset={currentScene.background.preset} />
          </g>

          {/* LAYER 15: PROCEDURAL ENVIRONMENT VFX (MÃ‚Y TRÃ”I, MÆ¯A, TIA Náº®NG, GIÃ“) */}
          <EnvironmentVfxLayer
            effects={currentScene.environmentVfx}
            lighting={currentScene.lighting}
            backgroundPreset={currentScene.background.preset}
            frame={frame}
            fps={fps}
            canvasWidth={DESIGN_WIDTH}
            canvasHeight={DESIGN_HEIGHT}
          />

          {/* LAYER 20: MIDGROUND BACK LAYER (Sofa, ChÃ¢n sau bÃ n há»c) */}
          <g id="layer-midground-back">
            {(() => {
              const hasJarWithSpider = currentScene.props.some((p) => {
                const aid = resolvePropAssetId(p.type);
                return (aid === "glass-jar" || /jar|thuy-tinh|glass/i.test(p.type)) && Boolean(p.properties?.hasSpiderInside);
              });

              return currentScene.props
                .filter((p) => {
                  if (p.visible === false || p.layer !== "midgroundBackLayer") return false;
                  // Chá»‘ng trÃ¹ng láº·p prop: náº¿u Ä‘Ã£ cÃ³ lá» chá»©a nhá»‡n bÃªn trong, tá»± Ä‘á»™ng xÃ³a bá» instance thá»«a cá»§a con nhá»‡n á»Ÿ ngoÃ i sÃ n
                  if (hasJarWithSpider && resolvePropAssetId(p.type) === "spider") return false;
                  return true;
                })
                .map((prop) => {
                  const t = computePropTransform(prop);
                  const opacity = computeEntranceOpacity(prop);
                  const isScreenSlot = Boolean(prop.slot && prop.slot.startsWith("screen."));
                  const isExplicitCenter = prop.anchorPoint === "center" || prop.pivotPoint === "center" || isScreenSlot;
                  const propAnchor = isExplicitCenter ? "center" : "bottomCenter";
                  return (
                    <g key={prop.id} opacity={opacity}>
                      <DynamicProp
                        id={resolvePropAssetId(prop.type)}
                        x={t.x}
                        y={t.y}
                        scale={t.scaleVal}
                        scaleX={prop.scaleX}
                        scaleY={prop.scaleY}
                        rotation={t.rotation}
                        opacity={prop.opacity}
                        alphaConfig={prop.alphaConfig}
                        frame={sceneLocalFrame}
                        sceneDurationInFrames={currentScene.durationInFrames}
                        anchorPoint={propAnchor}
                        pivotPoint={prop.pivotPoint}
                        pivotX={prop.pivotX}
                        pivotY={prop.pivotY}
                        foliageSway={hasFoliageSway}
                      />
                    </g>
                  );
                });
            })()}

            {isSitting && (
              <DynamicProp
                id="desk-back"
                x={STAGE_CONFIG.deskX}
                y={STAGE_CONFIG.deskY}
                scale={1.0}
                defaultDimensions={{ width: 1100, height: 400 }}
              />
            )}
          </g>

          {/* LAYER 25: DYNAMIC LINES â€” BEHIND CHARACTER (silk, light cones) */}
          <g id="layer-lines-back">
            {activeLines
              .filter((l) => l.style === "silk" || l.style === "light")
              .map((line) => {
                const { start, end } = resolveLineEndpoints(line);
                return (
                  <DynamicLine
                    key={line.id}
                    start={start}
                    end={end}
                    style={line.style}
                    color={line.color}
                    strokeWidth={line.strokeWidth}
                    animated={line.animated}
                    animationDelay={line.startFrame - currentScene.startFrame}
                    coneAngle={line.coneAngle}
                    coneLength={line.coneLength}
                  />
                );
              })}
          </g>

          {/* LAYER 30: CHARACTER LOWER LAYER KHI NGá»’I SAU BÃ€N (TOÃ€N Bá»˜ Äáº¦U, THÃ‚N VÃ€ CHÃ‚N á»ž SAU Máº¶T BÃ€N) */}
          {isSitting && isCharacterVisible && (
            <g id="layer-character-seated">
              <StickmanRig
                state={dynamicCharacterState}
                renderPart="bodyOnly"
                breathScaleY={humanBreathScaleY}
              />
            </g>
          )}

          {/* LAYER 35: Máº¶T BÃ€N Gá»– (TABLETOP) CHE KHUáº¤T HÃ”NG VÃ€ Bá»¤NG DÆ¯á»šI NHÃ‚N Váº¬T */}
          {isSitting && (
            <g id="layer-desk-front">
              <DynamicProp
                id="desk-front"
                x={STAGE_CONFIG.deskX}
                y={STAGE_CONFIG.deskY}
                scale={1.0}
                defaultDimensions={{ width: 1100, height: 400 }}
              />
            </g>
          )}

                  points={`${lt.x + 20 * lt.scaleVal},${lt.y - 115 * lt.scaleVal} ${lt.x - 180 * lt.scaleVal},${lt.y + 5} ${lt.x + 220 * lt.scaleVal},${lt.y + 5}`}

            {(() => {

      =============================================================== */}
      {(activeSubtitle || activeSpeechBubbles.length > 0) && (
        <AbsoluteFill
          style={{
            position: "absolute",
            inset: 0,
            zIndex: 99999,
            pointerEvents: "none",
          }}
        >
          <svg
            viewBox={`0 0 ${DESIGN_WIDTH} ${DESIGN_HEIGHT}`}
            className="w-full h-full select-none"
            preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet"
          >
            {/* Bong bÃ³ng lá»i thoáº¡i ná»•i trÃªn UI */}
            {activeSpeechBubbles.map((bubble) => {

                frame,
                fps,
                bubble.startFrame,
                { damping: 12, stiffness: 150, mass: 0.5 }
              );
              return (
                <g
                  key={bubble.id}
                  transform={`translate(${bubble.position.x}, ${bubble.position.y}) scale(${bubbleProgress}) translate(${-bubble.position.x}, ${-bubble.position.y})`}

                  <SpeechBubble
                    x={bubble.position.x}
                    y={bubble.position.y}
                    text={bubble.text}
                    hasMusicNote={bubble.hasMusicNote}
                    tailAnchor={bubble.tailAnchor}
                  />
                </g>
              );
            })}

            {/* Há»™p phá»¥ Ä‘á» ná»•i */}
            {activeSubtitle && (
              <SubtitleBox
                text={activeSubtitle.text}
                words={activeSubtitle.words}
                currentFrame={frame}
                fps={fps}
                x={1280}
                y={1320}
              />
            )}
          </svg>
        </AbsoluteFill>
      )}
    </AbsoluteFill>
  );
};

  QA01_IR,
  QA02_IR,
  QA03_IR,
  QA04_IR,
  QA05_IR,
} from "./compositions/demoPresets";
import { MasterSceneGraph } from "./types/sceneGraph";

export interface RemotionProps {
  [key: string]: unknown;
  sceneGraph?: MasterSceneGraph;
  scenes?: any[];
  totalFrames?: number;
  ir?: any;
}

const calculateMetadata: CalculateMetadataFunction<any> = async ({ props }) => {
  let graph: MasterSceneGraph | undefined;

  // 1. Æ¯u tiÃªn cao nháº¥t: Náº¿u props cÃ³ sceneGraph rÃµ rÃ ng vÃ  khÃ¡c defaultMockSceneGraph
  if (props?.sceneGraph && props.sceneGraph !== defaultMockSceneGraph) {
    graph = props.sceneGraph as MasterSceneGraph;
  }
  // 2. Náº¿u props truyá»n trá»±c tiáº¿p danh sÃ¡ch scenes cá»§a ká»‹ch báº£n ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng
  else if (props?.scenes && Array.isArray(props.scenes) && props.scenes.length > 0) {
    graph = {
      version: (props.version as string) || "3.1.0",
      title: (props.title as string) || "Stickman 2D Explainer Video",
      width: Number(props.width) || 2560,
      height: Number(props.height) || 1440,
      fps: Number(props.fps) || 60,
      totalDurationInFrames: Number(props.totalDurationInFrames) || Number(props.totalFrames) || 480,
      scenes: props.scenes,
    };
  }
  // 3. Fallback: Náº¿u cÃ³ sceneGraph
  else if (props?.sceneGraph) {
    graph = props.sceneGraph as MasterSceneGraph;
  } else {
    graph = defaultMockSceneGraph;
  }

  const safeScenes = Array.isArray(graph?.scenes) ? graph.scenes : defaultMockSceneGraph.scenes;
  const scenesEnd = safeScenes.reduce((acc, s) => {
    const start = Number(s?.startFrame) || 0;
    const dur = Number(s?.durationInFrames) || 120;
    return Math.max(acc, start + dur);
  }, 0);

  const requestedTotal = Number(props?.totalFrames) || 0;
  const sceneDuration = scenesEnd || Number(graph?.totalDurationInFrames) || 480;
  const actualDuration = Math.max(60, sceneDuration, requestedTotal);

  return {
    durationInFrames: actualDuration,
    props: {
      ...props,
      sceneGraph: graph,
      scenes: graph.scenes,
      totalFrames: actualDuration,
      width: 1920,
      height: 1080,
      fps: 60,
    },
  };
};

import { GoogleGenerativeAI } from "@google/generative-ai";
import fs from "fs";
import { sanitizeSceneGraph } from "../src/remotion/utils/sceneGraphSanitizer";

const apiKey = process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY;
if (!apiKey) {
  console.error("GEMINI_API_KEY not found in environment.");
  process.exit(1);
}

const secondScript = `Cáº£nh 1: Má»™t chÃº mÃ¨o con nghá»‹ch ngá»£m bÃªn cáº¡nh chiáº¿c há»™p giáº¥y Ä‘áº·t trÃªn sÃ n phÃ²ng khÃ¡ch.
Cáº£nh 2: PhÃ³ng cáº­n cáº£nh vÃ o chiáº¿c lá» thá»§y tinh Ä‘á»±ng nhá»¯ng Ä‘á»“ng xu láº¥p lÃ¡nh cÃ³ dÃ¡n nhÃ£n 'Tiáº¿t Kiá»‡m'.
Cáº£nh 3: Minh há»a biá»ƒu Ä‘á»“ tÄƒng trÆ°á»Ÿng tÃ i chÃ­nh vá»›i cÃ¡c chá»‰ sá»‘ Ä‘ang vÆ°Æ¡n lÃªn máº¡nh máº½.
Cáº£nh 4: ÄÃªm muá»™n, ngÆ°á»i ká»¹ sÆ° ngá»“i sau bÃ n há»c cáº·m cá»¥i gÃµ mÃ¡y tÃ­nh dÆ°á»›i Ã¡nh Ä‘Ã¨n bÃ n vÃ ng áº¥m.`;

async function main() {
  const genAI = new GoogleGenerativeAI(apiKey!);
  // Thá»­ nghiá»‡m cháº¡y vá»›i gemini-3.1-flash-lite Ä‘á»ƒ kiá»ƒm chá»©ng tÃ­nh tá»•ng quÃ¡t cá»§a pipeline
  const modelName = "gemini-3.1-flash-lite";
  console.log(`\n======================================================`);
  console.log(`TESTING GENERALIZED BENCHMARK SCRIPT WITH ${modelName}`);
  console.log(`======================================================`);

  const SYSTEM_PROMPT = `
You are the Lead 2D Animation Director and Spatial Scene Graph Architect for a 2K 60FPS Stickman Explainer Video Engine.
Your job is to transform a story script into a precise, valid MasterSceneGraph JSON structure.

Strict Rules:
1. Target resolution: 2560x1440 at 60 FPS.
2. Contiguous Timeline & Audio Synchronization.
3. FULL SCENE FIDELITY: Generate EVERY scene described.
4. SEMANTIC SLOTS:
   - Floor slots: 'floor.farLeft', 'floor.left', 'floor.centerLeft', 'floor.center', 'floor.centerRight', 'floor.right', 'floor.farRight'
   - Desk slots: 'desk.seat', 'desk.topLeft', 'desk.topCenter', 'desk.topRight'
   - Close-up slots: 'screen.center', 'screen.centerCloseUp'
5. CHARACTER POSES & VISIBILITY:
   - When a scene focuses purely on a prop close-up or diagram: character MUST have 'visible: false'!
   - When character is sitting at desk / working / typing: pose MUST be 'sittingBehindDesk' with slot 'desk.seat'!
6. CONTAINMENT & OBJECT INTEGRITY:
   - When an object is inside a container, set container properties (e.g. label), do not spawn duplicate props!
7. SUBTITLES & SPOKEN NARRATION:
   - 'subtitles': text must ONLY be the spoken dialogue or spoken narration without camera/scene directions.
Output ONLY a valid JSON object matching the MasterSceneGraph schema. No markdown formatting.
`;

  const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({
    model: modelName,
    generationConfig: { responseMimeType: "application/json" },
    systemInstruction: SYSTEM_PROMPT,
  });

  const startTime = Date.now();
  console.log(`Calling ${modelName}...`);
  const result = await model.generateContent(`Táº¡o Scene Graph JSON cho ká»‹ch báº£n sau:\n\n${secondScript}`);
  const text = result.response.text();
  const latency = Date.now() - startTime;
  console.log(`Model ${modelName} responded in ${latency}ms.`);

  const rawParsed = JSON.parse(text);
  fs.writeFileSync("test/benchmark_second_raw.json", JSON.stringify(rawParsed, null, 2));

  const sanitized = sanitizeSceneGraph(rawParsed, secondScript);
  fs.writeFileSync("test/benchmark_second_sanitized.json", JSON.stringify(sanitized, null, 2));

  console.log("\n--- SANITIZED SCENE GRAPH REPORT ---");
  for (let i = 0; i < sanitized.scenes.length; i++) {
    const scene = sanitized.scenes[i];
    console.log(`\nScene ${i + 1}: ${scene.name}`);
    console.log(`  Background: ${scene.background.preset}`);
    console.log(`  Camera zoom: ${scene.camera?.zoom}, focusSlot: ${scene.camera?.focusSlot}`);
    console.log(`  Character: visible=${scene.character.visible}, pose=${scene.character.pose}, slot=${scene.character.slot}`);
    console.log(`  Props (${scene.props.length}):`);
    for (const p of scene.props) {
      console.log(`    - [${p.type}] id=${p.id}, slot=${p.slot}, scale=${p.scale}, label=${p.properties?.label}`);
    }
    console.log(`  Subtitles: "${scene.subtitles?.[0]?.text}"`);
    console.log(`  Lighting: ${scene.lighting?.preset}`);
  }
}

main().catch(err => {
  console.error("Benchmark error:", err);
  process.exit(1);
});

import { normalizePropAssetId } from "../src/remotion/utils/propResolver";
import { sanitizeSceneGraph } from "../src/remotion/utils/sceneGraphSanitizer";
import { MasterSceneGraph } from "../src/remotion/types/sceneGraph";

console.log("=== 1. TESTING PROP NORMALIZATION (GAUGE METER & ARROW) ===");

const testCases: Record<string, string> = {
  "speed-meter": "gauge-meter",
  "speed_meter": "gauge-meter",
  "Speed Meter": "gauge-meter",
  "speedometer": "gauge-meter",
  "speedmeter": "gauge-meter",
  "speed-gauge": "gauge-meter",
  "meter": "gauge-meter",
  "gauge": "gauge-meter",
  "gauge-meter": "gauge-meter",
  "metabolic-chart": "gauge-meter",
  "metabolism": "gauge-meter",
  "trao-doi-chat": "gauge-meter",
  "pointer": "arrow-indicator",
  "arrow": "arrow-indicator",
  "pointer-arrow": "arrow-indicator",
  "arrow-pointer": "arrow-indicator",
  "arrow-indicator": "arrow-indicator",
  "mui-ten": "arrow-indicator",
  "label": "label-sticker",
  "sticker": "label-sticker",
};

for (const [input, expected] of Object.entries(testCases)) {
  const result = normalizePropAssetId(input);
  if (result !== expected) {
    throw new Error(`Failed for input '${input}': got '${result}', expected '${expected}'`);
  }
}
console.log(">>> All prop normalizations passed 100%!");

import {
  executeGeminiWithRetry,
  isRetryableGeminiError,
  extractRetryAfterSeconds,
  computeBackoffDelay,
} from "../src/lib/geminiRetry";

console.log("==================================================");
console.log("UNIT TEST 1: isRetryableGeminiError");
console.log("==================================================");

// 1. Should retry on 503 Service Unavailable
const err503 = new Error("503 Service Unavailable: This model is currently experiencing high demand.");
console.log("  503 Service Unavailable retryable:", isRetryableGeminiError(err503) === true ? "PASS" : "FAIL");

// 2. Should retry on 429 Too Many Requests
const err429 = new Error("429 ResourceExhausted: Quota exceeded");
console.log("  429 ResourceExhausted retryable:", isRetryableGeminiError(err429) === true ? "PASS" : "FAIL");

// 3. Should NOT retry on 400 Bad Request / INVALID_ARGUMENT
const err400 = new Error("400 INVALID_ARGUMENT: Prompt exceeds context window");
console.log("  400 Bad Request NOT retryable:", isRetryableGeminiError(err400) === false ? "PASS" : "FAIL");

// 4. Should NOT retry on 401/403 API Key Invalid
const err401 = new Error("API_KEY_INVALID: API key not valid. Please pass a valid API key.");
console.log("  401 API_KEY_INVALID NOT retryable:", isRetryableGeminiError(err401) === false ? "PASS" : "FAIL");

console.log("\n==================================================");
console.log("UNIT TEST 2: extractRetryAfterSeconds");
console.log("==================================================");

const errWithHeader = { message: "503 Service Unavailable", retryAfter: 8 };
console.log("  Direct retryAfter=8:", extractRetryAfterSeconds(errWithHeader) === 8 ? "PASS" : "FAIL");

const errWithMsg = new Error("The model is overloaded. Please retry after 12s.");
console.log("  In message 'retry after 12s':", extractRetryAfterSeconds(errWithMsg) === 12 ? "PASS" : "FAIL");

const errNoHeader = new Error("503 Service Unavailable without header");
console.log("  No retry-after returns null:", extractRetryAfterSeconds(errNoHeader) === null ? "PASS" : "FAIL");

console.log("\n==================================================");
console.log("UNIT TEST 3: computeBackoffDelay (5s, 15s, 30s + Jitter)");
console.log("==================================================");

for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= 3; attempt++) {
  const delays: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    delays.push(computeBackoffDelay(attempt, null));
  }
  const min = Math.min(...delays);
  const max = Math.max(...delays);
  console.log(`  Attempt ${attempt} delays (sample): [${delays.map(d => (d/1000).toFixed(2) + "s").join(", ")}] -> range: ${(min/1000).toFixed(1)}s - ${(max/1000).toFixed(1)}s`);
}

const withExplicitRetryAfter = computeBackoffDelay(1, 10);
console.log(`  With Retry-After=10s: ${(withExplicitRetryAfter/1000).toFixed(1)}s (PASS: matches 10s)`);

console.log("\n==================================================");
console.log("UNIT TEST 4: executeGeminiWithRetry - Immediate Failure on Non-Retryable Error");
console.log("==================================================");

async function testNonRetryable() {
  let callCount = 0;
  try {
    await executeGeminiWithRetry(async () => {
      callCount++;
      throw new Error("API_KEY_INVALID: Please check your API key");
    }, { maxRetries: 3 });
  } catch (e: any) {
    console.log(`  Call count for non-retryable error: ${callCount} (Expected: 1) -> ${callCount === 1 ? "PASS" : "FAIL"}`);
    console.log(`  Caught error correctly: ${e.message}`);
  }
}

console.log("\n==================================================");
console.log("UNIT TEST 5: executeGeminiWithRetry - Recovery on Attempt 2 (1 retry)");
console.log("==================================================");

async function testRecovery() {
  let callCount = 0;
  const t0 = Date.now();
  const retryLogs: string[] = [];

  const res = await executeGeminiWithRetry(
    async (attempt) => {
      callCount++;
      if (attempt === 1) {
        const err: any = new Error("503 Service Unavailable - High demand");
        err.status = 503;
        err.retryAfter = 1; // 1s mock for fast test
        throw err;
      }
      return "SUCCESS_DATA";
    },
    {
      maxRetries: 3,
      onRetry: (att, max, delay) => {
        retryLogs.push(`Retry ${att}/${max} after ${(delay/1000).toFixed(1)}s`);
      },
    }
  );

  console.log(`  Result: ${res} (Expected: SUCCESS_DATA) -> ${res === "SUCCESS_DATA" ? "PASS" : "FAIL"}`);
  console.log(`  Total calls: ${callCount} (Expected: 2) -> ${callCount === 2 ? "PASS" : "FAIL"}`);
  console.log(`  Retry log: ${retryLogs.join("; ")}`);
}

console.log("\n==================================================");
console.log("UNIT TEST 6: executeGeminiWithRetry - Exhaustion after 3 retries (4 total attempts)");
console.log("==================================================");

async function testExhaustion() {
  let callCount = 0;
  const retryLogs: string[] = [];

  try {
    await executeGeminiWithRetry(
      async (attempt) => {
        callCount++;
        const err: any = new Error("503 Service Unavailable - Model overloaded");
        err.status = 503;
        err.retryAfter = 0.5; // Fast mock
        throw err;
      },
      {
        maxRetries: 3,
        onRetry: (att, max, delay) => {
          retryLogs.push(`Retry ${att}/${max} after ${(delay/1000).toFixed(1)}s`);
        },
      }
    );
    console.log("  FAIL: Should have thrown after exhausting retries");
  } catch (e: any) {
    console.log(`  Total calls made: ${callCount} (Expected: 4 = 1 initial + 3 retries) -> ${callCount === 4 ? "PASS" : "FAIL"}`);
    console.log(`  Caught exhausted error: ${e.message} -> PASS`);
    console.log(`  Retry logs count: ${retryLogs.length} (Expected: 3) -> ${retryLogs.length === 3 ? "PASS" : "FAIL"}`);
  }
}

async function runAll() {
  await testNonRetryable();
  await testRecovery();
  await testExhaustion();
  console.log("\n>>> ALL MOCK 503 & RETRY TESTS COMPLETED SUCCESSFULLY! <<<");
}

runAll().catch(console.error);

import {
  solveTwoBoneIKHierarchical,
  calculateWalkCycleHierarchical,
  calculateLocalAngle,
  calculateSegmentAngle,
} from "../src/remotion/components/character/Kinematics";

console.log("=== 1. TESTING LOCAL ANGLE CALCULATION ===");
const diff1 = calculateLocalAngle(30, 75);
console.log(`Parent: 30Â°, Child Global: 75Â° => Child Local: ${diff1}Â° (Expected: 45Â°)`);
if (diff1 !== 45) throw new Error("calculateLocalAngle failed for positive angles");

const diff2 = calculateLocalAngle(170, -170);
console.log(`Parent: 170Â°, Child Global: -170Â° => Child Local: ${diff2}Â° (Expected: 20Â°)`);
if (diff2 !== 20) throw new Error("calculateLocalAngle failed across 180Â° boundary");

console.log("\n=== 2. TESTING HIERARCHICAL TWO-BONE IK ===");
const shoulder = { x: 0, y: -660 };
const handTarget = { x: 120, y: -450 };
const upperArmLength = 145;
const forearmLength = 145;

const ikResult = solveTwoBoneIKHierarchical(shoulder, handTarget, upperArmLength, forearmLength, true);
console.log("IK Result:", {
  upperAngleDeg: ikResult.upperAngleDeg.toFixed(1),
  lowerLocalAngleDeg: ikResult.lowerLocalAngleDeg.toFixed(1),
  lowerGlobalAngleDeg: ikResult.lowerGlobalAngleDeg.toFixed(1),
  elbow: ikResult.joint,
  reachedTarget: ikResult.reachedTarget,
});

// Verify Forward Kinematics matches IK solution exactly
// In SVG: rotating (0, L) by angle theta yields (-L * sin(theta), L * cos(theta))
const upperRad = (ikResult.upperAngleDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
const fkElbow = {
  x: shoulder.x - Math.sin(upperRad) * upperArmLength,
  y: shoulder.y + Math.cos(upperRad) * upperArmLength,
};
const lowerGlobalRad = (ikResult.lowerGlobalAngleDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
const fkHand = {
  x: fkElbow.x - Math.sin(lowerGlobalRad) * forearmLength,
  y: fkElbow.y + Math.cos(lowerGlobalRad) * forearmLength,
};

const elbowDist = Math.hypot(fkElbow.x - ikResult.joint.x, fkElbow.y - ikResult.joint.y);
const handDist = Math.hypot(fkHand.x - ikResult.reachedTarget.x, fkHand.y - ikResult.reachedTarget.y);

console.log(`FK Elbow vs IK Joint deviation: ${elbowDist.toFixed(4)}px (Zero detachment!)`);
console.log(`FK Hand vs IK Target deviation: ${handDist.toFixed(4)}px (Zero detachment!)`);

if (elbowDist > 0.001 || handDist > 0.001) {
  throw new Error("FK does not match IK in hierarchical nested transforms!");
}

console.log("\n=== 3. TESTING HIERARCHICAL WALK CYCLE ===");
const hip = { x: 0, y: -350 };
for (const progress of [0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0]) {
  const walk = calculateWalkCycleHierarchical(hip, progress, 65, 22, 175, 175);
  console.log(`Walk Progress ${(progress * 100).toFixed(0)}%:`, {
    leftThigh: walk.leftThighAngle.toFixed(1) + "Â°",
    leftCalfLocal: walk.leftCalfLocalAngle.toFixed(1) + "Â°",
    rightThigh: walk.rightThighAngle.toFixed(1) + "Â°",
    rightCalfLocal: walk.rightCalfLocalAngle.toFixed(1) + "Â°",
  });
}

console.log("\n>>> ALL HIERARCHICAL NESTED RIGGING TESTS PASSED PERFECTLY! <<<");

let allPassed = true;
function assert(condition: boolean, desc: string) {
  if (condition) {
    console.log(`  [PASS] ${desc}`);
  } else {
    console.error(`  [FAIL] ${desc}`);
    allPassed = false;
  }
}

console.log("==================================================");
console.log("TEST 1: Edge-TTS Disabled & Duration Estimation");

// Case 1: Short dialogue
const est1 = estimateBeatDuration("Con nhá»‡n Ä‘ang á»Ÿ Ä‘Ã¢y.", "spider in jar");

// Case 2: Longer dialogue
const longText = "Táº¡i sao con nhá»‡n nÃ y láº¡i cÃ³ thá»ƒ sá»‘ng sÃ³t vÃ  nhá»‹n Äƒn trong suá»‘t 8 thÃ¡ng mÃ  váº«n hoÃ n toÃ n bÃ¬nh thÆ°á»ng?";
const est2 = estimateBeatDuration(longText, "character looking curiously at jar with spider");
assert(est2.durationSeconds > est1.durationSeconds && est2.durationSeconds <= 12.0, `Longer dialogue estimated duration properly scales: got ${est2.durationSeconds.toFixed(2)}s`);

console.log("\n==================================================");
console.log("TEST 2: Safe Stream Lifecycle (No 'Controller is already closed')");
console.log("==================================================");

// Simulating the safe controller behavior implemented in route.ts
let isStreamClosed = false;
let isStreamCancelled = false;
const enqueueCalls: string[] = [];
let closeCallCount = 0;

const mockController = {
  enqueue: (chunk: string) => {
    if (isStreamClosed || isStreamCancelled) {
      throw new TypeError("Controller is already closed");
    }
    enqueueCalls.push(chunk);
  },
  close: () => {
    if (isStreamClosed) {
      throw new TypeError("Controller is already closed");
    }
    isStreamClosed = true;
    closeCallCount++;
  }
};

const safeEnqueue = (data: any) => {
  if (isStreamClosed || isStreamCancelled) return false;
  try {
    mockController.enqueue(JSON.stringify(data));
    return true;
  } catch (err) {
    return false;
  }
};

const safeClose = () => {
  if (isStreamClosed) return;
  try {
    mockController.close();
  } catch (e) {
    // idempotent
  }
};

// 2.1 Enqueueing when open
assert(safeEnqueue({ type: "init" }) === true, "safeEnqueue works normally when open");
assert(enqueueCalls.length === 1, "Chunk recorded");

// 2.2 safeClose called once
safeClose();
assert(isStreamClosed === true, "Stream marked closed");
assert(closeCallCount === 1, "Controller closed exactly once");

// 2.3 safeClose called again (idempotent)
safeClose();
assert(closeCallCount === 1, "Subsequent safeClose did not invoke controller.close again (No crash)");

// 2.4 safeEnqueue called after stream is closed
const enqAfterClose = safeEnqueue({ type: "late_event" });
assert(enqAfterClose === false, "safeEnqueue safely suppressed late events after stream closure");
assert(enqueueCalls.length === 1, "No late chunks enqueued");

// 2.5 Stream cancellation simulation
isStreamClosed = false;
isStreamCancelled = true;
const enqAfterCancel = safeEnqueue({ type: "event_cancelled" });
assert(enqAfterCancel === false, "safeEnqueue safely suppressed events when stream cancelled");

console.log("\n==================================================");
console.log("TEST 3: RequestId & Sensitive Log Sanitization");
console.log("==================================================");

// Redaction of API keys and tokens
const testRedactionKey = "AIza" + "Sy" + "x".repeat(33);
const dirtyLog1 = `Calling Gemini endpoint https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-2.5-flash:generateContent?key=${testRedactionKey}`;
const cleanLog1 = sanitizeLog(dirtyLog1);
assert(!cleanLog1.includes(testRedactionKey), "Google API Key redacted from log");
assert(cleanLog1.includes("key=[REDACTED]"), "Google API Key query param replaced with key=[REDACTED]");


const dirtyLog2 = "Authorization: Bearer ya29.a0AfH6SMD-fake-token-1234567890";
const cleanLog2 = sanitizeLog(dirtyLog2);
assert(!cleanLog2.includes("ya29.a0AfH6SMD-fake-token-1234567890"), "Bearer token redacted from log");

// HTTP Status extraction
assert(extractHttpStatus({ status: 503 }) === 503, "Extracted status 503 from error.status");
assert(extractHttpStatus(new Error("503 Service Unavailable: overloaded")) === 503, "Extracted status 503 from message");
assert(extractHttpStatus(new Error("429 ResourceExhausted")) === 429, "Extracted status 429 from message");
assert(extractHttpStatus(new Error("Generic network failure")) === 500, "Default fallback status 500");

assert(reusedScenes[0].id === "scene_1" && reusedScenes[1].id === "scene_2", "Scene 1 & 2 fully preserved without re-running");

console.log("\n==================================================");
console.log("TEST 5: Concurrency Lock & Abort Propagation");
console.log("==================================================");

let activeTask: { requestId: string; controller: AbortController } | null = null;

function requestPipelineRun(requestId: string): { ok: boolean; status: number; controller?: AbortController } {
  if (activeTask) {
    return { ok: false, status: 409 }; // 409 Conflict
  }
  const controller = new AbortController();
  activeTask = { requestId, controller };
  return { ok: true, status: 200, controller };
}

function cancelPipeline(requestId: string): boolean {
  if (activeTask && activeTask.requestId === requestId) {
    activeTask.controller.abort();
    activeTask = null;
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

// 5.1 First run starts
const run1 = requestPipelineRun("req_001");
assert(run1.ok === true && run1.status === 200, "First request successfully accepted (HTTP 200)");

// 5.2 Concurrent run while active
const run2 = requestPipelineRun("req_002");
assert(run2.ok === false && run2.status === 409, "Second concurrent request rejected with HTTP 409 Conflict");

// 5.3 User cancels run1
let signalAborted = false;
run1.controller?.signal.addEventListener("abort", () => {
  signalAborted = true;
});
const cancelled = cancelPipeline("req_001");
assert(cancelled === true, "Cancel pipeline returned true");
assert(signalAborted === true, "AbortSignal received abort event");
assert(activeTask === null, "Active task released upon cancellation");

// 5.4 Subsequent run can now start
const run3 = requestPipelineRun("req_003");
assert(run3.ok === true && run3.status === 200, "After cancellation, new request is accepted (HTTP 200)");
cancelPipeline("req_003");

console.log("\n==================================================");
console.log("TEST 6: 1080p60 Format & Frame Constraints");
console.log("==================================================");

// Frame calculation check at 60 fps
const fps = 60;
const testDuration = 4.5; // 4.5 seconds
const expectedFrames = Math.round(testDuration * fps);
assert(expectedFrames === 270, `4.5s at 60fps calculates exactly 270 frames: got ${expectedFrames}`);

const remotionCompositionProps = {
  width: 1920,
  height: 1080,
  fps: 60,
};
assert(remotionCompositionProps.width === 1920 && remotionCompositionProps.height === 1080, "Composition resolution is 1920x1080 Full HD");
assert(remotionCompositionProps.fps === 60, "Composition frame rate is 60 fps");

} else {
  console.error(">>> SOME TESTS FAILED! <<<");

}
